# BlueAI Product V2 Design System

The target design system the PM builds the next iterations of BlueAI Product V2 from. Every part
carries a status: **In V2** (carried as is), **Changed from V2** (V2 today next to the system, with
why), or **New** (why the product needs it). The live guide is `blueai-product-v2-ds/index.html`.

_Generated by `tools/build-design-md.js` from `design-md/*.md` in catalog order. Edit the partials,
not this file._

# Start

## 00 Start here

**Status:** Meta (no parts).

**Why:** One page that says what this is, who it is for and how to use it, so nobody has to reverse-engineer the folder.

### What this is

The target design system for BlueAI Product V2: the parts, colours, words and screens the next iterations of V2 are built from. It is minimal on purpose, every decision is argued from the product concept (section 01), and it is one family with the onBlue Agentic site the partner comes from and goes back to.

It is built for the PM building V2 next, and for anyone who picks up a V2 screen after him. V2's own files were not changed: the system sits next to V2 (`public/blueai-product-v2-ds/`) and shows V2 live where it can.

### Two looks

The system has two looks over the same tokens and kit. **V2 today** (`tokens-v2-today.css`) sets every token to what V2 ships now: Plus Jakarta Sans, #1990FF, Tailwind gray and slate, soft shadows, the indigo gradient on the user's chat bubble. **Target** (`tokens.css`) is where the system is going: Inter, a darker action blue, 1px lines instead of shadows, one accent. Build in V2 today, so anything new matches the V2 around it, until V2's old screens are moved onto the kit; then switch once. In V2's `index.html`, link `tokens.css` and then `tokens-v2-today.css`, and set `data-v2-look="today"` on the `html` element. To switch to Target, remove that attribute. The guide header has the same switch (Look: V2 today / Target); it changes the specimens and screens, not the guide around them.

### How to read a part

Every part carries one status; section headers count them.

| Status | What it means | What the section shows |
|---|---|---|
| In V2 | Carried from V2 as it is. | V2 rendered live where possible, its copy quoted exactly, with the V2 file and line. |
| Changed from V2 | V2 has it, the system changes it. | "V2 today" on the left, the system on the right, then what changed and why. |
| New | V2 has no equivalent. | Why the product needs it, and the copy marked Proposed. |

A value shown as `file:line` is a V2 source. The Spec drawer under a part lists each value, the token that now holds it and the V2 line it came from, and `tools/ds-check.js` proves that line still says it.

### Where to start

1. 01 The product concept and 02 Principles: the frame every decision is judged against.
2. 73 Recommendations for V2: every V2 difference as a fix list, P1 first, plus the decisions only the PM can make.
3. 74 How to add a feature: from a new task to a screen merged into V2.
4. Then the part or screen you need. 75 Coverage says what exists; 76 Known gaps says what the guide cannot prove.

### Where things live

All under `public/blueai-product-v2-ds/`. V2 itself is `public/blueai-product-v2/`.

| Path | What it holds |
|---|---|
| `index.html` | The live guide. Every section, live parts, V2 today next to the system. |
| `DESIGN.md` | This file: the written spec, built only from the `design-md/` partials by `tools/build-design-md.js`. It is not generated from the guide: each guide section writes its own notes and spec rows, so the two are kept in step by hand. When you change a part, change its partial and its guide section together (section 74, step 7), and where they disagree, the kit's code is the truth. |
| `tokens.css` | Every colour, size, radius, shadow, layer and timing as a `--v2-*` custom property: the Target look. With `tokens-v2-today.css`, the only place raw values live. |
| `tokens-v2-today.css` | The same tokens re-valued to V2 today, under `[data-v2-look="today"]` (Two looks, above). |
| `kit/` | The parts, on `window.V2Kit` (`button.jsx`, `run-card.jsx`, ...), and their `v2-*` styles (`actions.css`, `chat.css`, ...). |
| `screens/` | Whole screens composed from the kit, on `window.V2Screens`. |
| `guide/` | The guide's own chrome: `catalog.jsx` (the section list), `sections/`, `refs/` (the V2 sources), `app.jsx`. |
| `design-md/` | One partial per section; edit these, then rebuild DESIGN.md. |
| `tools/` | `ds-check.js` (the gate; check 6's named colours and checks 8 to 11 run in `ds-check-more.js`) and `build-design-md.js`. |

### How to run the checks

From `public/blueai-product-v2-ds/`, with Node and one dependency, Babel's parser. ds-check looks for it in this repo's `node_modules` (run `npm install` at the repo root), then in the main `N:/Antigravity Main/blueai` checkout's, or takes a `@babel/parser` build named in the `DS_BABEL_PARSER` environment variable. With none of these the run fails with an error instead of skipping the parse step. The parser parses; it does not compile, so a transform error that only Babel standalone raises in the browser can still pass.

- `node tools/ds-check.js` before every hand-off; it must end with 0 errors (add `--missing-ok` only while files are still being written). It proves: every V2 value or string the guide quotes still sits on its V2 line; no em dash in any form; every ref id a guide section names exists; every .jsx and .js parses; every .jsx, .js and .css is 300 lines or fewer; the catalog, section files, partials and index.html agree; no raw colour outside `tokens.css` and `tokens-v2-today.css`, no other stylesheet defines a `--v2-` property, and the two define the same tokens; every kit part and screen the catalog names is on its global; the size lists the JSX copies (Icon and Avatar sizes) equal their tokens; no .jsx in `kit/`, `screens/` or `guide/` uses object rest destructuring (Babel hoists its key list to one program-level `_excluded` that the next file overwrites, so use a local `omit(props, KEYS)`).
- `node tools/build-design-md.js` after editing anything in `design-md/`. Never edit DESIGN.md by hand.

The checks read files, not pixels. How a part looks is checked by eye in the guide.

### House rules

- Tokens, by the rule in `tokens.css:1-9`: every colour in `kit/` or `screens/` is a `--v2-*` token, and so is every size two parts share (the Size family, section 10); ds-check fails a raw colour and a rule that types a Size token's value raw. Geometry that belongs to one part only (a switch track, a checkbox tick, a badge dot) stays px in that part's rule, and a size one part repeats is a local custom property without the `--v2-` prefix (`--ledger-date-w`).
- Kit classes start `v2-`, guide classes `g-`. Hover, focus-visible, active, disabled, loading and error live in CSS, not in mouse handlers.
- Files are IIFEs for the browser's Babel: no import or export. Kit parts go on `window.V2Kit`, screens on `window.V2Screens`.
- At most 300 lines per .jsx, .js or .css file. Split before you pass it.
- Copy: quote V2 exactly where a part is In V2; use the glossary (section 70): Always ask and Automatic, AI credits, Sign in and Sign out. New copy is marked Proposed until the PM approves it. No em dash.
- Accessibility floor: visible focus, labels on icon buttons, 4.5:1 for text, 32px targets, reduced motion respected.

## 01 The product concept

**Status:** Meta (no parts).

**Why:** The frame every decision in this system is judged against. A part that does not serve it does not get built.

### The concept

BlueAI is a paid worker that lives on the partner's Windows PC. A partner is accepted on the onBlue Agentic site, installs BlueAI, signs in with the account they applied with, sets the worker up once in a short conversation, and from then on the worker runs once a day on its own: it warms up the partner's YouTube account (Instagram next), checks the jobs board, takes a paid job (asking first on Always ask, on its own on Automatic), and submits it.

Every successful day is checked within a day: one tick while pending, two ticks once verified. Each verified day counts toward a 20-day program that ends on Nov 1, with cash out on the site's dashboard. AI credits, granted every day, are the worker's fuel, not money.

So the product is not a chat assistant with a worker bolted on. It is a small, quiet control room for an agent acting on the partner's own accounts.

### The partner journey

Site, app daily, site. The app is the middle of a loop, so it has to look and speak like the two ends.

1. **The site (onBlue Agentic):** the partner applies and is accepted, then installs BlueAI from the dashboard link.
2. **The app, every day:** signs in with the account they applied with, sets the worker up once, then the worker runs daily on its own. Each day shows one tick while pending, two once verified.
3. **The site again (the dashboard):** days and money read the same as in the app; cash out happens here, never in the app.

### The product's three jobs

Everything else (chat, settings, credits) serves these. A feature that serves none of them needs a reason before it gets a screen.

| Job | Where the system does it |
|---|---|
| Show what the worker is doing today and whether the day counts. | 57 Home, 42 Worker card, 36 Run card, 43 Program progress, 58 Activity |
| Keep the partner in control: consent per app, pause, disconnect, see exactly what was posted. | 46 Always ask and Automatic, 45 Job preview, 62 Worker settings, 63 Connected apps, 59 Run detail |
| Make money and days read the same in the app and on the site. | 41 The Worker record, 44 Day done, 47 Credits balance, 70 Copy and voice |

### The numbers, as V2 has them today

The system reads these from one Worker record (section 41), never from a second constant.

| What | V2 today | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Program | 20 days, ends Nov 1, $1 per verified day, $10 day-20 bonus | `worker_day_done.jsx:38` |
| Money rule note | $1 credited at the end of each successful day (under review) | `worker_day_done.jsx:5` |
| Cash out | From $10, after the period ends; under $10 the balance carries over (the site's dashboard says the same: "Payment minimum: $10") | `worker_day_done.jsx:6`; `onblue/apply.html:1360, 2292-2293` |
| The site's program | At least 20 days each month to earn $30 | `onblue/apply.html:1285` |
| AI credits | 2,000 granted every day | `index.html:730` |
| Modes | Always ask and Automatic, per app | `blueai/worker_flow.jsx:78` |
| Ticks | Pending verification, then verified | `blueai/worker_day_done.jsx:97` |

The money model is decision D3: the app's $1 a day plus a $10 bonus, or the site's $30 for 20 days. Until the PM picks one, the system shows days, not dollars (section 73). The $10 cash-out minimum holds under both, so it is stated wherever cash out is: a line that says cash out is open is shown only when the site says this partner can cash out (`cashOutOpen` true, section 41); its under-the-minimum answer (false) gets the site's own words, and while it has not answered (null) the line only says where to look: 'See your balance and cash out on Earnings.'

## 02 Principles

**Status:** Meta (no parts).

**Why:** Every part in the system argues from one of these. When a new screen and a principle disagree, the principle wins, or the principle changes here first, with its reason.

### P1 The worker is the product; chat is a window onto it

- **Rule:** Home shows the worker and today's run first. A run, its waiting question and its result live in one Worker record above any screen; no navigation can erase them.
- **Why:** V2 unmounts the chat (and the run) when the partner opens Credits, History, Profile or another tab (index.html:965-981, chat_product.jsx:33, 79); the failed-day toast then opens a screen with no Retry (worker_day_done.jsx:203, index.html:1065). A worker that runs unattended needs a state that outlives its views.
- **Prevents:** Lost runs, lost Retry, a day count that goes backwards overnight (worker_day_done.jsx:39 vs :173).

### P2 Money and days are said once, the same way everywhere

- **Rule:** One ledger, one grant constant, one Earnings URL. Green means a state that was reached (verified, done); dollar figures stay in ink. Pending is never painted as verified.
- **Why:** Four credit numbers (2,000, 2,500, 500, 300), two Earnings URLs, green dollars on a pending day, and three money models across site and app (inv-dna findings) are the fastest way to lose a paid partner's trust.
- **Prevents:** 'Where is my $1' support tickets and the feeling of being shorted.

### P3 Say the consequence before the choice

- **Rule:** Any control that can cost a day, money or account control states that cost on or beside the control (Skip, Stop, Sign out, Disconnect, turning Automatic on). Disabled controls say why.
- **Why:** 'Not now' on the job ask silently forfeits the day and is only explained afterwards (worker_flow.jsx:121, 149; worker_day_done.jsx:118). The site already follows 'disabled with a reason' (onblue/apply.html:408-416).
- **Prevents:** Choices that feel like traps.

### P4 Every promise has a control

- **Rule:** 'Pause, stop, or disconnect anytime' and 'turn it off anytime' each map to a findable control in Settings > Worker and Connected apps, reachable from the home in two taps.
- **Why:** The promises exist in live copy (worker_flow.jsx:67, agent_permissions.jsx:188) but only one has a screen: since 2026-10-14 Settings has a Worker card that turns Automatic off (settings.jsx:11-20); pause, stop and disconnect have none (settings.jsx:144-159).
- **Prevents:** Consent that cannot be withdrawn.

### P5 Quiet by default

- **Rule:** Surfaces separate with 1px alpha-black lines, not shadows; shadows only on things that float (menus, dialogs). One action blue. The brand gradient only on the BlueAI mark and the credits glyph. Colour inside the thread is carried by icons, plus one attention tone for 'the worker needs you'.
- **Why:** V2 carries 154 shadow declarations (55 recipes, 15 coloured glows), three competing accents and eight tinted message surfaces (inv-foundations, inv-chat). The partner sits on this screen daily; the one thing that matters must stand out.
- **Prevents:** A UI where everything competes and nothing reads as the next action.

### P6 One family with onBlue Agentic

- **Rule:** Share the site's neutrals, line weights, blue, greens, error reds, card radius, easing, focus ring, sign-in gate scrim, reduced-motion rule and vocabulary. Do not share its marketing ladder, ambient effects, header or footer.
- **Why:** The partner moves site, app, site daily; V2 already contains the site's language in window.SiteKit (blueai/site_kit.jsx:12-20) but the app itself speaks a third style.
- **Prevents:** The journey reading as three companies at the moment trust is asked for.

### P7 Accessible baseline, not an afterthought

- **Rule:** Text 4.5:1 or better (including the primary button label), visible :focus-visible on every control, keyboard menus and dialogs with focus moved in and returned, hit targets 24px minimum and 32px for anything touching consent or money, role=log on the thread, and one global reduced-motion rule.
- **Why:** White on #1990FF is 3.24:1 on every primary button; faint caveats sit at 2.5:1; one :focus rule ships (index.html:57); no overlay moves focus.
- **Prevents:** Partners who cannot read the caveat that explains their money.

### P8 Real parts, measured values, nothing dead carried

- **Rule:** Every V2 value the guide prints cites its V2 file:line and needle and is re-checked by tools/ds-check.js. Parts marked In V2 render V2's own window.X where possible. Dead code is listed, never specimened as product.
- **Why:** About 3,000 of V2's 11,042 lines never render; harvesting them would import an abandoned violet 'earn credits' language (credits_widget.jsx holds 57 of 112 #7B4CFF uses).
- **Prevents:** The PM building from code that does not ship, and docs drifting from source.

### One family with onBlue Agentic (P6 in detail)

Shared means the same value as the site; adapted means the same idea tuned for a 419px daily tool; not shared is marketing the app leaves behind.

| Rule | Relation | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Neutral ink ramp #0b0b0d / #3c4049 / #5f636b | shared | Same text colours on the site and in the app (onblue.css:300-309). |
| Meta ink #6e737c (site dim #767b85) | adapted | One step darker so 12px captions clear 4.5:1; the site's dim measures about 4.2:1. |
| Alpha-black 1px lines 10/15/8/20% | shared | The site's clearest DNA, same values (onblue.css:274-275, 319, 376). |
| Lines alone separate in-flow surfaces | adapted (shadow dropped) | The site's light theme also casts a faint resting shadow on cards, panels and ghost buttons (onblue.css:388, 993, 1486; apply.html:85-90); the product drops it, so a shadow only ever means 'this floats'. |
| Surfaces a fraction apart, alternating card / panel / row | shared | Depth reads as structure, not murk. |
| Brand blue #156efe for focus, selection and progress | shared | One blue across the journey. |
| Action blue #1268f0 and blue ink #0d5ad6 | adapted | #156efe is 4.47:1 with white; the product needs 4.5:1 on every primary label. |
| Selection = 12% blue fill + 50% blue border + ink label | shared | One 'chosen' look on site and app. |
| Green means reached; money stays in ink | shared | Verified days look the same on the dashboard; pending money is not painted as paid. |
| Error red #c03a40 / #d0454a / ring 16% | shared | One danger set; also the destructive fill (5.36:1). |
| Card radius 16, controls 6, pills 999 | shared (pills on chips: adapted) | Strongest single 'same family' cue is the button shape. The site's application chips are radius-9 toggles; the product keeps the pill on chips (section 15). |
| Site near-step radii 7/9/11/12/14 | not shared | Too many near steps for a 419px tool. |
| Inter only; weights to 600; negative tracking scales with size; uppercase only for eyebrows | shared | Calm hierarchy from size and ink, not weight. |
| Dashboard working-screen type tier | adapted | The app is a working screen like the dashboard; scaled for a 419px window. |
| Marketing type ladder and responsive ladder | not shared | The product is a fixed desktop window. |
| Ease cubic-bezier(0.16,1,0.3,1), symmetric departures, nothing loops unattended, global reduced motion | shared | Nearly identical to V2's house curve; costs nothing. |
| 0.35s marketing transition standard | adapted | App state changes run at 120 to 240ms. |
| Focus: 2px blue outline offset 2; fields border + 3px 16% ring | shared | Keyboard users meet one system across site and app. |
| Sign-in gate scrim rgba(248,249,251,0.88) + 14px blur | shared | 'Sign in' looks the same on the site and in the app. |
| Frosted veil for every dialog | not shared | A white dialog on the site's pale veil separates only by its shadow (its 10% line 1.19:1 against the veil); the dim veil puts its edge at 3.3:1 (WCAG 1.4.11), so a decision reads as blocking at a glance (the Overlay family, `tokens.css:161-162`). The frost stays for sign-in. |
| Navy-tinted casts at low alpha, no glows | adapted | Three levels only (none, popover, dialog). |
| Button ladder: one primary per view, secondary, quiet, link; disabled with a reason | shared | Ranks Run, Got it, Try again, Allow, Not now. |
| Underlined ink links that turn blue on hover | shared | Frees blue for selection. |
| Recessed tabs with one raised chip and counts | adapted (shadow dropped, one selected look) | Activity and Notifications need tabs; take the family's shape without the lit tab's card shadow, and the lit tab wears the product's one selected look, the Segmented's select fill and blue edge (apply.html:467-473; section 28). |
| Ledger rows with a fixed date column; empty copy names what will appear | shared | The run log and the site's transactions read as one record. |
| Muted note with one bold ink figure | shared | Pending verification lines, credit notes. |
| Payout pill (green receipt) | adapted (plain line) | 'Yesterday's run verified.' is its job, drawn as a plain 12 ink-3 line with its tick (section 43): the site's pill (green line, 10% green fill, onblue.css:2102-2115) would put a second green box on a small strip whose bar is already green. |
| Progress bar toward a goal | adapted | Merged with the product's segmented DayBar, which adds the pending state the site lacks. |
| Sheet anatomy and motion | adapted | Solid dim veil instead of frost outside sign-in. |
| Grain, glyph field, aberration, WebGL model, card sheen, terminal, badge, band note, header, footer | not shared | Marketing ambience competes with a daily tool and costs GPU. |
| Copy voice and vocabulary | shared | Same words on both sides; conflicts go to the PM as decisions. |
| 'Log out' on the site | adapted | Product uses 'Sign out' to pair with 'Sign in'; recommend the site align. |
| 'Reward' and 'campaigns and tasks' on the site | adapted | The program card and its sheet say 'Reward' (apply.html:1280, 1470), How it works says 'its own reward' and 'its reward is added to your earnings' (:1434, 1436), the agent intro says 'completes campaigns and tasks' (:1849). The product says Earnings for money and job for paid work (section 70); recommend the site align, or the PM accepts the site's word for both. |
| 'Withdraw' on the site | adapted | The site's cash-out sheet says 'Withdraw' and 'withdrawal' (apply.html:1445, 1532, 1541); the product says cash out (section 70), the word the program rule uses; recommend the site align. |
| Review dock convention (onblue-dev.js) | shared | Review controls live outside the product and add no tokens; V2's Preview panel follows it. |
| window.SiteKit inside V2 | adapted | Seed for one family token object; it has drifted (48 vs 44 height, 0.12s vs 0.18s press, 32 vs 38 mark). |

# Foundations

## 03 Colour

**Status:** Ink ramp, Surfaces and lines, Action blue, Status tones, Brand gradient: all Changed from V2.

**Why:** BlueAI is a quiet control room for an agent working on the partner's own accounts, and the partner moves between it and the onBlue site every day. V2 paints with two Tailwind grey ramps split 178/178, seven blue fills, an indigo nav, four parallel status maps and the brand gradient in three places on one top bar; its primary blue fails contrast with its own white labels (3.24:1, index.html:351) and its faintest text carries its most important caveats (2.5:1, chat_bubbles.jsx:124, account_gate.jsx:31). The system keeps one hue per job and shares the site's neutrals, lines, blue, greens and reds, so the app and the site read as one family (P5, P6, P7).

### Ink ramp (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** All text and idle icon colour, in one hue.

**Anatomy.** Four steps plus placeholder and on-colour: `--v2-ink` #0b0b0d (titles, figures, money), `--v2-ink-2` #3c4049 (body, labels), `--v2-ink-3` #5f636b (descriptions, notes, idle icons, every consequence line), `--v2-ink-4` #6e737c (meta only), `--v2-ink-placeholder` #9ca2ad, `--v2-on-color` #ffffff, `--v2-on-color-muted`.

**Variants.** None: a role picks the step.

**Sizes.** Not applicable.

**States.** On surface: ink 19.67, ink-2 10.39, ink-3 6.03, ink-4 4.77 (:1). ink-4 on canvas 4.60, on panel 4.69. Everywhere else ink-4 drops under 4.5:1: sunken 4.42, blue tint 4.29, selected 4.06, success tint 4.36, attention tint 4.37, danger tint 4.17, hover wash 4.32, press wash 4.08. So ink-4 sits only on surface, panel or canvas at rest; meta on anything tinted, sunken, selected, hovered or pressed is ink-3 (the menu's hint already does this, kit/overlays.css:114; the type style for it is `.v2-text-caption`, section 04). V2 today: `'Working on it…'` in #94a3b8 (ChatBubbles.ThinkingBubble, any chat run), `'App Player will be signed out too.'` in #9ca3af (the sign-in gate, `?signin=browser&player=other`).

**Props.** Tokens only. Text styles carry their ink (section 04); override with a token, never a hex.

**Motion.** Colour changes use `--v2-dur-1` and `--v2-ease-standard`.

**Accessibility.** Body text 4.5:1 at least on its ground (section 09 computes every pair). ink-4 is never the only carrier of a consequence: a line that says what happens next ("signed out too", "won’t count") is ink-3 or stronger. Placeholder is never text that must be read.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do pick the step by role. Do put money and credits figures in ink. Don't use ink-4 for a caveat, or on anything but surface, panel or canvas at rest. Don't add a grey for one screen.

### Surfaces and lines (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** Grounds a fraction of a percent apart, separated by 1px alpha-black lines (the site's line values, shared). The site pairs those lines with faint resting card shadows; the product drops the shadows and lets the lines alone separate in-flow surfaces (section 02, P6: adapted).

**Anatomy.** Grounds: `--v2-canvas` #fafbfb (window), `--v2-surface` #fff (cards, bubbles, bars, dialogs), `--v2-surface-panel` #fdfdfd (inset panel inside a card), `--v2-surface-sunken` #f6f6f8 (wells, tracks, neutral chips). Lines: `--v2-line` 10% (every edge), `--v2-line-strong` 15% (fields, secondary buttons), `--v2-line-hair` 8% (dividers inside a card), `--v2-line-hover` 20% (hover firm-up), `--v2-link-underline` 32% (the resting underline under an ink link, the site's own value). Washes: `--v2-wash-hover` 4.5%, `--v2-wash-press` 7%, layered over the fill.

**Variants.** Alternation: canvas, then a white card, then a panel one step down, then rows back to white.

**Sizes.** All lines 1px (`--v2-stroke`).

**States.** Rest; hover (wash over the fill); pressed (press wash); pressable card hover (line to `--v2-line-hover`). V2 today: the Settings tab cards (BluestacksCard, shadow plus border) at `?start=worker`, then Settings.

**Props.** Tokens only.

**Motion.** Background and border colour over `--v2-dur-1`.

**Accessibility.** Hover never lowers opacity (V2 does, index.html:352), so contrast holds while hovering.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do separate in-flow surfaces with a line. Do hover with a wash. Don't add a shadow to a card. Don't hover by opacity.

### Action blue (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** One blue hue in roles, so action, selection and focus are one colour family without failing contrast.

**Anatomy.** `--v2-blue` #156efe (focus, selected border, progress, current step, lit nav: the site's blue), `--v2-action` #1268f0 (filled buttons, send: 4.92:1 with white), `--v2-action-hover` #0f5ad4 (6.12:1), `--v2-blue-ink` #0d5ad6 (blue text on tints: 6.09:1), `--v2-blue-tint` and `--v2-blue-tint-line` (info notice, user bubble), `--v2-select` and `--v2-select-line` (selected chip, choice card, segmented on).

**Variants.** Fill, text, tint, selected.

**Sizes.** Not applicable.

**States.** Rest, hover (`--v2-action-hover`), focus (2px `--v2-blue` outline, offset 2), disabled (`--v2-opacity-disabled` with a visible reason). V2 today: every primary is #1990FF with white (3.24:1): New chat on the top bar, Switch account on the gate (`?signin=browser&player=other`).

**Props.** Tokens only; the Button reads them (section 11).

**Motion.** Fill colour over `--v2-dur-1`.

**Accessibility.** White text goes on `--v2-action`, never on `--v2-blue` (4.47:1). Blue text uses `--v2-blue-ink`.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do keep brand blue for focus, selection and progress. Do use action blue only for fills. Don't bring back the indigo nav (#4f46e5 on #eef2ff, shared.jsx:305). Don't use a warm colour for link text.

### Status tones (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** Five tones every badge, banner, notice, tick, day bar and run card read from one table.

**Anatomy.** Each tone is tint, line, ink (and icon where it differs). Neutral: `--v2-surface-sunken`, `--v2-line`, `--v2-ink-2`. Info: `--v2-blue-tint`, `--v2-blue-tint-line`, `--v2-blue-ink`. Success: `--v2-success-tint`, `--v2-success-line`, `--v2-success-ink` #15803d (icon `--v2-success` #16a34a). Attention (the worker needs your answer, the PM's yellow): `--v2-attention-tint` #fdf6cf, `--v2-attention-line` #f3e8a8, `--v2-attention-ink` #8a6116. Danger: `--v2-danger-tint` #fdecec, `--v2-danger-line` rgba(208,69,74,0.32) (the pale banner and P1 tag edge, like the other tones' lines), `--v2-danger-ink` #c03a40 (also the destructive fill), plus one strong stroke, `--v2-danger-edge` #d0454a (4.53:1 on white, so it reads at 1px): the edge of an invalid TextField, Select or Checkbox (`kit/inputs.css:31, 149`).

**Variants.** Five tones. Orange heads-up is not a tone: it is a neutral notice whose icon is attention-coloured.

**Sizes.** Not applicable.

**States.** Neutral (paused, skipped, pending verification, out of credits in the composer), info (running, checking, a fact with nothing to do), success (verified, done, connected), attention (needs you, and nothing has gone wrong: a waiting job, a run that didn't start because the PC was off, out of credits on the RunCard, an account signed out), danger (something went wrong or a day is lost: failed, can't run, not counted). One rule picks between the last two: danger when the run failed, cannot start or the day is lost, even while Try again or a fix can still save today; attention when nothing failed and the partner's answer or action is what is needed. Out of credits in the composer is neutral: it explains a locked field, it does not ask.

**Pending verification, one rule.** A pending day is neutral wherever it is a status: one tick in `--v2-ink-4` (StatusTick, section 43), a neutral Badge with the `check` mark (section 16), the RunCard's Done badge (section 36). Blue touches it in exactly three places, all meaning "in, not yet checked" rather than a status: the DayBar's pending segment (1.5 `--v2-blue` inset outline, section 43), the run chip's pending dot (the same 1.5 blue ring, `kit/shell.css:76`, section 30) and the DayDone badge (`--v2-blue-tint` with its tick in `--v2-blue-ink`, section 44). Never green until verified. V2 today: the dev Preview's Chat states row (ChatBubbles.ChatStatesPreview); the AI Credits banner at `?credits=0`.

**Props.** The five tones are the table every part reads, but each part names its own prop (check this list before passing one):
- Badge `tone`: the five; anything else is neutral (`kit/display.jsx:21-26`).
- Banner `tone`: info, success, attention, danger, and `offline` for its neutral (sunken, line, ink-3 icon; section 21); anything else is info, so a neutral banner is `tone="offline"` with its own icon (the AI-mode conflict uses `alert`, section 49) (`kit/notice.jsx:16-18`).
- NoticeBubble `kind`: the five, plus the aliases headsup (attention), fail (danger) and needsInput (attention with the help glyph) for older callers; anything else is attention (`kit/thread.jsx:72-86`).
- ComposerNotice `tone`: drawn by the Banner, so the Banner's tones, plus `neutral`, its own name for the Banner's offline look with a pause glyph (`kit/composer.jsx:38-46`).
- Toast `tone`: success, info, error, loading; danger is read as error, so it is announced assertively (section 24, `kit/toast.jsx`).
- ProgressBar: no tone prop for status; `error` and `paused` booleans freeze it red or grey, and it turns success at max (`kit/progress.jsx:20-26`).

**Motion.** None of its own.

**Accessibility.** Each ink passes 4.5:1 on its own tint and on white. Tone never travels by colour alone: an icon or a word goes with it.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do use success for a reached state, matching the site's dashboard. Do keep money in ink ($6 earned). Don't paint dollars green (V2 stopped on 2026-10-14). Don't invent a sixth tone for one screen.

### Brand gradient (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** Identity, not action.

**Anatomy.** `--v2-gradient-brand`, linear-gradient(90deg, #0EA4C5, #7B4CFF).

**Variants.** The BlueAI mark and wordmark; the credits glyph (`<Icon name="credits" />`, the gradient through V2's star).

**Sizes.** Mark per section 30; glyph 16, 20, 24.

**States.** Mark, credits glyph. V2 today: the top bar at `?start=worker` shows it three times (wordmark, pill ring, pill number, index.html:335-346), and the user bubble uses a cousin gradient with an indigo glow (chat_bubbles.jsx:30).

**Props.** None: use BrandMark or the credits Icon.

**Motion.** None. The gradient never animates.

**Accessibility.** Never carries text that must be read: the credits number next to the glyph is ink.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do keep it on the mark and the credits glyph. Don't use it as a ring, number, bubble, button, border or text fill. Which mark the family uses is decision D8.

## 04 Type

**Status:** Type ladder, Page titles, Numerals: all Changed from V2.

**Why:** The partner reads this app every day in a 419px window, often at small sizes on Windows. V2 uses 30 font sizes, seven page-title recipes, weight 800 71 times (650, 750 and 900 are requested but never loaded), and four typefaces in a new partner's first minute. Its stack puts SF before Plus Jakarta Sans, so Macs and Windows show different products (index.html:34). The site is Inter end to end; one face, thirteen styles and three weights make the app read as the site's working screen (P6).

### Type ladder (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** One recipe per text role, so size, weight, leading, tracking and ink travel together and no screen re-decides how loud a line is.

**Anatomy.** Thirteen classes in kit/base.css (size / weight / leading / tracking, ink), plus the row label the list-row parts set themselves:
- `.v2-text-display` 40 / 600 / 1 / -0.03em, ink, tabular: credits balance.
- `.v2-text-greeting` 24 / 600 / 1.2 / -0.02em, ink: home greeting, welcome title.
- `.v2-text-title-lg` 20 / 600 / 1.2 / -0.02em, ink: every screen and dialog title.
- `.v2-text-numeral-sm` 20 / 600 / 1 / -0.02em, ink, tabular: "7" in "7 of 20 days".
- `.v2-text-title` 16 / 600 / 1.2 / -0.01em, ink: card titles, section heads.
- `.v2-text-mini-title` 13 / 600 / 1.35 / 0.02em, ink: the label over a figure in a card (StatCard), sentence case; the site's `.mini-title` (`onblue/onblue.css:1935-1942`).
- `.v2-text-body` 14 / 400 / 1.5, ink-2: bubbles, composer, paragraphs, fields.
- `.v2-text-body-sm` 13 / 400 / 1.5, ink-3: descriptions, notes, consequences. A `<strong>` inside turns ink 600 (fact muted, figure ink).
- `.v2-text-label` 13 / 500 / 1.35 / -0.01em, ink-2: buttons, chips, nav, menu rows.
- Row label (no class): 14 / 500 / 1.35, ink. The list-row parts set it in their own CSS (ListRow, section 18), so a screen never sets it by hand.
- `.v2-text-meta` 12 / 400 / 1.35, ink-4 (4.77:1): timestamps, counts, hints, and only on surface, panel or canvas at rest. Never the only carrier of a consequence.
- `.v2-text-caption` 12 / 400 / 1.35, ink-3 (6.0:1): a small line that says something: disabled reasons, field helpers and messages, dialog footnotes, notes under a block (`kit/base.css:145-151`; Button, Checkbox, TextField, Dialog, Feedback, BYOK). It is also the small line on a tint, wash or sunken surface, where meta's ink-4 drops under 4.5:1 (4.42:1 on sunken).
- `.v2-text-eyebrow` 11 / 500 / 1 / 0.08em, uppercase, ink-3: section labels only. The 0.08em is adapted from the site's 0.14em.
- `.v2-text-mono` 13 / 400 / 1.35, mono, ink-2: keys, endpoints, model ids.

**Variants.** Faces: `--v2-font-ui` (Inter, then system-ui and Segoe UI), `--v2-font-mono`, `--v2-font-serif` (Instrument Serif italic, site-language sign-in pages only). Weights: 400 body, 500 labels, 600 titles and figures. Helpers: `.v2-tabular`, `.v2-truncate`, `.v2-prose`.

**Sizes.** 11, 12, 13, 14, 16, 20, 24, 40 (`--v2-text-*`). Nothing in between.

**States.** One per style. Sample copy in the guide is quoted from V2 (for example `'Every task and job BlueAI does uses credits.'`, index.html:536).

**Props.** Classes only. Inline font sizes are not allowed in kit or screens.

**Motion.** None.

**Accessibility.** Smallest running text 12 (meta and caption); 11 only for uppercase eyebrows. Body ink-2 or darker. Sentence case everywhere except the eyebrow, which is uppercase by CSS, not in the source string.

**Responsive.** Sizes do not change with the window height.

**Do / Don't.** Do pick a style by role. Do load Inter 400/500/600 only. Don't use 700 or 800. Don't set status words as uppercase eyebrows: they are Badges in sentence case. Decision D11 lets the PM keep Plus Jakarta Sans; the scale and weights hold either way.

### Page titles, V2 today (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** One title recipe for every screen and dialog.

**Anatomy.** `.v2-text-title-lg`: 20 / 600 / 1.2 / -0.02em, ink. In a ScreenHeader (section 32) or a dialog head (section 25).

**Variants.** None.

**Sizes.** 20.

**States.** V2 today has seven: AI Credits 19/800 (index.html:448), Chat History 22/800 (history.jsx:91), Scheduled tasks 20/700 (schedule.jsx:118), Coming Soon 22/800 in #1e293b (jobs.jsx:140), Before you start 20/800 (terms_sheet.jsx:114), Logout 19/800 (profile.jsx:75), modal 18/600 (shared.jsx:75). Reach them from `?start=worker` through the header menus and tabs.

**Props.** The ScreenHeader and Dialog own the title; screens pass a string.

**Motion.** None.

**Accessibility.** The screen title is the screen's `h1` and takes focus on navigation (`tabIndex=-1`, no visible ring).

**Responsive.** One line; long titles truncate with an ellipsis rather than wrap.

**Do / Don't.** Do use the same title on every screen. Don't style a title inline. Copy changes (Logout becomes Sign out) live in section 70.

### Numerals (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** Figures that matter (credits, days, money) read as facts: ink, tabular, never a gradient or a status colour.

**Anatomy.** `.v2-text-display` for the credits balance; `.v2-text-numeral-sm` beside a label for counts; inside notes, `<strong>` turns a figure ink 600.

**Variants.** Balance, count, money in a sentence.

**Sizes.** 40, 20, and body size inside notes.

**States.** Credits balance (V2: 54/800 gradient, 38/800 at zero, index.html:527, byok.jsx:51; reach with `?credits=0`). "7 of 20 days" (V2: the home strip's "Day 6 of 20" 13.5/800 ink, worker_day_done.jsx:152, reach with `?day=returning`; the popup's 22/800 green count went with its minimal popup, 2026-10-14). "$6 earned" (V2: 600 muted grey after the day count since 2026-10-14, 13.5/800 green before, worker_day_done.jsx:152; reach with `?day=returning&card=money`).

**Props.** Classes only; format with `toLocaleString('en-US')`.

**Motion.** Numbers change in place without counting animations.

**Accessibility.** Tabular figures stop numbers jumping as they change. A figure is never the only thing that says whether a day counted: the tick and its words do.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do put money in ink (the site's rule: money is a figure, not a state). Don't colour a count green to mean counted. Don't gradient a number.

## 05 Space and layout

**Status:** Space scale, Window budget: both Changed from V2.

**Why:** A small window that the partner opens every day has no room for near-misses. 59% of V2's 927 spacing values sit off a 4px grid, card padding alone has eight recipes, the chat column is 420px inside a 419px box (index.html:976), modals size in vh against the browser instead of the window (shared.jsx:73), and the 866px window does not fit a 1366 x 768 laptop. One scale, one gutter and a compact rule keep every screen aligned and on screen.

### Space scale (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** A 4px scale with one 2px step, one gutter, one card padding.

**Anatomy.** `--v2-space-0` 0, `-05` 2, `-1` 4, `-15` 6, `-2` 8, `-3` 12, `-4` 16, `-5` 20, `-6` 24, `-8` 32, `-10` 40. `--v2-gutter` is `--v2-space-4`. Layout utilities in kit/base.css: `.v2-stack` (column, gap 12), `.v2-hstack` (row, centred, gap 8; modifiers `--top`, `--baseline`, `--between`, `--end`; `.v2-wrap`), `.v2-gap-0` to `.v2-gap-6`, `.v2-grow`, `.v2-screen` and `.v2-screen-body`, `.v2-divider`, `.v2-sr-only`, `.v2-root`. (The horizontal stack is `.v2-hstack` because `.v2-row` is ListRow's class, section 18.)

**Variants.** Uses: 2 day-bar segment gaps; 4 icon to text in chips; 8 row gaps and button icon gap; 12 card stack gap and bubble vertical padding; 16 card padding, bubble horizontal padding and screen gutter; 20 dialog padding; 24 section gap and sheet padding; 32 and 40 empty states.

**Sizes.** As above.

**States.** Not applicable. V2 today: the user bubble pads '11px 14px 8px' (chat_bubbles.jsx:30), the progress strip '12px 15px 11px' (worker_day_done.jsx:176), two settings cards 16 and 18 (settings.jsx:25, 112); reach with `?day=returning`.

**Props.** Tokens and utilities only.

**Motion.** None.

**Accessibility.** Spacing never replaces a divider or a label when the grouping carries meaning.

**Responsive.** Spacing does not shrink on the compact window; content scrolls instead.

**Do / Don't.** Do put the same token on both sides of what it separates. Do pad every card 16. Don't use 5, 7, 9, 11 or 13. Don't nest padding to fake alignment: align to the gutter.

### Window budget (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** The fixed geometry every screen is designed against, and what happens on a short screen.

**Anatomy.** Content box `--v2-shell-w` 419 x `--v2-shell-h` 830 in three bands: top bar `--v2-topbar-h` 52 (pinned), content band 722 (the only scroller, `.v2-screen-body`), bottom nav `--v2-nav-h` 56 (pinned). An overlay plane covers the whole box. The Windows caption above, `--v2-titlebar-h` 34, belongs to the desktop scene.

**Variants.** Full 830; compact `--v2-shell-min-h` 640 (content band 532, scrolls).

**Sizes.** Bubble and thread cards max `--v2-bubble-max` 300; dialogs `--v2-dialog-sm` 320 and `--v2-dialog-md` 382; controls 28 / 36 / 44; fields 40.

**States.** Full: V2 `?start=worker` (421 x 866 window, product-scene.css:76). Compact: new (resize the window to 640).

**Props.** AppFrame `compact` (section 29). Two screen recipes, one spacing: the first block always sits 16 under the ScreenHeader, the column keeps the 16 gutter, blocks sit 12 apart and the bottom pad is 24.
- `.v2-screen` + `.v2-screen-body` (`kit/base.css:185-193`, padding 16 16 24): the header stays pinned and the body is the only scroller. For screens the partner reads in place: Home, Activity, Run detail, Notifications, Chat.
- `.v2-acct` + `.v2-acct-body` (`screens/account.css:7-11`): no scroller of its own, so the content band itself scrolls (the ScreenHeader is sticky in the band, so it still stays). For the settings group, whose columns are long lists of sections: Settings, Worker settings, Connected apps, Account, AI credits. The band being the scroller is the reason: ScreenFrame's `scrollTo` moves the band to a `[data-section]` (Settings' `highlight`).
A new screen picks the first unless it is a settings-style list of sections that a door opens at one of its sections. The two differ only in which box scrolls, never in spacing.

**Motion.** None.

**Accessibility.** On the compact window the composer and nav stay reachable; the content band scrolls by keyboard.

**Responsive.** Two heights, one width. Overlays size in % of the window or by the dialog tokens, never in vh.

**Do / Don't.** Do design every screen at 640 as well as 830. Don't give a band its own width. Don't use vh inside the window.

## 06 Shape, stroke, elevation, layers

**Status:** Radius ladder, Elevation, Scrims and layers: all Changed from V2.

**Why:** The product should feel like the site's quiet working screen: one button shape, hairlines instead of shadows, one veil. V2 has twenty radii in live code (buttons alone 8, 9, 10, 20, 40 and 999, two of them on the same settings screen), 154 shadow declarations from 55 recipes with 15 coloured glows, seven scrim recipes, and two dialogs tied at z-index 120. Fewer, named steps make every surface say how far it sits from the page (P5, P6).

### Radius ladder (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** Six radii, each with a job.

**Anatomy.** `--v2-radius-xs` 4 (checkbox box, day-bar segment, tooltip arrow), `-sm` 6 (buttons, fields, segmented items, menu rows, icon buttons: the site's button radius), `-md` 10 (rows, banners, composer, menus, tooltips, segmented track), `-lg` 16 (cards, bubbles, dialogs, sheet top corners: the site's card radius), `-pill` (badges, quick-reply chips, credits chip, progress rail), `-round` (avatars, discs, send). Strokes: `--v2-stroke` 1px for every edge; `--v2-stroke-selected` 1.5px for a selected choice, the focused composer, and the two outlined marks that must still read at 6 to 8px: the ring status dot (paused, offline: `kit/shell.css:80`) and the pending DayBar segment (`kit/worker.css:78`). A 2px step is not a stroke token; it appears only as mark geometry, sized with its mark: the spinner ring and the checkbox tick (glyph lines), the 2px surface halo that cuts a count dot out of what it sits on (`kit/credits.css:33`, `kit/display.css:89`), and the 2px left rule of the job preview's quoted comment (`kit/worker.css:151`). Icon glyphs draw at their own weight (StatusTick 2.5, `kit/program.jsx:29`).

**Variants.** As listed.

**Sizes.** 4, 6, 10, 16, 999, 50%.

**States.** Selected uses 1.5px `--v2-select-line` on `--v2-select`. V2 today: Settings (`?start=worker`, then Settings) shows Connect Telegram at radius 20 (settings.jsx:59) and Save settings at 8 (settings.jsx:138) on one screen.

**Props.** Tokens only.

**Motion.** None.

**Accessibility.** Shape never carries meaning alone (a pill is not a status without its word).

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do make every button 6. Do nest radii inner-smaller (card 16, row 10, control 6). Don't use 8, 9, 12, 14, 18 or 20. Don't use pill buttons.

### Elevation (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** How far a surface sits from the page: in flow, floating, or blocking.

**Anatomy.** `--v2-shadow-0` none plus a 1px line (cards, bubbles, banners, rows, top bar, bottom nav, composer). `--v2-shadow-1` navy cast for menus, popovers, tooltips, coach mark, toast. `--v2-shadow-2` deeper navy cast for dialogs, sheets and day done.

**Variants.** Three levels.

**Sizes.** Not applicable.

**States.** Level 0, 1, 2. V2 today: the user bubble's indigo glow (chat_bubbles.jsx:30, any chat), the bottom nav casting upward (shared.jsx:300), day done at 0.45 black (worker_day_done.jsx:142, `?end=job`).

**Props.** Tokens only.

**Motion.** Shadows do not animate; the surface arrives with its own motion (section 07).

**Accessibility.** Elevation is never the only sign of a modal: the veil and focus management are (section 09).

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do separate in-flow things with a line. Don't add coloured glows. Don't lift a card on hover; firm up its line.

### Scrims and layers (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** One veil for every decision, the site's frosted gate for sign-in, and a named order of layers inside the window.

**Anatomy.** `--v2-scrim` rgba(11,18,32,0.48) under every dialog, sheet and day done. `--v2-scrim-frost` rgba(248,249,251,0.88) with `--v2-scrim-blur` 14px, the site's gate, for sign-in only. Z-scale: `--v2-z-raised` 1, `-sticky` 10, `-header` 20, `-popover` 30, `-dialog` 100, `-gate` 110, `-toast` 120, `-splash` 200.

**Variants.** Dim, frost.

**Sizes.** Veils cover the content box (inset 0), never the browser.

**States.** Dim and frost. V2 today: logout veil rgba(15,23,42,0.45) z 120 (profile.jsx:73), terms veil rgba(8,10,31,0.44) z 120, the tie (terms_sheet.jsx:97, bare URL), out-of-credits rgba(15,23,42,0.5) z 130 (byok.jsx:79, `?credits=0` then send), modal rgba(0,0,0,0.5) z 60 (shared.jsx:72), sign-in white 55% with blur 10 z 100 (login.jsx:74, `?signin=browser`).

**Props.** Dialog and Sheet own their veil (sections 25, 26).

**Motion.** The veil runs the `v2-fade` keyframe in its own rule (there is no class for it): in at `--v2-dur-3` ease-standard, out reversed at `--v2-dur-3` ease-in when the overlay closes (`kit/overlays.css:22, 28`; section 07).

**Accessibility.** A veil means the content under it is inert: focus is trapped above it.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Don't stack a dialog on a dialog (section 25); mount order on `--v2-z-dialog` only settles an overlap that cannot be avoided (one dialog still fading out while the next one opens), and the later one sits on top. Don't blur outside sign-in: a white dialog on a pale frosted veil separates only by its shadow, while the dim veil puts its edge at 3.3:1 (WCAG 1.4.11, the Overlay family in `tokens.css:161-162`). Don't invent a z-index: pick a layer.

## 07 Motion

**Status:** Durations and curves: Changed from V2. Reduced motion: New. Choreography budgets: Changed from V2.

**Why:** The partner opens this app every day, so motion has to be quick, quiet and identical everywhere. V2 has 38 durations and 12 curves, honours reduced motion in four components only, loops the thinking dots for everyone (its day-done ring went with the minimal popup, 2026-10-14), and spends 3.5s of splash and skeleton on every window open and 5.9s of welcome intro behind the terms sheet. The site's ease is a near twin of V2's house curve, so the family match is free (P5, P6, P7).

### Durations and curves (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** Five durations, three curves and nine keyframes that every part draws from.

**Anatomy.** Durations: `--v2-dur-1` 120ms (hover colour, press), `-2` 180ms (tab change, chips, toggles, message in), `-3` 240ms (popover, scrim, disclosure, departures), `-4` 320ms (dialog, sheet, window), `--v2-dur-celebrate` 450ms (day-done pop only). Loops: `--v2-dur-spin` 800ms, `--v2-dur-pulse` 1200ms, `--v2-dur-shimmer` 1400ms. Curves: `--v2-ease-out` cubic-bezier(0.16,1,0.3,1) (every arrival, the site's ease), `--v2-ease-in` cubic-bezier(0.5,0,0.75,0.4) (departures), `--v2-ease-standard` ease (colour, opacity). Keyframes in kit/motion.css: `v2-fade-up` (6px rise), `v2-scale-in` (from 0.98), `v2-sheet-up`, `v2-sheet-down`, `v2-fade`, `v2-pulse`, `v2-spin`, `v2-shimmer`, `v2-indet`. Six classes put one on an element (`kit/motion.css:65-77`): `.v2-anim-fade-up` (v2-fade-up at dur-2: EmptyState and Welcome), `.v2-anim-pop-in` (v2-fade-up at dur-3: the coach mark only), `.v2-anim-pulse` (Badge busy dot), `.v2-anim-spin` (Spinner, so the thinking bubble), `.v2-anim-shimmer` (Skeleton), `.v2-anim-indet` (ProgressBar); staggers `.v2-delay-1` and `.v2-delay-2` (120ms steps). There is no class for the overlays: their keyframe changes with their state (`.is-closing`, `.is-leaving`), so each names it in its own rule (`kit/overlays.css`): dialog v2-scale-in at dur-4, sheet v2-sheet-up at dur-4 and v2-sheet-down at dur-3 ease-in, scrim v2-fade at dur-3 (out: reversed, ease-in), toast v2-fade-up at dur-2 (out: v2-fade reversed at dur-1), menus and popovers v2-fade-up at dur-3, and the day-done badge v2-scale-in at `--v2-dur-celebrate` (`kit/worker.css:113`). The "Run by" table at the top of kit/motion.css (lines 7-21) lists every place each keyframe runs; read it before adding motion.

**Variants.** Arrival (ease-out), departure (ease-in, one step shorter), loop (only while the partner waits).

**Sizes.** Not applicable.

**States.** Fast, base, moderate, slow, celebrate. V2 today: tabFadeUp 0.18s (index.html:74), baMsgIn 0.22s (index.html:89), terms sheet up 0.45s and down 0.32s (terms_sheet.jsx:104, 106, bare URL), ddPop 0.45s (worker_day_done.jsx:142, `?end=job`).

**Props.** The six classes and the two stagger classes; an overlay names its keyframe in its own rule instead.

**Motion.** As above.

**Accessibility.** Covered by the reduced-motion rule below.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do ease arrivals out and departures in. Do animate opacity and transform only. Don't loop anything the partner is not waiting on. Don't add a keyframe for one part: the one celebration effect, Day 20's confetti (`v2-w-confetti`, the only local keyframe, `kit/worker.css:190`), lives in the day-done part and plays once.

### Reduced motion (New)

**Purpose.** When the partner asks Windows for less motion, every arrival is simply there and every loop stops, product-wide, from one rule.

**Anatomy.** One `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` block in kit/motion.css: animation and transition durations 0.01ms, one iteration, no delay, no smooth scroll. Two exceptions keep meaning: `.v2-anim-spin` keeps turning at half speed (it only exists while a control is busy), and `.v2-anim-indet` becomes a full, quiet track. `.v2-reduced-motion` applies the same rule to one subtree (guide previews).

**Variants.** Normal, reduced.

**Sizes.** Not applicable.

**States.** Normal; reduced (Windows Settings > Accessibility > Visual effects > Animation effects off). V2 honours it only in the terms sheet, the welcome, its logo and the onboarding picker (terms_sheet.jsx:107, moneymaker.jsx:129, moneymaker_logo.jsx:87, onboarding.jsx:37).

**Props.** None. Parts must use the motion classes or tokens, so the one rule reaches them.

**Motion.** Not applicable.

**Accessibility.** Busy states always carry words ("Saving…", "Running today’s run"), so nothing depends on movement.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do rely on the global rule. Don't write per-component reduced-motion blocks. Don't run JS-timed animations that ignore it.

### Choreography budgets (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** A time budget for every timed sequence, measured against a daily user.

**Anatomy.** Cold start: splash up to 1.2s, only when the app process starts; reopening the window restores the last screen at once, no splash, no skeleton. Welcome (signed out): title, steps and action fade up at `--v2-dur-2` (`.v2-anim-fade-up`, `.v2-delay-1`, `.v2-delay-2`), 120ms apart, about 0.42s, cap 1.2s, no logo drift (`screens/welcome.jsx:93, 136`). Day done: one pop at `--v2-dur-celebrate` (v2-scale-in), no ring; Day 20 adds its confetti once, none under reduced motion. Sheet: up 320ms ease-out, down 240ms ease-in.

**Variants.** Cold start, welcome, day done, sheet.

**Sizes.** Not applicable.

**States.** V2 today: splash messages at 1050ms, fade at 2150ms, done at 2650ms (boot_splash.jsx:31-33) then an 850ms skeleton (index.html:745) on every window open (bare URL); welcome CTA at 1.65s and terms finishing at 5.9s (moneymaker.jsx:107-110); day done `?end=job`.

**Props.** Splash `coldStart` (section 33).

**Motion.** As above.

**Accessibility.** Nothing interactive waits behind a choreography: controls are usable as soon as they appear.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do brand the first look only. Don't replay the splash on reopen. Don't make the partner wait for an animation to read terms.

## 08 Icons

**Status:** Icon set: Changed from V2.

**Why:** Icons label the controls the partner uses to keep the worker in check (Always ask, Automatic, pause, disconnect), so each meaning needs one glyph everywhere. V2 redraws its clock, chat, help and sign-out glyphs two or three times per file at 14 to 22px, with 16 different stroke widths and hard-coded hexes such as #64748b (index.html:303-328, shared.jsx:7-40). One registry ends that and matches the site's stroke style (onblue/index.html:307-330).

### Icon set (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** One named module, `window.V2Kit.Icon`, for every glyph the product draws.

**Anatomy.** A 24-unit glyph, round caps and joins, `stroke: currentColor`, `fill: none`. Names: home, activity, settings, chat, help, more, close, back, chevron-right, chevron-down, external, check, check-double, x-circle, alert, info, clock, calendar, play, pause, stop, send, refresh, spark (filled), credits (gradient), youtube, instagram, shield-check (Always ask), zap (Automatic), user, sign-out, bell, link, trash, copy, lock, wifi-off, download, thumb-up, thumb-down, eye; added for the kit: check-circle, chevron-left, chevron-up, arrow-up, arrow-right, plus, minus, search, edit, key, earnings, monitor (BlueStacks, App Player), telegram. Aliases resolve common guesses (x, arrow-left, warning, error, verified, always-ask, automatic, logout, external-link, dollar, bluestacks, offline).

**Variants.** Stroke (all but two); filled: spark (the site's mark); painted: credits (`--v2-gradient-brand` through V2's star, assets/Credits.svg).

**Sizes.** 16 `--v2-icon-sm` (inline, chips, menu rows), 20 `--v2-icon-md` (top bar, nav, list rows), 24 `--v2-icon-lg` (empty states, dialog marks). The stroke renders 1.75px at every size (`--v2-icon-stroke`, compensated per size in kit/base.css).

**States.** No states of its own: it takes the colour of its parent. Idle `--v2-ink-3`, active `--v2-ink` (or `--v2-blue` for the lit nav item), on a tint the tone ink, on a fill `--v2-on-color`. V2 today: nav glyphs 22px stroke 1.9 (shared.jsx:7-8), menu glyphs 18px stroke 2 in #64748b (index.html:303), close 2.5 (shared.jsx:28), mode glyphs stroke 2 (agent_permissions.jsx:42-45); reach with `?start=worker`.

**Props.** `Icon { name, size = 16 | 20 | 24, label, inline, className }`. Without `label` it is decorative (`aria-hidden`); with `label` it is `role="img"` named by it. `inline` aligns it to the text baseline. `V2Kit.ICON_NAMES` lists every name. An unknown name renders an empty box of the right size and warns once in the console.

**Motion.** None. A spinner is not an icon (section 20).

**Accessibility.** An icon next to a word adds nothing for screen readers, so it stays hidden; an icon alone is named (IconButton requires a label). Colour never carries the meaning alone.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do add a glyph to the registry, then use it by name. Do keep shield-check for Always ask and zap for Automatic. Don't draw an inline svg in a part. Don't set a colour on the icon itself. Don't use brand-coloured app logos: YouTube and Instagram are drawn in the product's stroke style.

## 09 Focus and accessibility

**Status:** Focus ring: Changed from V2. Contrast table: Changed from V2. Targets and keyboard: New.

**Why:** The partner agrees, through this app, to an agent acting on their own accounts and earning on their behalf, so every consent, money and control surface has to be readable and operable by everyone. V2 ships one focus rule (index.html:57) and removes the BYOK select's outline with nothing in its place (byok_settings.jsx:31); its primary labels are 3.24:1; its faintest text carries its caveats at 2.5:1; no overlay moves focus; the header menus work by mouse only (index.html:294); consent controls are 21 to 22px tall (P7).

### Focus ring (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** One visible focus for every interactive part.

**Anatomy.** Outline `--v2-focus-outline` (2px solid `--v2-blue`) at `--v2-focus-offset` 2px, from one global `:focus-visible` rule in kit/base.css. Fields and the composer replace it with a `--v2-blue` border plus `--v2-focus-ring` (3px at 16%), the site's own values.

**Variants.** Outline (buttons, chips, tabs, links, rows, icon buttons); border plus ring (fields, selects, composer).

**Sizes.** 2px outline, 2px offset; 3px ring.

**States.** Focus-visible only (keyboard). Elements focused by script (`tabindex="-1"`: dialog and screen titles) show no ring. The guide forces it with `.is-focus`. V2 today: browser default ring on most controls, none on the BYOK select (Settings at `?start=worker`, then Tab).

**Props.** None. Parts never set `outline: none` without a replacement.

**Motion.** None: focus appears at once.

**Accessibility.** Outline clears 3:1 against canvas and surface; it sits outside the fill so it shows on a primary button too.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do let the global rule work. Don't remove an outline. Don't show focus on mouse click (that is what `:focus-visible` is for).

### Contrast table (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** Every text token on every ground, computed live from tokens, so a pairing that fails is visibly forbidden.

**Anatomy.** Rows: ink, ink-2, ink-3, ink-4, blue-ink, success-ink, attention-ink, danger-ink. Columns: surface, canvas, panel, sunken, blue tint, selected, success tint, attention tint, danger tint. Plus on-colour on each fill.

**Variants.** Text (4.5:1), large text and icons (3:1).

**Sizes.** Not applicable.

**States.** Pass (AA), large only, fails. Rules it enforces: ink-4 is meta only, and only on surface, panel or canvas at rest (it drops under 4.5:1 on sunken 4.42, every tint 4.17 to 4.37, selected 4.06, and the hover and press washes 4.32 and 4.08; section 03); placeholder is never text that must be read (2.57:1); status inks go on their own tint or white; white labels go on action (4.92:1), never on brand blue (4.47:1). Field and composer edges stay `--v2-line-strong` (1.41:1): the visible label names the field, and focus turns the edge blue with a ring, so the edge is not the only cue. This is an exception to 3:1 non-text; Switch and Checkbox edges use ink-4. V2 pairs that fail: #94a3b8 'Working on it…' 2.56:1, #9ca3af 'App Player will be signed out too.' 2.54:1, #60a5fa 'PLAN' eyebrow, #f59e0b link 2.15:1, white on #1990FF 3.24:1, white on #ef4444 3.76:1.

**Props.** Not applicable.

**Motion.** Not applicable.

**Accessibility.** This is the accessibility rule for colour.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do check a new pairing in the guide's table before using it. Don't add a token without its contrast on every ground it will sit on.

### Targets and keyboard (New)

**Purpose.** Controls sized for a mouse in a small window, and one keyboard model for every overlay and list.

**Anatomy.** Targets: 24px absolute floor; `--v2-hit-min` 32px for consent, money and controls in a row; `--v2-control-md` 36px default; `--v2-control-lg` 44px for full-width dialog and sheet actions and the sign-in doors. Keyboard model:
- Menus: trigger has `aria-haspopup` and `aria-expanded`; Enter, Space or Down opens on the first item; Up, Down, Home, End move; Enter picks; Esc or Tab closes and focus returns to the trigger.
- Dialogs: focus moves in to one place by kind: the panel for a dialog read before acting (task, consent, day done), the first field (`initialFocus`) for a form dialog, Cancel (the first action, the safe answer) for an alert; Tab stays inside; Esc closes unless the dialog blocks; focus returns to the opener; the title labels the dialog.
- Sheets: as dialogs; the blocking terms sheet has no close and no Esc, its one way out is the agreement button.
- Tabs: one tab stop, Left and Right move and select.
- Thread: `role="log"`, `aria-live="polite"`.
- Toast: `role="status"`; its action is also reachable elsewhere.
- Errors: field errors linked by `aria-describedby`; a run or screen error uses `role="alert"` once.

**Variants.** As listed.

**Sizes.** 24, 32, 36, 44.

**States.** V2 today: the coach 'Okay' about 21px (moneymaker_home.jsx:57, `?start=worker` first run), the ribbon's 'Change' about 21px (agent_permissions.jsx:212), the info button 22px (credits_popup_parts.jsx:95, `?credits=0`).

**Props.** Overlay parts own their focus management (sections 25 to 27); the kit's IconButton requires `label`.

**Motion.** See section 07.

**Accessibility.** This is the keyboard rule.

**Responsive.** Targets do not shrink on the compact window.

**Do / Don't.** Do make every consent and money control at least 32px. Do return focus where it came from. Don't open a menu on hover only. Don't trap focus without a way out.

## 10 Tokens reference

**Status:** Token table: New.

**Why:** V2 has five runtime CSS variables and none of them are design tokens; its values are typed inline, and local constants (ACCENT, INK, MUTED, FAINT, repeated across five files such as ai_mode.jsx:10-13 and agent_permissions.jsx:37) were the system trying to exist. A single token file the PM can read, with what each token replaces, is what lets the next iteration change a colour once (P8).

### Token table (New)

**Purpose.** Every `--v2-` token in one place: its live value, its role and the V2 values it absorbs.

**Anatomy.** One `:root` block in `tokens.css`, prefix `--v2-`, grouped by family: Ink, Surface, Line, Blue, Status, Brand, Type, Space, Radius, Stroke, Elevation, Overlay, Z, Motion, Focus, Opacity, Icon, Shell, Size, Scrollbar. Size holds the part sizes more than one part shares or that step as one set (`tokens.css:240-254`): `--v2-mark-sm` 24, `--v2-mark-md` 32, `--v2-mark-lg` 40, `--v2-mark-xl` 56 (avatars, tiles, discs), `--v2-badge-h` 22, `--v2-row-min-h` 48, `--v2-rail` 6 and `--v2-rail-thin` 4 (bars), `--v2-popover-w` 364, `--v2-measure` 280 and `--v2-measure-tip` 260 (line lengths and floating widths). The kit and screen stylesheets read them, never the raw values (ds-check). Each family comment states its role, its onBlue link and why; each token line ends with its role and "Absorbs" (the V2 values it replaces, with file:line). The guide reads the file as text for roles and reads values with `getComputedStyle`, so the table can never drift from the file.

**Variants.** Light theme only. V2 is white end to end and the site never ships its dark theme, so a dark set would be speculative; the names leave room for one.

**Sizes.** Not applicable.

**States.** Used; orphan. The orphan report lists tokens no kit or screen stylesheet reaches, directly or through another token (inline JSX styles are not scanned).

**Props.** Not applicable.

**Motion.** Not applicable.

**Accessibility.** Contrast for every colour token is in section 09.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do add a value to tokens.css first, with its role and what it absorbs, then use it. Do read the orphan list before adding a token: one may already exist. Don't write a raw colour in kit or screens (tools/ds-check.js fails it). Don't add a token for one use.

# Components

## 11 Button, icon button, link

**Status:** Button, Primary in V2 today, IconButton, Link: all Changed from V2.

**Why:** The worker acts on the partner's own accounts, so every action needs a rank: the one that commits (I agree, turn on; Got it), the frequent second one (Cancel), the third (Not now), the one that destroys (Remove). V2 gives most of them the same blue fill in six radii with three hover models and blue glows, and white on its #1990FF is 3.24:1 (index.html:351, byok.jsx:22). The system takes the site's ladder and shape (radius 6, 13/500, a real press, disabled with a reason; onblue/onblue.css:959-999, onblue/apply.html:411) and an AA action blue (P2, P5, P6, P7).

### Button (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** Every pressable action that is not inside a sentence. One primary per view: the action that commits.

**Anatomy.** Box: height 28 / 36 / 44, radius 6 (`--v2-radius-sm`), 1px edge, padding 0 12 / 16 / 20. Label: 13/500 (`--v2-text-13`, `--v2-weight-medium`), tracking -0.01em, one line, ellipsis if it must. Optional 16px icon before (`icon`) or after (`iconRight`), gap 8 (6 at sm). Loading: a 16px Spinner takes the leading icon's place; the label stays. Disabled reason: 12/1.35 ink-3 under the button, 6 below.

**Variants.** `primary`: `--v2-action` #1268f0 fill and edge, white label (4.92:1); hover `--v2-action-hover`. `secondary` (default): white, 1px `--v2-line-strong`, ink label; hover adds `--v2-wash-hover` and `--v2-line-hover`. `quiet`: no box, ink-2 label; hover wash + ink. `danger`: `--v2-danger-ink` #c03a40 fill, white label (5.36:1); hover adds `--v2-wash-press`. Use danger for the primary of the confirm that names what is lost, and for the trigger that opens that confirm (Disconnect YouTube); never for an action that costs nothing (section 52 is the rule).

**Sizes.** sm 28 (`--v2-control-sm`) for dense rows; md 36 (`--v2-control-md`) default; lg 44 (`--v2-control-lg`) for full-width sheet and dialog actions (`full`). sm grows an invisible ::after to the 32px hit floor.

**States.** Rest; hover; focus-visible (2px `--v2-blue` outline, offset 2); active (translateY 1px, plus `--v2-wash-press` on secondary and quiet); disabled (0.45, `aria-disabled`, still focusable, reason printed); loading (`aria-busy`, spinner, clicks ignored, cursor progress). In V2 today: New Chat at `?start=worker` (index.html:351); Save settings on the Settings tab (settings.jsx:138); Cancel and I agree, turn on in the Automatic consent (agent_permissions.jsx:190-191, open the mode sheet at `?start=worker&v=1` and switch YouTube on); Got it on the day-done card (worker_day_done.jsx:148, `?end=job`) and the out-of-credits card (byok.jsx:26, `?credits=0` then send); Apply disabled at 0.45 with no reason (ai_mode.jsx:190, Settings, AI Mode, Hybrid, Custom).

**Props.** `variant` ('primary' | 'secondary' | 'quiet' | 'danger', default 'secondary'), `size` ('sm' | 'md' | 'lg'), `icon`, `iconRight` (icon names, section 08), `loading`, `disabled`, `disabledReason` (string printed under it), `full`, `type` ('button'), `onClick`, `href` (renders an a), `external` (new tab, external glyph, "opens in a new tab" for screen readers), `className`, `children`.

**Motion.** Colour and edge over `--v2-dur-1` (120ms) `--v2-ease-standard`; press 1px over `--v2-dur-1` `--v2-ease-out`. Reduced motion: no transitions, no press travel. No glow, no lift, no brightness filter.

**Accessibility.** White labels only on `--v2-action` or `--v2-danger-ink`. Disabled stays in the tab order with the reason linked by `aria-describedby`. Focus ring always visible. Targets at least 32px. The label is the action, in sentence case, no trailing ellipsis.

**Responsive.** Fixed 419px window: sheets and dialogs use `size="lg" full`; rows use sm or md. On the compact 640px window nothing changes size.

**Do / Don't.** Do keep one primary per view. Do print why a button is disabled. Do keep the label while loading. Don't put two filled buttons side by side. Don't hover by opacity (it lowers contrast that must hold). Don't add a glow, a lift or a pill. Primary fill blue or ink is decision D1: one token either way.

### Primary in V2 today (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** Shows the change where a partner meets it: the out-of-credits card's Got it.

**Anatomy.** V2: pill, 15/700, #1990FF, white at 3.24:1, opacity 0.9 hover (byok.jsx:26-27). System: radius 6, 13/500, #1268f0, white at 4.92:1, `--v2-action-hover`.

**Variants.** None.

**Sizes.** md; full width inside the card.

**States.** Rest. Reached in V2 at `?credits=0`, then send a message.

**Props.** `<Button variant="primary" full>Got it</Button>`.

**Motion.** As Button.

**Accessibility.** Passes AA where V2 fails it.

**Responsive.** As Button.

**Do / Don't.** Do keep V2's words ("Got it"). Don't keep the pill.

### IconButton (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** A bare glyph for the top bar, rows and dialog corners: help, more, close, copy, remove.

**Anatomy.** 36 square (32, 28), radius 6, no edge; glyph 20 (16 at 28) in `--v2-ink-3` (6.0:1). The `label` becomes `aria-label`.

**Variants.** `bare` (default); `outlined` (white, 1px `--v2-line-strong`); `tone="danger"` turns the glyph `--v2-danger-ink` on hover.

**Sizes.** 36 default, 32 for dense rows, 28 inside fields (the reveal button); 28 grows an invisible 32 target.

**States.** Rest; hover (`--v2-wash-hover`, ink glyph); focus-visible; active, menu open or pressed (`--v2-wash-press`, ink glyph, from `active`, `aria-expanded` or `aria-pressed`); disabled (0.45, `aria-disabled`). In V2 today: the help and more icons on the top bar at `?start=worker` (index.html:301, #64748b, #1990FF when open, no hover, no focus); the out-of-credits close at #94a3b8 (2.56:1, byok.jsx:81).

**Props.** `icon`, `label` (required), `size` (28 | 32 | 36), `variant`, `active`, `tone` ('default' | 'danger'), `disabled`, `tip` (default on: a label Tooltip showing the label, section 23; false opts out), `onClick`; `aria-expanded`, `aria-haspopup`, `aria-pressed` pass through.

**Motion.** Background and colour over `--v2-dur-1`.

**Accessibility.** Always a label; pair with a Tooltip (section 23) so sighted users see it too. Open is ink on a wash, not blue: blue means selection.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do use it for well-known glyphs only. Don't use it for an action that needs words (Disconnect, Sign out). Don't colour it blue when open.

### Link (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** An action inside a sentence (Not you? Switch account), or a quiet way out on its own line (Learn more, Earnings).

**Anatomy.** Ink text with a 1px underline at offset 3 in `--v2-link-underline` (32%, the site's `--link-underline`, onblue/onblue.css:326); hover turns text and underline `--v2-blue-ink` (the site's link, onblue/onblue.css:1224-1232). External adds the external glyph 16 after the text.

**Variants.** Inline (inherits the sentence's size and weight); `standalone` (13/500, inline-flex, min height 32); `external`; `tone="muted"` (ink-3). Without `href` it renders a button drawn as a link.

**Sizes.** Inherits; standalone 13.

**States.** Rest; hover (blue-ink, underline in the same colour); focus-visible; external. In V2 today: Learn more at 12.5/600 #1990FF with a typed arrow (credits_popup_parts.jsx:110, the (i) on the AI Credits screen); Earnings at 11.5/700 #1990FF with a typed arrow (worker_day_done.jsx:184, the home strip at `?day=returning`); Switch account at 600 #1990FF (account_gate.jsx:58, `?signin=browser&player=other`). All 3.24:1, none underlined.

**Props.** `href`, `external`, `tone` ('ink' | 'muted'), `standalone`, `onClick`, `children`.

**Motion.** Colour over `--v2-dur-1`.

**Accessibility.** The underline, not colour, says "link". External links say they open a new tab. One Earnings URL across the app (R29).

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do use links for leaving and for in-sentence actions. Do mark external links with the glyph. Don't colour a link blue at rest. Don't type an arrow character into a label. Don't make the main action of a view a link.

## 12 Text field and text area

**Status:** TextField, Secret field, TextArea: all Changed from V2.

**Why:** Typing is rare in a worker the partner sets up once, so every field the partner does meet (an API key, a feedback note, Report a problem, the advanced endpoint) has to be right first time and say what went wrong. V2 hand-builds five fields with three focus treatments and no error state at all; its key field shows the key in clear text and its Validate & Save never validates (byok_settings.jsx:54, 93). The system takes the site's question shape: a label, one box, a message line that exists only when it may hold something, the site's error line and ring, and "a placeholder is an example, not a watermark" (onblue/onblue.css:3654, 3703-3709; P6, P7).

### TextField (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** One line of typed input with a visible label and a place to say what is wrong or what is happening.

**Anatomy.** Label 13/500 ink, 6 above the box, with an optional 12px ink-4 flag ("Required" or "Optional"). Box: 40 tall (`--v2-field-h`), radius 6, 1px `--v2-line-strong`, white, padding 0 12; value 14/1.35 ink. Leading and trailing slots (16px icon in ink-3, a Spinner, or a 28px IconButton), gap 8. Message line: 12/1.35 ink-3, 6 below, with a 16px alert icon when it is an error; a counter at its right when counting.

**Variants.** Text; `mono` (`--v2-font-mono` 13, for model ids and endpoints); `secret` (next part); no visible label (`hideLabel`: the label goes to screen readers and the placeholder steps up to ink-4, the site's rule for a field with no caption).

**Sizes.** md 40 (default), sm 32 (`--v2-hit-min`) for dense rows.

**States.** Rest; hover (edge `--v2-line-hover`); focus (edge `--v2-blue` + `--v2-focus-ring` 3px at 16%); filled (no change); disabled (sunken fill, 10% edge, 0.45, helper says why); read-only (sunken fill, 10% edge, still selectable and focusable); invalid + message (edge `--v2-danger-edge` #d0454a, 4.53:1, `--v2-danger-ring` on focus, message in `--v2-danger-ink`, `aria-invalid`); validating (spinner in the trailing slot, `aria-busy`, helper says "Checking…"); validated (check in `--v2-success`, helper in `--v2-success-ink`); required (flag + native `required`). In V2 today: Settings, AI Mode, Hybrid, Custom shows two fields with a JS border swap and no ring (ai_mode.jsx:179-186); the shared `.ba-field:focus` ring exists only on the Settings number inputs and the schedule form (index.html:57); no field has an error state.

**Props.** `label`, `hideLabel`, `value` / `defaultValue`, `onChange(value, event)`, `placeholder`, `helper`, `error` (a string makes it invalid; `error={null}` reserves the message line on a field that validates later), `required`, `optional`, `disabled`, `readOnly`, `size`, `width`, `leading`, `trailing`, `mono`, `secret`, `defaultRevealed`, `maxLength` + `showCount` (a soft limit: counter turns attention from 90%, the field turns invalid past it), `status` ('validating' | 'validated'), `id`, `className`; other props reach the input.

**Motion.** Edge and ring over `--v2-dur-1`. Nothing else moves. Reduced motion: no transitions.

**Accessibility.** The label is a real label element. Message and counter are linked by `aria-describedby`; the message line is a polite live region, so a new error is announced once. Focus is the edge plus ring, never removed without replacement. Placeholder ink is only for examples under a visible label.

**Responsive.** Fields fill their column in the 419px window; `width` fixes one only beside a label in a settings row.

**Do / Don't.** Do keep a visible label. Do say what is wrong and how to fix it under the box. Do validate before saying saved. Don't use the placeholder as the label or the hint. Don't remove the outline without the ring. Don't pretend to validate.

### Secret field (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** An API key: hidden by default, checked before it is saved.

**Anatomy.** TextField with `secret`: password type, mono 13, a trailing eye IconButton 28 (`aria-pressed`, labelled "Show API key"), spellcheck and autocomplete off. Helper carries the where-to-find hint and the storage note.

**Variants.** None.

**Sizes.** md.

**States.** Hidden; revealed (eye pressed); validating ("Checking your key with Anthropic…"); invalid key ("That key didn’t work. Check it and paste it again."); validated ("Key works."); all proposed copy, owned by section 49 (`kit/advanced.jsx:37-42`), so this field never words them differently. In V2 today: Settings, Bring Your Own Key on, Add a provider, pick one: the key is typed in clear text (byok_settings.jsx:93), the hint "Find at https://console.anthropic.com/settings" is the placeholder and vanishes as you type (byok_settings.jsx:15), and the note "Your key is stored encrypted on this device only." is #9ca3af at 2.54:1 (byok_settings.jsx:96, 12).

**Props.** As TextField; `secret`, `defaultRevealed`, `status`, `error`.

**Motion.** As TextField.

**Accessibility.** The reveal button has a stable name and `aria-pressed`. The storage note is ink-3 (6.0:1).

**Responsive.** As TextField.

**Do / Don't.** Do validate with the provider before saving and say the result. Don't make up a key when the field is empty (V2 does). Don't show a key in clear text by default.

### TextArea (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** Longer answers: the feedback note, Report a problem.

**Anatomy.** The field box with the textarea inside: padding 8 12, 14/1.5, min 84 (3 rows), no drag handle. Counter optional.

**Variants.** Plain; with counter (`maxLength` + `showCount`).

**Sizes.** One; `rows` sets the starting height, `maxRows` the cap.

**States.** As TextField, plus auto-grow (grows a line at a time), max rows then scroll (past `maxRows`, default 6), counter near the limit (attention ink from 90%), counter over the limit (danger ink, field invalid).
