In V2 today: thumbs down on a finished run, reason Other: a 12px box, 31 tall, radius 10, outline removed, growing to 48px then scrolling (chat_feedback.jsx:96-102); the composer grows to 120px and then clips what is past it (chat_product.jsx:284).

**Props.** As TextField (no slots), plus `autoGrow` (true), `maxRows` (6), `rows` (3).

**Motion.** Height follows content with no animation.

**Accessibility.** Never clips: past the cap it scrolls. The counter is linked to the field.

**Responsive.** Full column width.

**Do / Don't.** Do let it grow, then scroll. Don't clip text past a fixed height. Don't shrink the text to 12px to fit.

## 13 Select

**Status:** Select: Changed from V2.

**Why:** The partner picks from a short plain list in two places (which provider, which model) and both live under Settings, Advanced (R31). The native select gives keyboard, screen readers and a list that fits a 419px window for free; it only needs to wear the field box. V2 shows three looks on one screen (radius 10 with a custom chevron, radius 8 with the OS arrow) and its provider select removes the focus outline with nothing in its place (byok_settings.jsx:31; settings.jsx:122; P2, P7).

### Select (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** One choice from a short plain list.

**Anatomy.** TextField's frame: label 13/500 above, the field box (40, radius 6, 1px `--v2-line-strong`), message line below. Inside: the native select spanning the box (padding 0 36 0 12, 14 ink), chevron-down 16 in ink-3 at right 12 (the whole box opens the list). Placeholder: a disabled first option in ink-3.

**Variants.** One: native with the shared chevron.

**Sizes.** md 40, sm 32.

**States.** Placeholder (ink-3, "Add a provider"); rest; hover (edge 20%); focus (blue edge + 3px ring); filled; open (the operating system draws the list; options always ink); disabled (sunken, 0.45, helper says why); invalid (edge `--v2-danger-edge`, message, as TextField). In V2 today: Settings, Bring Your Own Key switched on: "Add a provider" at #9ca3af (2.54:1), radius 10, custom chevron, no focus style (byok_settings.jsx:31-32, 113-114); two cards below, AI Model with the OS arrow, radius 8, width 200 (settings.jsx:122).

**Props.** `label`, `hideLabel`, `value` / `defaultValue`, `options` ([{value, label, disabled}]), `placeholder`, `onChange(value, event)`, `helper`, `error`, `required`, `optional`, `disabled`, `size`, `width`, `id`, `className`.

**Motion.** Edge and ring over `--v2-dur-1`.

**Accessibility.** Native control: arrow keys, type-to-find and the screen reader's list come from the system. The placeholder is an instruction, so it reads at ink-3 (6.0:1), not placeholder ink. Always a visible label (V2's provider select has none).

**Responsive.** Fills its column; `width` only beside a label in a settings row.

**Do / Don't.** Do use it for a short plain list. Do give it a label even when the placeholder says what to do. Don't build a custom list for plain text options. Don't use it for two or three options the partner should compare: use RadioCard or Segmented (section 14). Options that need an icon or a second line belong in a Menu (section 27).

## 14 Switch, checkbox, radio card, segmented

**Status:** Switch, RadioCard, Segmented: Changed from V2. Checkbox: New.

**Why:** "Keep the partner in control" is one of the product's three jobs, and its controls are choices: Always ask or Automatic per app, own key on or off, when to run, which notifications. V2 has three switches (one of which deletes the saved key when turned off; the per-app mode switch, since 2026-10-14, is the one real role=switch) and an option card built from plain buttons (shared.jsx:49; byok_settings.jsx:26, 64; agent_permissions.jsx:108; ai_mode.jsx:47). Its segmented control, which declared a radiogroup without the keys it promised, became that mode switch on 2026-10-14. The system gives each a real role and keyboard, the site's one chosen look (blue 12% fill, blue 50% edge, ink; onblue/apply.html:930), and edges that clear 3:1 (P4, P6, P7).

### Switch (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** On or off, applied at once. A switch enables or disables; it never deletes anything.

**Anatomy.** Track 36 by 20, pill. Off: 1px `--v2-ink-4` edge, white, a 12px ink-4 thumb at left 3. On: `--v2-action` fill and edge, a 16px white thumb at right 1. Row form: label 14/500 ink and an optional description 13/1.5 ink-3 on the left, the switch on the right, gap 16; the label is clickable.

**Variants.** With label row (default when `label` is visible); bare (`hideLabel`, for a list row that already names it).

**Sizes.** One, 36x20, with an invisible 32px target.

**States.** Off; on; hover (off: edge and thumb to ink-3; on: `--v2-action-hover`); focus-visible; disabled (0.45, `aria-disabled`, description says why); busy (`aria-busy`, held where it is, the thumb becomes a 12px spinner while a consent or a key check answers). In V2 today: the shared Toggle 32x18 with a spring (shared.jsx:49-51, only on the hidden Skills screen); Settings, Bring Your Own Key: 46x26, `aria-pressed`, and switching it off removes the saved key (byok_settings.jsx:25-26, 64).

**Props.** `checked`, `onChange(next)`, `label`, `hideLabel`, `description`, `disabled`, `busy`, `id`, `className`.

**Motion.** Thumb slides over `--v2-dur-2` (180ms) `--v2-ease-out`, no overshoot; colour over the same. Reduced motion: no transition.

**Accessibility.** role=switch, `aria-checked`, label by `label for` or `aria-label`, description by `aria-describedby`. Off is visible (the edge is 4.77:1; V2's grey tracks are 1.48:1).

**Responsive.** Row fills its column; long labels wrap, the switch stays top-right.

**Do / Don't.** Do apply at once. Do use busy while a consent decides. Don't delete data when switching off (R30: Remove is a separate, confirmed action). Don't use a switch inside a form that saves later: use Checkbox.

### Checkbox (New)

**Purpose.** Many-of-many inside a form that saves together: which notifications, include diagnostic info on Report a problem. Not for consent: in this product the agreement is the button ("I agree, turn on"), as V2 already does.

**Anatomy.** Row min 32, padding 6 0, gap 12: an 18px box (radius 4, 1px `--v2-ink-4`, white) aligned to the first line; label 14/1.35 ink; optional description 13/1.5 ink-3. A native checkbox sits under the drawn box. Group: `<fieldset className="v2-fieldset">` with `<legend className="v2-legend">` (13/500 ink).

**Variants.** Single; group (fieldset and legend).

**Sizes.** One.

**States.** Unchecked; hover (edge ink-3 + wash); focus-visible (outline on the box); checked (`--v2-action` fill, white tick); indeterminate (action fill, white bar); disabled and disabled checked (row at 0.45); invalid (edge `--v2-danger-edge` #d0454a, message in danger ink under the label). In V2 today: none in the product; the only checkbox is the review panel's install chip (index.html:923-926).

**Props.** `checked` / `defaultChecked`, `onChange(next, event)`, `label`, `description`, `disabled`, `indeterminate`, `required`, `error`, `name`, `value`, `id`, `className`.

**Motion.** Fill and edge over `--v2-dur-1`.

**Accessibility.** Native input, so Space, the form and screen readers work; description and error linked by `aria-describedby`. The whole row is the target.

**Responsive.** Full column width; label wraps.

**Do / Don't.** Do group related ones under a legend. Don't use it for a setting that applies at once (Switch). Don't use it to collect agreement.

### RadioCard (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** One of a few options the partner compares, each with a line of why: AI mode (Cloud or Hybrid), the run window.

**Anatomy.** Card: radius 10, padding 12, 1px `--v2-line`, white. Head: title 14/600 ink, an optional Badge (`badge`, or the pending tag in attention), a 16px check in `--v2-blue-ink` at the right when selected. Sub 13/1.35 ink-3. Disabled reason: 12 ink-2 with a lock icon, under the sub.

**Variants.** `card` (side by side, equal widths); `list` (stacked, full width).

**Sizes.** One.

**States.** Rest; hover (20% edge + wash); selected (`--v2-select` fill, `--v2-select-line` edge at 1.5, check); pending (dashed select-line edge + attention tag such as "Setup needed": picked, not active yet); disabled with reason (sunken, title and sub at 0.45, the reason at full strength); focus-visible (outline on the card). In V2 today: Settings, AI Mode (ai_mode.jsx:45-67): plain buttons, radius 12, 1.5px #e3e7ee, selected #eef6ff with 1.5px #1990FF, pending dashed with an amber tag, disabled at 0.5 with the reason "This PC doesn't meet the requirements for Hybrid." only in a hover title.

**Props.** `label` (the group's name), `name`, `options` ([{value, title, sub, disabled, disabledReason, pending, badge}]), `value` / `defaultValue`, `onChange(value)`, `variant`, `className`.

**Motion.** Fill and edge over `--v2-dur-1`.

**Accessibility.** Native radios: one tab stop, arrow keys move and select, title is the name and sub plus reason the description. Element ids come from `useId`, never from `name`, and the radio `name` is made unique per instance (name + id), so two panels never share one radio group (`kit/choice.jsx:111-113`).

**Responsive.** `card` holds two options in the 419px window; three or more use `list`.

**Do / Don't.** Do print why an option is unavailable. Do keep subs to one line. Don't use it for more than four options (Select). Proposed copy: Hybrid's sub becomes "Uses your PC and saves AI credits." ("tokens" is a banned word); the run window options are "At a set time", "In a window", "Leave it to me" (the last is V2's).

### Segmented (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** Two or three short, equal options in one line: the per-app Always ask / Automatic.

**Anatomy.** Track: `--v2-surface-sunken`, 1px `--v2-line`, radius 10, padding 2, gap 2. Segment: 32 tall, radius 6, padding 0 12, 13/500 ink-3, optional 16px icon (ModeRows passes shield-check for Always ask and zap for Automatic). Selected: the select fill (`--v2-select` over `--v2-surface`) with a 1.5px `--v2-blue` edge (`--v2-stroke-selected`; 4.15:1 on the track) and ink; no shadow (`kit/overlays.css:271-275`).

**Variants.** Text; icon and text.

**Sizes.** md 32, sm 28 (with an invisible 32 target).

**States.** Rest; hover (wash + ink); selected; focus-visible; disabled (0.45; say why beside it); gated (`beforeChange` is waiting: the target segment shows a dashed select-line edge, the group is `aria-busy`, the selection does not move until the answer). In V2 today: none since 2026-10-14; the composer chip at `?start=worker&v=1` opens a sheet with one switch per app (agent_permissions.jsx:98-116).

**Props.** `options` ([{value, label, icon, disabled}]), `value`, `onChange(value)`, `ariaLabel`, `beforeChange(value)` (return true, or a promise of true, to let the change through), `size`, `disabled`, `className`.

**Motion.** Colour over `--v2-dur-2`. The selected segment does not slide.

**Accessibility.** role=radiogroup and role=radio, one tab stop, arrows, Home and End. With a gate, arrows only move focus and Space or Enter asks, so a consent never opens from a stray key. Same weight selected and not, so nothing reflows.

**Responsive.** Inline beside a label; full width under it when the row is narrow.

**Do / Don't.** Do gate Automatic with the consent (beforeChange opens ConsentDialog, section 46). Don't use it to move between views (Tabs, section 28). Don't apply Automatic before the partner agrees.

## 15 Chip

**Status:** Chip: Changed from V2.

**Why:** The worker asks and the partner answers with a tap (Yes, Not now, Just this job), says what went wrong with a tap (Wrong result), and will filter Activity with a tap. V2 draws three chip looks for that: a 1.5px blue outline with #1990FF text at 3.24:1, a dark #334155 selected reason, and a grey back pill, with a hover that lifts but never changes colour (worker_cards.jsx:58; chat_feedback.jsx:77-84; index.html:100-101). One chip with the family's chosen look keeps blue meaning "chosen" and every label legible (P2, P5, P7).

### Chip (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** One small pressable label for three jobs: a one-shot reply to the worker, a reason you toggle, a filter.

**Anatomy.** Pill, 28 tall (`--v2-control-sm`; an invisible extension makes the target 32), padding 0 12, 1px `--v2-line-strong`, white. Label 13/500 ink, one line. Optional 16px icon before the label (zap marks the reply that turns on Automatic). Rows wrap with an 8px gap.

**Variants.** Reply (no `selected`: a plain button that answers once; the AskCard hides the row once answered). Reason and filter (`selected` true or false: a toggle with `aria-pressed`). One look for all three; only the behaviour differs.

**Sizes.** One, 28.

**States.** Rest; hover (`--v2-wash-hover` + `--v2-line-hover` edge); focus-visible; active (`--v2-wash-press`); selected (`--v2-select` fill, `--v2-select-line` edge, `--v2-ink` label (16.75:1 on the fill) and a check icon before it); selected hover; disabled (native, 0.45). In V2 today: the job ask's chips at `?end=job` with Always ask (worker_cards.jsx:58: 1.5px #c7dcf5, #1990FF 12.5/700); thumbs down on a finished run shows the reasons, the picked one dark #334155 with white (chat_feedback.jsx:77).

**Props.** `selected` (undefined for a reply; true or false for a toggle), `onClick`, `icon`, `disabled`, `className`, `children`.

**Motion.** Fill, edge and label colour over `--v2-dur-2` (180ms). No lift and no shrink (V2: translateY -1px, scale 0.96). Reduced motion: no transitions.

**Accessibility.** A real button with a visible focus ring; toggles carry `aria-pressed`; a row of toggles sits in a `role="group"` named by its prompt ("What went wrong?"). Labels are ink at rest, never blue.

**Responsive.** Rows wrap inside the 300px bubble column and the 419px window.

**Do / Don't.** Do use reply chips for short answers to the worker. Do show what an answer costs before the chips (AskCard, section 37). Don't colour chip text blue at rest. Don't lift or shrink on hover or press. Don't make a chip the main action of a screen (Button). The pill is adapted: the site's application chips are radius-9 toggles (apply.html:944-949); the product keeps the pill so a reply reads as tap to answer in the bubble column.

## 16 Badge

**Status:** Changed from V2.

**Why:** The money depends on a day's state (pending verification, verified, not counted), so that state needs one unmistakable label on every row and card. V2 has five pill systems for it: the AI Mode status chip (12/600, ai_mode.jsx:38), the Jobs badge (radius 4 with a line, jobs.jsx:31), "Active" (10/700 uppercase, index.html:483), "Verified" (11.5/700, profile.jsx:50) and "Not enrolled" (11/600 uppercase at 0.12em, account_gate.jsx:26). One Badge replaces all five.

### Badge (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** A short state on a row or card: a day (Pending verification, Verified, Not counted), a run (Running, Waiting for you), an app (Connected, Signed out), a mode or a download (Paused, Download failed). It labels; it never takes a click.

**Anatomy.**
1. Pill, 22 tall, padding 0 8 (`--v2-space-2`), radius `--v2-radius-pill`, no edge, no shadow.
2. Mark (optional): a 6px round dot in the tone colour, or one 16px icon in its place, never both. Days use the program's ticks: `check` for pending, `check-double` for verified.
3. Label: 12/500 (`--v2-text-12`, `--v2-weight-medium`) in `--v2-ink-2`, sentence case, three words at most, ellipsis if it must cut.

Tone lives in the fill and the mark; the words stay in ink-2. This is a style choice, not a contrast fix: every status ink passes 4.5:1 on its own tint (section 03; success ink on the 8% success tint is 4.59:1). One ink for every label keeps a row of badges calm and lets the mark carry the tone; ink-2 on every tint reads at 9:1 or better.

**Variants.** Five tones, each a fill plus a mark colour:
- neutral: `--v2-surface-sunken`, mark `--v2-ink-4` (pending verification, paused, soon). Pending is neutral by the one rule in section 03.
- info: `--v2-blue-tint`, mark `--v2-blue` (running, checking).
- success: `--v2-success-tint`, mark `--v2-success` (verified, connected, on).
- attention: `--v2-attention-tint`, mark `--v2-attention-ink` (the worker needs the partner; signed out; out of credits on a run).
- danger: `--v2-danger-tint`, mark `--v2-danger-ink` (not counted, failed: something went wrong or a day is lost). Signed out is attention: nothing failed and the partner can fix it (section 03's rule).
Plus: no mark, dot, icon, busy dot.

**Sizes.** One: 22 tall. Width hugs the label.

**States.**
- Static: every tone. In V2: Profile (Kebab > Profile, "Verified"), the gate (`?cases=signin&case=2`, "Not enrolled"), Settings > AI Mode > Hybrid > Auto (status chip), Preview "Own key: On" then the credits pill ("Active").
- Busy: the dot pulses (`.v2-anim-pulse`, `--v2-dur-pulse`) while the thing is working. In V2: the status chip while downloading (ai_mode.jsx:39).
- Reduced motion: the dot holds still (global rule in kit/motion.css).

**Props.** `Badge { tone = 'neutral' | 'info' | 'success' | 'attention' | 'danger', dot, busy, icon, className, children }`. `busy` implies the dot. `icon` replaces the dot. Other props pass to the span.

**Motion.** Only the busy dot: `.v2-anim-pulse`, loops while busy, stops under reduced motion.

**Accessibility.** The label is the state in words; colour and the mark only repeat it. Icons and dots are `aria-hidden`. A badge is never focusable and never the only place a consequence is said.

**Responsive.** Never wraps; long labels truncate with an ellipsis inside the row's trailing slot. Keep labels short enough not to need it.

**Do / Don't.** Do put one badge at a row's trailing edge. Do say the state in words. Do use the ticks for days. Don't uppercase or letter-space. Don't colour the label. Don't use a badge as a button or a filter (that is Chip, section 15). Don't write "Not enrolled": the glossary says "Not in the program".

## 17 Avatar

**Status:** Changed from V2.

**Why:** The partner signs in with the account they applied with, and BlueAI acts on their own YouTube account. Who is signed in, and which account a connected app is, must be plain at a glance. V2's Profile puts a 92px bat emoji on a green disc over a gradient banner where that identity goes (profile.jsx:31-34); the sign-in gate already does it right with an initial on the account's hue (account_gate.jsx:20, 51). The system keeps the gate's idea.

### Avatar (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** Who is signed in (Account, the sign-in gate, menus) and which app an account row is about (Connected apps).

**Anatomy.**
1. Round disc (`--v2-radius-round`).
2. Content, one of: initials, a photo over the initials, or an app glyph.
3. Initials: white (`--v2-on-color`), 600. One letter up to 32, first and last initial from 40.
4. Fill for initials: one of two hues picked from the account id (stable for that account): `--v2-action` or `--v2-ink`. Both clear 4.5:1 with white; V2's sign-in hue `#156efe` (signin_page.jsx:26) gives 4.47:1, so hue 0 is the action blue.
5. App mark: the app's glyph from the icon registry in `--v2-ink-2` on `--v2-surface-sunken` with a 1px `--v2-line` inset. No brand colours.
6. Status dot (optional): 28% of the size (8 minimum), bottom right, 2px `--v2-surface` ring; success, attention, danger or neutral.

**Variants.** Initials (hue 0, hue 1). Photo. App mark: YouTube, Instagram, Telegram.

**Sizes.** 24 (menus, initials 11px), 32 (rows, 13px), 40 (account rows, 14px), 56 (sign-in and Account, 20px). App glyph 16 / 16 / 20 / 24.

**States.**
- Photo: covers the initials once loaded. New (V2 has no photo).
- Photo failed: falls back to the initials, no broken image. New.
- Loading: a skeleton circle the same size. New.
- Status: connected, needs you, signed out. New (Connected apps, section 63).
- In V2 today: Kebab > Profile (emoji avatar); `?cases=signin&case=2` (56px initial on the gate).

**Props.** `Avatar { name, id, src, size = 24 | 32 | 40 | 56 (default 32), app = 'youtube' | 'instagram' | 'telegram', status = 'success' | 'attention' | 'danger' | 'neutral', statusLabel, loading, decorative, className }`. `id` picks the hue (falls back to `name`). With `app`, `name` is ignored.

**Motion.** None. Loading uses the shared shimmer.

**Accessibility.** Announced as an image named after the person or app plus the status ("YouTube, Connected"). When the name is printed beside it (rows, the gate), pass `decorative`. The status dot always repeats a status said in words nearby (badge or description).

**Responsive.** Fixed sizes; never stretches. In rows it sits in the leading slot.

**Do / Don't.** Do show the name and email beside it. Do use the account photo when there is one. Don't use emoji, mascots or illustrations for a person. Don't add brand colours to app marks. Don't add more hues: two tell two accounts apart, and the email says which. Don't bring back the gradient banner.

## 18 List row

**Status:** Changed from V2.

**Why:** Every screen the concept adds (Activity, Notifications, Worker settings, Connected apps, Account) is a list. V2 has four row shapes for one idea, with two label treatments: Profile rows (grey 14.5 label, 14.5/600 value, profile.jsx:17-24), Settings field rows (15/700 label over a 12px hint at 2.54:1, settings.jsx:97-102), the Sign out card (a whole card, hover from a mouse handler, profile.jsx:57-59) and History rows (a card each, a turning chevron, no aria-expanded, history.jsx:96-104). One row with variants replaces them.

### ListRow (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** The repeated line of every list: a fact (label + value), a setting (label + control), a place (navigation), a day or run you can open (expandable), an irreversible action (destructive).

**Anatomy.**
1. Row: min 48 tall, padding 12 16 (`--v2-space-3` `--v2-space-4`), gap 12, items centred.
2. Leading (optional), one of: a 20px icon in `--v2-ink-3`; an Avatar 32 / 40; or a mark (`mark` prop): a `--v2-mark-md` (32) round `--v2-surface-sunken` disc with a 16 glyph in its tone (ink-3, or attention, success or danger ink) and, when `dot`, an 8 `--v2-blue` dot at its top right with a 2px surface halo. The mark is for a row about a thing or an event, not a person: a notification (section 60), BlueStacks in Settings (section 61).
3. Text: label 14/500 `--v2-ink`; description 13 `--v2-ink-3` under it (also where a disabled row says why).
4. Trailing, one of: value (14/400 `--v2-ink-2`, tabular, right-aligned), a control (Switch, Select), a Badge, a chevron (16, `--v2-ink-4`), or the external mark.
5. Divider: 1px `--v2-line-hair`, inset 16 on both sides, between rows only (none after the last).

**Variants.** Value. Control (the row is a plain div; the control is the only target). Nav (a button or a link; chevron-right, or `external` for the site). Expandable (chevron-down that turns 180; a panel below). Destructive (label and icon in `--v2-danger-ink`).

**Sizes.** One. Min 48; grows with a two-line description.

**States.**
- Rest: all variants. In V2: Kebab > Profile; Settings tab (Chat AI Settings card); Kebab > Chat History.
- Hover (nav and expandable only): `--v2-wash-hover`. In V2: the Sign out card.
- Focus-visible: 2px `--v2-blue` outline inset 2 (rows sit in cards that clip). New; V2 has none.
- Pressed: `--v2-wash-press`. New.
- Disabled: label, icon, value and chevron at `--v2-opacity-disabled`; the reason, as the description, stays readable. `aria-disabled`, still focusable. New.
- Expanded: `aria-expanded="true"`, chevron turned, panel shown. In V2: History (without the attribute).

**Props.** `ListRow { leading, mark: {icon, tone: 'neutral' | 'attention' | 'success' | 'danger', dot}, label, description, value, trailing, badge, onClick, href, external, danger, expandable, expanded, onToggle, divider = true, disabled, disabledReason, children (the expandable panel), className }`. `onClick` makes a button, `href` a link, `expandable` a button with a panel; none makes a div. `expanded` controls it, or it keeps its own state. `mark` draws the mark leading and wins over `leading`; it is decorative (the label and description say the event, and an unread dot is said in words by the row).

**Motion.** Hover wash `--v2-dur-1`; chevron turn `--v2-dur-2` `--v2-ease-out`. Nothing under reduced motion.

**Accessibility.** One target per row. Expandable rows say `aria-expanded` and point at their panel with `aria-controls`. External links add "(opens in your browser)" for screen readers. Description text is 6.0:1. A control row labels its control with the row's words.

**Responsive.** Label and value wrap rather than truncate; the value takes at most half the row. At 419 wide a description of two lines is fine; three is a sign it belongs on its own screen.

**Do / Don't.** Do put a list in one Card with `padding="none"`. Do give a disabled row its reason. Do use a value row for facts and a nav row for places. Don't put a control in a nav row. Don't make a card per row. Don't add hover to rows that do nothing. Don't use bold labels as headings inside a list (that is the screen header's job).

## 19 Card and stat card

**Status:** Card: Changed from V2. StatCard: Changed from V2.

**Why:** BlueAI is a quiet control room: the surfaces should separate content and get out of the way, the way the onBlue site's 1px lines do; the site also adds a faint resting cast, which the product drops (section 02, P6). V2's in-flow cards carry seven shadow strengths over a different line on each (settings.jsx:25, profile.jsx:30, index.html:441, worker_day_done.jsx:176), and its one important figure, the credits balance, is 54/800 brand gradient (index.html:527). The partner returns to the site to cash out; the same figure should look the same in both places.

### Card (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** The one surface for grouped content: a settings group, the worker card, a list of rows, a run. Static unless the whole card goes somewhere.

**Anatomy.** `--v2-surface`, 1px `--v2-line`, radius 16 (`--v2-radius-lg`, the site's card radius), padding 16, no shadow (`--v2-shadow-0`).

**Variants.** Static (div, never answers hover). Pressable (a real button or link; the line firms up on hover). Inset panel (the panel step inside a card: `--v2-surface-panel`, `--v2-line-hair`, radius 10, padding 12).

**Sizes.** Padding md 16 (default), sm 12, none (for a list of rows; the card clips them to its radius).

**States.**
- Static: V2 Settings tab (BlueStacks, Telegram cards).
- Pressable hover: line to `--v2-line-hover`. Pressed: a `--v2-wash-press` layer. Focus-visible: 2px blue outline, offset 2. All new (V2 cards are not pressable).

**Props.** `Card { as = 'div' | 'button' | 'a', pressable, inset, padding = 'md' | 'sm' | 'none', href, external, onClick, className, children }`. `onClick` or `pressable` makes a button; `href` makes a link.

**Motion.** Line colour `--v2-dur-1`. None under reduced motion.

**Accessibility.** A pressable card is a single button or link whose accessible name is its content; never put another control inside it. Static cards are not focusable.

**Responsive.** Full width of the content band (419 minus the 16 gutter each side). Stack 12 apart.

**Do / Don't.** Do stack cards 12 apart on `--v2-canvas`. Do make the whole card the target when it opens one place. Don't add a shadow to anything in the flow. Don't nest cards: use the inset panel. Don't add hover to a static card.

### StatCard (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** One figure that matters, with the words that make it mean something: AI credits left today, your 20 days, the next run. Credits are an allowance and money is a figure; both stay in ink. Green means a state was reached.

**Anatomy.** A Card containing:
1. Label: mini-title 13/600 ink, sentence case, 0.02em (`.v2-text-mini-title`; the site's `.mini-title`, onblue.css:1935-1942). One label style per card (the site's mini-title rule, onblue/apply.html:1358).
2. Figure: optional icon (the credits glyph), value 40/600 `--v2-ink`, tabular, `--v2-tracking-display`; optional unit 14/500 `--v2-ink-3`.
3. Context: one line, 13 `--v2-ink-3`; figures inside it bold ink ("**6** verified, **1** pending").
4. Action (optional): one link or quiet button (Earnings opens the site).

**Variants.** Display (figure 40). Compact (figure 20, `--v2-tracking-title`, tighter gaps).

**Sizes.** Display and compact; full width.

**States.**
- Value: V2 opens the AI Credits screen from the credits pill (`?start=worker`).
- Zero: same size as any value; the Banner above says why. V2 shrinks the figure to 38 (`?start=worker&credits=0`, then the pill).
- Loading: label stays, figure and context become skeletons, `aria-busy`. New.
- Replaced (own key): the figure becomes "Running on your own key" with V2's line "No BlueAI credits are consumed while this is on." (index.html:482-485; Preview "Own key: On").
- Compact: V2's EarningsStrip (`?day=returning`).

**Props.** `StatCard { label, value (number formats as 2,000), unit, context, action, icon, loading, size = 'display' | 'compact', className }`, plus any Card prop.

**Motion.** None. Loading uses the shared shimmer.

**Accessibility.** Reads in order: label, figure, unit, context. Figures are text, never images. Context is 6.0:1.

**Responsive.** Left-aligned; the figure never wraps. Long text values (own key) use compact.

**Do / Don't.** Do keep one figure per card. Do keep the figure's size at zero. Do say credits as "left today". Don't paint figures in the gradient or green. Don't use two label styles in one card. Don't say "earn" or "balance" for credits.

## 20 Progress, spinner, skeleton

**Status:** ProgressBar: Changed from V2. Spinner: Changed from V2. Skeleton: Changed from V2.

**Why:** The worker does things the partner waits on: App Player installs, a key validates, a run moves through its steps, screens load. Each wait needs one honest signal. V2 draws the same 6px blue bar twice on two track blues (ai_mode.jsx:140, needs_bluestacks.jsx:138) and fakes "indeterminate" by pulsing a full bar (ai_mode.jsx:141); it has five busy marks in three colours (index.html:78, login.jsx:256, chat_bubbles.jsx:122, boot_splash.jsx:66, ai_mode.jsx:39); and its home skeleton draws a category list that never arrives (boot_splash.jsx:80-114). All loops come from kit/motion.css so one reduced-motion rule covers them.

### ProgressBar (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** An amount done: a download or install, a setup, progress toward a goal.

**Anatomy.**
1. Label row: label 13/500 `--v2-ink` left; value 13 `--v2-ink-3` right, tabular ("45%", "12 of 20 days", "Download failed").
2. Rail: `--v2-surface-sunken`, pill radius, 6 tall (4 inline).
3. Fill: `--v2-blue`, width eases over `--v2-dur-3` `--v2-ease-out`.

**Variants.** Determinate. Indeterminate: a 40% segment slides across (`.v2-anim-indet`; V2's own idea, product-scene.css:110-116, at 38%). Success tone for a goal (the site's program bar, onblue/apply.html:575, in `--v2-success`).

**Sizes.** 6 (cards, notices), 4 (inline).

**States.**
- 0 to 100: blue. V2: the install card in chat (`?start=worker&ask=Watch%20a%20YouTube%20video`, then Get BlueStacks), or Settings > AI Mode > Hybrid > Auto.
- Complete (value = max): fill turns `--v2-success` (reached). In V2 the ring swaps to a check (needs_bluestacks.jsx:133).
- Indeterminate: sliding segment, no value. V2: "Checking…" in AI Mode.
- Error: fill `--v2-danger-ink`, frozen where it stopped, value says what failed. New.
- Paused: fill `--v2-ink-4`, value "Paused". New.
- Reduced motion: indeterminate becomes a full, quiet track (0.45 opacity).

**Props.** `ProgressBar { value, max = 100, indeterminate, tone = 'success' (for goals), size = 4 | 6, label, valueText, paused, error, 'aria-label' (when there is no label), className }`. `valueText` overrides the percentage.

**Motion.** Width ease `--v2-dur-3`; indeterminate `.v2-anim-indet` (`--v2-dur-shimmer`, loops while waiting).

**Accessibility.** The rail is `role="progressbar"` labelled by the label, with `aria-valuenow` (omitted when indeterminate) and `aria-valuetext` ("62%, failed"). The visible value is hidden from screen readers to avoid reading it twice. State never by colour alone: error and paused say so in the value.

**Responsive.** Full width of its container; the label truncates before the value does.

**Do / Don't.** Do say what is happening in the label ("Getting ready"). Do freeze an error where it stopped. Don't pulse a full bar to mean "unknown". Don't show a percentage you cannot measure.

### Spinner (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** Working, where the result has no shape yet: a button that sent something, a key being checked, a run step in progress.

**Anatomy.** A ring in `currentColor`: track at 20%, a quarter-turn arc with round caps, turning `.v2-anim-spin` (`--v2-dur-spin`, linear).

**Variants.** Inherit (takes the text colour). On colour (`--v2-on-color`, inside a filled button). Done (a `check` in `--v2-success`).

**Sizes.** 12 (the Switch's busy thumb), 16 (buttons, rows, steps, beside text), 32 (standalone). Stroke 1.5 / 2 / 4.

**States.**
- Delayed: hidden for the first 300ms, space held, so fast work never flashes. New.
- Spinning. V2: the install card's ring (same URL, then Get BlueStacks), the signing-in ring (`?signin=browser`).
- Done: swaps to the check, so a finished step never reads as stuck (V2's install card does this, needs_bluestacks.jsx:123-135).
- Reduced motion: turns at half speed (it only exists while the partner waits).

**Props.** `Spinner { size = 12 | 16 | 32, label (screen-reader text; makes it a status), delay = 300 (ms; 0 shows at once), done, variant = 'on-color', className }`.

**Motion.** `.v2-anim-spin`, loops only while shown.

**Accessibility.** Decorative by default (the button or row says "Sending" or "Working on it…"). With `label` it is a polite status that announces when it appears and says "Done" when done. In a button, the button carries `aria-busy`.

**Responsive.** Fixed sizes.

**Do / Don't.** Do keep words next to it. Do swap to the check when done. Don't use dots, indigo, or the boot bounce outside the splash. Don't show a spinner for a view that is loading: that is a skeleton.

### Skeleton (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** A placeholder in the shape of what is on its way: rows of Activity, the worker card, a settings group.

**Anatomy.** `--v2-surface-sunken` shapes with the shared shimmer (`.v2-anim-shimmer`, `--v2-dur-shimmer`).

**Variants.** Line (12 tall, radius 4), title (16 tall, 60% wide), circle (32 by default, round), rect (64 tall, radius 10), lines (n lines, the last at 60%).

**Sizes.** Free: `width` and `height` props (numbers are px).

**States.**
- Shimmering. V2: every cold start shows HomeSkeleton for 850ms (index.html:745).
- Still under reduced motion (V2 has no rule, boot_splash.jsx:14).
- Replaced by content: the screen swaps the composite for the real thing in the same place.

**Props.** `Skeleton { shape = 'line' | 'title' | 'circle' | 'rect', width, height, lines, className }`.

**Motion.** Shimmer loops while loading; none under reduced motion.

**Accessibility.** Hidden from screen readers; the region that is loading carries `aria-busy="true"` and its heading stays real.

**Responsive.** Widths in % follow the container.

**Do / Don't.** Do build each screen's composite line for line from its content (the worker home's composite lives with Home, section 57). Don't show a skeleton for under 300ms of work. Don't reuse one screen's skeleton on another.

## 21 Banner

**Status:** Changed from V2.

**Why:** Some conditions decide whether today counts: out of AI credits, offline, App Player signed out, Automatic on. The partner has to see them above what they affect, said the same way every time. V2 has five banners in two blues, two ambers and two reds: an edgeless InfoBanner (ai_mode.jsx:71-78), a centred out-of-credits block at radius 14 (index.html:508-519), a second amber for BYOK (byok_settings.jsx:109), a sign-in notice at radius 12 (login.jsx:235), and the one-line Automatic ribbon (agent_permissions.jsx:203-216). None can be dismissed, none has an offline tone.

### Banner (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** A condition that holds until it ends, placed directly above what it is about. One-time news (a verified day) may use it too, and only then can it be dismissed.

**Anatomy.**
1. Box: radius 10 (`--v2-radius-md`), 1px tone line, tone tint, padding 12, gap 8, left-aligned.
2. Icon: 16, in the tone ink.
3. Text: title 13/600 `--v2-ink` (optional), body 13 `--v2-ink-2`, `--v2-leading-body`. Figures in the body are bold ink, tabular.
4. Actions (trailing, optional): one action as `V2Kit.Link` standalone (an underlined 13/500 ink link with a 32px target, `kit/notice.jsx`), and/or a 32px dismiss (`close`, labelled "Dismiss").

The words stay in ink so every tone passes 4.5:1; the tone lives in the tint, the line and the icon.

**Variants.** Five tones:
- info: `--v2-blue-tint`, `--v2-blue-tint-line`, icon `--v2-blue-ink` (`info`). A fact, nothing to do.
- success: `--v2-success-tint`, `--v2-success-line`, icon `--v2-success-ink` (`check`). News.
- attention: `--v2-attention-tint`, `--v2-attention-line`, icon `--v2-attention-ink` (`alert`). The partner has to do something.
- danger: `--v2-danger-tint` #fdecec, `--v2-danger-line` rgba(208,69,74,0.32) (the pale edge; the solid `--v2-danger-edge` is for invalid fields only), icon `--v2-danger-ink` (`x-circle`). Today is blocked.
- offline: `--v2-surface-sunken`, `--v2-line`, icon `--v2-ink-3` (`wifi-off`). New. This is the Banner's neutral tone (section 03): a neutral condition that is not about the connection uses it with its own icon (the AI-mode conflict: `tone="offline" icon="alert"`, section 49). There is no `neutral` value; an unknown tone renders as info.
Any icon can replace the default (Automatic uses `zap`).

**Sizes.** One. Full width of its container.

**States.**
- Static. V2: Settings > AI Mode with Preview "Hardware: No" (Hybrid unsupported); Settings > BYOK on with no key; `?signin=browser` (the in-app "Check your browser" step: Use the account you applied with).
- With action. V2: `?start=worker&mode=auto`, then Run (the ribbon's "Change").
- Dismissible: news only. New.
- Live value: the out-of-credits countdown (`?start=worker&credits=0`, then the credits pill). The ticking value sits in `<strong aria-live="off">` so it is read once.
- Action hover (blue ink, underline firms), focus-visible (2px blue outline): the Link's own states. New.

**Props.** `Banner { tone = 'info' | 'success' | 'attention' | 'danger' | 'offline', icon (name, or null for none), title, children, action ({ label, onClick } or { label, href, external } or a node), onDismiss, dismissLabel = 'Dismiss', className }`.

**Motion.** None of its own. A screen may bring it in with `.v2-anim-fade-up`.

**Accessibility.** Danger is `role="alert"`; the others `role="status"`, so a banner that appears is announced once. The action and the dismiss are real buttons or links with visible focus and 32px targets. Never colour alone: the title or body says the condition.

**Responsive.** Text wraps; the action stays at the trailing edge, top-aligned with the first line through its negative margin.

**Do / Don't.** Do place it above what it is about and remove it when the condition ends. Do say what it means for today and the one next step ("Today’s run starts when BlueAI is back online."). Don't dismiss a condition: it would hide something still true. Don't centre the text or invent a sixth tone. Don't stack two: show the one that blocks today.

## 22 Empty state

**Status:** Changed from V2.

**Why:** A new partner opens Activity before their first run and Notifications before anything has happened; an empty view has to say what will be there and when. V2's two empty states use off-palette art (Schedule: a purple icon on a purple disc, schedule.jsx:126-132; Jobs: a 120px disc with a brown briefcase over "Coming Soon", jobs.jsx:129-145, standing in for a whole tab). The onBlue site already has the family's answer: the dashed ghost and copy that names what will appear (onblue/apply.html:323-327, 1422).

### EmptyState (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** What a view shows with nothing in it: names what will appear, gives one line of why, and offers the one way forward when there is one.

**Anatomy.**
1. Box: 1px dashed `--v2-line-strong`, radius 16, `--v2-surface-panel`, padding 32 24, centred column, gap 8. A dashed edge means "a thing will be here".
2. Icon: 24 (`--v2-icon-lg`) in `--v2-ink-3`. No disc, no colour.
3. Title: 16/600 `--v2-ink`, an h2 by default.
4. Body: 13 `--v2-ink-3`, at most 280 wide.
5. Actions (optional): one medium primary Button (36), and a quiet one beside it only if needed.

**Variants.** Five, which change the words and the default icon, never the colours:
- first use (`first`, icon `activity`): "No days yet" / "Each run shows here with its verification: one tick while pending, two once verified." plus the next run time ("Next run today at 11 am."); before setup, + Set up your worker (`screens/activity.jsx:138-140`).
- no results (`noresults`, icon `info`; Notifications passes `check`): "Nothing unread" / "You’ve seen everything your worker sent.", no action (`screens/notifications.jsx:123`).
- error (`error`, icon `alert`): "Couldn’t load your days" / "Nothing is lost: your runs and days are not affected. Try again in a moment." + Try again (`V2Screens.LoadError`'s words, `screens/home.jsx:216-217`).
- offline (`offline`, icon `wifi-off`): "You’re offline" / "BlueAI can’t reach the internet, so there’s nothing to show yet. Today’s run starts when you’re back online." + Try again (section 69, `screens/system-states.jsx:87-89`).
- locked (`locked`, icon `lock`; the update state passes `download`): "Update BlueAI to keep running" / "This version can’t run jobs anymore, so runs are paused. Update now and today can still count. It takes about a minute." + Update now (section 69, `screens/system-states.jsx:121-123`).
(All proposed copy.)

**Sizes.** One; adapts to the width of its container.

**States.**
- Static. V2 today: the Jobs tab (`?start=worker`, then Jobs); Schedule's empty state only after deleting both seeded rows.
- Action loading: the primary Button shows its spinner and `aria-busy` (error, Try again). New.

**Props.** `EmptyState { variant = 'first' | 'noresults' | 'error' | 'offline' | 'locked', icon (name, or null), title, body, primaryAction, secondaryAction, headingLevel = 2, className }`. An action is `{ label, onClick, href, external, loading, icon }` or a node.

**Motion.** Arrives with `.v2-anim-fade-up` (180ms, 6px rise); simply there under reduced motion.

**Accessibility.** The title is a real heading. The error variant is `role="alert"`. Body text is 6.0:1 (V2's Schedule body is 2.54:1). The button is the only target.

**Responsive.** Centred; the body keeps a readable measure of 280 at any width.

**Do / Don't.** Do name what will appear and when, in the site's voice ("Nothing here yet. Programs you finish will show up here, with what each one earned."). Do offer the action that fills the view, if there is one. Do say what unlocks a locked view. Don't use "Coming Soon" for a tab. Don't use coloured discs or illustrations. Don't add a button that does not fill the view.

## 23 Tooltip and info tip

**Status:** Tooltip: Changed from V2.

**Why:** The partner meets two words the product cannot leave unexplained (AI credits, your 20 days) and a top bar of icon-only buttons with no visible names. V2 already built the right disclosure for the first job, the credits (i): hover, focus, click to pin, Esc, a 200ms close delay (credits_popup_parts.jsx:135-146). It only works for one centred title (left -189, line 148), and nothing covers the second job. The system keeps the behaviour, fits it to any window, and adds a label kind (P7: every control has a visible name; P5: one float look).

### Tooltip (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** `kind="info"` explains a concept right after its name, in two short sentences and at most one link. `kind="label"` shows an icon-only button's name.

**Anatomy.** Info: a 24px round mark (icon `info` 16, `--v2-ink-3`) inside a 32px target, then a float 8px away: `--v2-surface`, 1px `--v2-line`, radius 10 (`--v2-radius-md`), `--v2-shadow-1`, padding 12, text 13/1.5 `--v2-ink-2`, max 260 wide, an 8px arrow pointing at the mark. Label: the same float without the arrow, padding 4 8, radius 6, 12/500, one line. Layer `--v2-z-popover` (30).

**Variants.** `info` (opens on hover, keyboard focus and click; may hold one link) and `label` (hover after 400ms, keyboard focus at once; text only, equal to the button's `aria-label`).

**Sizes.** One. Info max 260px wide; label hugs its word.

**States.** Hidden; hover; keyboard focus; pinned by click (the mark turns `--v2-blue-ink` on `--v2-select`, an outside click unpins); Esc (shut until the pointer leaves or focus moves); flipped (no room on the asked side: it opens on the other and slides to stay 8px inside the window). In V2 today: `?credits=0`, open the credits pill: the red (i) in the AI Credits banner (index.html:513); or `?credits=0` and send a message: the (i) on the out-of-credits card (byok.jsx:59).

**Props.** `kind` ('label' | 'info'), `content` (node), `placement` ('up' | 'down', default up), `label` (names the default info mark; proposed "What are AI credits?"), `open` (force open), `className`, `children` (the trigger; info adds `aria-expanded`, `aria-describedby` and click-to-pin to it).

**Motion.** Fade-up over `--v2-dur-3` on `--v2-ease-out`; out over `--v2-dur-1` after a 200ms grace, with a bridge over the gap so the pointer can reach the link. Open and close are CSS states (`:hover`, `:focus-within`, `.is-open`); JS only pins, dismisses and measures.

**Accessibility.** The info trigger is a real button with a name, `aria-expanded` for the pinned state and `aria-describedby` to the text; Tab reaches the link next. A label tip is `aria-hidden`: the button's own `aria-label` carries the name, the tip shows it. Mark 6.0:1 at rest (V2 #94a3b8 was 2.56:1). Target 32px.

**Responsive.** Fits the window it is in: the nearest `[data-v2-bounds]` or AppFrame. Works the same on the compact 640px window.

**Do / Don't.** Do give every icon-only button a label tip (IconButton does this by default, kit/button.jsx). Do put an info tip right after the term it explains. Do keep V2's approved explainer copy word for word. Don't put the only statement of a consequence in a tip; say it on the control. Don't put a second control in a tip; that is a Popover. Don't hard-code an offset.

## 24 Toast

**Status:** Toast: New.

**Why:** Confirm, destroy, undo (section 52) says a cheap mistake gets Undo, not a confirm. Undo needs a home, and V2 has none: its only in-app confirmation is an inline "Settings saved successfully." that vanishes after 2.5 s (settings.jsx:93, 136), while removing your own key and unlinking an app act at once with no confirm and no undo (byok_settings.jsx:75, settings.jsx:59). The toast is that home. It is not the Windows notification (desktop_toast.jsx:12), which reaches a partner who is not looking at BlueAI; a toast only confirms something the partner did in this window a second ago.

### Toast (New)

**Purpose.** Confirm a change just made here (saved, removed, sent, copied) and offer the one follow-up that matters: Undo or Try again.

**Anatomy.** Bottom centre of the window, 12px above the bottom nav. A row that hugs its words, max 388, at least 44 tall: `--v2-surface`, 1px `--v2-line`, radius 10, `--v2-shadow-1`. Icon 16 (check-circle `--v2-success`, info `--v2-blue`, alert `--v2-danger-ink`, a Spinner while loading), title 13/500 `--v2-ink`, an optional text action (13/500 `--v2-blue-ink`, 32px target), a 32px Dismiss button. Layer `--v2-z-toast` (120, above dialogs).

**Variants.** `success`, `info`, `error` (with Try again), `loading` (turns into its result in place). Any of them may carry an `action`; with Undo the change applies now, Undo reverses it, and it commits when the toast goes.

**Sizes.** One.

**States.** Entering; visible; paused (hover or keyboard focus holds the timer); leaving; replaced (a new toast replaces the current one: a 419px window cannot stack). Timing: 4s, 8s for an error, loading waits for `update()`. A toast with Undo or Try again has no timer: it stays until Dismiss, its action, the next toast, or the screen calls `dismiss()` on leaving, so the action can be reached by keyboard and screen reader at any pace (WCAG 2.2.1, `kit/toast.jsx:17-23, 41-45`); a change it can undo commits then. Not in V2 (New): the guide shows every state forced, and its Try it window fires each one.

**Props.** `ToastHost {offset 'nav' | 'none', region, className, children}` holds the one toast and provides `useToast()`. AppFrame draws the window's one `ToastRegion` (`kit/shell.jsx:32`) and mounts its own host when none sits above it, so a part drawn inside the frame can call `useToast()`. A screen that shows toasts from its own body (above its frame) wraps it in `V2Screens.ToastScope` and calls `useScreenToast()` (`screens/home.jsx:157-163`; section 74, step 4); an app-level host above AppFrame must pass `region={false}`, or it draws a second live region beside AppFrame's and every toast is drawn and announced twice (`kit/toast.jsx:130`). `ToastRegion {offset, className}` is AppFrame's; screens never render it. `useToast()` returns `show({tone: 'success' | 'info' | 'error' | 'loading', title, action: {label, onClick}, duration}) -> id`, `update(id, opts)`, `dismiss(id)`; tone 'danger' is read as 'error' (section 03). `Toast {tone, title, action, duration, leaving, onClose}` is the drawn part; `duration={0}` shows it without a timer.

**Motion.** Fade-up `--v2-dur-2` (180ms) on `--v2-ease-out`; fade out `--v2-dur-1` (120ms) on `--v2-ease-in`. The timer is a CSS animation, so pausing is a CSS state; under reduced motion everything else is instant but the timer keeps its length.

**Accessibility.** Two live regions, polite and assertive, both always mounted; the toast goes into the one its tone needs (an error into the assertive one). Toasts never take focus; the action and Dismiss are reachable by Tab, and focus pauses the timer. Never the only route to a fix: a condition that holds is a Banner (section 21).

**Responsive.** Max 388 inside the 419 window with 16px gutters; on a screen without the bottom nav the region sits 16px from the bottom (AppFrame passes `offset="none"` itself when it has no nav).

**Do / Don't.** Do confirm what the partner just did, in a few words (proposed copy: "Settings saved", "Key removed" + Undo, "Sending your report…" then "Report sent", "Couldn’t save your change" + Try again, "Automatic is off for YouTube"). Do prefer Undo to a confirm for anything cheap to reverse. Don't use a toast for something the worker did while the partner was away: that is the Windows notification and the inbox (section 54). Don't stack toasts.

## 25 Dialog

**Status:** Dialog, AlertDialog (confirm, destructive): both Changed from V2.

**Why:** The partner's consent, sign-out and disconnect decisions happen in dialogs, and V2 builds each one separately: five widths (280, 300, 340, 360, 382), radii 12, 16 and 20, five different scrims, z-indexes from 50 to 130 with a tie at 120 (shared.jsx:72-73, profile.jsx:73-74, byok.jsx:79-80, worker_day_done.jsx:139-142, terms_sheet.jsx:97). None moves focus in or gives it back, the close button has no name (shared.jsx:76) and none has a busy state, so a slow consent can be clicked twice. One Dialog on one scrim and one layer fixes all of it (P5, P7, R24), with the onBlue sheet's quiet manners: the panel takes focus, not its close button (onblue/onblue.css:942), and the title takes the row (onblue.css:1384).

### Dialog (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** A decision or a short task the partner must finish or decline before going on: consent to Automatic, sign out, disconnect, out of credits, the day-done moment.

**Anatomy.** Scrim `--v2-scrim` over the window. Panel centred with a 16px inset: `--v2-surface`, 1px `--v2-line`, radius 16 (`--v2-radius-lg`), `--v2-shadow-2`. Header: title 20/600 `--v2-ink` (tracking -0.02em) taking the row, a 32px IconButton named Close beside it. Body: 14/1.5 `--v2-ink-2`, padding 20, scrolls when tall. Actions: 44px Buttons of equal width, 8px apart, secondary first, primary last. Optional footer note 12 `--v2-ink-3`. Layer `--v2-z-dialog` (100). One dialog at a time; if two ever overlap, mount order decides which is on top (section 06), which only settles the overlap, it does not make stacking a pattern.

**Variants.** `task` (md, Close, scrim click cancels). `consent` (md, Close, scrim click does nothing, the primary's label is the agreement: "I agree, turn on"). `alert` (see AlertDialog). `celebration` (sm, centred, no Close, scrim click closes: DayDone). `scrim="frost"` puts it on the sign-in gate veil and layer (`--v2-z-gate`), sign-in only.

**Sizes.** sm 320 (`--v2-dialog-sm`): alert, celebration, out of credits. md 382 (`--v2-dialog-md`): task, consent. Height up to the window less 16px each side.

**States.** Closed (nothing mounted); opening; open; busy (the primary loads; Esc, the scrim and Close wait); closing; over a menu (the menu closes first, focus comes back to the menu button). In V2 today: ModalOverlay as Confirm delete on the Schedule tab (schedule.jsx:144); the Automatic consent at `?start=worker&v=1`, open the mode chip, pick Automatic, in ModalOverlay's plain frame since 2026-10-14 (no header, divider or X; agent_permissions.jsx:189); out of credits at `?credits=0` then send a message (byok.jsx:76).

**Props.** `open`, `onClose`, `title`, `description`, `variant`, `size` ('sm' | 'md'), `role` (override), `dismissible` (false blocks: no Close, no Esc, no scrim), `busy`, `actions` ([{label, onClick, variant 'secondary' | 'primary' | 'danger' | 'quiet', disabled}]), `footer`, `initialFocus` (ref or selector), `labelledBy` (when the title lives in the body), `scrim`, `className`, `children`. `preview` renders it open in place without focus (guide specimens only).

**Motion.** Scale-in `--v2-dur-4` (320ms) on `--v2-ease-out`; scrim fades `--v2-dur-3`; exit fades `--v2-dur-3` on `--v2-ease-in`. Reduced motion: instant (motion.css).

**Accessibility.** role dialog, `aria-modal`, named by its title, described by `description`. Where focus lands on open, one rule (section 09): the panel itself for dialogs read before acting (task, consent, celebration), with no ring until Tab; the first field, through `initialFocus`, for a dialog that is a form; Cancel, the first action, for an AlertDialog. Tab cycles inside; Esc, Close and Cancel return focus to the opener. `aria-busy` while busy. Close is named. Every label at least 4.5:1.

**Responsive.** Scoped to the window (absolute inside AppFrame's overlay plane), never the page. On the compact window the body scrolls; header and actions stay.

**Do / Don't.** Do write the title as the question and the body as what changes and how to undo it. Do set `busy` while an answer is recorded. Don't confirm something cheap to reverse; act and offer Undo (section 24). Don't stack a dialog on a dialog. Don't use a dialog for a long form; that is a Sheet.

### AlertDialog (confirm, destructive) (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** The confirm before something that cannot be taken back cheaply, or that changes what the worker may do.

**Anatomy.** Dialog sm with role alertdialog, no Close: the title is the question, the body says what changes, two actions: Cancel and the action named for itself.

**Variants.** Confirm: a neutral primary (`--v2-action`, 4.92:1). Destructive: `danger` (`--v2-danger-ink` #c03a40, 5.36:1), only when it destroys or costs a day.

**Sizes.** sm 320.

**States.** Confirm; destructive; busy. In V2 today: Profile, Sign out opens Logout (profile.jsx:69-84), reached at `?start=worker`, more menu, Profile.

**Props.** `variant="alert"` plus the Dialog props.

**Motion.** As Dialog.

**Accessibility.** Focus starts on Cancel, the safe answer. Esc cancels; a scrim click does nothing.

**Responsive.** As Dialog.

**Do / Don't.** Do name the primary for the action (Sign out, Disconnect, Delete), never OK or Yes. Do say the consequence; the sign-out confirm's strings are owned by section 64, the disconnect confirm's by section 63. Don't paint Sign out as destructive: V2's "Logout" on #ef4444 (3.76:1, profile.jsx:79) suggests loss, and confirming it does not even sign out (index.html:1010, R03).

## 26 Sheet

**Status:** Sheet: Changed from V2.

**Why:** The Program Terms are the partner's first agreement, so they block until accepted; longer panels (Report a problem, a run's detail on a short window) need a place over the current screen that is roomier than a dialog. V2's Terms sheet is its one overlay with a proper entrance, exit and reduced-motion rule (terms_sheet.jsx:103-108), so it becomes the family sheet. Its shell joins the system (radius, title weight, an AA primary, one scrim, one layer instead of a z 120 tie with Logout); its legal wording stays exactly as legal approved it.

### Sheet (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** `blocking`: an agreement the partner must give to continue. Dismissible (default): a longer panel that belongs to the current screen.

**Anatomy.** Scrim `--v2-scrim`. Panel on the window's bottom edge, full width: `--v2-surface`, 1px top `--v2-line`, radius 16 16 0 0, `--v2-shadow-2`, padding 24. Header: title 20/600 `--v2-ink` taking the row; a 32px Close when dismissible. Body 14/1.5 `--v2-ink-2`, scrolls when tall (the sheet stops 40px short of the window top). Actions 44px: one spans the width, two share it (secondary, primary). Layer `--v2-z-dialog`.

**Variants.** `blocking` (no Close, no Esc, no scrim dismiss: the button is the only way on) and dismissible (Close, Esc, the scrim and Cancel all close it).

**Sizes.** One: the window's width; height to content.

**States.** Blocking; dismissible; entering; leaving; busy (the primary loads while the agreement is recorded); reduced motion (arrives and leaves at once). In V2 today: a bare `index.html` opens on the first-run Terms sheet; the Preview panel row Terms switches First run, Terms update, Off (index.html:1163); `?start=worker` hides it (index.html:670). Since 2026-10-14 V2 has a second, dismissible sheet: the mode sheet ('Apps BlueAI can run on its own', agent_permissions.jsx:124-157), opened by the composer chip (`?start=worker&v=1`) or the ribbon's Change; the scrim, Esc and a close button close it, focus moves to the close button and back, radius 20, a lighter scrim (rgba(15,23,42,0.24)), it slides up 0.36s with no exit, and the app list scrolls inside.

**Props.** `open`, `onClose`, `title`, `description`, `blocking`, `busy`, `actions` (as Dialog), `footer`, `initialFocus`, `className`, `children`; `preview` for guide specimens.

**Motion.** Slide up `--v2-dur-4` (320ms) on `--v2-ease-out`; slide down `--v2-dur-3` (240ms) on `--v2-ease-in`; scrim fades `--v2-dur-3`. V2's 0.45s and 0.32s on the house curve become these. Reduced motion: instant (motion.css).

**Accessibility.** role dialog, `aria-modal`, named by its title; focus moves in, stays in, and returns to the opener. Legal links open in a new tab and keep the partner's place. The primary is the action blue (4.92:1), not V2's #1990FF pill (3.24:1).

**Responsive.** Always the window's full width; on the compact window the body scrolls and the actions stay.

**Do / Don't.** Do keep the Terms copy exactly: "Before you start" / "By continuing, you agree to the Program Terms and acknowledge the Privacy Policy and Cookie Policy." / "Agree and continue" (terms_sheet.jsx:62-71). Do use the dismissible sheet for Report a problem, prefilled from the run (proposed copy, R34). Don't block for anything the partner could decline and come back to. Don't show two sheets at once.

## 27 Menu and popover

**Status:** Menu, Popover: both Changed from V2.

**Why:** Earnings and Sign out live in a header menu, so the only route to the partner's money and account must work from the keyboard. V2's help and more menus close only on an outside mousedown, with no role, no Esc and no arrow keys, and paint hover with onMouseEnter (index.html:289-301). Until 2026-10-14 the per-app mode menu (ModeChip) handled Esc and outside clicks but called a panel of radio groups a menu and returned no focus; V2 now opens a bottom sheet for it instead (section 26). The system splits the two jobs: Menu for actions, Popover for a small panel with its own controls (P4, P7, R25).

### Menu (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** A short list of actions or destinations off a button: the top bar's help and more menus, a row's overflow (View run, Report a problem, Disconnect).

**Anatomy.** Panel 6px off its button: `--v2-surface`, 1px `--v2-line`, radius 10, `--v2-shadow-1`, padding 4, min 200, max 280. Rows 36 (`--v2-control-md`), radius 6, icon 16 `--v2-ink-3`, label 13/500 `--v2-ink-2`, an optional hint 12 `--v2-ink-3` (the reason a row is off), a trailing external icon for rows that leave the app. A 1px `--v2-line-hair` rule before Sign out. Optional eyebrow header and 12px footnote. Layer `--v2-z-popover`.

**Variants.** Action rows; external rows (`href`, `external`); danger rows (`--v2-danger-ink`, only for a row that destroys); disabled rows (0.45, still focusable, with a hint).

**Sizes.** One.

**States.** Closed; open; row hover (`--v2-wash-hover`, label to ink); row focus-visible (2px blue ring inside the row plus the wash); row active (`--v2-wash-press`); row disabled; danger row; external row. Placement down or up, aligned to the button's end or start; it flips and swaps to stay in the window. In V2 today: `?start=worker`, then the ? (FAQ, Report Issue) or the three dots (Chat History, Profile, Earnings, Logout) (index.html:364-378).

**Props.** `trigger` (render prop receiving `aria-haspopup`, `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`, `onClick`, or an element to clone them onto), `items` ([{icon, label, onSelect, href, external, danger, disabled, hint} | {separator: true}]), `placement` ('down' | 'up'), `align` ('end' | 'start'), `header`, `footer`, `label`, `open` / `defaultOpen` / `onOpenChange`, `className`.

**Motion.** Fade-up `--v2-dur-3` on `--v2-ease-out`. Rows change colour over `--v2-dur-1`.

**Accessibility.** role menu with menuitems; the button carries `aria-haspopup` and `aria-expanded`. Enter, Space or Down opens on the first row (Up on the last); arrows move and wrap, Home and End jump, Esc closes and returns focus to the button, Tab closes; an outside click closes without moving focus. Choosing a row closes the menu and returns focus before the row acts, so a dialog it opens gives focus back to the button. External rows say "opens in a new tab" to screen readers.

**Responsive.** Fits the window it is in (nearest `[data-v2-bounds]` or AppFrame).

**Do / Don't.** Do keep Sign out last, after a rule. Do use glossary words (proposed: Account, Report a problem, Sign out; V2's FAQ and Earnings are kept). Do give a disabled row its reason (Instagram: Coming soon). Don't put choices or settings in a menu; that is a Popover. Don't use danger for Sign out.

### Popover (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** A small panel anchored to a button with its own controls: the per-app Always ask / Automatic choice opened from the ribbon's Change or the composer chip.

**Anatomy.** The Menu's panel at min(364, window less two gutters) wide; eyebrow header `.v2-text-eyebrow` (11/500 uppercase `--v2-ink-3`, the one eyebrow recipe); a content slot (ModeRows, section 46); footnote 12/1.35 `--v2-ink-3`.

**Variants.** Anchored down or up, start or end.

**Sizes.** One.

**States.** Open down; open up; Esc (closes, focus back to the button); outside click (closes); focus leaving (closes). In V2 today: `?start=worker&v=1`, the mode chip in the composer; `&v=2` with Automatic on, the ribbon's Change (agent_permissions.jsx:113-156).

**Props.** `trigger`, `placement`, `align`, `header`, `footnote`, `label`, `open` / `defaultOpen` / `onOpenChange`, `className`, `children`.

**Motion.** As Menu.

**Accessibility.** A non-modal dialog named by its header; the button says `aria-haspopup="dialog"`. Focus moves to its first control on open. Header and footnote at 4.77:1 and 6.0:1 (V2's #9ca3af was 2.54:1).

**Responsive.** Never wider than the window less its gutters; flips and swaps to fit.

**Do / Don't.** Do keep the footnote word for word: "Automatic starts and finishes that app’s jobs without asking. Each app has its own setting, and a new app starts on Always ask." Do drop "Mode" from the header (the glossary retires it; proposed "Paid jobs, per app"). Don't let picking Automatic skip the consent dialog (section 46).

## 28 Tabs

**Status:** Tabs: New.

**Why:** Activity (This program / Past) and Notifications (All / Unread) each hold two views of one list. V2 has no tabs a partner can reach: the only ones are on the hidden Skills screen, an indigo #4f46e5 underline that is a second accent (skills.jsx:238). The onBlue dashboard already switches Active and Past programs with a recessed track, one raised chip and counts (onblue/apply.html:443-475, 1390), so the product takes the family's control and the partner meets the same switch on the site and in the app (P6).

### Tabs (New)

**Purpose.** Switch between sibling views of one screen. Never navigation between screens (that is the bottom nav, section 31).

**Anatomy.** Track: `--v2-surface-sunken`, 1px `--v2-line`, radius 10, padding and gap 2. Tab: 32 tall (`--v2-hit-min`), radius 6, 12px sides, label 13/500 (`--v2-weight-medium` in every state, so nothing reflows) `--v2-ink-3`, optional count 12/400 tabular `--v2-ink-3`. Selected: the Segmented's selected look (section 14, `kit/overlays.css:271-275`): `--v2-select` over `--v2-surface`, a 1.5px `--v2-blue` edge (`--v2-stroke-selected`), label 13/500 `--v2-ink`; no shadow. Adapted from the site: label 12.5 to 13, every state 500 where the site steps the selected tab to 550, radius 9 to 10, padding 3 to 2, the selected chip takes the product's one selected look (select fill, blue edge) instead of the site's raised white chip and drops its shadow, and the track is `--v2-surface-sunken` #f6f6f8, which is the site's `--bar-bg`; the site's tab track is its `--panel-bg` #fdfdfd (`onblue/apply.html:449`, `onblue/onblue.css:400, 404`). The count goes one ink darker: the site's `--dim-ink` #767b85 on its #fdfdfd track is 4.18:1, and the system's ink-4 on the sunken track would be 4.42:1, both under AA for 12px; ink-3 is 5.59:1 there.

**Variants.** With counts (count 0 still shows, so a tab never changes width); `full` stretches the track and splits it evenly.

**Sizes.** One: 32px tabs.

**States.** Rest; hover (`--v2-wash-hover`, label to ink); selected; focus-visible (2px `--v2-blue` ring); active (`--v2-wash-press`); disabled (0.45, skipped by the arrows; rare: prefer an enabled tab with 0). Not in V2 (New): the guide forces each state and its Try it runs both sets.

**Props.** `Tabs {tabs: [{id, label, count, disabled}], value, onChange(id), ariaLabel, idBase, full, className}`. `TabPanel {idBase, id, hidden, children}`: pass the same `idBase` so the tab and its panel point at each other.

**Motion.** Colour, fill and edge over `--v2-dur-2` (180ms, one duration shared with the Segmented, `kit/overlays.css:264-265`) on `--v2-ease-standard`. The panel swap is instant.

**Accessibility.** tablist (named by `ariaLabel`), tabs with `aria-selected` and `aria-controls`, tabpanels labelled by their tab. One tab stop (the selected tab); Left and Right move and select, wrapping; Home and End jump. Label ink-3 on the sunken track 5.59:1 at rest.

**Responsive.** Hugs its labels; `full` in a 419 window for two-tab screens like Activity. Labels stay one or two words so nothing wraps.

**Do / Don't.** Do use tabs for two or three views of the same list. Do show the count on every tab, 0 included. Do keep labels short (proposed copy: This program, Past; All, Unread). Don't use tabs to move between screens. Don't hide a tab when it is empty. Don't nest tabs.

# Shell

## 29 Window and app frame

**Status:** AppFrame: Changed from V2. Window semantics and tray: New.

**Why:** BlueAI is a worker that runs once a day on its own. Its window has to fit the partner's laptop, and the worker has to outlive the window. V2's window is a fixed 866px tall (product-scene.css:76), which does not fit a 1366 x 768 laptop or a 1080p screen at 125%; its close button unmounts the whole app (index.html:943) and Minimize does nothing (index.html:562), so nothing can run or notify while it is closed.

### AppFrame (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** The window's content box. Every screen lives inside it, and every dialog, sheet, toast and the splash is positioned against it, so nothing escapes the window.

**Anatomy.** Three bands in a column, plus an overlay plane:
1. Top bar, 52 tall, pinned (section 30).
2. Content band: the only part that scrolls. A screen puts its ScreenHeader and body here.
3. Bottom nav, 56 tall, pinned (section 31).
4. Overlay plane: the frame itself (`isolation: isolate`), so overlay layers stay inside the window.

**Variants.** Full (signed in). Compact (signed in, short screen). Chrome-free (signed out: no top bar, no nav; Welcome and Sign in).

**Sizes.** Content box 419 x 830 (`--v2-shell-w`, `--v2-shell-h`); compact 419 x 640 (`--v2-shell-min-h`). Content band 722 full, 532 compact. The Windows caption adds 34 (`--v2-titlebar-h`). The window opens at the content box plus caption, capped to the screen's work area, resizes in height only, never below 640. Ground `--v2-canvas`. One gutter, 16 (`--v2-gutter`).

**States.** Full: V2 `?start=worker`. Compact: new, no V2 equivalent (resize the window). Signed out: V2 bare URL (the welcome renders chrome-free, index.html:945).

**Props.** `AppFrame { compact, topBar, nav, children, overlays, landmark = true, className }`. Leaving out both `topBar` and `nav` gives the chrome-free frame. `landmark={false}` only for guide specimens (the content band is the page's `<main>`).

**Motion.** None on the frame. Screens change inside the content band with their own motion (section 07).

**Accessibility.** The content band is the `<main>` landmark; the top bar is a `<header>`, the nav a `<nav>`. Overlays live in the same window, so focus trapping (section 25) stays inside it. The content band scrolls with the keyboard because every screen has focusable content.

**Responsive.** Two heights only, 830 and 640; the width never changes. At 640 the content band scrolls and the bars stay. Never use `vh` inside the window: it measures the browser, not the frame.

**Do / Don't.** Do mount every overlay in `overlays`. Do design every screen for 640 too. Don't scroll the bars away. Don't give a band its own width (V2's chat column was 420 inside a 419 box, index.html:976).

### Window semantics and tray (New)

**Purpose.** What the window does when it is closed, minimized or quit, so the worker keeps its schedule and the partner always has one place to see it, skip a day or stop it. It keeps the setup promise "You can pause, stop, or disconnect anytime." (worker_flow.jsx:67).

**Anatomy.** Window behaviours plus a tray menu (Windows draws it; the kit's `TrayMenu` fixes its items, order and words): a head (mark, "BlueAI", a status line with a dot), a separator, Open BlueAI, then Skip today's run (only while today's run is still to come: scheduled, or it didn't start) or Resume runs (while paused), a separator, Quit BlueAI. Skip and Quit lead to a confirm step that states the cost (`kit/shell.jsx:39-63, 96-100`). Pausing runs for days is not a tray item: it lives in Settings > Worker, always with an end date (section 62).

**Variants.** Tray status: any RUN_STATUS key (section 30), in its tray words: scheduled ("Next run 11 am"), running ("Running today’s run"), pending ("Done today, pending verification"), missed ("Today’s run didn’t start. Run it by 9 pm"), paused ("Runs paused"), and the rest of section 30's table. Confirm: skip, quit.

**Sizes.** Specimen width `--v2-dialog-sm` (320); items 32 tall (`--v2-hit-min`), radius 6; surface, hairline, `--v2-shadow-1`, radius 10.

**States.** (All proposed copy; none exist in V2.)
- Close: hides to the tray, worker keeps its schedule. First time only, a Windows notification: "BlueAI is still running. Your next run is at 11 am."
- Minimize: to the taskbar; the running dash stays.
- Reopen (taskbar, tray, notification): restores the last screen at once, no splash.
- Tray click: opens BlueAI. Right-click: the tray menu.
- Skip today's run (offered only while today's run is still to come): confirm "Skip today’s run?" / "Today won’t count toward your 20 days. Runs start again tomorrow." [Keep today’s run] [Skip today] (danger). It skips one day; it never pauses runs.
- Resume runs (offered only while paused): resumes at once, no question.
- Quit BlueAI: confirm "Quit BlueAI?" / "Today’s run won’t happen while BlueAI is closed, so today won’t count." [Keep running] [Quit] (danger). Once today is settled (pending, done, not counted, verified, period ended): "Today is done. Your next run won’t happen while BlueAI is closed." No question while runs are paused: the host passes no confirm.
- PC restarts: BlueAI starts with Windows into the tray, window closed (proposed; a switch in Settings > Worker).
- Sign out: signs out and pauses the worker, and the confirm says so (section 64; V2's Logout confirm does not sign out, index.html:1010).

**Props.** `TrayMenu { runStatus (a RUN_STATUS key, section 30; 'done' reads as 'pending'), statusLabel (the real time or count; default the tray words), onOpen, onSkip, onResume, onQuit, confirm: null | 'skip' | 'quit', onConfirm, onCancel }`.

**Motion.** None in the product (Windows animates its own menu).

**Accessibility.** Specimen uses `role="menu"` / `menuitem`; the confirm is an `alertdialog` labelled by its title and described by its body. The status dot is decorative; the words carry the state.

**Responsive.** Not applicable (OS surface).

**Do / Don't.** Do say the cost of Skip and Quit before the click. Do use the same status words as the top bar chip. Don't stop runs when the window closes. Don't replay the splash on reopen. Don't hide Quit.

## 30 Top bar

**Status:** TopBar: Changed from V2. WorkerStatusChip: New. BrandMark: Changed from V2. Help and more menus: Changed from V2.

**Why:** The partner opens BlueAI to check one thing: did today's run happen, and does the day count. V2's header gives its loudest slot, the only filled control in the chrome, to a "New Chat" pill (index.html:351) and shows the run nowhere; it runs the brand gradient three times in 419px (index.html:335, 342, 346); and two of its six menu rows do nothing (index.html:364-365).

### TopBar (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** Brand, today's run, credits, help and the account doors, on every signed-in screen.

**Anatomy.** Left: BrandMark (logo 24 + wordmark). Right, in reading order: WorkerStatusChip, credits chip (section 47), Help (IconButton 32, `help`), More (IconButton 32, `more`). New chat is not here: it is a trailing IconButton in the chat screen's header (section 32).

**Variants.** Signed out (brand only). Signed in.

**Sizes.** 52 tall (`--v2-topbar-h`). Padding 0 12 0 16: the brand sits on the 16 gutter; the right edge is 12 because the icon buttons carry a 6px inset. Gap 8 between chips, 2 between the two icon buttons. Surface, 1px `--v2-line` bottom, no shadow. Layer `--v2-z-header` (20). The longest status labels are "Next run 9 am to 8 pm" (a run window) and "Waiting for credits"; the bar must fit 419 with them (Responsive says what gives if it does not).

**States.** Signed out: V2 bare URL then Sign in. Signed in: V2 `?start=worker`. Menu open: click Help or More (V2 has no keyboard path).

**Props.** `TopBar { signedIn, status, statusLabel, credits (number or { balance, state }), onOpenStatus, onOpenCredits, helpItems, moreItems, onAction(id), openMenu }`. Default items come from `topBarMenus(onAction)`; `openMenu` ('help' | 'more') is for guide specimens.

**Motion.** None on the bar; chips and buttons change colour over `--v2-dur-1`.

**Accessibility.** The bar is a `<header>`. Each icon button has a label ("Help", "More"); its button stays lit while its menu is open (`aria-expanded`). Tab order follows reading order.

**Responsive.** One width. The WorkerStatusChip label is the one part that gives: it shrinks and ends in an ellipsis, and its full words stay in the accessible name. Brand, credits, Help and More never shrink or move out, so More (the way to Account and Sign out) is always reachable.

**Do / Don't.** Do keep one status chip and one credits chip. Don't add a filled button to the bar. Don't add a second gradient. Don't show the right cluster when signed out.

### WorkerStatusChip (New)

**Purpose.** Today's run in one glance, on every screen; clicking it opens where the partner can act.

**Anatomy.** Pill button: an 8px dot in the status tone, a 13/500 label, and visually hidden "Today’s run: " before the label.

**Variants.** (Labels are proposed copy except "Didn’t finish", which echoes the PM's toast, worker_day_done.jsx:203.)

Every `RUN_STATUS` key (`kit/topbar.jsx:35-50`), its chip label, its tray line (section 29) and the record condition `topBarProps(record)` maps to it (`screens/home.jsx`, `chip`). The chip's label is the RUN_STATUS label unless the record gives a time or a count. Opens is `chipTarget(record)` (`screens/home.jsx`, section 32): running, waiting, failed and done open today's run as its Activity row does; stopped, missed, blocked, no-credits, offline and ended open Home; paused opens Settings > Worker. "Waiting for credits" shows only when `creditsDay` is 'waiting'; when it is 'stopped' the chip is not-counted, "Not counted today". The credits chip's dot shows only until today's run is done.

| Status | Chip label | Tray line | Worker record (`workerState`) | Opens |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| scheduled | Next run 11 am; Next run 9 am to 8 pm; Runs later today (from `runWindow`: a time, a window, any time) | Next run 11 am | scheduled | Home, Today |
| running | Running | Running today’s run | running | the run in the chat |
| needs-you | Needs you (yellow tint) | A paid job needs your OK by 9 pm | waiting | the job preview |
| pending | Day 7 of 20 (blue ring: in, not yet checked) | Done today, pending verification | done, and today's ledger row is not verified yet (`done` is read as `pending`) | the run in Activity |
| not-counted | Not counted today (neutral dot) | Today doesn’t count toward your 20 days | done, with `today.outcome` skipped or expired; noCredits with `creditsDay` 'stopped' | the run in Activity; out of credits: Home, Today |
| verified | Day 7 of 20 once today is verified; All 20 days done (`numbers().complete`) | All 20 days verified | done and verified; complete | the run in Activity; complete: Home, Today (the card's Earnings) |
| failed | Didn’t finish (red tint) | Today’s job didn’t finish. Try again by 9 pm | failed | the run, with Try again and its deadline |
| stopped | Stopped (ring dot) | You stopped today’s run. Run again by 9 pm | stopped | Home, Today: Run again and its deadline |
| missed | Didn’t start (neutral dot) | Today’s run didn’t start. Run it by 9 pm | missed: the PC was off or asleep at run time | Home, Today: Run now and its deadline |
| blocked | Can’t run (red tint) | Today’s run can’t start. Open BlueAI to fix it | blocked: `blockedBy(record)`, no app, signed out or no BlueStacks | Home, Today: the card's one fix |
