| no-credits | Waiting for credits (ring dot) | Out of AI credits for today’s run | noCredits: `credits.left` below `runCost` before today's run and the refill lands before 9 pm (`creditsDay` 'waiting'); when it lands after 9 pm ('stopped') the chip says Not counted today | Home, Today: the card says when credits refresh |
| paused | Paused (ring dot); Update needed (section 69, the label passed) | Runs paused | paused; an update is required | Settings > Worker; the update screen |
| offline | Offline (ring dot) | Offline. Today’s run waits for a connection | offline | Home, Today: the card says what happens to today |
| ended | Period ended (ring dot) | Your 20 days have ended | ended: the 20 days ran out before all were verified (`daysLeft` 0) | Home: the period-ended state with Earnings (section 69) |
| (none) | not rendered | | setup (not set up yet), signed out, not in the program, between programs | |

One rule for the ended chip: once the period has ended, every signed-in screen says "Period ended", the period-ended system states included (section 69); a partner who verified all 20 days keeps "All 20 days done" instead.

**Sizes.** 28 visible (`--v2-control-sm`), 32 hit area (`--v2-hit-min`, via `::after`). Padding 0 12 0 8. Pill, 1px `--v2-line`, surface.

**States.** Rest, hover (line firms to `--v2-line-hover`, `--v2-wash-hover` layered on the fill), pressed (`--v2-wash-press`), focus-visible (2px blue outline, offset 2). New: no V2 equivalent; V2 shows run state only in the thread, a taskbar flash and an 8 second toast.

**Props.** `WorkerStatusChip { status, label, onClick, className }`.

**Motion.** Colour over `--v2-dur-1`. The running dot does not pulse: nothing loops unattended.

**Accessibility.** A real `<button>`; accessible name "Today’s run: Needs you". Colour is never the only carrier: every status has words. Tinted labels: attention ink on tint and danger ink on tint, both above 4.5:1.

**Responsive.** One line, no wrap.

**Do / Don't.** Do use the same words as the tray and the WorkerCard. Don't make the label a verb: the chip names the state; the run holds the action.

### BrandMark (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** The BlueAI mark and wordmark: the one place the brand gradient is a fill.

**Anatomy.** Logo image (`assets/Logo.png`) and the "BlueAI" wordmark in `--v2-gradient-brand`.

**Variants.** Mark + wordmark; mark only (alt "BlueAI"); stacked (splash).

**Sizes.** 24 logo + 16/600 wordmark, gap 6 (top bar). 48 logo + 24/600 wordmark, gap 12, stacked (splash). V2: logo 32 + 22/700 (index.html:334-335).

**States.** None.

**Props.** `BrandMark { size: 24 | 48, wordmark = true }`.

**Motion.** None.

**Accessibility.** Logos are exempt from text contrast; in forced-colours mode the wordmark falls back to system text so the name never disappears. With the wordmark shown, the image is decorative (alt "").

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do keep the gradient here and on the credits glyph only. Don't use the gradient as a ring, border or text fill elsewhere. Decision D8 may switch the product to the onBlue site's blue spark; only BrandMark changes.

### Help and more menus (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** Help when something looks wrong; the account and money doors.

**Anatomy.** Two Menus (section 27) off the top bar's icon buttons.

**Variants.**

| Menu | Item | Icon | Does | V2 today |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Help | FAQ | help | Opens the onBlue site's FAQ (index.html#faqs) in the browser | only closes the menu (index.html:364) |
| Help | Report a problem | alert, ink | Opens the Report a problem sheet, filled in from the last run (section 65) | "Report Issue" only closes the menu; red glyph (index.html:365) |
| More | Notifications | bell | Pushes Notifications (section 60) | not in V2; "Chat History" sat here (index.html:375) |
| More | Account | user | Pushes Account (section 64) | "Profile" (index.html:376) |
| More | Earnings | earnings + external | Opens the Earnings dashboard: the one `EARNINGS_URL` (kit/worker-data.jsx) | same label, one of two URLs (index.html:329) |
| (separator) | | | | |
| More | Sign out | sign-out | Asks, then signs out and pauses the worker | "Logout"; confirm does nothing (index.html:378, 1010) |

Activity is a tab, so the menu does not repeat it; Notifications has no other door.

**Sizes.** Menu spec, section 27.

**States.** Closed, open, item hover, item focus-visible. V2: open only by mouse; close on an outside mousedown (index.html:294).

**Props.** `helpItems`, `moreItems` on TopBar (Menu items: `{ icon, label, onSelect, href, external }` or `{ separator }`).

**Motion.** Menu motion, section 27.

**Accessibility.** `role="menu"`, `aria-haspopup`, `aria-expanded`; Down or Enter opens on the first item, arrows move, Esc closes and returns focus to the button. External items say "opens in a new tab".

**Responsive.** Menus fit themselves inside the window (they flip and slide at its edges).

**Do / Don't.** Do give every row a real destination. Don't paint a help row red. Don't keep a second Earnings URL anywhere.

## 31 Bottom navigation

**Status:** BottomNav: Changed from V2.

**Why:** Navigation should name the three things a partner does: see the worker today, check runs and verified days, change how the worker works. V2's first tab, Jobs, is a "Coming Soon" page (jobs.jsx:140) in a product about paid jobs; Schedule shows sample tasks for another app; the active tab is indigo #4f46e5 on #eef2ff (shared.jsx:305), a second accent beside the blue every other control uses; and the bar carries both a border and a shadow (shared.jsx:300).

### BottomNav (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** Move between the three places of the product. Home: the worker, today and progress (the chat is reached from here). Activity: every run and whether each day was verified (replaces Jobs). Settings: Worker first, Advanced last.

**Anatomy.** A `<nav>` of three equal buttons; each is a 20 icon over a 12/500 label. The selected item has a 2px blue bar on the bar's top hairline and ink icon and label. An optional count badge sits on the icon's top right.

**Variants.** Three items: Home (`home`), Activity (`activity`), Settings (`settings`). Four items only if decision D12 keeps chat as a tab: Home, Chat, Activity, Settings (`NAV_ITEMS_WITH_CHAT`). The recommendation is three.

**Sizes.** 56 tall (`--v2-nav-h`; V2 64). Padding 0 8; items 47 tall inside the bar (56 less the 1px top line and a 4 margin above and below), radius 6. Icon 20, stroke 1.75 (V2 22, stroke 1.9). Label 12/500 in every state (V2 10.5px, 700 active / 600 rest). Selected bar 24 x 2, `--v2-blue`. Badge 16 tall pill, `--v2-action` fill, white 11/600 (4.92:1), a 2px surface ring.

**States.**
- Rest: ink-3 icon and label (6.0:1).
- Hover: `--v2-wash-hover` on the item, ink-2. (V2 has no hover.)
- Pressed: `--v2-wash-press`.
- Selected: blue bar + ink; no fill, no weight change, so labels never shift.
- Focus-visible: 2px blue outline, inset 2.
- Badge: something in Activity needs the partner (a waiting job, a rejected day); hidden at 0, "9+" above 9.
- Pushed screen open: the parent tab stays lit (V2 lights none, index.html:992).
- Signed out: no nav (AppFrame chrome-free).
V2: `?start=worker` shows the nav with Chat active (the default, index.html:613).

**Props.** `BottomNav { items = NAV_ITEMS ([{ id, label, icon, badge }]), value, onChange(id), ariaLabel = 'Main', forceState, className }`. `value` is the parent tab while a pushed screen is open. `forceState` is for guide specimens only.

**Motion.** Colour and wash over `--v2-dur-1` (120ms). No indicator slide.

**Accessibility.** `<nav aria-label="Main">`; the selected item has `aria-current="page"`. The badge number is hidden from screen readers and said as ", 2 new" after the label. Every item is a 47 tall target.

**Responsive.** One width; labels truncate rather than wrap (none do at three or four items).

**Do / Don't.** Do keep the parent tab lit on a pushed screen. Do use the badge only for something that needs the partner. Don't add a tab for a page with nothing on it. Don't change weight or size on selection. Don't show the nav before sign-in.

## 32 Screen header and navigation model

**Status:** ScreenHeader: Changed from V2. Navigation model: Changed from V2.

**Why:** A partner who opens Credits or Account must always know where they are and how to get back, and must never lose today's run by looking elsewhere. V2 has five title treatments (19/800 index.html:448, 22/800 history.jsx:91, 20/700 schedule.jsx:118, 22/800 in #1e293b jobs.jsx:140, none on Profile) and no Back anywhere; it picks the screen from a chain of booleans (index.html:965) and mounts the chat, with the run inside it, only on the chat tab (index.html:975).

### ScreenHeader (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** One title band for every screen.

**Anatomy.** Optional Back (IconButton 32, `back`, label "Back"), the title (`<h1>`), an optional trailing slot for one action.

**Variants.** Title only (a tab root: Activity, Settings). With Back (a pushed screen: Worker settings, Account). With a trailing action: an IconButton (Chat's "New chat", `plus`) or a quiet md Button (Notifications' "Mark all read", `screens/notifications.jsx:157`).

**Sizes.** Min 52 tall (`--v2-topbar-h`), padding 8 16 (`--v2-space-2`, `--v2-gutter`); with Back the left padding is 10, so the chevron lands on the 16 gutter. Title 20/600, -0.02em, ink, one line (`--v2-text-20`, `--v2-weight-semibold`, `--v2-tracking-title`). Ground `--v2-canvas`.

**States.** None of its own (Back follows IconButton states, section 11). A long title truncates with an ellipsis. V2: open AI Credits, Chat History or Schedule from `?start=worker` to see the five treatments.

**Props.** `ScreenHeader { title, onBack, trailing, titleId, className }`.

**Motion.** None. It is sticky to the top of the content band (`--v2-z-sticky`), so Back stays reachable on the 640 window.

**Accessibility.** The title is the screen's `<h1>` with `tabIndex -1`: when a screen is pushed, focus moves to it, so a screen reader announces where the partner landed. Back has a label. One trailing action only.

**Responsive.** One line at 419; truncate, never wrap.

**Do / Don't.** Do give every pushed screen a Back. Don't use a second title style. Don't put Back on a tab root or on an overlay.

### Navigation model (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** Where every screen sits, how the partner gets back, and which tab is lit, kept apart from the Worker record.

**Anatomy.** Three levels.

| Level | Screens | Way back | Tab lit |
|---|---|---|---|
| Tab | Home, Activity, Settings | the nav | itself |
| Pushed screen | Chat, Run detail, Notifications, Account, Worker settings, Connected apps, AI credits (Help is the top bar menu plus the Report a problem sheet, not a screen) | Back in the ScreenHeader | its parent tab (rule below) |
| Overlay | Dialog, Sheet, day done, splash, toast | its own close or answer | unchanged |

**Which tab is lit for a pushed screen, one rule.** A screen with a home in Settings (Worker settings, Connected apps, Account) lights Settings from any door, the top bar's More menu included. A pushed screen with no home in Settings goes on the stack of the tab it was opened from and keeps it lit; Back returns there (Run detail, AI credits, Notifications); Chat belongs to Home. The props are `tab` (Run detail, AI credits) or `parentTab` (Notifications).

**Variants.** None.

**Sizes.** Not applicable.

**States.** Tab; pushed (one or more screens on a tab's stack); overlay open. Rules: each tab keeps its own stack; tapping another tab switches and keeps the stack; tapping the lit tab returns to its root; a notification or the tray opens the screen it names, pushed over its parent tab. V2: none of these exist; a kebab screen is left only by tapping a tab (index.html:867).

**Props.** Navigation state is `{ tab, stacks: { home: [], activity: [], settings: [] } }`, held by the host router: the code that mounts the screens (V2's app root when it adopts the kit; the guide's demo is `guide/sections/screens-home.jsx`, `Flow`), never inside a screen and never in the kit. A stack entry is `{ id, run, row }`. BottomNav's `value` is `tab`. Every screen emits `onNavigate(id, opts)`; the router resolves each id by this table.

| id | opts | Screen | Level | Tab lit | Back |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `home`, `activity`, `settings` | none | Home, Activity, Settings | tab | itself | none; tapping the lit tab returns it to its root |
| `settings` | `{highlight}`: a section id ('worker', 'apps', 'notifications', 'bluestacks', 'account', 'help', 'advanced') | Settings, scrolled to that section and holding the highlight | tab | Settings | none |
| `chat` | `{state}`: setup, running, waiting, retrying | Chat | pushed on Home's stack, from any door | Home | the screen under it on Home's stack |
| `run` | `{state, row, tab}`: a Run detail state, its ledger row (`rowTarget`, section 58) and the tab it was opened from | Run detail (`tab`) | pushed on the stack of the tab it was opened from (`opts.tab`; Activity by default) | that tab | the screen under it on that tab's stack |
| `notifications` | none | Notifications (`parentTab`) | pushed over the tab you are on | that tab | that tab |
| `credits` | none | AI credits (`tab`) | pushed over the tab you are on | that tab | that tab |
| `account` | none | Account | pushed on Settings' stack | Settings | Settings |
| `worker-settings` | none | Worker settings | pushed on Settings' stack | Settings | the screen under it |
| `connected-apps` | `{app}`: 'youtube' or 'telegram'; none for the list | Connected apps | pushed on Settings' stack | Settings | the screen under it |
| `report` | none | the Report a problem sheet (`V2Screens.ReportProblemSheet`) | overlay | unchanged | its own close |
| `sign-out` | none | the sign-out confirm (`CONFIRM_COPY.signOut`, section 64) | overlay | unchanged | Cancel |
| `terms` | `{docs}`: the changed documents' names | the Program Terms sheet, `update` mode (`V2Screens.TermsSheet`, section 53), from a Terms updated notification | overlay | unchanged | none: it asks for the agreement |

The run chip, the WorkerCard's Details and the RunCard's details emit no id of their own: they open `V2Screens.chipTarget(worker)` (`screens/home.jsx`), which is today's row as Activity opens it (`chat` {state} while the run is live, `run` {state, row} once it has an outcome), `worker-settings` while paused, and `home` otherwise. Earnings is not a route: it opens `EARNINGS_URL` on the site in the browser. A pushed screen whose id is already on top is replaced, so it shows the new state. A new screen gets a row here before it ships (section 74, step 3).

**Motion.** A pushed screen enters with `v2-anim-fade-up` (section 07); Back reverses it. Reduced motion: instant.

**Accessibility.** Focus moves to the pushed screen's title; on Back it returns to the control that pushed it.

**Responsive.** The same on both window heights.

**Do / Don't.** Do keep the worker's run in the Worker record (section 41) so no navigation can unmount it. Don't model screens as a chain of booleans. Don't open a pushed screen from a menu without lighting its parent tab.

## 33 Splash and cold start

**Status:** Splash: Changed from V2.

**Why:** A partner opens BlueAI most days to check one run. V2's splash says "Loading your skills…" (boot_splash.jsx:25), naming a feature the partner never sees, puts its status in #94a3b8 at 2.56:1 (boot_splash.jsx:73), bounces its dots with no reduced-motion rule (boot_splash.jsx:66), and replays for about 3.5 s every time the window opens (2650 ms, boot_splash.jsx:33, plus an 850 ms skeleton, index.html:745).

### Splash (Changed from V2)

**Purpose.** A short branded launch on a true cold start (the app process starting), in words that say what the app is doing.

**Anatomy.** Centred on the surface: BrandMark stacked (logo 48, wordmark 24/600 in the brand gradient), three 6px blue dots, one status line 13/400 in ink-3.

**Variants.** Cold start only. Reopening the window from the tray, taskbar or a notification restores the last screen at once, with no splash.

**Sizes.** Fills the frame (absolute, inset 0), above every overlay (`--v2-z-splash`, 200). Dots 6, gap 6, 40 below the brand; status 12 below the dots.

**States.**

| Moment | V2 today | System |
|---|---|---|
| Message 1 | 0 ms "Starting BlueAI…" (boot_splash.jsx:24) | 0 ms "Starting BlueAI…" (kept) |
| Message 2 | 1050 ms "Loading your skills…" | 600 ms "Checking today’s run…" (proposed copy), signed in only |
| Fading | from 2150 ms, 500 ms ease | from 900 ms, 320 ms `--v2-ease-in` |
| Content | about 3.5 s | about 1.2 s; then the worker-home skeleton only while the record loads |
| Reduced motion | dots bounce forever | static dots, no fade, done at 900 ms |

V2: every page load and every window reopen shows it. The real app calls `onDone` when the Worker record has loaded, never later than this budget plus the skeleton.

**Props.** `Splash { messages = SPLASH_MESSAGES, onDone, coldStart = true, state, reduced }`. `coldStart: false` renders nothing and calls `onDone` at once. `state` ('message-1' | 'message-2' | 'fading') and `reduced` freeze a state for guide specimens.

**Motion.** Dots: `v2-pulse` over `--v2-dur-pulse` (1200 ms), staggered by a sixth; they loop only while the splash is up (about a second). Fade: opacity over `--v2-dur-4`. Both stop under `prefers-reduced-motion`.

**Accessibility.** The status line is `role="status"`, so each message is announced once; the splash is `aria-busy` until it fades. Status ink-3 is 6.0:1. The wordmark is a logo (exempt from contrast).

**Responsive.** Centred at both window heights.

**Do / Don't.** Do show it only when the process starts. Do hand over to the skeleton if loading takes longer. Don't replay it on reopen. Don't name features or versions in the status line. Don't loop the dots under reduced motion.

# Chat

## 34 Chat thread

**Status:** Changed from V2 (Thread); New (DaySeparator and times, JumpToLatest).
**Why:** The thread is a window onto the Worker record, not the record itself (P1). V2 forces the scroll to the bottom on every step (chat_product.jsx:77), has no live region, and stamps every user bubble with the same fixed date (chat_bubbles.jsx:15). A partner checking a run needs to read back while it plays and to know when things happened, because a run is verified a day later.

### Thread (Changed from V2)
- **Purpose:** The conversation with the BlueAI worker: today's run card, its questions, replies and notices, grouped by day.
- **Anatomy:** Scroll region (role=log, aria-live=polite, label "Conversation with BlueAI", Tab-focusable) with 16px padding; inner list bottom-anchored (min-height 100%, flex-end), gap 8, plus 8 when the sender changes; JumpToLatest floats 12px above the bottom.
- **Variants:** Message kinds (V2's role map kept as canonical, plus run and notice): separator, user, status, thinking (last step only), plan, warning, final, notice, ask, needsbs, installing-bs, run. `daydone` no longer renders in the thread: the RunCard's day line and the DayDone moment (section 44) say it.
- **Sizes:** Fills its parent (the content region of the 419px window, compact 640 high).
- **States:** Empty (shows the Home body passed as `empty`); running; awaiting an answer; done; scrolled up with new steps (JumpToLatest with a count). V2: `index.html?day=next&end=job` plays a scheduled run in its own chat; `?start=worker` then Run plays onboarding.
- **Props:** `steps` (array of step objects), `renderStep` (defaults to `V2Kit.renderMessage`), `stickToBottom` (default true), `unseen` (force the count), `onJump`, `label`, `empty`, `className`.
- **Motion:** Jump scrolls smoothly; instant under reduced motion. No forced scroll while the reader is up.
- **Accessibility:** role=log announces additions politely; each bubble has a hidden "You:" / "BlueAI:" prefix; the region takes focus so arrow keys scroll it; focus ring is the shared 2px blue outline, inset.
- **Responsive:** Content region scrolls; top bar, composer and nav stay pinned at the compact 640 height.
- **Do / Don't:** Do keep whatever needs the partner last (the AskCard); otherwise the RunCard keeps the bottom, so answers land above it. Do derive the steps from the Worker record. Don't print each run step as its own bubble. Don't yank the scroll.

### DaySeparator and times (New)
- **Purpose:** Say when each thing happened.
- **Anatomy:** Centred label 12/500 ink-4 between day groups; one time (12 ink-4) under the last message of a group from the same sender.
- **Variants:** Today, Yesterday, a date ("Oct 12").
- **Sizes:** Text only, 12px.
- **States:** Static. Not in V2 (V2 shows "Jun 12, 2026, 11:03 AM" on every user bubble).
- **Props:** `DaySeparator {label}`; steps carry `time` ("11:05 am"); helpers `chatTime.day(date, now)` and `chatTime.time(date)`.
- **Motion:** None.
- **Accessibility:** Plain text inside the log, read in order. ink-4 is 4.77:1 (meta only).
- **Responsive:** Centred at any width.
- **Do / Don't:** Do use the partner's local time, "11 am" style. Don't stamp a time on every bubble.

### JumpToLatest (New)
- **Purpose:** Bring the reader back down after reading up during a run.
- **Anatomy:** Pill, white, 1px line, shadow-1 (it floats), chevron-down 16 + "3 new messages" 13/500 ink-2, min height 32.
- **Variants:** One.
- **Sizes:** 32px tall, hugs its label.
- **States:** Hidden (no new steps, renders nothing), visible with a count, hover (line firms + wash), focus-visible, pressed. Forced with `.is-hover`, `.is-focus`, `.is-active`.
- **Props:** `count`, `onClick`, `className`.
- **Motion:** Colour 120ms; the scroll it triggers is smooth unless reduced motion.
- **Accessibility:** Real button with a text label; the count is in the label.
- **Responsive:** Centred over the thread.
- **Do / Don't:** Do show it only when the reader is up and something arrived. Don't auto-scroll instead.

## 35 Message bubbles and notices

**Status:** Changed from V2 (all three parts).
**Why:** V2 has six bubble looks in five colours, each with a shadow, and the loudest is the partner's own message (an indigo gradient with a 30% glow, chat_bubbles.jsx:30). The system is quiet by default (P5): white with a 1px line, colour carried by an icon, one attention fill for "needs you".

### MessageBubble, user (Changed from V2)
- **Purpose:** What the partner said, or the answer chip they tapped (echoed).
- **Anatomy:** Right-aligned; blue tint fill (`--v2-blue-tint`), 1px `--v2-blue-tint-line`, radius 16, padding 12 16, max 300, text 14/1.5 ink, no shadow; optional time under it.
- **Variants:** One.
- **Sizes:** Max 300, hugs short text.
- **States:** Rest; with time (last of a group). V2: `?start=worker&ask=<text>`, or the Preview panel's Chat states row.
- **Props:** `MessageBubble {from: 'user', time, groupStart, children}`.
- **Motion:** Arrives with the shared fade-up when the screen animates it; none of its own.
- **Accessibility:** Hidden "You:" prefix; ink on the tint is above 15:1.
- **Responsive:** Wraps; long words break.
- **Do / Don't:** Do keep it the quietest bubble. Don't use the brand gradient on it (gradient is for the mark and credits glyph only).

### MessageBubble, worker (Changed from V2)
- **Purpose:** A plain sentence from the worker. Run progress is not here; it is in the RunCard.
- **Anatomy:** Left-aligned; white, 1px `--v2-line`, radius 16, padding 12 16, max 300, text 14/1.5 ink-2. Thinking: Spinner 16 + "Working on it…" ink-3.
- **Variants:** Plain; thinking (last step only); plan (title + numbered list).
- **Sizes:** Max 300.
- **States:** Rest; with time; thinking. V2: any running script (`?day=next`).
- **Props:** `MessageBubble {from: 'worker', time, groupStart, children}`; thinking via `renderMessage({role: 'thinking'})`.
- **Motion:** Spinner spins at 800ms; slowed by the global reduced-motion rule.
- **Accessibility:** Hidden "BlueAI:" prefix; ink-2 is 10:1; thinking caption is 6.0:1 (V2's was 2.56:1).
- **Responsive:** Wraps.
- **Do / Don't:** Do keep it plain. Don't add fills or shadows to say anything.

### NoticeBubble (Changed from V2)
- **Purpose:** An outcome or a heads-up the partner should read.
- **Anatomy:** White, 1px line, radius 16, padding 12 16, max 300; icon 16 in tone, optional title 14/600 ink, body 14 ink-2 (one line per item when given `lines`); optional action row under it and feedback below the bubble.
- **Variants:** `kind` is one of the five token tones (`kit/thread.jsx` NOTICE): `info` (info, blue-ink; DependencyCard), `success` (check, success-ink), `attention` (alert, attention-ink), `danger` (x-circle, danger-ink), `neutral` (info, ink-3). Aliases kept for older callers: `headsup` is attention, `fail` is danger, and `needsInput` is attention (help icon) on the PM's yellow (`--v2-attention-tint` / `--v2-attention-line`), the one attention fill.
- **Sizes:** Max 300.
- **States:** Static. V2: Preview panel, Chat states On shows success, heads-up, needs input and fail; Scheduled job Out of credits shows the titled heads-up.
- **Props:** `NoticeBubble {kind, title, lines, children, action, feedback, time, groupStart, className}`.
- **Motion:** None of its own.
- **Accessibility:** Without a title, a hidden word says the tone ("Heads up:", "Done:"), so colour never carries meaning alone.
- **Responsive:** Wraps.
- **Do / Don't:** Do let the icon carry the tone. Don't tint a bubble green or red; don't use a notice for run steps.

## 36 Run card

**Status:** New.
**Why:** The partner opens the app to ask one thing: "is today done, and does it count?". In V2 a daily run prints 6 to 15 separate grey status bubbles (worker_flow.jsx:88, 102; chat_product.jsx:161), the outcome is a separate bubble, and the day status appears only in a centre overlay. One card answers it at a glance and is the same object Home, Activity and Run detail show (P1). It salvages the AgentProgress idea from V2's unloaded chat.jsx:250.

### RunCard (New)
- **Purpose:** One card per run: the live step, finished steps folded, the outcome, whether today counts, and the one action that changes today.
- **Anatomy:** Card (white, 1px line, radius 16, padding 16, gap 12, no shadow). Header: "Today’s run" 14/600 ink + start time 12 ink-4, Badge on the right. Live row (running, waiting): Spinner 16 or help icon + one line 14. Disclosure: "7 steps done" 13/500 ink-3, 32px tall, chevron; list of past-tense steps (check 16 ink-3, label 13, time 12 tabular). Outcome: icon 16 in tone + one line 14 ink. Day line: hairline above, StatusTick + 13 text. Actions: one Button md and/or "See run details" (`V2Kit.Link` standalone: underlined ink link, 32px).
- **Variants (status):** `running` (Badge Running, info, busy dot), `waiting` (Waiting for you, attention; attention border; deadline line), `done-pending` (Done, neutral; one tick; "Day 7 of 20, pending verification"; pending is neutral by the rule in section 03), `verified` (Verified, success; two ticks), `failed` (Didn’t finish, danger; "Try again" primary), `stopped` (Stopped; "Run again" secondary), `nojob` (No job today; pending tick, the day counts), `nocredits` (Badge neutral "Out of credits", a clock icon in neutral, the outcome "Not enough credits for this job." with no "tomorrow", the day line `OOC_COPY.day[dayStatus]`; the danger tone and the not-counted tick only when the status is 'stopped'; "See AI credits"), `skipped`, `expired` (outcome `DAY_COPY.expired`: "No answer by 9 pm, so I skipped the job.") and `rejected` (Run detail, Activity).
- **Sizes:** 300 in the thread; `full` fills the column (Run detail).
- **States:** Each status above; steps collapsed (default) or expanded. V2 has no equivalent; the nearest V2 states are `?day=next&end=job|nojob|failed` and the Preview panel's Scheduled job Out of credits.
- **Props:** `RunCard {run, worker, state, onAction(kind), onOpenDetail, feedback, full, defaultExpanded, className}`; `run {status, title, startedAt, current, since, steps[{label, time, status}], outcome, dayText, note, dayStatus, retryBy, reason, action}`. Missing fields fall back to `V2Kit.RUN_FIXTURES[status]`, which no longer hold day lines. `onAction` receives `retry`, `restart` or `credits`.
- **Day lines, from the record:** `runDayText(worker, state)` gives "Day 7 of 20, pending verification" (`numbers().done` and StatusTick's word), and with no `worker` the tick word alone, never a fixed count (`kit/run-card.jsx:88-100`). The deadline lines come from `DAY_COPY` ("by 9 pm", the record's `retryBy`, 9 pm when absent); the running day line is `RUN_COPY.keepOpen` ("Leave App Player open until it’s done."); waiting says "Answer by 9 pm or today won’t count." (`kit/run-card.jsx:101-115`).
- **Motion:** Chevron turns in 180ms; the card updates in place (no new card per step).
- **Accessibility:** `article` labelled "Today’s run, Running"; the disclosure is a real button with aria-expanded and aria-controls; the tick glyph is hidden because the day line says it in words; all text at least 4.77:1.
- **Responsive:** Header wraps the Badge under the title below 260px; steps grid keeps time right-aligned.
- **Copy:** Quoted from V2 where V2 has the line: "Done. I finished “Watch the video and provide feedback” and submitted the job." (worker_day_done.jsx:103), "No paid job on the board today." (worker_flow.jsx:111), "I couldn’t finish “…”. App Player closed during the job." (worker_flow.jsx:132), "Not enough credits for this job." (the first sentence of V2's "Not enough credits for this job. Credits will be refreshed tomorrow.", chat_bubbles.jsx:189; the refill time is said once, by the composer's notice), "Today doesn’t count toward your 20 days." (worker_day_done.jsx:95), "Pending verification" (worker_day_done.jsx:97). Proposed copy (PM to approve): the badges, "Waiting for your answer since 11:04 am", "Answer by 9 pm or today won’t count." (R08), "Today doesn’t count yet. Try again by 9 pm.", "You stopped the run at 11:04 am.", "You skipped today’s paid job.", the nocredits day line from `OOC_COPY.day` by `creditsDay(worker)`: "Today’s run starts once they’re back, so today can still count." (the refill lands before 9 pm; the outcome then reads "Not enough credits for this job.") or "Today won’t count toward your 20 days." (R14, section 48; `kit/run-card.jsx:113, 136-141`), the rejected line (R19), past-tense step labels.
- **Do / Don't:** Do keep one card per run, updated in place, and say the day status in words beside the tick. Do offer one action. Don't colour a pending day green. Don't use "Retry" in new copy; the system word is "Try again".

## 37 Ask card

**Status:** Changed from V2.
**Why:** V2's one-shape-for-every-question idea is right and stays, with its copy. What changes: the chips are a fifth look at 3.24:1 (worker_cards.jsx:58), bullet markers are hidden by the Tailwind preflight (worker_cards.jsx:49), the hint is 2.54:1, yellow stays on after the answer, and "Not now" forfeits the day with the cost told only afterwards (worker_flow.jsx:149, P3).

### AskCard (Changed from V2)
- **Purpose:** The worker's question, answered with chips, with what each answer costs visible before tapping.
- **Anatomy:** Card: attention fill `#fdf6cf`, 1px `#f3e8a8`, radius 16, padding 12 16, max 300, no shadow. Inside: optional title 14/600 ink, lead 14 ink-2, bullets (disc markers on, indent 20), question 14. Under the card: the consequence lines 13 attention-ink ("Not now: today won’t count toward your 20 days."), above the chip row so the cost is read before the tap; then the Chip row (shared Chip, gap 8; a consent option carries the zap icon), each chip's `aria-describedby` pointing at its consequence line (`kit/ask.jsx:56-68`); optional hint 13 ink-3.
- **Variants:** Plain (lead + chips), titled (job ask, failed day), with bullets (onboarding pitch). Tone `attention` (default) or `neutral` (decision D13 option B: onboarding asks while the partner watches).
- **Sizes:** Card max 300; chip row wraps at 300.
- **States:** Pending (chips shown, composer read-only with "Answer with the buttons above"); answered (chips go, card settles to white, the label echoes as a user bubble); stopped (Stop pressed while waiting: "The run stopped before you answered."); expired (proposed: "No answer by 9 pm, so I skipped this job. Today doesn’t count."). V2: `?start=worker` then Run for onboarding asks; `?day=next` for the job ask; `?day=next&end=failed` for the failed-day ask.
- **Props:** `AskCard {step: {title, lead, bullets, question, options[{id, label, consent, consequence}], hint, expiresAt}, answer, onAnswer(id, option), state, tone, className}`.
- **Motion:** Chips leave without animation; the card's fill change is instant.
- **Accessibility:** A hidden "BlueAI needs your answer." leads the pending card; chips are a group labelled by the question; text on yellow is ink / ink-2 (above 9:1); consequence 13 attention-ink (5.07:1).
- **Responsive:** Chips wrap to new lines inside 300.
- **Consent:** An option with `consent: true` turns on Automatic for that app. The caller opens ConsentDialog (section 46) and calls back only after "I agree, turn on"; Cancel leaves the question waiting (as V2, worker_cards.jsx:39).
- **Copy:** In V2 strings quoted exactly: the pitch (worker_flow.jsx:66-69), "Paid job available", "Watch the video and provide feedback.", "Want me to take this one?", "Yes, switch to Automatic" / "Just this job" / "Not now" (worker_flow.jsx:50, 120-121), "Today’s job didn’t finish." / "Retry to count the day." / "Retry" (worker_day_done.jsx:94, worker_flow.jsx:127), kept in the failed-day AskCard specimen as the V2 reference. The screens do not draw that ask: a failed day is the RunCard's and the WorkerCard's 'Try again' (`kit/run-card.jsx:47, 61`, `kit/worker-card.jsx:77-78`, `screens/chat.jsx:38, 151`), a proposed change to the PM's words listed in section 73 (V2 strings changed as proposals) under the glossary's one retry word (section 70). Proposed: the consequence line (R06), the stopped and expired lines (R08), Try again.
- **Do / Don't:** Do say the consequence before the choice and settle the card once answered. Don't bundle a mode switch into "Yes" silently (R07, R11). Don't leave yellow on an answered question.

## 38 Composer

**Status:** Changed from V2 (Composer, ComposerNotice, Stop).
**Why:** V2 clips text past 120px (chat_product.jsx:78, 284), rests on a blue-tinted 1.5px border at 1.40:1 that reads as focus (chat_product.jsx:260), mixes "..." and "…" in placeholders (266, 278, 283), reveals zero credits only after Send (124), and stops a paid job on one click with no trace (141). The Automatic ribbon (agent_permissions.jsx:203-216) is the right pattern and is generalised.

### Composer (Changed from V2)
- **Purpose:** One field to talk to the worker, with Send that becomes Stop.
- **Anatomy:** Block padding 4 16 12; optional notice and stop-confirm above; box radius 10, 1px `--v2-line-strong`, white, padding 4 4 4 12; textarea 14/1.5 ink, placeholder ink-4; SendStop 36 round on the right, bottom-aligned.
- **Variants:** Default; with a notice.
- **Sizes:** Field grows from 1 to 6 lines (`maxRows`), then scrolls.
- **States:** Rest; hover (line 20%); focus (1.5px blue + 3px 16% ring); typing; running (read-only, "BlueAI is working…", Stop); awaiting a choice (read-only, "Answer with the buttons above", Stop); disabled with a reason (a neutral placeholder, "You can’t send messages right now", paired with the notice that says why and until when); max height then scroll. Forced with `.is-hover`, `.is-focus`. V2: `?start=worker` (rest, typing), Run (running, awaiting), Preview Scheduled job Out of credits (disabled).
- **Props:** `Composer {value, defaultValue, onChange(text), onSend(text), onStop, running, awaiting, costsDay, paidJob, confirming, disabledReason, placeholder, notice, maxRows, className}`; `notice` is a ComposerNotice props object or an element.
- **Motion:** Border and ring 120ms.
- **Accessibility:** Labelled "Message BlueAI"; described by the notice; read-only (not disabled) while busy, so it stays reachable; Enter sends, Shift+Enter is a new line; placeholder 4.77:1 because it carries state.
- **Responsive:** Full width of the window; pinned above the nav at the compact height.
- **Do / Don't:** Do show standing conditions before typing. Do use "…" only for work in progress. Don't clip text. Don't use a blue border at rest.

### ComposerNotice (Changed from V2)
- **Purpose:** A condition that holds before the partner types, with its one action.
- **Anatomy:** A compact Banner: tone in the fill, the edge and the icon, words ink-2. Radius 10, 1px line in tone, padding 2 4 2 12; icon 16; text 13/1.35; the action is `V2Kit.Link` standalone (underlined ink, 32px tall, blue-ink on hover; `kit/composer.jsx:38-46`).
- **Variants:** `info` (blue tint, blue-ink icon: Automatic on, BlueStacks needed), `attention` (yellow, attention-ink icon: the partner must answer before the run can go on), `neutral` (sunken, ink-3 icon: paused; out of AI credits until a time, section 48). The words stay ink-2 in every tone.
- **Sizes:** Composer width.
- **States:** Shown while the condition holds; action hover, focus. V2: `?start=worker&v=2&mode=auto` then Run shows the Automatic ribbon.
- **Props:** `ComposerNotice {tone, icon, text, children, action: {label, onClick}, id, className}`.
- **Motion:** None of its own.
- **Accessibility:** Text 13 ink-2 on every tone's fill (above 9:1); the action is the shared Link (a real button); links are never warm-coloured (ink in every tone).
- **Copy:** "Automatic is on for YouTube. BlueAI takes paid YouTube jobs without asking." and "Change" are V2's former words; since 2026-10-14 V2 says "Automatic on YouTube" (a count once several apps are on) and Change opens its mode sheet (agent_permissions.jsx:62, 210-214). The out-of-credits line is owned by section 48 ("Out of AI credits until **5:30 am tomorrow**.", `V2Kit.OOC_COPY.until`); the locked field's placeholder is neutral ("You can’t send messages right now"), so the time is said once, in the notice. Proposed: "Today’s run needs BlueStacks App Player." / "Get BlueStacks", "The BlueAI worker is paused until tomorrow." / "Resume".
- **Do / Don't:** Do keep one notice at a time, the most urgent. Don't announce zero credits only after Send.

### Stop (SendStop) (Changed from V2)
- **Purpose:** Send the message, or stop the run.
- **Anatomy:** 36 round. Send: `--v2-action` fill, white send glyph 16. Stop: white, 1px line-strong, ink-2 stop glyph 16. Stop confirm (today not counted yet, `costsDay`): attention panel above the field, "Stop now? Today counts only if you run again by 9 pm." (the stopped RunCard and WorkerCard say the same); with a paid job under way (`paidJob`) it says "Stop now? The paid job won’t finish. Today counts only if you run again by 9 pm."; Keep running (secondary md) / Stop run (danger md). The confirm is role=alertdialog labelled by its question and takes focus on Keep running; while it is open, Stop moves focus back to it, so a double press never stops a paid run (`kit/composer.jsx:102-135`).
- **Variants:** send, stop.
- **Sizes:** 36 (control-md).
- **States:** Send ready, hover (`--v2-action-hover`), focus, pressed (1px down), disabled (sunken + ink-4, not pale blue); Stop, hover (wash + line), pressed; stop confirm.
- **Props:** `SendStop {mode, disabled, onClick, className}`; Composer `costsDay` (running, retrying or waiting, before the day counts) turns the confirm on, `paidJob` does too and names the job, `confirming` forces it.
- **Motion:** Colour 120ms, press 120ms.
- **Accessibility:** Labels "Send message" / "Stop the run" (V2 said "Stop task"; "task" is banned for paid work); visible focus ring; 36px target.
- **Responsive:** Fixed size.
- **Do / Don't:** Do ask before stopping a paid job and leave the stopped RunCard with its day status. Don't show hover by opacity (V2 used onMouseEnter opacity 0.85).

## 39 Feedback

**Status:** Changed from V2.
**Why:** V2 ships three placements and uses one (chat_product.jsx:153); its buttons carry shadows and fade the unvoted thumb to 0.55 (chat_feedback.jsx:171, 176); the label is 2.56:1 (197); selected reasons are a third chip look (#334155, line 77). The system keeps the behaviour and the copy, on shared parts.

### FeedbackThumbs (Changed from V2)
- **Purpose:** Thumbs up or down under a finished outcome; a thumbs down asks what went wrong.
- **Anatomy:** Row: two IconButtons 32 (thumb-up "Good result", thumb-down "Bad result"), gap 8, then "Thanks for the feedback" 12/500 ink-3. On thumbs down: "What went wrong?" 12/500 ink-3, Chip row (gap 6) with the five V2 reasons; "Other" opens a TextArea sm ("Provide additional feedback") and a send IconButton "Submit feedback".
- **Variants:** Below placement only (inside and stamp are dropped).
- **Sizes:** 32 buttons; reasons wrap at 300.
- **States:** Idle; up (Thanks for 2200ms); down (reasons); reason picked (chips close, Thanks); Other with text and submit. V2: Preview panel, Chat states On, first example.
- **Props:** `FeedbackThumbs {vote, onVote, reason, onReason, onSubmitOther(text), thanks, className}`; under an outcome via NoticeBubble `feedback` or RunCard `feedback`.
- **Motion:** None beyond the shared control transitions.
- **Accessibility:** Buttons expose aria-pressed; thanks is a role=status line; reasons are a group labelled by the prompt; nothing fades below 4.5:1.
- **Responsive:** Chips wrap.
- **Copy:** Quoted exactly from chat_feedback.jsx: "Good result", "Bad result", "Thanks for the feedback", "What went wrong?", "Didn't complete the task", "Wrong result", "Took too long", "Didn't understand me", "Other", "Provide additional feedback", "Submit feedback".
- **Decision:** V2 offers it on successful outcomes only (PM config, chat_product.jsx:150). Offering it on failed jobs, where a partner most wants to report a problem, is a PM call (decision D18, section 73).
- **Do / Don't:** Do keep both thumbs readable after a vote. Don't invent a new chip look for reasons.

## 40 Dependency card

**Status:** Changed from V2.
**Why:** V2 answers in the thread when a task needs BlueStacks, with the fix as a button and the install inline (needs_bluestacks.jsx:77, 106): the right idea, kept. It is a fifth tinted surface with an opacity hover and a 3.24:1 button, and it never gates the worker (needs_bluestacks.jsx:40), which narrates App Player steps on PCs that do not have it (R13).

### DependencyCard (Changed from V2)
- **Purpose:** The reply where the question was when a run needs BlueStacks or App Player; also the worker's preflight before every run.
- **Anatomy:** An info NoticeBubble (white, 1px line, radius 16, width 300, info icon 16 blue-ink) with one line 14 ink-2, and under it a full-width Button md, or the shared ProgressBar 6 with label "Getting ready" and value "42%".
- **Variants (phase):** `needs` (Get BlueStacks, primary), `installing` (title "Installing BlueStacks", "You can keep using BlueAI while it installs.", ProgressBar, then a quiet sm "Cancel install" when `onCancel` is given: the over-10s wait of section 50), `failed` (a danger notice "BlueStacks didn’t finish installing, so today’s run is waiting. Check your connection, then try again." + Try again, primary), `done` (success notice "BlueStacks is ready."), `signedOut` (Open App Player, secondary) (`kit/ask.jsx:152-191`).
- **Sizes:** 300 wide; button 36.
- **States:** As the phases. V2: `?start=worker&ask=Play%20Whiteout%20Survival%20for%2010%20min` (needs install), then Get BlueStacks (installing, done).
- **Props:** `DependencyCard {app, phase ('needs' | 'installing' | 'failed' | 'done' | 'signedOut'), progress (0 to 100), onGet, onCancel, onRetry (falls back to onGet), onOpen, className}`. The caller swaps the phase; the button is gone once installing (V2 hides it after one click).
- **Motion:** Progress fill moves with the shared ProgressBar transition; 3000ms install in the prototype.
- **Accessibility:** ProgressBar is role=progressbar with a value text; the button label says the fix; the notice's hidden "Info:" prefix keeps tone off colour alone.
- **Responsive:** Full bubble width at any window size.
- **Copy:** "This one runs inside BlueStacks App Player. I drive YouTube there, and BlueStacks isn’t installed on this PC yet.", "Get BlueStacks", "Getting ready", "BlueStacks is ready." are V2's. Proposed: "Installing BlueStacks", "You can keep using BlueAI while it installs.", "Cancel install", the failed line and "Try again", "App Player isn’t signed in to YouTube. Sign in there, then I’ll pick today’s run back up." / "Open App Player" (V2's only signed-out line, worker_flow.jsx:162, is a dead end).
- **Do / Don't:** Do answer in the thread with one button, and run the same check before every worker run. Don't open a modal for a missing dependency.

# Worker

## 41 The Worker record

**Status:** New.

**Why:** BlueAI is a worker that runs once a day on the partner's own accounts, so its state has to outlive every screen. V2 keeps it in a component's local state, module globals and constants: the day count is a constant (`worker_day_done.jsx:39`), so tonight's popup says Day 7 of 20 and tomorrow's strip says Day 6 of 20 (`:163`); the next run is hard-coded to 11 am (`chat_product.jsx:213`); the mode per app resets on reload (`agent_permissions.jsx:66`); opening another tab unmounts the run (R01, R02).

### Worker record and fixtures

- **Purpose:** the one state every worker surface reads (WorkerCard, ProgramStrip, DayBar, DayDone, JobPreviewCard, ModeRows, RunCard, Home, Activity, the top-bar status chip). Counts are derived, never stored.
- **Anatomy:** plain data, `kit/worker-data.jsx`:
  - `account` {name, first, email}: one neutral identity (Alex Morgan, @alexwatches), never staff data (R39).
  - `setUp`, `coachSeen`, `online`, `paused` (null or {until}).
  - `blocked`: null, or {reason 'no-app' | 'signed-out' | 'no-bluestacks', app}: what stops today's run before it starts (no connected app, App Player signed out of YouTube, BlueStacks not installed). `blockedBy(record)` reads it, and also returns {reason 'no-app'} for a set-up record with no connected app. Decision: a disconnect the partner chose stays blocked 'no-app' on the card and the Activity badge, and its copy says daily runs can’t start, never that they pause (section 63).
  - `apps.youtube` / `apps.instagram`: {status connected | not-connected | coming-soon, handle, mode always | auto, history allowed | notnow | revoked, historyOn}. `historyOn` is the day watch history was answered ('Oct 6' by default when `history` is set, null when it was never asked; the revoked fixture says 'Oct 11'); Account's What you agreed to reads it.
  - `runWindow` {kind time | window | any, label}.
  - `credits` {left, grant 2000, refillAt, runCost}.
  - `cashOutOpen`: the site's answer to whether this partner can cash out (the balance carries over between periods, so only the site knows it); null until it sends one, and while it is null every cash-out line says only where to look ('See your balance and cash out on Earnings.'), never that cash out is open or closed from the app's own count.
  - `today` {date, status scheduled | running | waiting | done | stopped | failed | missed | none, nextRunAt, startedAt, step, stepsDone, ask {job, since, expires}, outcome job | nojob | skipped | expired | stopped | failed | nocredits (once the run ends), error, retryBy, missedAt, checkedAt ('Oct 12, 7:02 am'), ranOn 'always' | 'auto' (once a job ran, in the apps' mode words)} (`kit/worker-data.jsx:88-91`).
  - `ledger`: one row per day {date, outcome job | nojob | skipped | expired | stopped | failed | nocredits | norun, status verified | pending | late | rejected | notcounted, reason, checkedAt ('Oct 12, 7:02 am', on a verified row), ranOn 'always' | 'auto' (on a row where a job ran)} (`kit/worker-data.jsx:75-79`). The outcome says what the run did, the status whether the day counts; Run detail reads checkedAt and ranOn (section 59).
  - `program` {target 20, endsOn 'Nov 1'}. `daysLeft` is no longer stored: `numbers().daysLeft` is the calendar days from `today.date` to `endsOn` (0 once it is reached; `program.daysLeft` is used only when the dates cannot be read), and today's slot is spent once today's row is in the ledger.
  - **The day's deadline: 9 pm local** (`DAY_DEADLINE_HOUR` 21, `kit/worker-data.jsx:29-30`). Every 'by 9 pm' line reads it: answering a waiting job, Try again, Run again, a late start, fixing a blocked run; at 9 pm a waiting job expires and the day closes as not counted. It is fixed, not the run window: the window is when a run may start (default 9 am to 8 pm, section 62), and the last start is an hour before the deadline so there is time to answer or try again. Sections 42, 54 and 60 and R06, R15 refer here.
  - Helpers on `window.V2Kit`: `numbers(record)`, `workerState(record)`, `blockedBy(record)` (null or {reason, app}), `creditsDay(record, now)` ('covered' | 'waiting' | 'stopped': with fewer credits than the run needs, whether the refill lands before the 9 pm deadline, `kit/worker-data.jsx:240-249`), `modeSummary(record)`, `nextRunText(record)`, `dayWords(row)` {result, tick, tickWord, hasRun} (the result words from the row's outcome, the tick from its status) and `dayGap(from, to)` (whole days between two 'Oct 12' dates, or null) (`kit/worker-data.jsx:169-286`); constants `WORKER_RULE` {rate 1, target 20, bonus 10, programEnd 'Nov 1', cashOutMin 10} (V2's RULE, `worker_day_done.jsx:38`, plus the cash-out minimum from its rule note, `:6`), `WORKER_APPS` (each with `actions`, every action a paid job on that app can include: youtube watch, like, comment, subscribe; instagram none yet, `kit/worker-data.jsx:37-42`), `WORKER_JOB`, `WORKER_OTHER_ACCOUNT` (the second identity the sign-in mismatch screens name), `EARNINGS_URL` (one URL; V2 has two) and `SITE_FAQ_URL` (the site's FAQ, on the same stand-in host) (exports, `kit/worker-data.jsx:288-291`). The program's words built from `numbers()` live in `kit/program.jsx:177-202`: `endedCopy(n)` ({title, body}: the period-ended words of sections 42 and 69, from `numbers()` and `cashOutOpen`; the body adds 'Your worker won’t run again until the next program.'), `minimumCopy(min)` and `RUN_COPY` {keepOpen, over, checking(pending)}.
- **Variants:** 24 fixtures in `FIXTURE_ORDER`: new, setUp, running, waiting, donePending, verified, failed, stopped, missed, blocked, paused, offline, complete, ended, endedBelow, noCredits, rejected, automatic, nojob, declined, day20, checking (all 20 done, the last one pending, today idle), behind, revoked (verified, with `apps.youtube.history` 'revoked' and `historyOn` 'Oct 11'; Account's 'history-revoked' state reads it).
- **Sizes:** n/a (data).
- **States:** `numbers()` returns done (verified + pending: the count every surface prints), verified, pending, rejected, notCounted, toGo, daysLeft, reachable, outOfReach, complete, latestRejected, last, the 20 `segments` (verified | pending | rejected | missed | future), and `cashOutMin` with `cashOutOpen` (the record's, passed through, never derived from the ledger). `cashOutOpen` is the one switch for every cash-out line: 'Cash out is open' only when it is true, the below-the-minimum line in the site's words (sections 43, 54, 69) when it is false, and 'See your balance and cash out on Earnings.' while it is null. `workerState()` returns one of 15 card states, in priority order (`kit/worker-data.jsx:212-238`): setup, complete, ended, checking (all 20 done, at least one pending, today idle: the worker stops taking jobs and runs again only if a day is not counted), running, waiting, done, offline, paused, blocked, noCredits, stopped, failed, missed. scheduled when none applies. A run that is going, waiting or done today outranks offline and paused, which only stop the next run; what blocks a run (no app, no credits) outranks a way to start one, so the card never offers Try again, Run again or Run now when the run cannot start. In V2 these come from `?start=worker`, `?day=returning`, `?day=next&end=job|nojob|day20|failed`, `?mode=auto`.
- **Props:** n/a. Surfaces take `worker` (the record) and read through the helpers; the RunCard reads it through its `worker` prop (section 36).
- **Motion:** none.
- **Accessibility:** the record feeds the spoken values (DayBar's aria-valuetext, StatusTick names), so they always match the visible count.
- **Responsive:** n/a.
- **Do / Don't:** Do change a count by adding a ledger row; read times from `runWindow`; keep the record above every screen. Don't store a count, a time, a mode or whether cash out is open in a component; don't hard-code Day 6 or 11 am; don't invent a second identity.

## 42 Worker card and coach mark

**Status:** Changed from V2 (both parts).

**Why:** the home card is the partner's daily answer to "what is my worker doing today". V2's card has an infinite gradient border that cannot animate, a green dollar tile, a whole-card role=button wrapped around a second button, and a Run that always says "my first paid job" and can complete the day again (`moneymaker_home.jsx:70, 122, 132, 141`).

### WorkerCard

- **Purpose:** the home's one card: today's state and the one action that matters now. Never a second Run once today's run has happened.
- **Anatomy:** card radius 16, 1px `--v2-line`, padding 16, no shadow. Tile 40, radius 10, `--v2-surface-sunken`, state icon 20 (tone only for running info, waiting and missed attention, failed and blocked danger, complete success; `kit/worker-card.jsx:24-29`). Title 'BlueAI worker' 16/600 ink. Status line 13 ink-3; optional second line 12 for the deadline or consequence. Mode line 12/500: shield-check 'Always ask', or zap 'Automatic · YouTube' in blue ink. One Button md (36) on the right.
- **Variants (state, copy, action):**
  - setup: 'Finds jobs that fit you and runs them on your account.' (V2) / primary 'Run' (V2), with the coach mark.
  - scheduled: 'Next run today at 11 am.' (from the record) / secondary 'Run now'.
  - running: spinner + the live step ('Watching a tech Short…') + the meta line 'Leave App Player open until it’s done.' (`RUN_COPY.keepOpen`) / quiet 'Details'.
  - waiting: 'A paid YouTube job is waiting for your OK.' + 'Since 11:04 am. Answer by 9 pm or today won’t count.' / primary 'Review job'.
  - done: 'Today’s run is done.' + one tick 'Pending verification' (V2 strings); no job: 'No paid job today, but your run counts.' (V2); skipped says 'You skipped today’s paid job.' + 'Today doesn’t count toward your 20 days.' (`DAY_COPY.notCounted`); expired (no answer by the deadline): 'No answer by 9 pm, so I skipped the job.' + 'Today doesn’t count toward your 20 days.' / quiet 'Details'.
  - stopped (proposed): 'You stopped today’s run.' + 'Today doesn’t count yet. Run again by 9 pm.' (`DAY_COPY.stopped`) / secondary 'Run again'.
  - failed: 'Today’s job didn’t finish.' (V2) + reason + 'Today doesn’t count yet. Try again by 9 pm.' (`DAY_COPY.failed`) / primary 'Try again'.
  - missed (proposed): 'Your PC was off or asleep at 11 am, so today’s run didn’t start.' + 'Run now by 9 pm to count today.' / primary 'Run now'. A late start never begins on its own: the partner may be using the PC when it wakes (P3).
  - blocked (proposed), one per reason from `blockedBy(worker)`, each 'Today’s run can’t start: ' + the reason, a deadline line and one fixing action that calls `onFix(reason)`: no-app 'YouTube isn’t connected.' / 'Connect it by 9 pm to count today.' / primary 'Connect YouTube' (also after a disconnect the partner chose: the same blocked 'no-app' card and Activity badge, and its copy says daily runs can’t start, never that they pause; section 41's decision, section 63); signed-out 'App Player isn’t signed in to YouTube.' / 'Sign in there by 9 pm to count today.' / primary 'Open App Player'; no-bluestacks 'BlueStacks isn’t installed.' / 'Install it by 9 pm to count today.' / primary 'Get BlueStacks'. Home routes them (section 57).
  - paused: 'Paused until Oct 16.' + 'Days don’t count while the worker is paused.' / secondary 'Resume'.
  - noCredits: 'Not enough AI credits for today’s run.' + `OOC_COPY.refreshAt` and the time ('AI credits refresh at 5:30 am.') + the day line `OOC_COPY.day[creditsDay(worker)]` ('Today’s run starts once they’re back, so today can still count.' or 'Today won’t count toward your 20 days.', section 48) / secondary 'AI credits'.
  - offline: 'You’re offline.' + 'Today’s run starts when you’re back online.' / secondary 'Try again'.
  - checking (all 20 done, at least one still pending, today idle): 'All 20 days done.' + a pending tick and 'One day is still being checked. I’ll run again only if it doesn’t count.' (`RUN_COPY.checking`) / secondary 'Earnings' (external); no mode line. The worker stops taking jobs and runs again only if a day is not counted.
  - complete: 'All 20 days verified.' + 'Your worker won’t run again until the next program. Cash out on Earnings after Nov 1.' / secondary 'Earnings' (external); no mode line. Twenty verified days clear the $10 cash-out minimum under either money model (D3; the site's answer is `cashOutOpen`, section 41), so this line needs no below-the-minimum variant; it still waits for the period to end.
  - ended (proposed; the period is over before all 20 were verified): the `endedCopy(n)` words (section 69), 'This program ended on Nov 1.' + 'You finished 18 of 20 days. Your worker won’t run again until the next program. Cash out is open on Earnings.' (the verified count, then `RUN_COPY.over`), or under the minimum 'You finished 6 of 20 days. Your worker won’t run again until the next program. You need $10 to cash out. Your balance carries over to the next period.' / secondary 'Earnings' (external); no mode line. Home shows section 69's period-ended state instead of this card once the period is over; the state stays for review and other surfaces.
  - Everything not marked V2 is proposed copy.
- **Sizes:** full width of the content column (387 in the 419 window).
- **States:** the fifteen above (setup, scheduled, running, waiting, done, stopped, failed, missed, blocked, paused, noCredits, offline, checking, complete, ended), from `workerState(worker)` (section 41's priority order) or forced with `state`. In V2: `?start=worker` (setup, coach on), `?day=returning` (scheduled). The other states do not exist in V2.
- **Props:** `worker`, `state`, `onRun` (setup, scheduled, missed), `onOpenToday`, `onReview`, `onRetry` (failed, stopped, offline), `onResume`, `onOpenCredits`, `onFix(reason)` (blocked: 'no-app' | 'signed-out' | 'no-bluestacks'), `coaching`, `onDismissCoach`.
- **Motion:** none on the card; the spinner follows the global reduced-motion rule.
- **Accessibility:** an article named 'BlueAI worker'; the first status line is role=status while running or waiting; only the Button is interactive (36, 4.92:1 primary); the tile is decorative.
- **Responsive:** the body wraps; at compact height the card stays first in the scroll.
- **Do / Don't:** Do put the consequence for today on the card; make the action primary only when the partner is needed (setup, waiting, failed, missed, blocked). Don't offer Run after today's run; don't animate the border or paint money green; don't make the card one big button.

### CoachMark

- **Purpose:** point a brand-new partner at Run, once.
- **Anatomy:** ink fill, radius 10, no shadow (it is an in-flow note, not a float), caret 6 aimed at the action, padding 12/12/8/16. Title 13/500 on-colour 'Run this to begin' (PM copy). 'Okay' (PM copy): a 32px inverse button, radius 6, 13/500 ink on surface.
- **Variants:** one.
- **Sizes:** max width 260.
- **States:** visible until Okay or Run; dismissed (remembered as `coachSeen` on the record, so a remount never brings it back; V2 keeps it in local state, `moneymaker_home.jsx:105`). Okay unmounts, so focus moves to the card's action (Run), never to the page. Okay has rest, hover, focus-visible and pressed. In V2: `?start=worker`.
- **Props:** `title`, `onOkay`, `okayClassName` (review only). `coaching` on WorkerCard draws it under the card; nothing is dimmed or locked.
- **Motion:** rises 6px over 240ms with the site ease, once; none under reduced motion.
- **Accessibility:** role=note; Okay 32px at 19:1 (V2 about 21px at 3.24:1).
- **Responsive:** right-aligned under the card.
- **Do / Don't:** Do keep the PM's words; dismiss on Okay or Run. Don't add a body line or a promise; don't show it twice.

## 43 Program progress

**Status:** Changed from V2 (StatusTick, DayBar, ProgramStrip); New (ProgramSummary).

**Why:** the 20 days are the partner's pay. V2's strip says 'Day 6 of 20' the morning after the popup said Day 7 (R02), '14 to go' in green had no unit (V2 dropped it on 2026-10-14), a rejected day has no way to show (R19), the rules are never stated before the first day ends (R12), and its Earnings link goes somewhere other than the menu's (R29). The site's dashboard says '12 of 20 days' and 'Ends Nov 1, 2026' (`onblue/apply.html:1404, 1409`); the app uses the same words and drops the year while it is the current one ('Ends Nov 1', section 70's date rule).

### StatusTick

- **Purpose:** where one day stands, in V2's messaging-app model: one tick pending, two verified.
- **Anatomy:** polyline glyph in a 24-unit box, round caps, its stroke compensated per size so it renders `--v2-icon-stroke` px at every size, as Icon does (`kit/worker.css:65-70`); the double tick is 1.4 times as wide. Colour on the glyph only; optional label 12 ink-3; rejected adds its reason 12 in danger ink.
- **Variants:** pending (one tick, `--v2-ink-4`: the neutral pending tone, section 03), verified (two ticks, `--v2-success`), notcounted (dash, ink-4: skipped or no run), rejected (cross, `--v2-danger-ink`, with reason), late (clock, ink-3: past the one-day promise).
- **Sizes:** `TICK_SIZES` [12, 14, 16, 28] (28 inside the day-done badge); any other size renders at 14.
- **States:** the five variants. V2 has pending and verified only (`worker_day_done.jsx:62-68`).
- **Props:** `status`, `size`, `label` (true prints the word, a string replaces it), `reason`. `tickWord(status)` returns the word StatusTick says ('Pending verification', 'Verified', 'Not counted', 'Still checking'), for any surface that prints it.
- **Motion:** none.
- **Accessibility:** the glyph is role=img named 'Pending verification', 'Verified', 'Not counted' or 'Still checking'; with a label, the label is read and the glyph is hidden.
- **Responsive:** inline; wraps with its text.
- **Do / Don't:** Do pair a tick with words where the day matters. Don't colour a pending tick green.

### DayBar

- **Purpose:** the 20 days at a glance; the site's progress bar plus the pending state it lacks.
- **Anatomy:** 20 segments, height 6, radius 4, gap 2. Verified solid `--v2-success-ink` (the site's goal-bar green); pending surface with a 1.5 inset `--v2-blue` outline (in progress, not reached: one of the three blue exceptions to the neutral pending tone, with the run chip's pending dot and the DayDone badge, section 03); rejected `--v2-danger-tint` with an inset 1px `--v2-danger-edge` (4.53:1, so it reads at 1px), in the slot it would have filled until the next run takes it; out of reach hatched `--v2-line-strong` (slots that can no longer be reached before the end); future `--v2-line`. Legend 12 ink-3 with ticks: '6 verified', '1 pending', '1 not counted', 'n days out of reach'. V2 since 2026-10-14: one continuous bar, height 4, radius 2, green fill for the verified share (`worker_day_done.jsx:155`), so a pending, rejected or missed day cannot show.
- **Variants:** with or without legend; optional `bonusLabel` at the right end only if decision D3 keeps a day-20 bonus (V2 had an amber segment until 2026-10-14).
- **Sizes:** fills its container.
- **States:** verified, pending, rejected, out of reach, future. V2: `?day=returning` (strip), `?day=next&end=job` (overlay).
- **Props:** `days` (segment states) or `worker` (derived by `numbers()`), `target`, `legend`, `bonusLabel`, `label`.
- **Motion:** none.
- **Accessibility:** role=progressbar, aria-valuenow = verified + pending, aria-valuetext '7 of 20 days, 1 pending verification' plus ', N out of reach' when slots can no longer be reached; the legend says it in words, so colour is never the only carrier. In forced colours every state keeps a shape of its own: verified solid, pending a 2px edge, rejected a 2px edge over hatching, out of reach a grey hatch, future a 1px edge (`kit/worker.css:90-101`).
- **Responsive:** segments shrink with the width.
- **Do / Don't:** Do use the same component for the site's tile. Don't add amber or a second green.

### ProgramStrip

- **Purpose:** the home's 'how many of my 20 days count', shown from day 0.
- **Anatomy:** card radius 16, 1px line, padding 16. Head: '6 of 20 days' 14 ink-3 with the figure 600 ink (the site's muted note with one bold figure), 'Ends Nov 1' 12 ink-4, and the ProgramSummary info tip (32). DayBar. Foot over a hairline, type `.v2-text-caption`: one receipt line 12 ink-3 with its tick, and 'Earnings' (`V2Kit.Link` standalone with `href: EARNINGS_URL`, external: underlined ink, blue on hover, external icon).
- **Variants (receipt line):** day 0 'No days counted yet.'; today pending 'Today’s run is pending verification.'; yesterday verified 'Yesterday’s run verified.' (V2); yesterday rejected 'Yesterday’s run wasn’t counted.' in danger ink + 'See why' (opens Activity); skipped 'Today doesn’t count toward your 20 days.' (V2); ended 'This program ended on Nov 1. Cash out on Earnings.' (no cash sentence when `cashOutOpen` is false); complete: no receipt, since the WorkerCard says it once ('All 20 days verified.', cash out after Nov 1).
- **Sizes:** full width.
- **States:** the variants, from the record. V2: `?day=returning` only (returning partners).
- **Props:** `worker`, `onOpenActivity`, `receipt` (default true; false drops the receipt line and leaves Earnings alone in the foot, for Home while the WorkerCard says today's status, section 57).
- **Motion:** none.
- **Accessibility:** a section named 'Your 20 days'; Earnings and the info trigger are 32 tall and announce 'opens in a new tab' / 'How your 20 days work'.
- **Responsive:** foot wraps under 320.
- **Do / Don't:** Do say the count once, from the ledger. Don't show money or a remaining count in green (V2 dropped both on 2026-10-14); don't use a second Earnings URL.

### ProgramSummary

- **Purpose:** the rules in five lines, before the first day ends (onboarding close, Help) and one tap away afterwards (strip info tip).
- **Anatomy:** title 13/600 'How your 20 days work'; five lines 13 ink-2, each with its own 16 icon: calendar 'Run on 20 days before Nov 1. Keep your PC on and awake at run time, and App Player open while a job runs.' / check 'A day counts when a job is verified, or when there was no job to take. Keep the comment up until it’s verified.' / check-double 'We check every run within a day. The tick doubles once it’s verified.' (the PM's line, `worker_day_done.jsx:98`) / earnings 'Cash out on Earnings after the period ends.' / lock 'You need $10 to cash out. Your balance carries over to the next period.' (`minimumCopy`, the site's two sentences, `onblue/apply.html:2292-2293`). Proposed copy built from V2's rule comment (`worker_day_done.jsx:5-7`) except those two. `programRules(rule)` is the one place a rule is written, and `programRule(id)` one rule's text (DayDone, Run detail and Chat read 'ticks'). The $10 minimum is in both D3 options, so it is the one amount shown before D3; no rate or bonus until then.
- **Variants:** full; compact (the kit Tooltip, info kind, behind a 32px info button).
- **Sizes:** full width; tip max 260.
- **States:** full; compact closed, hover, focus, pinned open, Esc closes.
- **Props:** `rule`, `compact`, `title`, `open` (review), `triggerClassName` (review).
- **Motion:** the tip's own (section 23).
- **Accessibility:** the trigger is named; the tip is aria-describedby.
- **Responsive:** the tip flips at the window edge.
- **Do / Don't:** Do show it in the onboarding close; do state the $10 minimum with cash out. Don't promise a rate or a bonus before D3.

## 44 Day done

**Status:** Changed from V2.

**Why:** V2 adopted the minimal popup on 2026-10-14, as this system recommended (R28): a 44 green tick badge, the title, one status line ('Day 7 of 20 · Pending verification'; the money option leads with '+$1 · '; Day 20 drops the day part), the verification note only the first time it shows in a session, and Got it, on a 280 panel, radius 20 (`worker_day_done.jsx:132-151`). The eyebrow, the big green count, the 20-day bar and legend, the confetti and the pulsing ring are gone; the bar lives on the home strip. Still different: V2's badge is verified green while the day is pending, so colour says paid while the words say not yet, and the panel is a div with role=dialog that never takes focus. The system keeps the DayBar above its one count line, confetti once on Day 20 only, and the note on every showing. The PM's titles are DRAFT strings and are quoted, not rewritten.

### DayDone

- **Purpose:** the end-of-run moment: today's run is in, here is where the 20 days stand, and it is pending until checked.
- **Anatomy:** the kit Dialog, celebration variant, size sm (320), dim scrim, no close button. Badge 56 round, `--v2-blue-tint` with one tick 28 in `--v2-blue-ink` (the badge's colour; the tick inherits it instead of StatusTick's pending ink-4). One of the three blue exceptions to the neutral pending tone, with the DayBar's pending segment and the run chip's pending dot (section 03). Title 20/600 ink. Sub 13 ink-3 (Day 20 only). DayBar without legend, then one count line 13: '7 of 20 days' (figure 600 ink) and one tick 'Pending verification'. Note 13 ink-3 'We check every run within a day. The tick doubles once it’s verified.' One primary 'Got it', full width, 44.
- **Variants (copy is the PM's, `worker_day_done.jsx:87-89`):** job 'Today’s run is done.'; nojob 'No paid job today, but your run counts.'; day20 'Day 20 done.' + 'That’s every day for this month.' + the proposed line 'Cash out on Earnings after {endsOn}, once your days are verified ($10 minimum).' (`kit/day-done.jsx:22, 57`). Failed and skipped have no moment: the run card and the worker card say those, with their action.
- **Sizes:** Dialog sm, 320.
- **States:** open (job, nojob, day20); dismissed by Got it, Esc or the scrim. In V2: `?day=next&end=job`, `?day=next&end=nojob`, `?day=next&end=day20`; the Preview panel's Day end row.
- **Props:** `end` (job | nojob | day20), `worker` (count from `numbers()`), `open`, `onClose`, `preview` (guide only: drawn open in place, no focus).
- **Motion:** the badge pops once over 450ms (`--v2-dur-celebrate`, site ease). Confetti only on Day 20: 12 fixed bits in blue and neutral only (`--v2-blue`, `--v2-action`, `--v2-ink-4`), never green or yellow while the day is pending, once, about 900ms; hidden entirely under reduced motion. No ring, no loop.
- **Accessibility:** aria-modal, labelled by the title (it sits under the badge, so `labelledBy`); focus moves in, Tab stays inside, focus returns to where it was; the note is ink-3 (V2's was 2.54:1).
- **Responsive:** centred in the window; fits the 640 compact height.
- **Do / Don't:** Do keep the badge blue with one tick and the count line's tick neutral until verified (section 03); say the count once; keep money off it (the days style ships). Don't paint a pending day green; don't celebrate every day; don't loop animation.

## 45 Job preview

**Status:** New (R07). Chat's waiting state asks paid jobs in this card (decision D17, option A, built; `screens/chat.jsx` jobCard). V2's AskCard ask (section 37) is option B, one constant away, and asks every other question.

**Why:** the riskiest moment in the product is agreeing to public activity under the partner's name. V2's ask shows only 'Watch the video and provide feedback.' (`worker_flow.jsx:51, 119`); the comment it posts is never shown (`:136`); the first chip, 'Yes, switch to Automatic', bundles yes with a standing consent (`:121`); and the cost of 'Not now' (the day) is told only afterwards (`:149`).

### JobPreviewCard

- **Purpose:** before a paid job runs on the partner's account, show which video, which channel, what will be done (with the exact comment) and what skipping costs; after an Automatic job, show the same facts as a receipt.
- **Anatomy:** card radius 16, max 300, 1px line (attention line while it waits). Head 13/500 with the app mark: 'Paid job available' (V2) and when ('Just now' or 'Since 11:04 am'), on `--v2-attention-tint` in attention ink while waiting, panel grey otherwise. Thumbnail 16:9, radius 10, duration 11/500 on ink. Video title 14/600 ink; 'TechLine on YouTube' 13 ink-3. A plain list with 16 icons (facts, not chips): 'Watch the whole video', 'Like it', 'Post this comment'. The exact comment in a quote block 13/1.5 ink on sunken with a 2px line-strong edge. Deadline line 13 ink-2 with an attention clock: 'Answer by 9 pm or today won’t count.' Actions, full width, 36: primary 'Take this job'; secondary 'Skip: today won’t count'; quiet 'Switch YouTube to Automatic' (zap, opens the consent for Automatic).
- **Variants:** ask (Always ask); receipt: head 'Paid job done' 'At 11:12 am', past-tense list, foot with the StatusTick from `verify` (pending by default; null in Run detail, where the Verification card says the day once) and, only when `ranOn` is auto, quiet 'Switch back to Always ask' (shield-check) (`kit/job.jsx:71-74`).
- **Sizes:** max 300 in the thread; full width in Run detail.
- **States:** ask (partner present); waiting (since 11:04 am, expires 9 pm: the Windows notification brings the partner here, R08); taking (primary loading, others disabled); answered (`answer` set: the deadline line and the actions go, the head loses its attention tint, and one line takes their place: 'You took this job.', 'You skipped this job. Today won’t count toward your 20 days.' with a not-counted tick, or 'Automatic is on for YouTube, so I took this job.'; focus moves to the card, tabIndex -1 and `data-v2-anchor`, as the AskCard does, `kit/job.jsx:58-64, 125-127`); expired ('No answer by 9 pm, so I skipped the job. Today doesn’t count toward your 20 days.', `DAY_COPY.expired` + `DAY_COPY.notCounted`, with a not-counted tick, no actions); receipt. V2 reaches only its plain ask: `?day=next&end=job` on Always ask.
- **Props:** `job` {app, label, video {title, channel, duration, thumb}, actions [watch | like | comment | subscribe], comment}, `mode` (ask | receipt), `waitingSince`, `expiresAt`, `expired`, `doneAt`, `taking`, `verify`, `ranOn`, `answer` ('once' | 'deny' | 'always': the answered state), `onTake`, `onSkip`, `onStopAsking`, `onChangeMode`. Fixture: `V2Kit.WORKER_JOB`.
- **Motion:** none beyond the Button's loading spinner.
- **Accessibility:** an article labelled by its head; the list is named 'What I’ll do' / 'What I did'; the thumbnail is decorative; every action 36 tall; consequences are words on the controls, never colour alone.
- **Responsive:** single column; buttons stay full width.
- **Do / Don't:** Do show the exact comment before agreement; put the cost on the skip button; keep Automatic a separate, quieter action through the consent. Don't bundle yes with Automatic; don't tell the cost after the tap; don't draw facts as chips.

## 46 Always ask and Automatic

**Status:** Changed from V2 (ModeRows, ConsentDialog, AutoRibbon); New (PermissionRequest).

**Why:** the PM's model is right and stays: one setting per app, every app starts on Always ask, turning Automatic on is consent, asked every time (`agent_permissions.jsx:1-33`). What changes is where it lives and how it is built: V2 changes it from a bottom sheet in chat and, since 2026-10-14, from a Worker card in Settings, one switch per app (R05, D4); Instagram can be consented to before its jobs exist (R40), the dialog never takes focus, its agree button is 3.24:1, and watch history is read without asking (R18).

### ModeRows

- **Purpose:** the per-app mode, with one home in Settings > Worker (section 62). The ribbon's Change opens that screen (Chat's `onChangeMode`), so the consent dialog never stacks over a float.
- **Anatomy:** one kit ListRow per app: mark 20 ink-3, name 14/500, account as the description, kit Segmented md (32) 'Always ask' (shield-check) / 'Automatic' (zap), the icons the consent table and the worker card use (`kit/permissions.jsx` OPTIONS). Note under the rows 12 ink-3: 'Automatic starts and finishes that app’s jobs without asking. Each app has its own setting, and a new app starts on Always ask.' (V2's former menu footnote; V2's sheet now says 'New apps always start on Always ask.'). 'Mode, per app' is not carried (Mode is not a partner word).
- **Variants:** connected (switch); coming soon ('Instagram jobs aren’t available yet.' + Badge 'Coming soon', no switch); not connected ('Not connected' + Connect).
- **Sizes:** full width.
- **States:** Always ask (default); Automatic (only after the consent: `beforeChange` opens it and commits on agree); coming soon; not connected. In V2: `?v=2&mode=auto` (ribbon, Change) or `?v=1` (chip) open the mode sheet, one switch per app (agent_permissions.jsx:98-157); Settings > Worker > Apps lists the same switches.
- **Props:** `worker` or `apps` + `modes`, `onChange(appId, mode)`, `beforeChange(appId, mode)` (return a promise of true to commit), `onConnect(appId)`, `note` (false hides).
- **Motion:** the Segmented's own (180ms).
- **Accessibility:** each Segmented is a radiogroup named 'YouTube paid jobs' with arrow keys; segments 32 tall; aria-busy while the consent is open.
- **Responsive:** the description truncates before the switch shrinks.
- **Do / Don't:** Do send every turn-on through the consent; apply Always ask at once. Don't show a switch for an app with no jobs or no account.

### ConsentDialog

- **Purpose:** the consent for Automatic, per app, every time.
- **Anatomy:** kit Dialog, consent variant, md (382): title 20/600 'Run YouTube jobs automatically?', close 32, no scrim dismiss. Body 14 ink-2, the Dialog's description (aria-describedby, so it is read with the dialog), proposed, PM and legal (R51): 'BlueAI will take YouTube jobs and do them on your account without asking first: watching, liking, posting comments and subscribing to channels in your name.' The line is built from every action a job on that app can include (`WORKER_APPS[].actions`, section 41; `kit/permissions.jsx:75-79`), never a fixed list. V2 adopted the minimal consent dialog on 2026-10-14 (a plain frame with no header, divider or X: title, one sentence, a lighter second line, Cancel / I agree, turn on): its sentence, 'BlueAI will take paid YouTube jobs on its own and like, comment, follow and post from your account.' (`agent_permissions.jsx:191`), names posting videos, which the worker never does, and leaves out the actions it does take; on Automatic the job preview never appears, so this line is the partner's only notice of what will be done in their name. The system keeps what V2 dropped: a per-app table radius 10 (the app being changed tinted blue, Automatic in blue ink with zap, Always ask with shield-check, coming soon in words), a note 12 ink-3 'Each app has its own setting. The others stay on Always ask until you switch them.' and the agreement line 14 'By turning this on, you agree to let BlueAI do this for you on YouTube. You can turn it off anytime.' (V2's words until 2026-10-14). Added, proposed, 12 ink-3: 'Turn it off in Settings > Worker, or with Change above the chat.' Footer 44: 'Cancel' (secondary) then 'I agree, turn on' (primary).
