The title and buttons are V2 verbatim (`agent_permissions.jsx:186-191`); the body, table, note, agreement line and turn-off line are not.
- **Variants:** one per app.
- **Sizes:** Dialog md.
- **States:** open; agree busy (primary loading, Esc, scrim and close held), then done (closes, the ribbon appears, the agreement time is recorded per account on the server); cancel (nothing changes, the job question keeps waiting); not saved (`error`: Banner danger 'Not saved, nothing changed. Try again.' in the body, and 'I agree, turn on' is the retry; proposed copy). V2: the Automatic chip on the daily job ask, or the onboarding choice (`?setup` default).
- **Props:** `app`, `open`, `busy`, `error`, `onAgree`, `onCancel`, `worker`, `preview` (guide only).
- **Motion:** the Dialog's (320ms in, 240ms out, none under reduced motion).
- **Accessibility:** aria-modal, labelled by the title and described by the consent line, focus in, trapped, returned; the agreement is the button (no checkbox), 44 tall at 4.92:1.
- **Responsive:** scrolls inside the window at compact height.
- **Do / Don't:** Do keep V2's title and button verbatim, send the proposed body to the PM and legal (section 70's register) and record the version shown; say where to turn it off. Don't remember consent forever; don't let a busy agree fire twice.

### AutoRibbon

- **Purpose:** while any app is Automatic, say so above the composer, with a way to change it.
- **Anatomy:** the chat kit's ComposerNotice, info tone: zap 16, 13 blue ink 'Automatic is on for YouTube. BlueAI takes paid YouTube jobs without asking.' (V2's words until 2026-10-14; V2 now says 'Automatic on YouTube', or a count), trailing 'Change' (32) opening Settings > Worker, the mode's one home (Chat's `onChangeMode`).
- **Variants:** one.
- **Sizes:** composer width.
- **States:** visible while some app is Automatic; hidden otherwise (`autoNotice()` returns null). V2: `?v=2&mode=auto`.
- **Props:** `autoNotice(worker, onChange)` returns `{tone, icon, text, action}` for `ComposerNotice` or the Composer's `notice`.
- **Motion:** none.
- **Accessibility:** Change is a real button, 32px.
- **Responsive:** text wraps; Change stays on the right.
- **Do / Don't:** Do keep it as the in-chat shortcut. Don't make it the only place to change the mode (D4).

### PermissionRequest

- **Purpose:** ask before the worker reads viewing data, say what Not now costs, and say where to take it back.
- **Anatomy:** card radius 16, max 300, padding 16. The worker speaks as 'I' throughout, as in the thread. Head 14/600: app mark on a 32 disc + 'I’d like to see'. Three lines 13 ink-2 with 16 icons: 'Your watch history', 'Your subscriptions', 'Your channel’s joined date and stats'. Why and cost 13 ink-3: 'So I can watch what you’re into and pick jobs that fit. If you choose Not now, I’ll still run, with general videos.' 'Not now' (secondary) then 'Allow' (primary), 36. Revoke note 12 ink-3: 'You can turn this off anytime in Connected apps.' All proposed copy (PM decision).
- **Variants:** full (asked); compact one-line result: allowed 'I can see your YouTube watch history. Turn it off anytime in Connected apps.'; not now 'Not allowed. I’ll watch general videos instead of what you’re into.' + Allow; revoked 'You turned off watch history on Oct 14. I’ll watch general videos until you allow it again.' + Allow.
- **Sizes:** max 300.
- **States:** asked; allowing (Allow busy); allowed; not now (with Allow); revoked in Connected apps (with Allow, which opens the full request in place, section 63). V2 has none: it reads history during setup (`worker_flow.jsx:80, 161`).
- **Props:** `app`, `state` (asked | allowed | notnow | revoked), `lines`, `busy`, `onAllow`, `onNotNow`, `decidedOn`.
- **Motion:** none.
- **Accessibility:** a named section; results are role=status; buttons 36.
- **Responsive:** single column.
- **Do / Don't:** Do give every permission a Revoke row in Connected apps. Don't ask for data access and Automatic in one tap.

# Credits and settings

## 47 Credits balance and allowance

**Status:** Changed from V2 (CreditsChip); New (CreditsMeter, useCountdown).
**Why:** AI credits are the worker's fuel, not money (concept). A partner has one question about them: will today's run happen. V2 shows a bare number that is only ever 2,000 or 0 (index.html:731), paints it and its ring in the brand gradient (index.html:342, 346), fires a "low" dot under 1,000 that can therefore only mean empty (index.html:733), and keeps a second grant of 2,500 in the shared popup parts (credits_popup_parts.jsx:14). The system says the allowance in one place, in ink, against one grant read from the Worker record (section 41).

### CreditsChip (Changed from V2)
- **Purpose:** The top bar's quiet status of today's allowance, and the way into AI credits (section 66).
- **Anatomy:** A real `<button>` with a 32px hit area holding a 28px pill: 1px `--v2-line` on `--v2-surface`, radius pill, padding 0 12 0 8; credits glyph 16 (the brand gradient through V2's star, its one sanctioned use besides the mark); number 13/500 ink, tabular; an 8px dot at the top right with a 2px surface ring when low or empty.
- **Variants:** full (number), low (number + attention dot), empty (0 + danger dot), paused (glyph + "Own key" in ink-3).
- **Sizes:** One: pill 28, target 32.
- **States:** Full; low (less than one run's cost); empty; paused (running on your own key); hover (edge to `--v2-line-hover` + 4.5% wash); focus-visible (2px blue outline on the pill); active (7% wash). Forced with `.is-hover`, `.is-focus`, `.is-active`. V2: `index.html?start=worker` (2,000), `?start=worker&credits=0` (0 + red dot), Preview row "Own key: On" (still shows 2,000, dot hidden).
- **Props:** `balance`, `state` ('full' | 'low' | 'empty' | 'paused', derived from balance and runCost when absent), `runCost`, `onClick`, `className`. TopBar passes `{balance, state}`.
- **Motion:** Edge and wash 120ms (`--v2-dur-1`). Nothing else moves.
- **Accessibility:** Accessible name says the state in words ("AI credits: 120 left today, not enough for today’s run"), so the dot is never the only carrier. Number contrast 19:1; focus ring on the visible pill.
- **Responsive:** Fixed size in the 52px top bar; the number never wraps.
- **Do / Don't:** Do derive low from the Worker record (`credits.left < credits.runCost`). Do say "Own key" instead of a balance that is not being spent. Don't paint the number, ring or fill with the gradient. Don't use a raw threshold like 1,000.

### CreditsMeter (New)
- **Purpose:** Answer "will today's run fit": how much of today's grant is left, when it refreshes, and whether today's run is covered. Lives on AI credits (section 66) under the stat.
- **Anatomy:** 6px rail (`--v2-surface-sunken`, radius pill, role=meter) with a `--v2-blue` fill; caption row 12 ink-4: "1,250 of 2,000 left today" left, "Refreshes in 6h 42m" right; one covered line 13/500 ink-2 with a 16px icon: check in `--v2-success`, alert in `--v2-attention-ink`, x-circle in `--v2-danger-ink`.
- **Variants:** Covered line: "Enough for today’s run", "Not enough for today’s run", "Today’s run can’t start until credits refresh", "Today’s run is done" (when runDone).
- **Sizes:** Full width of its container.
- **States:** Full; partial; low (less than one run); empty; empty with today's run already done; refreshed (rail full, caption "Refreshed"). Not in V2: it is the unshipped comparison idea (credits_page_options.jsx:147-170, `credits-popup-options.html`).
- **Props:** `balance`, `grant`, `runCost`, `refillAt` (Date or ms; default the next 00:00 UTC), `now` (freezes the clock), `runDone`, `refreshed`, `figure` (default true; false, under a StatCard that already shows the balance, makes the caption "of 2,000 today" and keeps aria-valuetext whole), `className`.
- **Motion:** Fill width 240ms `--v2-ease-out`; the countdown text ticks once a second and stops at zero. Reduced motion: the global rule removes the width transition.
- **Accessibility:** role=meter with aria-valuemin 0, aria-valuemax grant, aria-valuenow balance and aria-valuetext "1,250 of 2,000 left today". The covered line is text, not colour. Caption ink-4 is 4.77:1 (meta only); the consequence sits in the ink-2 line.
- **Responsive:** Caption halves wrap to two lines below about 260px.
- **Do / Don't:** Do read grant and runCost from the Worker record (2,000 and 360 in the fixtures). Do keep the fill blue: the line carries the tone. Don't fill it with the brand gradient. Don't show the meter while your own key is in use.

**Proposed copy:** chip "Own key"; meter "{n} of {grant} left today" and "Refreshes in {6h 42m}" (from the unshipped comparison page), "Refreshed", "Enough for today’s run", "Not enough for today’s run", "Today’s run can’t start until credits refresh", "Today’s run is done".

### useCountdown and creditsTime (New helpers, V2 maths kept)
- **Purpose:** One clock for every zero-balance moment, so the chip, meter, dialog and notices say the same time.
- **Anatomy:** `useCountdown(target?, {now?})` returns `{ms, at, left, clock, done, underMinute}`. `creditsTime.formatLeft` is V2's (credits_popup_parts.jsx:27): "6h 42m", "42m", "6h", "8s". `formatClock` gives the partner's local time in the house format: "5:30 am tomorrow", "11 am today".
- **Variants:** Relative (countdown) and clock (local time).
- **Sizes:** n/a.
- **States:** Counting; under a minute (seconds appear); done (stops; components switch to refreshed). A display string such as "5:30 am" is ignored and the next 00:00 UTC is used.
- **Props:** `target` (Date or ms), `opts.now`.
- **Motion:** 1000ms tick (credits_popup_parts.jsx:46), no interval once done or when `now` is fixed.
- **Accessibility:** Counting text is not a live region; announce only the change to refreshed.
- **Responsive:** n/a.
- **Do / Don't:** Do show local time ("5:30 am") wherever a clock appears. Don't say "tomorrow" without a time.

## 48 Out of credits

**Status:** Changed from V2 (out-of-credits popup, retired); New (zero-credits notice and run end).
**Why:** Every zero-balance moment must say the same time and what zero means for today's day (R14). In V2 the popup counts down (byok.jsx:66) while the chat says "refreshed tomorrow" twice (chat_bubbles.jsx:188, 189), the main chat reveals zero only after Send (chat_product.jsx:124), and nothing says whether the day still counts.

### Out-of-credits popup (retired) (Changed from V2)
- **Purpose:** none left. V2 lets the partner type at zero, then stops the send with a popup. The system locks the composer at zero and says so before anyone types (section 38), so the moment the popup answered never happens and there is no dialog to dismiss or buy from.
- **V2 today:** `index.html?start=worker&credits=0`, type in the composer, press Send. Width 300, radius 12, its own scrim rgba(15,23,42,0.5) at z 130, backdrop click only, no role, focus never moves in (byok.jsx:78-80); a gradient 38/800 zero (byok.jsx:51); an unlabelled 22px (i) "What are credits?" (credits_popup_parts.jsx:94); "You’ve used all of today’s AI credits." / "Credits will be refreshed in **6h 42m**." (byok.jsx:65-66).
- **Where its parts went:** the time and the day line to the composer notice (below); the explainer stays V2's, on the AI credits screen (section 66, `index.html:534-546`); the refreshed moment to the AI credits screen's success banner.
- **Do / Don't:** Do lock the field before anyone types. Don't reveal zero only after Send. Don't add a top-up or "earn credits" action.

**Proposed copy (day line, pending D6):** covered "Today’s run isn’t affected." / waiting "Today’s run starts once they’re back, so today can still count." / stopped "Today won’t count toward your 20 days." The PM confirms whether a run stopped for credits loses the day.

**Explainer (a proposal listed in section 73, not shown anywhere):** "Your daily run and your chats use AI credits. You get a fresh set every day, and chats use the same set, so leave enough for today’s run.", built around the daily run rather than a count of jobs, and true until decision D6 holds credits back for the run (section 76); it never promises room to chat: V2's approved line, "enough for 4 to 5 jobs" (credits_popup_parts.jsx:105), was written beside a 2,500 grant and holds at 2,000 only while a job costs 400 or less.

### Zero-credits notice and run end (New)
- **Purpose:** Say zero before anyone types, and close a run that stopped for credits, with the same time and the day status.
- **Anatomy:** Composer variant: `V2Kit.ComposerNotice` tone neutral, clock 16, "Out of AI credits until **5:30 am tomorrow**." + day line. This section owns the zero-credits wording (`V2Kit.OOC_COPY`); the composer (38), RunCard (36) and the guide read it. Run variant: x-circle 16 in `--v2-danger-ink`, day line 13/500 ink, then "AI credits refresh at 5:30 am tomorrow." 12 ink-4, a fixed local time, never a ticking countdown. In Chat each fact is said once: the run's end carries the day line only (`OOC_COPY.day`), the notice says until when, and the locked field's placeholder is neutral ("You can’t send messages right now").
- **Variants:** `composer`, `run` (RunCard's out-of-credits end, section 36).
- **Sizes:** Full width of the composer or the card.
- **States:** Composer, run covered; composer, run still to come; run ended for credits. V2: Preview row "Scheduled job: Out of credits" (chat tab, signed in) plays the run that dies; its placeholder says "BlueAI is out of credits until tomorrow..." (chat_product.jsx:278).
- **Props:** `variant`, `refillAt`, `now`, `dayStatus`, `className`. Strings on `V2Kit.OOC_COPY` so RunCard and screens say exactly the same thing.
- **Motion:** None.
- **Accessibility:** role=status; the time is text in local time, not only a countdown.
- **Responsive:** Wraps to two lines in the 387px content width.
- **Do / Don't:** Do show the notice when the composer mounts at zero. Don't let the field accept a message the product will refuse. Don't say "tomorrow" without the time.

## 49 Settings blocks

**Status:** Changed from V2 (SettingsSection, AdvancedDisclosure, ByokPanel, AiModePanel).
**Why:** Settings is where a partner looks for the controls the worker's promises point to (P4). V2 copies one card shell five times with two paddings and two title weights (settings.jsx:25, 112; byok_settings.jsx:61), and leads with developer knobs (Max Output Tokens, Max Steps, Timeout, two AI Model pickers: settings.jsx:120-134, byok_settings.jsx:78). Its own-key switch deletes the key (byok_settings.jsx:64) and AI mode's failure states are unreachable. The system has one section block, one Advanced disclosure, and panels whose every state exists.

### SettingsSection (Changed from V2)
- **Purpose:** One card per topic the partner thinks about (Worker, Connected apps, Notifications, BlueStacks, Account and privacy, Help, then Advanced: section 55).
- **Anatomy:** Card: `--v2-surface`, 1px `--v2-line`, no shadow, radius 16, padding 16, gap 12. Header: title 16/600 ink (h2), optional description 13 ink-3, optional control at the right. Body: ListRows run full-bleed (their 16px padding aligns with the title) with their own inset hairlines; any other block gets one `--v2-line-hair` above it with 12 on each side.
- **Variants:** Plain; with description; with a header control.
- **Sizes:** Full width of the settings column (387 in the window).
- **States:** Rest; highlighted (routed here: `--v2-blue` edge + `--v2-focus-ring` for 1900ms, then fades over 240ms). V2: Settings tab at `index.html?start=worker`; the own-key route from AI credits highlights and also turns the key on (index.html:740).
- **Props:** `id`, `title`, `description`, `action`, `highlight` (truthy change = scroll into view + highlight), `className` (`is-highlighted` forces the look), `children`.
- **Motion:** Scroll smooth, instant under reduced motion; edge and ring 240ms.
- **Accessibility:** `<section aria-labelledby>` on its h2, so each block is a landmark-like group; highlight is visual only and never moves focus or changes a value.
- **Responsive:** Stacks with 12 between sections; text wraps, controls stay right.
- **Do / Don't:** Do group by what the partner thinks about and apply on change with a quiet toast; a Save button only for a multi-field form. Don't add a shadow or a second padding. Don't change a setting by routing to it.

### AdvancedDisclosure (Changed from V2: V2 has no grouping)
- **Purpose:** The one place developer controls live: AI mode, your own key, the chat model (one picker).
- **Anatomy:** Full-width button row: surface, 1px line, radius 16, min 44, padding 12 16; "Advanced" 14/500 ink + "AI mode, your own key and the chat model. Most partners never need these." 12 ink-3; chevron-down 16 ink-3 that turns 180° open. Body: the panels, 12 apart.
- **Variants:** One.
- **Sizes:** Full width.
- **States:** Closed; hover (edge firms + wash); focus-visible; active; open. Forced with `.is-hover`, `.is-focus`, `.is-active`.
- **Props:** `open` + `onToggle(next)` (controlled) or `defaultOpen`; `label`, `description`, `className`, `children`.
- **Motion:** Chevron 180ms; the body appears without animation.
- **Accessibility:** Real button, aria-expanded, aria-controls on a body that stays in the DOM with `hidden`.
- **Responsive:** Text wraps; chevron stays right.
- **Do / Don't:** Do keep it closed by default. Don't put anything a partner needs for the worker inside it.

### ByokPanel (Changed from V2)
- **Purpose:** Run BlueAI on your own provider key so AI credits are not used.
- **Anatomy:** SettingsSection "Bring your own key" + V2's description (with "no AI credits used"); `V2Kit.Switch` row "Use my own key" / "Turning this off keeps your saved key."; then one block: no key (note "No API key configured. Add one to get started." + Select "Provider", placeholder "Add a provider"); entry (Select, TextField "API key" secret mono with the provider hint as helper, "Your key is stored encrypted on this device only." with lock 16, Cancel + Check and save); saved (provider 14/500, "•••• qAAA" mono 13 ink-3, Remove secondary sm, Select "Model", note "No AI credits are used while this is on.").
- **Variants:** Off; off with a key kept; on.
- **Sizes:** Full width.
- **States:** Off; off, key kept; on, no key; provider picked; validating (the button keeps "Check and save" with its loading spinner, field read-only, helper "Checking your key with Anthropic…"); invalid key (field error "That key didn’t work. Check it and paste it again."); provider unreachable (danger Banner "Couldn’t reach Anthropic" + "Your key wasn’t saved.", action Try again); saved; remove confirm ("Remove your Anthropic key? BlueAI goes back to using your AI credits." Keep key / Remove key danger); removed (no-key state + Toast "Anthropic key removed" with Undo). V2: Settings tab, Preview row "Own key: On"; V2 has no validating, invalid, unreachable or confirm states, and its Validate & Save makes up a key when empty (byok_settings.jsx:54).
- **Props:** `on`, `keyInfo` {provider, model, last4 | key}, `providers` (default V2's five), `status` ('idle' | 'validating' | 'invalid' | 'unreachable' | 'confirm-remove'), `draft` {provider, key}, `highlight`, `onToggle(next)`, `onSave({provider, key, model})`, `onCancel`, `onRemove(keyInfo)`, `onModel(model)`.
- **Motion:** Switch 180ms; nothing else.
- **Accessibility:** role=switch with a visible label; the key is a password field with a reveal button; Check and save stays focusable when disabled and says why ("Paste your key first"); Remove asks before it acts.
- **Responsive:** Actions wrap right-aligned.
- **Do / Don't:** Do let the switch only enable. Do confirm Remove and offer Undo. Don't show the key in clear text. Don't report success without a check.

**Proposed copy:** "Use my own key", "Turning this off keeps your saved key.", "Check and save", "Checking your key with {provider}…", "That key didn’t work. Check it and paste it again.", "Couldn’t reach {provider}", "Your key wasn’t saved.", "Paste your key first", "Remove your {provider} key? BlueAI goes back to using your AI credits.", "Keep key", "Remove key", "{provider} key removed", "No AI credits are used while this is on." (adapted from index.html:485), "Your key is saved but not in use, so AI credits are used.". Kept from V2: the description (with "no AI credits used" for "no BlueAI credits consumed"), "No API key configured. Add one to get started.", "Add a provider", the five providers and hints, "Your key is stored encrypted on this device only.".

### AiModePanel (Changed from V2)
- **Purpose:** Choose where AI runs: Cloud (default) or Hybrid on the partner's PC, so tasks use fewer AI credits.
- **Anatomy:** SettingsSection "AI mode" / "Choose where AI processing happens."; `V2Kit.RadioCard` Cloud ("Default. Uses cloud AI.") and Hybrid ("Runs on your PC and uses fewer AI credits."), Hybrid pending while picked but not active; body by status: pending text + "Additional files of 3 to 4 GB are required to enable Hybrid mode." 13/500 ink + Download and turn on (primary sm, download icon); ProgressBar size 6 ("Downloading Hybrid AI…" 42%, "Installing Hybrid AI…" sliding) + "Cloud mode is still available." + Cancel download (quiet); "Hybrid status" row + Badge; Banner for conflict (`offline` tone, the Banner's neutral one, with the `alert` icon) or failure (danger) with Try again as its action; a link "Use my own local model instead" (endpoint + model name TextFields, Apply).
- **Variants:** method auto (BlueAI's own model, default) or custom (your local model endpoint).
- **Sizes:** Full width.
- **States:** Cloud; Hybrid pending; downloading; installing; on; on with another local model running; download failed; couldn't start; paused; hardware not supported (Hybrid disabled with its reason "This PC doesn’t meet the requirements for Hybrid. Cloud mode will be used."); own local model. V2: Settings tab, Hybrid then Auto (modal) for the happy path; Preview rows "Hardware: No" and "AI conflict: Detected"; nothing in V2 sets download failed, couldn't start or paused (ai_mode.jsx:300).
- **Props:** `mode`, `method`, `status` (V2's lifecycle keys), `hwSupported`, `conflict`, `pct`, `endpoint`, `model`, `onPick(mode)`, `onMethod(method)`, `onDownload`, `onCancel`, `onRetry`, `onEndpoint`, `onModel`, `onApply`.
- **Motion:** ProgressBar's own (fill 240ms, sliding segment; still under reduced motion).
- **Accessibility:** Native radios (arrow keys); the disabled card states its reason in text; progress is role=progressbar with value text; failure banners are role=alert.
- **Responsive:** Radio cards sit side by side; the body stacks below.
- **Do / Don't:** Do default to BlueAI's model and state the download size before the button. Do say "AI credits", never "tokens". Don't open a second dialog to set up Hybrid. Don't say Retry: say Try again.

**Proposed copy:** "Runs on your PC and uses fewer AI credits.", "You’re still on Cloud until Hybrid is set up.", "Download and turn on", "Hybrid mode uses your PC (your local model) for supported tasks, so they use fewer AI credits.", "Use my own local model instead", "Use BlueAI’s model instead", "Model endpoint", "Model name", "Enter the endpoint and model name first". Kept from V2: "Choose where AI processing happens.", "Default. Uses cloud AI.", "Setup needed", the 3 to 4 GB line, "Downloading Hybrid AI…", "Installing Hybrid AI…", "Cloud mode is still available.", "Cancel download", the conflict, failure and hardware lines, the status labels On, Download failed, Couldn't start, Paused.

# Patterns

## 50 Loading

**Status:** Changed from V2.

**Why:** V2 stages its cold start (splash until 2650 ms, then an 850 ms skeleton, `boot_splash.jsx:31-33`, `index.html:745`), but the skeleton is the retired category list, the splash replays on every window open, and no button has a loading state, so Agree and continue, I agree, turn on, Validate & Save and Save settings can each be pressed twice while their work is in flight. A partner waiting on a consent or a save must see that it is happening, and fast work must never flash.

### Loading by duration (Changed from V2)

- **Purpose:** what the partner sees while waiting, chosen by how long the wait is.
- **Anatomy:** no new part. The rule picks between the kit's Spinner, Skeleton, Button `loading`, Banner and the run's own cards.
- **Variants (by wait):**
  - under 300 ms: nothing. Spinner waits 300 ms before it appears and holds its space meanwhile.
  - 300 ms to 2 s: a Spinner 16 inside the control that started it (label kept, clicks ignored, `aria-busy`), or a Skeleton composite in the shape of what arrives (the worker home: a 40 circle and two lines in a Card, then a Card of three lines).
  - over 2 s: say what is happening in words beside the spinner ("Checking that this account is in the program.", proposed copy).
  - over 10 s: say it is still going and give the way out: keep going in the background, or Cancel (Banner info: "Still checking your key" / "This is taking longer than usual." + Cancel, proposed copy).
  - a run (minutes): never block. The RunCard, the WorkerCard and the top bar's WorkerStatusChip carry it; the partner can leave.
- **Sizes:** Spinner 16 in buttons and inline, 32 alone (sign-in). Skeletons take the size of the content they stand for.
- **States:** cold start (Splash, section 33, true cold start only), screen (skeleton), action (button loading), background run.
- **Props:** none of its own. Use `Button loading`, `Spinner {size, delay, label}`, `Skeleton {shape, width, height, lines}`. A list screen's default loading block is `V2Screens.ListSkeleton {rows, label}` (Activity, Run detail, Notifications, AI credits), and Home's is `V2Screens.HomeSkeleton`, both in `screens/home.jsx` with their styles in `screens/core.css`. When the data does not arrive, the same screens show `V2Screens.LoadError` ("Couldn’t load <what>" / "Nothing is lost: your runs and days are not affected. Try again in a moment." + Try again, section 51).
- **Motion:** spinner `--v2-dur-spin` 800 ms linear; skeleton shimmer `--v2-dur-shimmer` 1400 ms. Under reduced motion the shimmer stops and the spinner turns at half speed.
- **Accessibility:** a loading button keeps its name and sets `aria-busy`; a standalone spinner with a label is `role=status`; skeletons are `aria-hidden`; a waiting caveat is ink-3 (6.0:1), not V2's `#94a3b8` (2.56:1, `chat_bubbles.jsx:124`).
- **Responsive:** skeletons follow the column width; nothing is fixed to 419.
- **Do / Don't:** Do keep the label in a loading button; draw skeletons in the shape of what replaces them; say what is happening after 2 s. Don't flash a spinner for work under 300 ms; don't block the window while a run plays; don't let a consent or save be pressed twice.

## 51 Errors and offline

**Status:** Changed from V2 (error scopes); New (offline).

**Why:** V2 designed only its scripted failures: the run that fails (`worker_flow.jsx:132`, red bubble `chat_bubbles.jsx:88`, then Retry and Not now) and three AI-mode banners (`ai_mode.jsx:169, 205`). A failed save, a rejected field, a screen that cannot load, a lost connection and a service outage have no place and no words. Nothing checks connectivity, and a scheduled run that silently fails to start costs a counted day.

### Error scopes (Changed from V2)

- **Purpose:** an error goes where its scope is; every message says what happened, what it means for today, and the one next step.
- **Anatomy (scope: where it goes):**
  1. Field: message under the field (TextField `error`), red border plus 3px ring. Never a toast.
  2. Action: Toast error with Try again (assertive; with its action it stays until Dismiss, Try again, the next toast, or the screen calls `dismiss()` on leaving, section 24).
  3. Section: Banner danger at the top of the section (V2's AI-mode banners, kept word for word).
  4. Run: RunCard failed and WorkerCard failed, with the deadline and Try again ("Today’s job didn’t finish. Try again by 9 pm to count the day.").
  5. Screen: EmptyState error in the content band; top bar and nav stay (SystemStates `error`). A list screen that failed to load uses `V2Screens.LoadError {what, onRetry, busy}` ("Couldn’t load your notifications" / "Nothing is lost: your runs and days are not affected. Try again in a moment." + Try again), as Activity, Run detail, Notifications and AI credits do; Settings, Worker settings, Connected apps and Account reach it through `V2Screens.pendingBody`.
  6. App: offline banner under the top bar (below); a full state only when nothing is cached.
- **Variants:** the six scopes.
- **Sizes:** inherits each part's size.
- **States:** shown, retrying (the button loads), recovered (the message goes; a toast only if the partner acted).
- **Props:** none of its own; see TextField, Toast, Banner, RunCard, EmptyState.
- **Motion:** inherits each part (toast rise 180 ms; banner and card appear in place).
- **Accessibility:** field messages and danger banners are `role=alert`; toasts announce through AppFrame's one ToastRegion (an error in its assertive region, section 24); error text is danger ink 5.36:1 or ink on a tint.
- **Responsive:** fits the column at any width.
- **Do / Don't:** Do say what it means for today's run or the 20 days; name the next step for what it does (Try again, Switch account); keep the partner's input. Don't show codes; don't use a toast for a field or a full screen for a section; don't stop at "Something went wrong". Use Try again everywhere (V2 also says Retry, `worker_flow.jsx:127`, PM copy, flagged).

### Offline (New)

- **Purpose:** losing the connection shows at once, says what will not happen today, and recovers by itself.
- **Anatomy:** Banner tone `offline` (neutral fill, wifi-off icon, ink words) pinned under the top bar, on the gutter, outside the content band, so it never scrolls and needs no layer. `V2Screens.ScreenFrame` draws it, in the top bar slot it hands AppFrame, whenever the record says `online: false`, on every signed-in screen but Home, which passes `offlineBanner={false}` because its WorkerCard says it, so no screen builds its own; the top bar's WorkerStatusChip says `offline`. In Chat the Composer locks with the neutral placeholder every locked composer uses, `disabledReason` "You can’t send messages right now" (section 38), and the offline ComposerNotice above it says why and until when (tone `neutral`, icon wifi-off, "You’re offline. You can send messages once you’re back online."). On Home the WorkerCard `offline` says it instead, and its Try again reconnects in place (Home `onReconnect`, `screens/home.jsx`).
- **Variants (proposed copy):** offline: "You’re offline" / "Today’s run starts when you’re back online." + Try again; in Chat, the composer notice above. Reconnecting: "Reconnecting…". Back online: Toast success "You’re back online." Service down: "BlueAI can’t reach its service" / "This is on our side, not your PC. Today’s run starts as soon as it’s back."
- **Sizes:** full column width.
- **States:** offline, reconnecting (checks every 30 s and at once on Try again), back online, service down.
- **Props:** none on a screen: ScreenFrame reads `worker.online`, takes `onReconnect` for the banner's Try again (without it the banner has no action and the 30 s check still runs) and `offlineBanner` (default true; Home passes false). The Banner it draws is `{tone: 'offline', title, action}`; Chat passes the Composer `disabledReason` and the notice.
- **Motion:** the banner appears in place; the recovery toast rises 180 ms.
- **Accessibility:** banner `role=status`; the composer's placeholder is the neutral reason and the notice says the cause, so the field is disabled, not hidden, and described by the notice.
- **Responsive:** the banner stays pinned while the content band scrolls (compact 640 too).
- **Do / Don't:** Do show it as soon as it is known and keep it until it ends; start the missed run when the connection returns. Don't blank the window; don't ask the partner to restart BlueAI; don't blame the partner's connection for a service outage.

## 52 Confirm, destroy, undo

**Status:** Changed from V2.

**Why:** V2's one sign-out confirm says "Logout" over a body that says sign out, paints a non-destructive action red (`profile.jsx:75-79`), and its confirm does not sign out (`index.html:1010`). The actions that do lose something never ask: switching your own key off deletes the key (`byok_settings.jsx:64`), Remove and Unlink act at once (`byok_settings.jsx:74`, `settings.jsx:59`), and "Not now" on a paid job ends the day as not counted in one tap, the cost told only afterwards (`worker_flow.jsx:121, 149`, `worker_day_done.jsx:95`).

### Rules (Changed from V2)

- **Purpose:** decide, by what an action costs, whether to confirm, say the cost on the control, or act and offer Undo. One rule for every destructive action, so a new one has one answer.
- **Anatomy (the decision table):**
  1. Can cost today's day or future days (skip today's job, skip today's run, stop a run, quit, pause daily runs, disconnect YouTube): say the cost before the choice, and ask once. Either the control carries the cost and one tap acts (the job card's "Skip: today won’t count"), or the control opens one confirm that names it ("Stop now? Today counts only if you run again by 9 pm.", "Skip today’s run?"); never a second question, never the cost told afterwards. No Undo: a day cannot be given back by a toast.
  2. Costs account control, AI credits or money: sign out, disconnect YouTube and remove my own key (BlueAI goes back to the partner's AI credits, and the key must be pasted again) use an AlertDialog; turning on Automatic uses the ConsentDialog (section 46: Dialog `consent` variant, md, with Close, a scrim click does nothing, the primary is the agreement "I agree, turn on"). Either way the title is the question with the object, the body says what changes and how to undo it, and the primary names the action.
  3. Cannot be undone (delete my data, leave the program): AlertDialog with a danger primary named for the action.
  4. Costs no day, no money and no control (disconnect Telegram, dismiss a notification): do it at once, no question, then a Toast with Undo.
  5. A switch only turns something on or off; it never deletes, signs out or costs a day as a side effect (a switch that would cost days, Daily runs, opens rule 1's confirm first, section 62).
  - Undo: any change the system can restore while its toast is up gets Undo in that toast, asked first or not (a removed key, rule 2; a disconnected Telegram, rule 4). A toast with Undo stays until Dismiss, Undo, the next toast, or the screen calls `dismiss()` on leaving (section 24); commit the change then.
  - Red: danger is for the confirm's primary when something is lost (a day, an account link, a key, data, the program), and for a trigger that opens that confirm (Disconnect YouTube, Account's Leave the program and Delete my data). An action that costs nothing is never red (section 11).
- **Variants:** confirm first; cost on the control; act then Undo.
- **Sizes:** AlertDialog sm (320); Toast as wide as the window minus the gutter.
- **States:** confirm, busy (primary loading; Esc, scrim and Cancel held), done (toast), undone (toast "Key restored.").
- **Props:** `Dialog {variant: 'alert', title, description, actions, busy}`; inside a screen, `useScreenToast().show({tone, title, action: {label: 'Undo', onClick}})` under `V2Screens.ToastScope` (a toast with an action has no timer, section 24). The screens' confirm helpers, on `window.V2Screens` from `screens/settings.jsx` (`:25-31`, header):
  - `useConfirmStep(forced) -> {busy, failed, run(done), reset()}`: the primary's work. `run(done)` sets busy, then calls `done()` once the work lands (failed keeps the dialog); `reset()` clears it on Cancel. `forced` 'busy' | 'failed' draws that cell in the guide.
  - `ConfirmDialog {copy, tone, step, preview, onCancel, onConfirm}`: the AlertDialog for one `CONFIRM_COPY` entry. `copy` is the entry, `tone` the primary's variant ('danger' when something is lost, else primary), `step` a `useConfirmStep()`; busy holds Esc, the scrim and Cancel, and a failure keeps the dialog, shows a danger Banner with `copy.failed` and turns the primary into Try again.
  - `pendingBody(state, what, onRetry)`: the account screens' 'loading' (ListSkeleton) and 'error' (LoadError with Try again) bodies, or null when the screen has its data (section 50).
- **Motion:** dialog scale-in 320 ms, leaves 240 ms ease-in; toast rises 180 ms.
- **Accessibility:** `role=alertdialog`, focus on Cancel (the first action, the safe answer; section 09), focus back to the opener on close; Undo is a real button; a toast that carries it never times out, so it can be reached at any pace (WCAG 2.2.1).
- **Responsive:** dialogs centre in the window, compact 640 included.
- **Copy:** each destructive confirm's strings have one owner, `V2Screens.CONFIRM_COPY` in `screens/settings.jsx` (`signOut(email)`, `disconnect(handle)`, `leave(handle, endsOn, n)`, `deleteData(n)`), and this section and the screens read them from it. Each entry also carries `failed`, its failure title. The busy and failed states come from `V2Screens.useConfirmStep` + `ConfirmDialog` (`screens/settings.jsx`), used by Account and Connected apps. Worker settings' pause dialog is not a `CONFIRM_COPY` entry: it uses `useConfirmStep` with its own `K.Dialog` `variant="task"` ("Pause daily runs?", Keep running / Pause, with an until picker; `screens/worker-settings.jsx:81, 164-165`). Sign out, section 64 ("Sign out of BlueAI?", Cancel / Sign out, primary not danger); disconnect YouTube, section 63 ("Disconnect YouTube?", Cancel / Disconnect, danger); delete my data and leave the program, on Account (section 64) as danger AlertDialogs. Removing your own key: section 49 ("Remove your {provider} key?" with Keep key / Remove key, then "{provider} key removed" + Undo). Proposed copy, the PM to approve.
- **Do / Don't:** Do name the primary for what it does; say what is kept as well as what is lost; prefer Undo to a question when the action is cheap. Don't use "Are you sure?" as the whole message; don't paint an action that costs nothing red; don't offer Undo for a lost day; don't let a switch delete data.

## 53 Consent

**Status:** Changed from V2.

**Why:** V2's consent moments are right: the agreement is the button (`agent_permissions.jsx:178`; V2 adopted the minimal consent dialog on 2026-10-14 (a plain frame with no header, divider or X: title, one sentence, a lighter second line, Cancel / I agree, turn on)), Automatic is asked every time it is turned on (`agent_permissions.jsx:28`), contracts are agreed and notices acknowledged (`terms_sheet.jsx:49-53`), and every agreement is meant to be recorded server-side with the exact copy shown (`terms_sheet.jsx:23-27`). What V2 lacks is a place to see and withdraw them: only Automatic has one, Settings' Worker card since 2026-10-14 (`settings.jsx:11-20`); watch history is read without asking (`worker_flow.jsx:80`), and the consent buttons have no loading state although each one is a server write.

### Consent rules (Changed from V2)

- **Purpose:** every agreement is asked at the moment it matters, recorded with what was shown, and can be seen and withdrawn later.
- **Anatomy (moment: how agreed; recorded; seen and withdrawn):**
  1. Program Terms: blocking Sheet on first run (`V2Screens.TermsSheet`); the button is the agreement ("Agree and continue"); re-asked in full on every terms change. Records account ID, time, terms version, IP and the exact copy, server-side. Seen under Account and privacy > Agreements; withdrawing means leaving the program, from the same place.
  2. Automatic, per app: the kit ConsentDialog ("Run YouTube jobs automatically?" / "I agree, turn on"), asked every time it is turned on. Its body is proposed copy for the PM and legal (R51, section 46); whichever wording ships, the record keeps the version shown. Records app, time and copy version, server-side (V2 keeps only a time in localStorage, `agent_permissions.jsx:85`). Changed back in Settings > Worker or Connected apps, at once and without a question.
  3. Access to watch history: the kit PermissionRequest in setup, Allow or Not now (the worker still runs on Not now). Turned off in Connected apps > YouTube > Watch history.
  4. Windows notifications: Windows asks the first time; two switches in Settings > Notifications (section 61) turn off the job-waiting and run-ends notifications; section 54 lists which events each covers and which always notify.
- **Telegram:** a Telegram command never gives or takes back consent. Turning on Automatic, disconnecting an account and leaving the program happen only in the app, where the exact copy is shown and recorded; Telegram can only point the partner there.
- **Wording rules:** agree to contracts, acknowledge notices (the Privacy Policy and Cookie Policy are never agreed to); no checkbox before the button; say what changes before the button and where to turn it off; cookie consent is the site banner's job, never part of the terms.
- **The terms sheet:** kit Sheet `blocking`, the dim veil `--v2-scrim`, radius 16 top, title 20/600 ink, the consent line 14 ink-2 with underlined ink links (new tab, announced), one primary lg full button "Agree and continue". Copy is legal's and the PM's, verbatim (`terms_sheet.jsx:61-84`).
- **Variants:** firstrun ("Before you start"); update: names the changed documents from `docs` (data, not fixed copy; default 'Terms of Use', 'Privacy Policy', the names the inbox row carries), titled "Updates to our <first doc>".
- **Sizes:** Sheet full window width; ConsentDialog md (382).
- **States:** not asked; agreeing (the button loads while the record is written); not saved (Banner danger "Your agreement wasn’t saved." / "Check your connection, then press Agree and continue again.", proposed copy); agreed (a row under Agreements, for example "Program Terms, agreed Oct 6, version Sep 14"); declined; withdrawn; re-asked after a terms change. Declined, for the terms (update mode only): a quiet "Not now" beside "Agree and continue", its cost said first: "Not now pauses your worker until you accept, and paused days don’t count. You can still see Earnings or leave the program." (proposed copy). Declining closes the sheet and pauses the worker (days don't count); the terms are asked again on the next open and from the inbox row; Account > Leave the program and Earnings stay reachable.
- **Props:** `TermsSheet {mode, docs, open, busy, error, onAgree, onDecline, preview}`; `ConsentDialog {app, open, busy, onAgree, onCancel}`; `PermissionRequest {app, state, decidedOn, onAllow, onNotNow}`.
- **Motion:** the sheet rises in 320 ms (V2 450 ms) and leaves in 240 ms ease-in; nothing moves under reduced motion.
- **Accessibility:** sheet and dialogs are modal and keep focus inside; the terms sheet has no close, no Esc and no scrim dismiss, by design; each link is announced "(opens in a new tab)"; the button is 44 tall with a 4.92:1 label (V2 3.24:1).
- **Responsive:** on a compact window the sheet body scrolls and the button stays visible.
- **Do / Don't:** Do keep consent copy word for word once the owner approves it (a proposed change goes to the PM and legal first, section 70); record what was shown at the click; give every agreement a place to be seen and turned off. Don't add a checkbox before the button; don't ask once per lifetime for Automatic; don't bundle cookie consent into the terms.

## 54 Notifications

**Status:** Changed from V2.

**Why:** V2 has good pointers with nothing to point at. The Windows notification leaves after 8 s and is the only record (`desktop_toast.jsx:12`); a click opens a cold-started home with no Retry (`index.html:1065`); and the most urgent moment, a paid job waiting for an Always ask partner, raises only an amber taskbar flash after 4 s (`chat_product.jsx:97`, `product-scene.css:141-142`), with no notification and no deadline, so the job waits forever and the day is lost unseen.

### Channel map (Changed from V2)

- **Purpose:** reach a partner who is not watching, by urgency, with one record for everything.
- **Anatomy (channels):**
  1. Windows notification: a pointer for a partner not looking at BlueAI. A click opens the entry's own place (for a waiting job, the job ask in Chat, Chat waiting, the same destination section 60 names; the run card with Try again; Activity; Earnings), never a cold home. It stays in the Windows action centre.
  2. Taskbar attention: a finite flash while something waits for an answer (job waiting, run failed).
  3. Notifications, the inbox (section 60): the record. Every event lands here, read or not. Pushed from the top bar's More menu.
  4. Badge: on Activity, the count of what needs the partner today (a waiting job; a run that failed, was stopped, didn't start or can't start) plus the latest day not counted. Never on Home.
  5. In-app toast: confirms what the partner just did in this window. Never an event.
- **Events (Windows title; when it expires; the Settings switch that turns its Windows notification off, section 61):**
  - Day done: "Today’s run is done." (V2, DRAFT); never; "Today’s run ends".
  - No job today: "Your run counts today." (V2); never; "Today’s run ends".
  - Job waiting (Always ask): "A paid YouTube job is waiting for your OK. Answer by 9 pm."; at 9 pm, the day's deadline (section 41), which closes the run with outcome `expired` and the day with status `notcounted` (a separate outcome from `skipped`, which means the partner declined; `kit/worker-data.jsx:145, 245`); "A paid job needs you". Also flashes the taskbar.
  - Run failed: "Today’s job didn’t finish." (V2); at 9 pm; "Today’s run ends". Also flashes the taskbar.
  - Run didn't start (missed: the PC was off or asleep at run time), raised when the PC wakes: "Today’s run didn’t start. Run it by 9 pm to count today."; at 9 pm; always on (it costs a day). A late start never begins on its own (section 42).
  - Run can't start (blocked: no YouTube, App Player signed out or no BlueStacks, `blockedBy`, section 41): "Today’s run can’t start: <reason>. Fix it by 9 pm.", the reason in the WorkerCard's words (section 42); at 9 pm; always on.
  - Out of credits for today's run (`creditsDay` 'stopped': the refill lands after 9 pm): "Not enough AI credits for today’s run. They refresh at 5:30 am, so today won’t count."; at 9 pm; always on. When the refill lands before 9 pm ('waiting') there is no event: the run starts once they are back.
  - Day verified: inbox only, so no switch.
  - Day not counted: "Oct 12 wasn’t counted. See why."; always on (a lost day).
  - Program ending: "3 days left in this program."; always on.
  - Cash out open, only when the period has ended and the site says so (`cashOutOpen` true, section 41): "Cash out is open on Earnings."; always on.
  - Cash out, under $10 (the period has ended, the minimum not reached): the inbox row says "This program has ended. You need $10 to cash out. Your balance carries over to the next period." (the last two sentences are the site's, `onblue/apply.html:2292-2293`); inbox only, no Windows notification. Never tell a partner under the minimum that cash out is open.
  - Terms updated: inbox only; the terms sheet asks on the next open.
  Titles not marked V2 are proposed copy. The two switches only silence the Windows notification; every event still lands in the inbox. Events marked always on cost or pay the partner, so no switch hides them. Each event has its inbox kind, glyph, tone and target in section 60's kind table. An event that expires at 9 pm turns its inbox row into its result then (section 60).
- **Telegram:** not a notification channel. A linked Telegram (section 63) carries the partner's commands to BlueAI; it does not carry these events, and nothing here waits on it. Whether it should carry the day-losing events (job waiting, run didn't start, run can't start) to a partner away from the PC is a product call (section 76).
- **Variants:** pointer (Windows, taskbar), record (inbox row), count (badge), confirmation (toast).
- **Sizes:** inbox rows are section 60's rows (it owns their anatomy); the badge is the BottomNav badge.
- **States:** as section 60 draws them: unread (blue dot, 600 title, counted), read, actioned (once the ask is answered its row says what was chosen, 'You took the paid job' / 'Day 7 of 20, pending verification.' or 'You skipped the paid job' / 'Today didn’t count toward your 20 days.', and opens that run), expired (the waiting row turns into its result, 'Paid job expired' / 'No answer by 9 pm, so today didn’t count.', and still opens the run).
- **Props:** none of its own. The inbox is `V2Screens.Notifications {items, state}`; the count is `BottomNav items[].badge`.
- **Motion:** Windows draws its own notification; the taskbar flash is finite (seven times, then it holds); nothing in the app loops.
- **Accessibility:** unread rows are announced as unread first; the badge is read as ", 2 new"; a Windows notification makes sense without opening the app.
- **Responsive:** as section 60: a row wraps its body (one short sentence) and never truncates it; at 640 only the list scrolls.
- **Do / Don't:** Do put every event in Notifications; give anything that waits a deadline and its cost; open the place the notification is about. Don't make the Windows notification the only record; don't flash the taskbar without saying why anywhere; don't use an in-app toast for something the worker did while the partner was away.

## 55 Settings model

**Status:** Changed from V2.

**Why:** V2's Settings is one stack of five cards with no groups (`settings.jsx:144-155`): BlueStacks first, claiming "Connected" even when it is not installed (`settings.jsx:31`), then Telegram, AI mode, your own key, and Chat AI Settings with Max Output Tokens, Max Steps and a timeout (`settings.jsx:132-134`). A Save button with a 2.5 s success line (`settings.jsx:93, 136`) sits next to switches that apply at once. A partner looking for the worker's controls ("pause, stop, or disconnect anytime") finds none.

### Grouping and save model (Changed from V2)

- **Purpose:** group settings by what the partner thinks about, worker first and developer controls last, with one rule for when a change applies.
- **Anatomy (groups, in this order):**
  1. Worker: Always ask or Automatic per app, run window, pause until, start with Windows, interest profile.
  2. Connected apps: YouTube (account, watch history, Disconnect), Instagram (coming soon, no switch), Telegram.
  3. Notifications: two switches for Windows notifications, "A paid job needs you" and "Today’s run ends" (section 61); section 54 maps each event to its switch or to always on.
  4. BlueStacks: App Player status from the real check (not installed, signed out, connected as).
  5. Account and privacy: the signed-in account, the program, agreements, what BlueAI keeps, Sign out.
  6. Help: FAQ (opens the site), Report a problem.
  7. Advanced, closed by default: AI mode, your own key, chat model.
  Each group is a SettingsSection (card radius 16, title 16/600, ListRows full-bleed); Worker and Connected apps push their own screens (sections 62, 63).
- **Variants (the save model):**
  - Apply on change, for a switch, segmented control or select: the change applies at once; a quiet Toast says what changed ("BlueAI starts with Windows.", proposed copy); on failure the control goes back and a Toast error offers Try again.
  - Save, only for a form whose fields make sense together (your own key: provider and key; Report a problem): Save is disabled with a reason until something changes ("No changes yet"), loads while it saves, ends in a Toast ("Key saved."), shows errors on the field and keeps the input; leaving with unsaved changes asks first.
- **Sizes:** Settings fills the content band; sections are full column width.
- **States:** clean, dirty, saving, saved, error (both models).
- **Props:** see `V2Screens.Settings {worker, state, highlight}`, `SettingsSection {title, description, action, highlight}`, `AdvancedDisclosure {open, onToggle}`.
- **Motion:** a routed section scrolls into view and holds the highlight for 1900 ms (V2's timing, `byok_settings.jsx:47`); instant under reduced motion.
- **Accessibility:** each section is a labelled region; Advanced is one button with `aria-expanded`; a switch has role switch and a visible label; the disabled Save keeps focus and its reason is linked with `aria-describedby`.
- **Responsive:** the stack scrolls inside the content band; nothing is side by side.
- **Do / Don't:** Do put the worker's controls first; apply a single control at once and say so quietly; keep Save for forms whose fields belong together. Don't mix apply-on-change and Save on one card; don't show developer controls by default; don't show a status the app has not checked.

## 56 Sign-in flow

**Status:** Changed from V2.

**Why:** The handoff logic is the PM's (ROB-21388, `signin_page.jsx:5-17`, `index.html:211-224`) and it is sound: the download link only reaches accepted partners, so the only failure is the app running on another account. Two stops cost a click and say nothing new: the success step asks for Continue and greets a first sign-in with "Welcome back!" (`login.jsx:270-275`), and the browser step offers two controls with one job (`login.jsx:242-247`). Two site lines need the PM and legal (below).

### Sign-in cases (Changed from V2)

- **Purpose:** sign the partner in with the account they applied with, stopping them only when the account is wrong.
- **Anatomy (the five cases):**
  1. App Player signed in with the accepted account: silent sign-in, Home. No screen.
  2. App Player signed in with another account: silent sign-in, then the gate. The browser never opens.
  3a. App Player signed out, the browser holds the accepted account: the site's continue page, then in the app Signing in, You're signed in (900 ms, no click), Home.
  3b. App Player signed out, the browser holds another account: the continue page says "Not in the program" with "Not you? Continue with a different account" (V2 says "ID"); continuing anyway lands on the gate.
  3c. App Player signed out, the browser signed out: the site's sign-in with Google's chooser, then Signing in, then Home or the gate.
  Whatever account BlueAI ends up with, App Player ends up on it too.
- **Variants (decision D5):** option A, Check account (recommended): interrupt only when the account is wrong. Option B, Continue as: whenever an account is already signed in, ask "Continue as Alex" first. Ship one and delete the other path.
- **Sizes:** the in-app steps are section 68 (Dialog md on the frost gate).
- **States:** Welcome, browser, signing, success, gate, confirm (option B), Home.
- **Props:** `V2Screens.SignIn {step, from, account, onOpenBrowser, onCancel, onSwitch, onContinue, onDone}`.
- **Motion:** success moves on after 900 ms (a timer, kept under reduced motion); every step change is in place, no slide.
- **Accessibility:** each step is announced (a polite live region inside the dialog); the gate and confirm steps block (no close, no Esc) and have one way out.
- **Responsive:** the gate dialog centres in the window, compact 640 included.
- **Family:** the frost veil is the site's sign-in gate veil (`onblue/onblue.css:411-412`), the only frosted surface in the product; Sign in and Sign out on both sides.
- **For the PM and legal (site copy):** the site's gate tells accepted partners "Sign in to start your application." (`site_signin.jsx:27`); the app variant should read "Sign in to continue to BlueAI." Its legal line agrees to the Privacy Policy (`site_signin.jsx:42`), wording legal rejected for the app's terms sheet; align it with "agree to the Program Terms and acknowledge the Privacy Policy and Cookie Policy".
- **Do / Don't:** Do stop the partner only when the account is wrong; say that App Player follows the account before Switch account; use Sign in and Sign out on both sides. Don't confirm a sign-in the browser already confirmed; don't offer Apply or other exits on the gate; don't use a different veil for signing in on the site and in the app.

# Screens

## 57 Home (Today)

**Status:** Changed from V2 (Home and the Chat screen reached from it).

**Why:** the partner opens BlueAI to check one thing: is my worker on, did today happen, did the days count, is anything waiting for me. V2's home is the chat's empty state: 'Hi, Alex' with an emoji and "You're all set. The BlueAI worker is all it takes to start earning." above a coach that says 'Run this to begin' (`chat_product.jsx:204-213`); the progress strip shows only for a returning partner (`moneymaker_home.jsx:120`) and counts from a constant (`worker_day_done.jsx:39`); the next run time is typed into the copy. The chat itself unmounts on any other tab (`index.html:975`), taking a waiting job or a Retry with it.

### Home

- **Purpose:** today at a glance, answered from the Worker record (section 41), with the one action that matters now.
- **Anatomy:** inside `ScreenFrame` (AppFrame + TopBar + BottomNav, Home lit). ScreenHeader as the greeting: 'Hi, Alex' (the account's first name; 'Today' when there is none; no emoji, no second line). Then `.v2-screen-body` (padding 16 16 24, blocks 12 apart, the only part that scrolls): WorkerCard (section 42), ProgramStrip (section 43), a Banner only for a program fact the card cannot say, and one standalone Link 'Chat with BlueAI' (13/500, 32 target, left aligned). The top bar's run chip and credits come from `topBarProps(record)`, which maps the record to section 30's RUN_STATUS: 'Next run 11 am', 'Next run 9 am to 8 pm' (a run window), 'Runs later today' (Any time), 'Running', 'Needs you', 'Day 7 of 20' (the blue pending ring until verified; green only for verified and complete), 'Not counted today', 'Didn’t finish', 'Stopped', 'Didn’t start', 'Can’t run', 'Paused', 'Offline', 'Waiting for credits' (only while the refill lands before 9 pm; when it lands after, `creditsDay` 'stopped', the chip says 'Not counted today'), 'Period ended', 'All 20 days done' (section 30's table). The credits chip warns only until today's run is done. Activity carries the section 54 badge: a waiting job, a failed, stopped, missed or blocked run, and the latest day not counted (rejected); never Home.
- **Variants:** one screen; the content changes with the record. The only Banner: info 'You can still reach 17 of 20 days', a title with no body, only when `numbers().outOfReach` > 0 (proposed; the loss is said once, by the DayBar legend; no money until decision D3).
- **Actions:** Run opens setup in Chat for a new partner (Chat 'setup') and starts today's run otherwise (Chat 'running'); Review job opens the waiting ask (Chat 'waiting'); a failed day's Try again opens Chat 'retrying'; offline Try again reconnects in place (`onReconnect`; without one, a toast 'Still offline. BlueAI keeps trying on its own.'), and Home passes ScreenFrame `offlineBanner={false}`, since the card already says it (section 51); Resume calls `onResume`, and without one a toast says 'Daily runs are back on.'; a blocked run's fix does what its button says: Open App Player and Get BlueStacks act in place (`onFix`), Connect YouTube opens Connected apps.
- **Sizes:** window 419 x 830, compact 640 (bars pinned, body scrolls). Content column 387.
- **States (`state` prop, or derived from `worker`):** new (day 0: Set up card, the coach mark under it, nothing dimmed), returning before today's run ('Next run today at 11 am.', Run now secondary), running (live step), waiting for you (deadline and cost, Review job primary), done today pending (one tick, no second Run), done with no job (the day still counts, nojob), skipped (today doesn't count), day 20 done (the last day, pending, day20), failed today (reason, Try again by 9 pm), stopped (Run again by 9 pm), didn't start (the PC was off or asleep; Run now by 9 pm), can't run (one fix per reason), paused, out of credits (refresh time), offline (what happens to today), program complete (Earnings), period ended (section 69's SystemStates period-ended or period-under, not a card), out of reach (the banner), loading (the worker-home skeleton: a title, the worker card, the strip card, sized from `--v2-mark-lg`, `--v2-control-md` and `--v2-rail`; never V2's five-card skill list, `chat_product.jsx:222`). `HOME_STATES` adds nojob, skipped and day20 to the list above.
- **Props:** `worker`, `state`, `loading`, `coaching` (force the coach on or off), `onNavigate(id, opts)` (ids: `activity` from See why; the run chip and Details open `chipTarget(record)` (`screens/home.jsx`, section 30's Opens column): today as its Activity row opens it, `chat` {state} while live or `run` {state, row} once it has an outcome, `worker-settings` while paused, `home` otherwise; `chat` with `opts.state` from Run, Review job, Try again and the link; `credits`, `notifications`, `account`, `report`, `sign-out`; `connected-apps` {app: 'youtube'} from a blocked run's Connect YouTube (Open App Player and Get BlueStacks act in place through `onFix`). Every id is in section 32's route table), `onRun`, `onRetry`, `onReconnect`, `onResume`, `onFix`, `onDismissCoach`, `compact`, `landmark` (false in specimens), `frame` (false: content only).
- **Motion:** none of its own. The coach mark rises once (section 42); skeleton shimmer stops under reduced motion.
- **Accessibility:** the greeting is the screen's h1 and the focus target when the app returns to Home; the coach mark is a note; Okay or Run dismisses it; dismissal is stored on the record (`coachSeen`), never local state (`moneymaker_home.jsx:105`). Loading is `aria-busy` with a 'Loading your worker' status.
- **Responsive:** at 640 the header and bars stay put and the column scrolls; nothing reflows sideways.
- **Do / Don't:** Do read every number and time from the record; keep one card with one action; use the banner only while a program fact holds. Don't add a second greeting line that repeats the card; don't hide the strip until the partner has been back once; don't make Home the chat.

### Chat (from Home)

- **Purpose:** the thread as a window onto the same Worker record; leaving it loses nothing.
- **Anatomy:** ScreenHeader 'Chat' with Back (to Home) and a 32px IconButton 'New chat' (plus), moved out of V2's top bar (`index.html:350-355`). Thread (section 34) filling the height; today's run as one RunCard (section 36); a paid job as the JobPreviewCard (section 45), any other question as an AskCard (section 37); Composer (section 38) on the bottom edge with its notice slot. Home stays lit in the nav.
- **Variants:** `CHAT_STATES`: empty, setup, setupDone, running, waiting, done, nojob, skipped, day20, failed, retrying, noCredits (Short of credits), creditsZero, automatic, request, offTopic.
  - Setup follows V2's order (`worker_flow.jsx:65-171`): 'Welcome! Let’s set up your BlueAI worker.', the pitch, confirm @alexwatches, watch history (PermissionRequest, section 46), the run time, then paid jobs per app, Always ask first and Automatic only through ConsentDialog. The run time's window option is '9 am to 8 pm' (proposed; V2 '9am–9pm', `worker_flow.jsx:73-74`), since runs start by 8 pm (section 62); the schedule ask's hint is 'Pick one for now; change the time anytime in Settings > Worker.' No on the handle answers 'Okay. I can only use the YouTube account you applied with, or a channel under it. If you meant a different channel, report a problem and we’ll help.' with a Report a problem link (the sheet, topic 'My account or sign-in', section 65). It closes with ProgramSummary plus the D7 line: option A 'Your first run starts now, and today counts as day 1 once it’s verified.' (shown until the PM picks), followed by today's RunCard running, and the record says running; or B 'Your first counted day is tomorrow, at your run time.'
  - Waiting asks in the JobPreviewCard (section 45): the video, the channel, what will be done with the exact comment, and 'Answer by 9 pm or today won’t count.', which the run card then drops so it is said once. Answers: 'Take this job', 'Skip: today won’t count', and a quiet 'Switch YouTube to Automatic' through ConsentDialog; the answer shows as the partner's reply and the card settles (section 45's answered state). Option B, V2's words ('Paid job available', 'Watch the video and provide feedback.', 'Want me to take this one?', answers 'Yes, switch to Automatic' / 'Just this job' / 'Not now', `worker_flow.jsx:50-51, 120-121`) plus the R07 bullets in an AskCard, is `D17 = 'B'` in `screens/chat.jsx` (decision D17, section 73).
  - Any answer that turns Automatic on opens ConsentDialog first and answers only on 'I agree, turn on'; Cancel leaves the question waiting.
- **Sizes:** as Home. Bubbles max 300 (section 35).
- **States:** empty (EmptyState 'Chat with BlueAI' / 'Ask about today’s run, your days, or where to change a setting.'), running (field read-only, Send becomes Stop), waiting (the ask), retrying (the second try, live), failed (Try again by 9 pm), done (run card + See run details). Stop asks first while running, retrying or waiting (Composer `costsDay`, section 38). Out of credits says each fact once: the RunCard 'Not enough credits for this job.' + `OOC_COPY.day`; the field locks only at zero (creditsZero), where the OutOfCreditsNotice above it says until when and the locked field's placeholder is neutral, 'You can’t send messages right now' (section 48); short of today's run (noCredits, 120 of 360) the field stays open with the ComposerNotice 'Not enough AI credits for today’s run. They refresh at 5:30 am.' Automatic on (ComposerNotice info: 'Automatic is on for YouTube. BlueAI takes paid YouTube jobs without asking.' + 'Change', V2's ribbon words until 2026-10-14; V2 now says 'Automatic on YouTube', `agent_permissions.jsx:62, 210-214`).
- **Typed messages (proposed copy):** a status question is answered from the record ('Did today count?': 'Today’s run is done. Day 7 of 20, pending verification. We check every run within a day.'); an action request gets 'I don’t change settings from chat, so nothing has changed. You can set the run time in Worker settings.' plus a Link 'Open Worker settings'; out of scope gets 'That’s not something I can do here. I run your daily YouTube job, answer questions about your runs and days, and point you to the setting you need.' The worker never acts silently on typed text.
- **Props:** `worker`, `state`, `steps` (renderMessage shape), `onBack`, `onNewChat`, `onSend`, `onStop`, `onAnswer`, `onChangeMode`, `onOpenRun`, `onOpenCredits`, `onNavigate`, `compact`, `landmark`, `frame`.
- **Motion:** the thread's own (smooth jump to latest, none under reduced motion).
- **Accessibility:** the thread is a polite log; New chat and Back are labelled icon buttons at 32; the notice is tied to the field with aria-describedby.
- **Responsive:** the thread takes the height left between header and composer; the composer grows to 6 lines, then scrolls.
- **Do / Don't:** Do show a run as one card that updates in place; say what an answer costs before the tap (on the skip button itself); put a condition that holds before typing in the notice slot. Don't make the chat the only record of a run; don't put New chat in the top bar; don't let the partner type a message credits will refuse.

## 58 Activity (runs and verification)

**Status:** New.

**Why:** the partner is paid per verified day and every day is checked within a day, so after "what is my worker doing" the next question is "which days counted?". V2 answers it nowhere: the first tab, Jobs, says 'Coming Soon' / 'We are working on this section to bring you new opportunities. Check back soon for updates!' (`jobs.jsx:140-142`), Schedule lists sample tasks, Chat History lists past chats (`history.jsx:91-93`), the day count is a constant that turns tonight's Day 7 into tomorrow's Day 6 (`worker_day_done.jsx:39`), and a rejected day has no state (R19). Activity replaces Jobs and Schedule in the nav: Home, Activity, Settings (R04).

### Activity

- **Purpose:** one row per program day: date, result, whether it counted; the place to open a run and to dispute a day.
- **Anatomy:** inside `ScreenFrame` with Activity lit. ScreenHeader 'Activity'. Body (padding 16 16 24, 12 apart): ProgramStrip (section 43, no See why here), Tabs 'This program' / 'Past' (full width, section 28), then a Card (padding none) of ledger rows, newest first. A row is a ListRow read from the ledger row {date, outcome, status} through `V2Kit.dayWords`: words from its outcome, the tick from its status, so a no-job day says so, never 'Paid job done'. A 96px date column ('Today', 'Oct 11'; 14/400 ink-3, tabular), the result (14/500 ink, from `V2Kit.dayWords`: 'Paid job done', 'No job on the board', 'Job skipped', 'No answer, job skipped', 'Run stopped', 'Job didn’t finish', 'Out of AI credits', 'No run'), and on the right the StatusTick with its word ('Verified' two ticks, 'Pending' one tick, 'Still checking', 'Not counted'). Rows are separated by a hairline. A rejected row puts its reason under the result and a standalone Link 'Report a problem' on its own line under the row (32 target; never inside the row).
- **Variants:** today's live row while the run is live or can still count, keyed on `workerState`: 'Run in progress' + Badge info busy 'Running'; 'Paid job waiting' + 'Answer by 9 pm or today won’t count.' + Badge attention 'Needs you'; 'Job didn’t finish' + 'Try again by 9 pm to count the day.' + Badge danger 'Not counted yet'; 'Run stopped' + 'Run again by 9 pm to count the day.' + Badge neutral 'Not counted yet'; 'Run didn’t start' + 'Run it by 9 pm to count the day.' + Badge attention 'Not counted yet' (opens Home, where Run now is); 'Run can’t start' + the fix line per reason ('YouTube isn’t connected. Connect it by 9 pm to count the day.', 'App Player isn’t signed in to YouTube. Sign in there by 9 pm to count the day.', 'BlueStacks isn’t installed. Install it by 9 pm to count the day.') + Badge danger 'Can’t run' (opens Connected apps, or Settings > BlueStacks for no BlueStacks) (`screens/activity.jsx:58-72, 95-97`). Past tab rows: program name, its dates, and 'n of 20 days' with a tick.
- **Sizes:** row min 48, padding 12 16; date column 96; the window 419 x 830 or 640.
- **Opens (`V2Screens.rowTarget`, `screens/activity.jsx:94-103`):** a running or waiting run opens Chat at its card; every other run opens Run detail by its outcome, then its status (today's failed or stopped run in that state, a nocredits day in 'nocredits'); a day with no run (norun) is static. Report a problem opens `ReportProblemSheet` with about '<date> run' plus the reason, and the topic 'verification' (a day that wasn't counted). 'Set up your worker' opens Chat 'setup'.
- **States:** empty before day 1 (EmptyState 'No days yet' + 'Each run shows here with its verification: one tick while pending, two once verified.' + the next run time; before setup the one action 'Set up your worker'), no job today (the day still counts), loading (`V2Screens.ListSkeleton`, the ledger's shape), error (`V2Screens.LoadError` 'Couldn’t load your days', with Try again), mid-program (today pending, the rest verified), today in progress, rejected day, failed today, program complete (20 rows verified), Past tab with a past program, Past tab in the first program (EmptyState 'No past programs' / 'When this program ends, its days move here. Cash out happens on Earnings.'). All copy here is proposed except 'Today doesn’t count toward your 20 days.' (`worker_day_done.jsx:95`).
- **Props:** `worker` (the record; rows come from `ledgerRows(worker)`), `state` (ACTIVITY_STATES), `tab` + `onTab`, `past` [{id, title, range, done, target}], `onOpenRun(row)` (rows with a run; default `onNavigate(...V2Screens.rowTarget(row))`), `onReport(row)` (default: the sheet in place, from `V2Screens.reportAbout(row)`), `onRetry`, `onNavigate`, `compact`, `landmark`, `frame`.
- **Motion:** none; the Running badge pulses under the global reduced-motion rule.
- **Accessibility:** each row reads in order: date, result, tick word ('Oct 12 Paid job done, The comment was removed before we checked it., Not counted'); ticks never rely on colour; tabs are a real tablist (arrows, Home, End) with both panels in the DOM; Report a problem is a separate button.
- **Responsive:** at 640 the strip and tabs scroll with the rows; long results wrap inside their column, the date column stays 96.
- **Do / Don't:** Do derive every row from the ledger so Home, Activity, the toast and the site agree; say "Not counted" in words with the reason when there is one. Don't show a Coming Soon tab; don't nest a control in a row that opens Run detail; don't show money on rows until decision D3.

## 59 Run detail

**Status:** New.

**Why:** the worker acts on the partner's own YouTube account while nobody watches. In V2 the only record of a run is the chat thread, step by step as bubbles (`chat_product.jsx:159`), gone when another tab opens; the comment it posts under the partner's name is never shown ('Leaving a comment…', `worker_flow.jsx:136`); nothing says when a day was checked or why it did not count.

### RunDetail

- **Purpose:** what the worker did in one run, the job and its exact comment, whether the day counted, and the credits it used.
- **Anatomy:** pushed over Activity (also opened from Home's Details and the run chip); Activity stays lit. ScreenHeader 'Run details' with Back. Body (padding 16 16 24, 12 apart): RunCard (section 36) full width, steps open, no day line; JobPreviewCard receipt (section 45) when a paid job ran: video, channel, what was done, the exact comment; it draws no tick (`verify={null}`: Verification says the day once) and offers 'Switch back to Always ask' only when the job ran on Automatic (`ranOn`); Verification card (radius 16, 1px line, padding 16): title 'Verification' 14/600, StatusTick 16 with its word, one sentence 13 ink-2, and on a rejected day 'Report a problem' (secondary md); Details card (padding none), only when a job ran: 'Paid jobs', how this job ran ('Always ask' / 'Automatic'), from the row's `ranOn` (today: `today.ranOn`) and left out when unknown, and 'AI credits used' (360); a standalone Link 'Report a problem' under every run that was not rejected.
- **Variants:** the RunCard title says which run: 'Today’s run', 'Oct 11 run'. The day is said once, in Verification.
- **Sizes:** window 419 x 830 or 640; cards full column width (387).
- **States:** job done ('Pending verification' / 'We check every run within a day. One tick while pending, two once verified.'), no job (RunCard 'No paid job on the board today.', pending), skipped ('Not counted' / 'Today doesn’t count toward your 20 days.'), failed (RunCard reason 'App Player closed during the job.' + Try again; 'Not counted yet' / 'Try again by 9 pm to count the day.'), stopped ('Not counted yet' / 'Run again by 9 pm to count the day.', with Run again), pending past a day ('Still checking' / 'This one is taking longer than a day. There is nothing to do; the day counts once it’s verified.'), verified (its day number from the row's place in the ledger and `checkedAt` from the row: 'Checked Oct 12, 7:02 am. Day 6 of 20 counts.', or 'Day 5 of 20 counts.' without `checkedAt`), rejected (reason in danger ink + Report a problem), out of credits ('Not counted' / 'The run stopped when AI credits ran out, so today doesn’t count.'; decision D6 may change it), loading (the run card's shape), error (`V2Screens.LoadError` 'Couldn’t load this run', with Try again). A settled ledger row gets no Try again or Run again, and a past day says its date ('Oct 10 didn’t count toward your 20 days.'). Report a problem opens the sheet with this run attached (`V2Screens.reportAbout`). Copy is proposed except the V2 lines cited.
- **Props:** `worker`, `state` (RUN_STATES: done, nojob, skipped, failed, stopped, nocredits, pending, verified, rejected, loading, error), `run` overrides {card, title, job, taken 'ask' | 'auto', verify, text, checkedAt, reason, day}, `row` (the ledger row it was opened from: its date names the run and its status decides Verification), `tab` (the tab it was opened from, which stays lit and which Back returns to; default 'activity'), `onBack`, `onRetry`, `onRunAgain`, `onReport`, `onChangeMode`, `onOpenCredits`, `onNavigate`, `compact`, `landmark`, `frame`.
- **Motion:** none of its own; the RunCard's disclosure follows the global reduced-motion rule.
- **Accessibility:** Verification and Details are labelled sections; the tick is always paired with its word; the rejected reason uses danger ink at 5.4:1 on white; every action is 32 or taller.
- **Responsive:** at 640 the header stays and the cards scroll.
- **Do / Don't:** Do say the day once, with the reason when it did not count; show the exact comment that went out; keep the run after the chat moves on. Don't repeat the day line in the RunCard; don't drop Report a problem from runs that counted; don't show money before decision D3.

## 60 Notifications

**Status:** New.

**Why:** the worker runs while nobody watches, so what it says has to reach the partner later. In V2 a finished run raises an 8 second Windows toast and nothing else (`desktop_toast.jsx:12`, words from `worker_day_done.jsx:191-204`); a waiting job only flashes the taskbar button after 4 seconds, with no deadline (`chat_product.jsx:89-97`); a skipped day never notifies. The words existed; the place did not. Channels (toast, taskbar, nav badge) are section 54.

### Notifications

- **Purpose:** the record of everything the worker told the partner, with what is still unread, each row opening the place to act.
- **Anatomy:** pushed from the top bar's More menu; the tab the partner came from stays lit. ScreenHeader 'Notifications' with Back and, only while something is unread, a quiet md Button 'Mark all read'. Tabs 'All' / 'Unread' with counts (full width). A Card (padding none) of ListRows, each led by the ListRow `mark` (section 18): a 32px round sunken disc with the event's glyph 16 in its tone (attention, success, danger, ink-3) and an 8px blue dot at its top right when unread; the title (14, 600 unread, 500 read, ink); the body, one short sentence (13 ink-3, wraps, never truncated); the time on the right (12 ink-3: '11:04 am' today, 'Oct 11' before).
- **Variants (rows, proposed copy):** job waiting: 'Paid YouTube job waiting for your OK' / 'Answer by 9 pm or today won’t count.' (opens the job ask in Chat, Chat waiting, the destination section 54 names); expired: 'Paid job expired' / 'No answer by 9 pm, so today didn’t count.'; verified: 'Oct 11 run verified' / 'Day 6 of 20.'; done: 'Run done' / 'Day 6 of 20, pending verification.'; failed: 'Job didn’t finish' / 'App Player closed during the job.' (V2 reason). Rows are written to last: the toast's 'Today’s run is done.' is right for 8 seconds and wrong in a list tomorrow, so a row keeps the event and the day count, and the date goes on the right.
- **Kinds (one per section 54 event; kind: glyph, tone; title / body, proposed copy except V2's reasons; target):**
  - `ask`: bell, attention; 'Paid YouTube job waiting for your OK' / 'Answer by 9 pm or today won’t count.'; `chat` (the waiting ask), only while the record has a job waiting (otherwise its run, or Activity). Once answered it turns into 'You took the paid job' / 'Day 7 of 20, pending verification.' (`done`) or the `skipped` row.
  - `expired`: clock, neutral; 'Paid job expired' / 'No answer by 9 pm, so today didn’t count.'; `run`.
  - `done`: check, neutral; 'Run done' / 'Day 6 of 20, pending verification.'; `run`.
  - `skipped`: info, neutral; 'You skipped the paid job' / 'Today didn’t count toward your 20 days.'; `run`.
