  - `nojob`: info, neutral; 'No job today' / 'Your run counts. Day 6 of 20, pending verification.'; `run`.
  - `failed`: x-circle, danger; 'Job didn’t finish' / the V2 reason ('App Player closed during the job.'); `run`.
  - `verified`: check-double, success; 'Oct 11 run verified' / 'Day 6 of 20.'; `run`.
  - `rejected`: x-circle, danger; 'Oct 12 wasn’t counted' / the reason; `run`.
  - `missed`: clock, attention; 'Today’s run didn’t start' / 'Run it by 9 pm to count today.'; `home` (the WorkerCard's Run now). At 9 pm it turns into 'Today’s run didn’t happen' / 'Today didn’t count.', neutral, `run`.
  - `blocked`: alert, danger; 'Today’s run can’t start' / the reason and 'Fix it by 9 pm.'; `home` (the card's one fix). At 9 pm it turns into its result, as missed does.
  - `nocredits`: credits, attention; 'Out of AI credits for today’s run' / 'They refresh at 5:30 am, so today won’t count.'; `credits`.
  - `ending`: calendar, neutral; '3 days left in this program' / '17 of 20 days done. It ends Nov 1.'; `activity`.
  - `cashout`: earnings, success; 'Cash out is open' / 'Your 20 days ended on Nov 1. Cash out on Earnings.'; `earnings` (external: `EARNINGS_URL`, opens the site in the browser, the row carries the external glyph).
  - `ended`: calendar, neutral; 'This program has ended' / 'You need $10 to cash out. Your balance carries over to the next period.' (the site's words, `onblue/apply.html:2292-2293`); `earnings` (external).
  - `terms`: info, neutral; 'Terms of Use and Privacy Policy updated' / 'Review them and accept the updated terms to keep your worker running.' (the names come from the row's `docs` and are passed to the TermsSheet, which names them too); `terms` (the TermsSheet, `update` mode, section 53).
  A kind not in this list draws the neutral info glyph, never a check: a check would say a run is done.
- **Sizes:** row min 48, padding 12 16; window 419 x 830 or 640.
- **States:** empty (EmptyState 'No notifications yet' / 'Your worker tells you here when a run is done, a job needs your OK, a day is verified or lost, and when cash out opens.'), with unread, all read (Mark all read gone; sits on the donePending record), actioned (once answered, the ask row becomes 'You took the paid job' / 'Day 7 of 20, pending verification.', `done`, `run`, or the `skipped` row), expired job ask (the waiting row turns into its result), Unread tab with nothing unread (EmptyState 'Nothing unread' / 'You’ve seen everything your worker sent.'; on the donePending record), loading (`V2Screens.ListSkeleton`, 'Loading notifications'), error (`V2Screens.LoadError`, 'Couldn’t load your notifications' with Try again). Opening a row marks it read. Mark all read moves focus to the first row.
- **Props:** `items` [{id, kind (the Kinds list above), title, body, time, unread, target: chat | run | home | credits | activity | earnings | terms, run}], `state` (NOTIFICATION_STATES: empty, unread, read, actioned, expired, unreadTab, loading, error), `tab`, `onOpen(item)` (default: a `chat` row opens Chat waiting only while the record has a job waiting, else its run, else Activity; a `run` row carries `run` {date, outcome, status}, its ledger row, and opens `V2Screens.rowTarget(run)`, the day as it stands now, section 58, as Run detail on the current tab's stack; `home`, `credits` and `activity` go to that screen through section 32's route table; `earnings` opens `EARNINGS_URL` in the browser; `terms` opens the TermsSheet with the row's `docs`), `onMarkAllRead`, `onBack`, `onRetry`, `parentTab`, `worker` (for the top bar), `onNavigate`, `compact`, `landmark`, `frame`.
- **Count in the chrome:** what needs the partner reaches the chrome as the Activity badge (ScreenFrame, section 54); the More menu carries no badge, and the unread count itself is this screen's Unread tab.
- **Motion:** none.
- **Accessibility:** unread is said first ('Unread: Paid YouTube job waiting for your OK'), never by the dot or weight alone; the disc is decorative; tabs are a real tablist with counts that never change their width; in forced colours the dot draws in CanvasText.
- **Responsive:** at 640 header and tabs stay with the bars; rows scroll.
- **Do / Don't:** Do write a row so it is still true tomorrow; open the place to act from every row; turn a waiting job into its result when its deadline passes. Don't copy the toast's "Today’s …" into the list; don't show Mark all read with nothing unread; don't rely on the toast or the taskbar flash as the only record.

## 61 Settings

**Status:** Changed from V2.
**Why:** The partner opens Settings to control the worker that acts on their account. V2's Settings has no title, opens on a BlueStacks card that says "Connected to BlueStacks App Player" even when BlueStacks is not installed (settings.jsx:31, R16), then, since 2026-10-14, a Worker card whose Apps group is the mode sheet's switch list (settings.jsx:11-20), then Telegram, AI mode, own key and a Chat AI Settings card of Max Output Tokens, Max Steps and Timeout with its own Save button and a second AI Model picker (settings.jsx:120-138, R31). Beyond that switch list nothing about the worker, its accounts or the partner is there. The system puts partner topics first and developer controls last, closed.

### Settings (Changed from V2)
- **Purpose:** The Settings tab: one place to see and reach every control over the worker, the accounts it uses, what it tells you, the app it drives, your account and help. Developer controls stay out of the way.
- **Anatomy:** `ScreenHeader` "Settings" (20/600, no Back: a tab root). Then `SettingsSection` cards, 12 apart on the 16 gutter, in this order:
  1. **Worker**: one nav `ListRow` "BlueAI worker", leading calendar 16 (pause 16 when paused), description from the Worker record ("Runs daily at 11 am. Paid jobs: Always ask." or "Paused until Oct 16. Paused days don’t count."), `Badge` neutral "Paused" only when paused. Pushes Worker settings (62).
  2. **Connected apps**, description "The accounts BlueAI uses for you.": `V2Screens.ConnectedAppRows` (63): YouTube (handle · mode, Badge Connected / Signed out / Not connected), Telegram, Instagram (static, Badge "Coming soon"). Each live row pushes that app's detail.
  3. **Notifications**: two control rows, `ListRow` + `Switch`: "A paid job needs you" / "A job you don’t answer by 9 pm doesn’t count for today."; "Today’s run ends" / "Done, or stopped with a way to try again." (Proposed copy). They turn off the Windows notification for exactly the events section 54 maps to them (job waiting; day done, no job today, run failed). A day not counted, the program ending and cash out always notify, and every event still lands in the inbox, so there is no third switch.
  4. **BlueStacks**: one row "BlueStacks App Player" led by the ListRow `mark` (monitor, neutral: section 18). Connected: V2's "To switch, open BlueAI from another instance." + `Badge` success "Connected". Signed out: "App Player isn’t signed in to YouTube. Sign in there so today’s run can start." + `Button` secondary sm "Open App Player". Not installed: "Not installed. BlueAI runs your jobs inside App Player, so today’s run can’t start without it." + `Button` primary sm "Get BlueStacks". With no `bluestacks` prop the row's state comes from the record (`V2Screens.accountStates`: signed-out, not-installed, else connected). "Open App Player" and "Get BlueStacks" call `onFix(reason)` ('signed-out', 'no-bluestacks') through `fixBlocked`, the same fix Home's blocked card does; without a host `onFix` a toast stands in (Settings sits in `ToastScope`).
  5. **Account and privacy**: one nav row, `Avatar` 32 + name + email. Pushes Account (64).
  6. **Help**: "FAQ" (external row, opens the site in the browser) and "Report a problem" (opens the sheet, 65).
  7. `AdvancedDisclosure` "Advanced" / "AI mode, your own key and the chat model. Most partners never need these.", closed: `AiModePanel`, `ByokPanel` (section 49), and a "Chat model" section with one `Select` (Auto + V2's six models, settings.jsx:123-129).
- **Variants:** One screen. The BlueStacks row has three variants (connected, signed out, not installed) bound to the worker's preflight (R13), never a fixed claim.
- **Sizes:** The 419 window, content 387 wide; full 830 or compact 640 (the content band scrolls, bars stay pinned).
- **States:** Default; Advanced open; BlueStacks not installed; BlueStacks signed out; a section highlighted after routing (blue edge + ring for 1900ms, value unchanged); worker paused; Report a problem sheet open; loading (the rows' shape, `V2Screens.ListSkeleton`); error (`V2Screens.LoadError` "Couldn’t load your settings" / "Nothing is lost: your runs and days are not affected. Try again in a moment." + Try again), both from `V2Screens.pendingBody`. V2: `index.html?start=worker`, then Settings in the nav.
- **Props:** `worker`, `state` ('default' | 'advanced' | 'no-bluestacks' | 'report' | 'loading' | 'error'), `bluestacks` ('connected' | 'not-installed' | 'signed-out'), `telegram` ('linked' | 'not-linked'), `highlight` (section id), `onNavigate(id, opts)`, `onReport` (the host's Report a problem; without it Settings opens its own sheet), `onRetry`, `onFix(reason)`, `frame`, `compact`, `landmark`, `preview` (guide). Settings, Worker, Connected apps, Account, Help and AI credits sit in `V2Screens.ScreenFrame`, the one frame for every signed-in screen, inside one `.v2-acct` column, and every chrome event reaches `onNavigate(id, opts)`; the lit tab follows section 32's rule: Settings for this screen and the screens it pushes (62 to 64; Settings > Help is not a push: it opens the Report a problem sheet in place, and FAQ in the browser, sections 65 and 32), the tab you came from for AI credits (66), which only the top bar opens. `screens/settings.jsx` also exports `V2Screens.CONFIRM_COPY` {signOut(email), disconnect(handle), leave(handle, endsOn, n), deleteData(n)}: the one title, body, primary label and failure line per destructive confirm, read by Account, Connected apps and sections 25 and 52 (`screens/settings.jsx:21-24, 44-73`), with `useConfirmStep`, `ConfirmDialog` and `pendingBody` beside it.
- **Motion:** None of its own. Routing scrolls the section into view (instantly under reduced motion); the kit's row and disclosure transitions only.
- **Accessibility:** One h1, one h2 per section. Nav rows are buttons or links with a visible focus ring; the FAQ row says "(opens in your browser)". Switches carry their row's label as their name. Every control is at least 32px tall. Body 14 ink, descriptions 13 ink-3 (6.0:1 on white).
- **Responsive:** One column; long descriptions wrap; badges and buttons never shrink.
- **Do / Don't:** Do keep the order. Do bind BlueStacks to the preflight. Do apply each change at once with a toast. Don't show tokens, steps or timeouts. Don't add a Save button to toggles. Don't open Advanced by default. Don't change a value by routing to it.

**Proposed copy:** the two notification rows, the BlueStacks signed-out and not-installed lines, the worker summary sentence, the Advanced description and "The model your chat replies use. Auto picks one for each message." Kept from V2: "To switch, open BlueAI from another instance.", "Connect Telegram", the model names.

## 62 Worker settings

**Status:** New.
**Why:** BlueAI acts on the partner's account every day, so every promise made in setup needs a control the partner can find later (P4, R05). V2's pitch says "You can pause, stop, or disconnect anytime." (worker_flow.jsx:67) and its consent says "Switch back anytime in Settings." (agent_permissions.jsx:188), and since 2026-10-14 the mode can be turned off outside an open chat (Settings' Worker card, settings.jsx:11-20), but nothing can be paused, modes reset on reload (agent_permissions.jsx:21), and the run time is asked once in chat (worker_flow.jsx:73-74) and never shown again.

### WorkerSettings (New)
- **Purpose:** Settings > Worker. Turn daily runs off for a while (and know what it costs), choose Always ask or Automatic per app, set when the run happens, and see the interest profile jobs are picked from.
- **Anatomy:** `ScreenHeader` "Worker" with Back (Settings stays lit; the consent dialog's own note says "Settings > Worker"). Four `SettingsSection` cards:
  1. **Pause and start-up**: `ListRow` "Daily runs" + `Switch`. On: `nextRunText` ("Next run today at 11 am."). Off: "Paused until Oct 16. Paused days don’t count toward your 20 days." Turning it off opens the pause `Dialog` (task, md) "Pause daily runs?": `Select` "Pause until" first (Tomorrow, Oct 14 / Oct 16 / Oct 20 / Until I turn them back on), then the cost for the date picked, from `numbers()` (role=status, so a new date's cost is announced: "Pausing until Oct 20 skips 7 days. You’d have 11 left for the 13 you need, so 20 days would be out of reach."; for "Until I turn them back on": "Days you pause don’t count toward your 20 days. You need 13 more, and 18 days are left."); actions Keep running / Pause (danger: it loses days, section 52). Turning it on resumes at once. Then `ListRow` "Start BlueAI with Windows" + `Switch` (on): "If it’s off, runs skip after a restart until you open BlueAI." (so tomorrow's run survives a PC restart, section 29).
  2. **Paid jobs**, description "Choose for each app whether BlueAI asks before every paid job.": `ModeRows` (section 46), full-bleed. Picking Automatic opens `ConsentDialog` first and applies only on "I agree, turn on"; Always ask applies at once. Instagram: "Coming soon", no control.
  3. **Run time**, description "Runs start by 8 pm, so there’s time to answer a job or try again before 9 pm. Changes start tomorrow if today’s run has already begun.": `RadioCard` list "A set time" ("BlueAI starts within that hour.") / "A window" ("BlueAI picks a different moment in it each day.") / "Any time" ("BlueAI picks the moment each day."); then one `Select` "Start at" (6 am to 8 pm, hourly) or two side by side, "From" and "To" (gap 8; both 6 am to 8 pm, hourly). When To is not after From, To shows the inline error "Pick a time after <From>." and no toast is shown until it is fixed (`screens/worker-settings.jsx:17-19, 49, 116-123, 158`).
  4. **Interest profile**, description "Built from your watch history. BlueAI picks jobs that fit these topics." when watch history is allowed, otherwise "General topics. BlueAI picks jobs that fit these topics." plus the `Link` "Allow watch history to tailor jobs" (Connected apps, YouTube): `Chip` toggles with V2's four topics (worker_flow.jsx:85, names only): Tech & Smartphones, Automotive, Motivation & Finance, Entertainment; note 12 ink-3 "Turn a topic off and BlueAI stops picking jobs about it." (whether a topic can be turned off is decision D19, section 73) Each topic change toasts ("BlueAI won’t pick Automotive jobs." / "BlueAI can pick Automotive jobs again."). The last topic on is disabled, and the note says "Keep at least one topic on, so BlueAI has jobs to pick from."
  Every change confirms in one `Toast` once it settles: a burst of picks (a kind, then a time) says it once ("Daily run set at 11 am.", "Daily run set between 9 am and 8 pm.", `screens/worker-settings.jsx:116-123`), "Automatic is on for YouTube.", "Daily runs paused.", "Daily runs are back on.", "BlueAI starts with Windows.", "BlueAI won’t start with Windows.").
- **Variants:** One screen.
- **Sizes:** The 419 window; content 387; selects full width or two equal columns.
- **States:** Always ask everywhere (default); Automatic on YouTube; consent open; pause dialog open; paused until a date; editing the run window (A window, From 9 am, To 8 pm, the default); run window invalid (From 7 pm, To 9 am: the inline error, no toast); pause busy (the dialog's Pause loading); saving (the switch busy while the change settles); not saved (the switch goes back, error toast "Couldn’t turn off Start with Windows. Nothing changed." with Try again); history off (watch history not allowed: general topics and the Allow link); one topic left (the last chip disabled, the note says why); loading; error.
- **Props:** `worker`, `state` ('ask' | 'auto' | 'consent' | 'pause' | 'paused' | 'window' | 'window-invalid' | 'pause-busy' | 'saving' | 'not-saved' | 'history-off' | 'one-topic' | 'loading' | 'error'), `onBack`, `onNavigate`, `onRetry`, `frame`, `compact`, `landmark`, `preview`. Reads and writes the Worker record: `apps[app].mode`, `paused.until`, `runWindow {kind, label}`.
- **Motion:** The kit's: dialog scale-in, toast rise, switch thumb; nothing under reduced motion.
- **Accessibility:** The switch is role=switch named "Daily runs" and is busy while the pause dialog decides; Segmented and RadioCard are radio groups with arrow keys; chips carry aria-pressed. Dialog focus moves in and returns to the switch. The cost sentence is in the dialog body, read before the buttons.
- **Responsive:** One column; the From and To selects stay side by side at 387.
- **Do / Don't:** Do say the cost of pausing before it happens. Do apply at once and confirm in a toast. Do send Automatic through consent every time. Don't keep the mode only in chat. Don't let a pause have no end and no cost. Don't change today's run silently once it has begun.

**Proposed copy:** every string on this screen except "Always ask", "Automatic", "I agree, turn on", the consent body (section 46) and the four topic names. The run window lines are V2's chat lines (worker_flow.jsx:73) moved from the worker's "I" to "BlueAI"; "Leave it to me" becomes "Any time" because on a settings screen "me" reads as the partner. **Decisions:** D4 (this screen is the permanent home of the mode either way); whether a partner may turn a topic off is a product call (decision D19, section 73).

## 63 Connected apps

**Status:** New.
**Why:** The worker watches, likes and comments as the partner. V2 confirms the YouTube account once in chat (worker_flow.jsx:71) and never shows it again, reads watch history unasked (worker_flow.jsx:80, R18), lets Instagram be set to Automatic before its jobs exist (agent_permissions.jsx:57, R40), and its Telegram card "Unlink"s at once (settings.jsx:59). The partner needs one place that says which accounts are in use, what BlueAI does with each, and how to stop it.

### ConnectedApps (New)
- **Purpose:** List the accounts BlueAI acts on; per account, what it does there and the way to stop it, with the cost said first.
- **Anatomy:** `ScreenHeader` "Connected apps" with Back (pushed over Settings).
  - **List:** `SettingsSection` "Your accounts" / "BlueAI acts on these accounts for you. It never sees or types your passwords." holding `ConnectedAppRows`: one nav `ListRow` per app, `Avatar` app 32, name 14/500, account 13 ink-3, one `Badge`:
    YouTube "@alexwatches · Always ask" + Connected (success) / "Signed out in App Player" + Signed out (attention) / "Not connected" + Not connected (neutral);
    Telegram "Connected to @BlueAIBot" + Connected / "Send commands to BlueAI from any device." + Not connected;
    Instagram "Instagram jobs aren’t available yet." + Coming soon, static (no chevron, no switch). Note 12 ink-3: "A new app starts on Always ask. Instagram turns on here when its jobs go live."
  - **YouTube detail** (titled "YouTube"): section "Account": `Avatar` app 40 + handle + "Signed in to App Player on this PC." + Connected. Signed out: "App Player isn’t signed in to YouTube. Sign in there so today’s run can start." + `Button` secondary sm "Open App Player", which calls `onFix('signed-out')` (`fixBlocked`: the host acts in place; without one a toast stands in for the App Player launch). Without a YouTube `state` the record decides, from its apps and the `blockedBy` reason (`accountStates`), so a blocked run's fix route never lands on a row that says Connected. Section "What BlueAI does here": "Daily warm-up" / "Watches and likes videos that fit you, every run, in both modes." ("general videos" when watch history is off), then "Paid jobs", a nav row with the mode as value (pushes Worker settings, 62). Section "Watch history", a permission (section 46): allowed is a row "Allowed on Oct 6" / "BlueAI sees your watch history, subscriptions and channel stats to pick jobs that fit you." + `Button` secondary sm "Turn off" (one tap, toast "Watch history off. BlueAI no longer sees your watch history, subscriptions or channel stats."); not now or turned off is the `PermissionRequest` compact result (revoked with its date) + Allow, which opens the full request in place (Not now / Allow); never asked shows the full request. Each answer records its date. Then the consequence 13 ink-3 "BlueAI stops using @alexwatches, and daily runs can’t start until you connect YouTube again." over `Button` danger "Disconnect YouTube" (red because it opens the confirm for an action that costs days, section 52). It opens a `Dialog` alert "Disconnect YouTube?" / "BlueAI stops using @alexwatches, and daily runs can’t start until you connect YouTube again. Days without a run don’t count toward your 20 days." Cancel / Disconnect (danger). After: "Not connected" row, "Daily runs can’t start until BlueAI has a YouTube account to use.", + `Button` primary sm "Connect", toast "YouTube disconnected".
  - **Telegram detail:** linked: "Connected to @BlueAIBot" + Connected; "You won’t be able to send commands to BlueAI from Telegram. Your worker keeps running." over `Button` secondary "Disconnect Telegram" (not red: it costs nothing), no confirm, toast with Undo (section 52, rule 4). Not linked: V2's "Connect your Telegram account and send commands to BlueAI from any device." + `Button` primary "Connect Telegram". It opens the pairing `Dialog` (task) "Connect Telegram": "In Telegram, open @BlueAIBot and send this code.", then the code 4829 (24/600 tabular, sunken, dashed line) and a status line with `Spinner` 16, "Waiting for Telegram. The code works for 10 minutes." Expired: "This code expired before Telegram got it." with primary "Get a new code". Failed: "Couldn’t connect Telegram. Check your connection and try again." with primary "Try again". Both say "Nothing was linked." and offer Cancel (`screens/connected-apps.jsx:60-78`). V2's QR placeholder and its "I've paired, continue" are left out (V2 `settings.jsx:63-79`) until there is a real code to scan. A Telegram command never gives or takes back consent (Automatic, a disconnect and leaving happen only in the app, section 53), and Telegram does not carry the notifications (section 54).
- **Variants:** List; YouTube detail; Telegram detail. `ConnectedAppRows` is also Settings' Connected apps section (61).
- **Sizes:** The 419 window; rows min 48; account avatar 40 on details, 32 in lists.
- **States:** List (YouTube connected, Telegram not connected, Instagram Coming soon); YouTube connected (watch history allowed); watch history turned off (the compact result, Allow opens the full request); watch history not now; watch history never asked (the full request); YouTube signed out in App Player (also from a blocked run, where the record says signed out); disconnect confirm; disconnected; Telegram linked; Telegram not linked; Telegram pairing; pairing expired; pairing failed; disconnect busy (the confirm's Disconnect loading); disconnect failed (`Banner` danger "Couldn’t disconnect YouTube" / "Nothing changed. Try again in a moment.", primary Try again); loading; error.
- **Props:** `ConnectedApps {worker, app (null | 'youtube' | 'telegram'), state ('connected' | 'signed-out' | 'disconnect' | 'disconnect-busy' | 'disconnect-failed' | 'disconnected' | 'tg-pairing' | 'tg-expired' | 'tg-failed' | 'loading' | 'error'), telegram ('linked' | 'not-linked'), onBack, onNavigate, onRetry, onFix(reason), frame, compact, landmark, preview}`. `ConnectedAppRows {worker, youtube, telegram, onOpen(app)}`.
- **Motion:** The kit's dialog and toast only.
- **Accessibility:** Rows are buttons with a focus ring; a status is a word in the Badge, never colour alone; the alert dialog starts focus on Cancel and returns it to the Disconnect button. The pairing code's digits are aria-hidden, with an sr-only "Pairing code 4 8 2 9"; after Connect or Disconnect, focus moves to the first control that replaced the pressed one.
- **Responsive:** One column; handle and descriptions wrap; badges never shrink.
- **Do / Don't:** Do show the handle BlueAI uses. Do say what disconnecting costs above the button and in the confirm. Do confirm a disconnect that costs days and offer Undo for one that costs nothing. Don't say "Unlink". Don't let a partner consent to automation on an app with no jobs. Don't read watch history without a way to turn it off.

**Proposed copy:** everything except "Connected to @BlueAIBot", "Connect your Telegram account and send commands to BlueAI from any device." and "Connect Telegram" (settings.jsx:55-60). "It never sees or types your passwords." adapts the pitch's "I never see or type your password." (worker_flow.jsx:67). **Settled:** a disconnect the partner chose is not called a pause. Daily runs can’t start without the account, so the card and badge show it as blocked 'no-app' (section 41), and days without a run don’t count, the same rule as pausing (62).

## 64 Account and privacy

**Status:** Changed from V2.
**Why:** The gate's whole point is "use the account you applied with" (R39), and signing out has to sign out (R03). V2's Profile opens on a 96px gradient banner and a 92px emoji avatar (profile.jsx:31-34), shows a real staff member's identity in a prototype (profile.jsx:7, described, not quoted), lists Handle, Country and Mobile the product never uses, and its "Logout" confirm only closes the dialog and returns to chat (index.html:1010). What BlueAI keeps about the partner (the interest profile, watch-history access, consent records) is shown nowhere.

### Account (Changed from V2)
- **Purpose:** Who is signed in, the program they are in, the account BlueAI acts on, what BlueAI keeps and what was agreed, the legal documents, and a Sign out that signs out.
- **Anatomy:** `ScreenHeader` "Account" with Back (pushed over Settings; also More > Account, which replaces "Profile"). Then:
  - **Identity** (no card): `Avatar` 56 initials, name 16/600 ink, email 13 ink-3. No banner, no emoji.
  - **Program** section: external `ListRow` "Partner Starter Program" (the site's name, apply.html:1403) / "7 of 20 days. Ends Nov 1." from `numbers()`, opening the site dashboard (`EARNINGS_URL`).
  - **Connected account** section: `Avatar` app 32 + "YouTube" + handle; pushes Connected apps (63).
  - **Privacy** section, "What BlueAI keeps about you, what you agreed to, and how to leave.": "Interest profile" / "4 topics from your watch history" when watch history is allowed, otherwise "General topics. Allow watch history to tailor jobs." (pushes 62); "Watch history" with value Allowed (allowed) or Not allowed (notnow, revoked and not asked yet) (pushes 63); expandable "What you agreed to": Program Terms, agreed, Oct 6; Privacy Policy, acknowledged, Oct 6; "Watch history, allowed | not allowed | turned off | not asked yet" with its `historyOn` date (none when never asked); Automatic, Off on every app (or On for YouTube). Dates tabular, ink-3. Then two danger rows, "Leave the program" and "Delete my data", each opening a danger `AlertDialog` with `V2Screens.CONFIRM_COPY.leave(handle, endsOn, n)` / `deleteData(n)` (`screens/settings.jsx:44-73`): the withdrawal section 53 puts here. Each carries a cash-out line from `numbers().cashOutOpen` (section 41): open "...cash out is open there.", under the minimum "...a balance under $10 can’t be cashed out once you leave.", and while the site has not answered "You need $10 to cash out once the program ends on Nov 1. Check your balance on Earnings before you leave."
  - **Legal** links (`Link` external, 13): "Program Terms" and "Privacy Policy" on `/onblue/terms#program` and `#privacy` (terms_sheet.jsx:32, 67-68).
  - **Sign out**: `Card` padding none holding one `ListRow` with sign-out 16. Opens `Dialog` alert "Sign out of BlueAI?" (`CONFIRM_COPY.signOut(email)`) / "Your worker pauses while you’re signed out, and days without a run don’t count toward your 20 days. Sign in again with alex@example.com to pick up where you left off." Cancel / Sign out (primary). Confirm signs out, pauses the worker and lands on Welcome's `signedOut` variant (67).
- **Variants:** One screen.
- **Sizes:** The 419 window; avatar 56; rows min 48.
- **States:** View; watch history turned off (history-revoked: Not allowed, the record says turned off, Oct 11); sign out confirm; leave the program confirm (leave); delete my data confirm (delete); signed out (Welcome's signedOut variant, worker paused); busy and failed for each confirm, sign out, leave and delete (the primary loading; then `Banner` danger "Couldn’t sign out" / "Couldn’t leave the program" / "Couldn’t delete your data", "Nothing changed. Try again in a moment.", primary Try again); left and deleted (section 69's left-program state, deleted adds that the data is being deleted); loading; error. V2: `index.html?start=worker`, More (kebab) > Profile, then Sign out.
- **Props:** `worker` (account, apps, ledger), `state` ('view' | 'history-revoked' | 'signout' | 'leave' | 'delete', each also '-busy' and '-failed', | 'signed-out' | 'left' | 'deleted' | 'loading' | 'error'), `onBack`, `onNavigate(id, opts)`, `onSignOut`, `onLeave`, `onDeleteData`, `onRetry`, `frame`, `compact`, `landmark`, `preview`.
- **Motion:** The kit's: the expandable row's chevron turn, the dialog. None under reduced motion.
- **Accessibility:** Identity is plain text (the avatar is decorative next to the name). The expandable row has aria-expanded and aria-controls. The alert dialog starts on Cancel. All rows and links have the focus ring; external links say they open in the browser. Hover is CSS, not a mouse handler (profile.jsx:59).
- **Responsive:** One column; the email wraps anywhere; legal links wrap to two lines if needed.
- **Do / Don't:** Do show the account the partner applied with, as the site does. Do list what BlueAI keeps, each with the way to change it. Do say in the confirm that the worker pauses and days stop counting. Do say "Sign out" everywhere. Don't put real people's details in a prototype. Don't decorate identity. Don't build a confirm that does nothing. Don't show fields the product never uses.

**Proposed copy:** the section titles and descriptions, the record lines and the sign-out dialog. Kept: "Sign out" (profile.jsx:62), "Program Terms", "Privacy Policy". "Logout" (profile.jsx:75, 79; index.html:378) becomes "Sign out" (glossary). **Decision D3:** the program row shows days, never dollars, until the money model is chosen. The agreed dates are fixture values; the real record is the acceptance log legal asked for (terms_sheet.jsx:23).

## 65 Help and Report a problem

**Status:** New.
**Why:** Help is the escape hatch when a day or a payout looks wrong, the moment money is at stake (R34). V2 draws a Help menu with "FAQ" and "Report Issue" whose rows only close the menu (index.html:364-365), with a red warning glyph on a help entry (index.html:315). There is nothing behind them.

### Help (New)
- **Purpose:** Two entries reachable from every screen: FAQ opens the site article in the browser; Report a problem opens one sheet, already attached to the run it started from when it started from one.
- **Anatomy:** Not a screen. Entry points: the top bar's Help `Menu` (section 30: "FAQ" external, "Report a problem"), Settings > Help (61), a failed `RunCard` and a rejected day in Activity (58). `ReportProblemSheet` is the kit `Sheet` (full window width):
  - title "Report a problem", description "We reply by email to alex@example.com." (the signed-in email);
  - when started from a run: a sunken block (radius 10, 13 ink-2) with activity 16, the run in 600 ("Today’s run, Oct 13.") and what happened ("Didn’t finish: App Player closed during the job.");
  - `Select` "What is it about?" (placeholder "Choose one"): A run / A day that wasn’t counted / Earnings or cash out / My account or sign-in / Something else in the app; "A run" when attached to a run;
  - `TextArea` "What happened?", required, 4 rows growing to 6, 1,000 characters, no placeholder; the example sits in the helper line under it (12 ink-3): "Example: The run stopped after the second video and the day shows as not counted.", so it stays readable while the partner types;
  - `Checkbox` "Include diagnostic info", on, "Today’s run log and the app version. Never your passwords or messages.";
  - actions Cancel / Send (primary, disabled until something is written). Failed: `Banner` danger "Couldn’t send your report" / "Check your connection and try again. What you wrote is kept." and Send becomes "Try again". Sent: the sheet closes, `Toast` success "Report sent. We’ll reply by email."
- **Variants:** From a run (attached, topic picked) or blank (from the menu or Settings).
- **Sizes:** Sheet full window width, 419; fields full width.
- **States:** Menu open; draft (blank or from a run); draft with text (Send enabled); sending (sheet held, Send spinner); sent (toast); failed (text kept, Try again).
- **Props:** `Help {worker, state ('menu' | 'draft' | 'sending' | 'sent' | 'failed'), about (true reads today's run, or {label, detail}), topic, frame, compact, landmark, preview}`; `ReportProblemSheet {open, state, about, topic, text, email, preview, onClose, onSent}`: `topic` preselects the subject ('verification' picks "A day that wasn’t counted"). The draft is kept per run outside the sheet, so a stray scrim click or Esc keeps it for the next open, and Cancel or Send clears it. `V2Screens.reportAbout(row)` returns {about: {label '<date> run', detail: the row's reason}, topic} ('verification' for a rejected day), used by Activity and Run detail (`screens/help.jsx:11-17, 40-45`).
- **Motion:** The kit's sheet rise and toast; none under reduced motion.
- **Accessibility:** The menu is role=menu with arrows, Esc and focus return (R25). The sheet opens with focus on its topic `Select`, or on "What happened?" when the topic is preset (`initialFocus`), and traps focus; Esc and the scrim close it except while sending. Fields have visible labels; the example is helper text, never the label or a placeholder; the failure banner is role=alert; the toast is polite.
- **Responsive:** One column inside the sheet; the textarea grows to 6 rows, then scrolls.
- **Do / Don't:** Do open the FAQ in the browser. Do attach the run and say which. Do keep the text when sending fails. Do say where the reply goes. Don't draw a menu row that does nothing. Don't use red for a help entry. Don't make the partner retype which run went wrong. Don't send diagnostics without saying what they contain.

**Proposed copy:** everything in the sheet. "Report Issue" becomes "Report a problem"; "FAQ" is kept. **Open:** the reply channel and its timing are the support team's to state; the sheet promises email only.

## 66 AI credits

**Status:** Changed from V2.
**Why:** Credits are the fuel for the paid run, so the partner must be able to read at a glance whether today's run is covered (R14). V2's AI Credits screen has no Back and lights no tab (index.html:992), a Refresh button with no handler (index.html:449), a 54/800 gradient numeral (index.html:527), and never states the daily grant or when it refreshes until the balance is zero. Its banner and explainer copy are right and stay.

### Credits (Changed from V2)
- **Purpose:** The allowance screen: what is left today, whether today's run is covered, when credits refresh, and that credits are paused while the partner's own key runs.
- **Anatomy:** `ScreenHeader` "AI credits" (sentence case) with Back, pushed from the top bar's `CreditsChip` over the tab you came from (that tab stays lit). Then:
  - **Empty only:** `Banner` "Out of credits", its tone from `creditsDay` (section 41): danger when 'stopped', attention when 'waiting', neutral (the Banner offline tone with the clock glyph) when today is covered or the run is done; V2's banner line only: "You’ve used all of today’s AI credits." (index.html:512-517). No countdown in the banner: the meter says when credits refresh and what zero means for today (`screens/credits.jsx:63-65`).
  - **Refreshed only:** `Banner` success, dismissible, "New credits are here" / "You have 2,000 AI credits for today."
  - `StatCard` "Left today": credits glyph 24 (the one gradient, section 08) + the figure 40/600 in ink; its action slot holds `CreditsMeter` (section 47) stretched to the card: the rail, "of 2,000 today" (`figure={false}`: the stat above already says 1,640) and "Refreshes in 6h 42m", and the covered line ("Enough for today’s run" / "Not enough for today’s run" / "Today’s run can’t start until credits refresh" / "Today’s run is done"). Low and empty read `dayStatus` (`creditsDay` at the balance shown) and say `OOC_COPY.day`'s line for it, so every short-credit surface says the same thing about today.
  - **Own key instead of the figure:** `SettingsSection` "Running on your own key" / "No AI credits are used while this is on." with `Badge` success "On"; rows Provider, Model; nav row "Manage in Settings" (Settings, Advanced open, own key highlighted).
  - Explainer (no card, V2 copy kept): "BlueAI runs on credits" 14/600; "Every task and job BlueAI does uses credits." "You get limited credits for your daily tasks." 13 ink-3; `Link` external "Learn more" to the site's AI credits article (credits_popup_parts.jsx:13).
- **Variants:** Allowance; own key.
- **Sizes:** The 419 window; figure 40; meter rail 6.
- **States:** Healthy (1,640, covered); low (120, less than one run of 360); empty (the banner line; the meter says when credits refresh and whether today's run can start or is already done); refreshed; own key active; loading (the `StatCard` loading skeleton, the top bar's credits chip hidden); error (`V2Screens.LoadError` 'Couldn’t load your AI credits' with Try again, `screens/credits.jsx:77-85`). V2: `index.html?start=worker&credits=0`, then the credits pill.
- **Props:** `worker` (credits {left, grant, runCost}, today), `state` ('healthy' | 'low' | 'empty' | 'refreshed' | 'own-key' | 'loading' | 'error'), `refillAt` (default next 00:00 UTC), `now` (freezes the clock), `keyInfo`, `tab`, `onBack`, `onRetry`, `onManageKey`, `frame`, `compact`, `landmark`, `preview`.
- **Motion:** The meter fill is static; the meter's "Refreshes in" time ticks once a second as text; nothing animates.
- **Accessibility:** The meter is role=meter with "1,640 of 2,000 left today" as its value text; a danger banner is role=alert, attention and neutral banners are role=status, and none holds a ticking value; the figure is ink (19.7:1 on white), never gradient text. No dead control.
- **Responsive:** One column; meter caption wraps under itself at narrow widths.
- **Do / Don't:** Do state the grant and the refresh time before zero. Do say what zero means for today's run once, on the covered line. Do keep V2's banner and explainer copy. Do offer Back. Don't draw a Refresh that does nothing. Don't paint the figure. Don't offer to buy or earn credits. Don't show a balance while the own key is in use.

**Kept from V2:** "Out of credits", "You’ve used all of today’s AI credits.", "Credits will be refreshed in", "BlueAI runs on credits", both explainer lines, "Running on your own key", "Manage in Settings". Changed: "AI Credits" to "AI credits"; "No BlueAI credits are consumed" to "No AI credits are used"; "Active" to "On"; "Learn more ↗" to a Link with the external icon. **Decisions:** the explainer says "task" for paid work, which the glossary bans (PM copy, flagged not changed); D6 (reserve the run's cost from the grant).

## 67 Welcome (signed out)

**Status:** Changed from V2.

**Why:** V2's welcome is dressed in a third visual family, creator-brand (`moneymaker.jsx:15-21`): Space Grotesk 27/700 (`:153`), a perspective grid, orbs and grain (`:135-137`), a hero logo with a cursor morph (`:142`), a gradient pill with a glow (`:184`), and a 5.9 s intro (`:125-128`) that plays behind the terms sheet where nobody sees it. Its "Terms & Conditions" link is a dead button at 2.41:1 (`:200-204`). A partner meets three typographic identities in their first minute. The PM's two steps are the right amount of explanation and stay word for word.

### Welcome (Changed from V2)

- **Purpose:** the signed-out start, on the site's DNA: one promise, two steps, one action; the Program Terms sheet over it on first run.
- **Anatomy:**
  1. Ground `--v2-canvas`, padding 40 24 16; no decoration. AppFrame chrome-free (no top bar, no nav).
  2. BrandMark 48, logo only (the title does the talking).
  3. Title 24/600 ink, leading 1.2, `-0.02em`, balanced, at most 320 wide, with one serif italic word in blue ink (`--v2-font-serif`, `--v2-blue-ink`, 1.06em: the site's title em, `onblue/onblue.css:1446-1453`).
  4. Steps: one Card (radius 16, 1px line, padding 16) holding an ordered list of two rows. Each row: a 28 step disc (`--v2-select` fill, `--v2-select-line` edge, ink figure 13/600, the site's step-num) and the step text 14 ink-2, first line centred on the disc.
  5. One Button, primary lg (44) full width: "Sign in to get started".
  6. Foot: a muted standalone Link "Program Terms" (13/500 ink-3, 32 target) that opens the legal page in a new tab.
- **Copy:** PM copy, verbatim. Title "An AI that turns your social accounts *into income.*" (`moneymaker.jsx:154`; decision D3: the site pays $30 for 20 days and the glossary bans "income"; if D3 follows the site, use the proposed "Put your BlueAI worker *to work.*"). Steps "Sign in with the same account you applied with." and "Run the BlueAI worker: it finds jobs and runs them for you." (`:51, 55`). Action "Sign in to get started" (`:187`). The link says Program Terms (glossary), not "Terms & Conditions".
- **Variants:** `headline` pm (default) or proposed. `signedOut` (the account's email), the variant after Sign out (section 64): BrandMark 48, title "You’re signed out", line "Your worker is paused, and days without a run don’t count toward your 20 days.", and one Button primary lg full "Sign in as alex@example.com" (`screens/welcome.jsx:134-152`). Proposed copy.
- **Sizes:** the 419 window; compact 640 centres the same column in less height.
- **States:** first run (the Program Terms sheet over it, section 53); agreeing (sheet button loading); not saved (sheet error banner); after terms; signed out (after Sign out, section 64); reduced motion. In V2: the bare URL `index.html` (terms sheet), then Agree and continue.
- **Props:** `Welcome {terms: null | 'firstrun' | 'update', termsDocs (the changed documents' names, for update), termsBusy, termsError, headline, signedOut (email), onSignIn, onAgree, onDecline (update mode's Not now), compact, reduced, preview, landmark, animate}`; `Welcome.Body {headline, onSignIn, animate}` for screens drawn over it (sign-in).
- **Motion:** title, steps and action rise once with `.v2-anim-fade-up` (180 ms, `--v2-ease-out`), 120 ms apart (`.v2-delay-1`, `.v2-delay-2`); everything is in place by about 420 ms. Under reduced motion it is simply there.
- **Accessibility:** the title is the page h1; the list says "Step 1:" and "Step 2:" to screen readers (the discs are decorative); the action label is 4.92:1; the terms link is 6.0:1 (V2 2.41:1) with a 32 target and "(opens in a new tab)".
- **Responsive:** one centred column; the title balances its lines at any width.
- **Do / Don't:** Do keep the PM's steps and their order; open the Program Terms from the foot link; let the terms sheet come first on a first run. Don't add backdrop effects, a cursor morph or a gradient button; don't make the intro longer than reading the title takes; don't promise income (D3).

## 68 Sign-in steps

**Status:** Changed from V2.

**Why:** The steps work, but each carries a defect that costs trust at the first contact: an indigo spinner (`login.jsx:256`) that is a second accent; "Not seeing the browser tab?" in amber at 2.15:1 next to "Try again", two controls with one handler (`login.jsx:242-247`); "Logged in" / "Welcome back!" plus a Continue click on a first sign-in (`login.jsx:270-275`); the pill "Not enrolled" (`account_gate.jsx:26`), jargon; the consequence "App Player will be signed out too." at 2.54:1 (`account_gate.jsx:31`); a 34px close with no name (`login.jsx:81`); and a white 55% veil (`login.jsx:74`) that is not the site's gate.

### SignIn (Changed from V2)

- **Purpose:** the app's side of the sign-in handoff: one step at a time, one way forward each, on the same frosted gate the site uses.
- **Anatomy:** AppFrame chrome-free, the Welcome content underneath (inert), and the kit Dialog md (382, radius 16, `--v2-shadow-2`) with `scrim="frost"` (`--v2-scrim-frost` 88% + `--v2-scrim-blur` 14px, layer `--v2-z-gate`). Inside, a centred column, gap 12: a 48 mark (icon 24 in `--v2-blue-tint` / `--v2-blue-ink`; success uses `--v2-success-tint`), title 20/600 ink, body 13 ink-3, then the step's parts. Actions are lg full-width buttons.
- **Variants (the steps; V2 copy quoted, the rest proposed):**
  1. browser: mark `external`; "Check your browser"; "Finish signing in from your web browser." (V2 said "logging in"); Banner info "Use the account you applied with" (V2); one secondary action "Open the browser again". Close cancels.
  2. signing: Spinner 32 blue; "Signing in…" (V2); "Checking that this account is in the program." (replaces "Please wait while we complete your sign-in"). Close cancels.
  3. error (after signing, when the check fails or times out): danger alert mark; "Couldn’t finish signing in"; "Check your connection and try again."; actions "Open the browser again" (secondary) and "Try again" (primary). Close cancels (`screens/sign-in.jsx:59-70`).
  4. success: check mark on success tint; "You’re signed in"; the email. No button: Home after 900 ms by itself.
  5. gate (blocking): Avatar 56; "Signed in as" (V2), the email 16/600, "from App Player on this PC" (V2, case 2 only); Badge neutral "Not in the program" (was "Not enrolled"); "BlueAI works with the account you applied with."; the consequence "App Player will be signed out too." (V2) at 13 ink-3, right above the one primary "Switch account" (V2); under it, in the dialog footer, a muted external Link "Get help" (`SITE_FAQ_URL`, the site's FAQ) for a partner sure this is the account they applied with.
  6. confirm (option B only, decision D5, blocking): "Continue to BlueAI" and "Signed in on App Player on this PC" (V2); the account as one pressable Card (Avatar 32, "Continue as Alex" 14/600, email 13 ink-3, chevron); "Not you? Switch account" with an underlined ink Link.
- **Sizes:** Dialog md; the gate's avatar 56, confirm's 32; marks 48.
- **States:** the six steps; plus hover, focus and active on the account card and buttons (kit CSS); busy on Switch account while the session signs out. In V2: `?signin=browser` (handoff), `?signin=browser&player=other` (gate), `?cases=signin` (all cases).
- **Props:** `SignIn {step: 'browser' | 'signing' | 'error' | 'success' | 'gate' | 'confirm', from: 'browser' | 'player', account, onOpenBrowser, onRetry, onCancel, onSwitch, onContinue, onDone, doneAfter = 900, compact, reduced, preview, landmark, under}`.
- **Motion:** the dialog scales in once (320 ms); steps change in place; success's 900 ms is a timer, kept under reduced motion; the spinner turns at half speed under reduced motion.
- **Accessibility:** modal, focus kept inside, focus to the panel on open; the dialog is named by the step title (the gate by "Signed in as" and the email); step changes are announced through a polite live region; Close is a labelled 32 IconButton (browser, signing and error only); body and consequence text 6.0:1.
- **Responsive:** centred in the window, compact 640 included; long emails wrap.
- **Do / Don't:** Do keep one action per step; say the side effect before the button; move on by itself once the account checks out. Don't give two controls the same job; don't greet a first sign-in with "Welcome back!"; don't use warm colours for links or a second accent for spinners.

## 69 System and program states

**Status:** New.

**Why:** The worker runs on a schedule on the partner's PC, usually while nobody watches. When the PC is offline, the app is out of date, or the account is the wrong one, a day is at stake, and V2 designs none of these screens. After Day 20 V2's last words are "Day 20 done." and "That’s every day for this month." (`worker_day_done.jsx:93`), with nothing about cash out or what comes next, although the program has an end and a cash out (`worker_day_done.jsx:6, 38`).

### SystemStates (New)

- **Purpose:** what the app says when it cannot do its job, and around the program's end: what it means for today, and the one next step.
- **Anatomy:** AppFrame with the TopBar always (so the app is visibly alive) and the BottomNav whenever there is somewhere to go. The body is one column (`--v2-gutter`, 16 top), centred when it holds a single EmptyState (dashed ghost, icon 24 ink-3, title 16/600 as the page h1, body 13 ink-3, one primary). A condition banner is sticky under the top bar (`--v2-z-sticky`).
- **Variants (all copy proposed):**
  1. offline (nothing cached): chip Offline, no credits. EmptyState offline "You’re offline" / "BlueAI can’t reach the internet, so there’s nothing to show yet. Today’s run starts when you’re back online." + Try again.
  2. error (a screen failed): EmptyState error "Couldn’t load Home" / "Nothing is lost: today’s run and your days are not affected." + Try again, Report a problem.
  3. update optional: Banner info "An update is ready" / "It installs the next time BlueAI starts. Today’s run isn’t affected." + Restart now, over the normal home (WorkerCard, ProgramStrip).
  4. update required: chip Paused "Update needed". EmptyState locked, icon download, "Update BlueAI to keep running" / "This version can’t run jobs anymore, so runs are paused. Update now and today can still count. It takes about a minute." + Update now. It does not name the installed version or what the update changes: no screen shows the app version or release notes yet (an open item in section 76).
  5. not in the program: no chip, no credits, no nav. EmptyState locked, icon user, "This account isn’t in the program" / "You’re signed in as {email}. BlueAI runs on the account you applied with." + Switch account, then "App Player will be signed out too." (V2's consequence line), and a quiet secondary "Get help" (`SITE_FAQ_URL`, the site's FAQ, external) for a partner sure this is the account they applied with.
  6. program complete (Day 20 verified, period not over): chip "All 20 days done"; WorkerCard complete and ProgramStrip.
  7. period ended, cash out open (`cashOutOpen` true, section 41; while it is null the last sentence is "See your balance and cash out on Earnings."): chip "Period ended" (section 30's one rule), no credits. EmptyState "This program ended on Nov 1" / "You finished 18 of 20 days. Your worker won’t run again until the next program. Cash out is open on Earnings." + Earnings (the one Earnings URL, opens the site).
  8. period ended, under the minimum (`cashOutOpen` false): chip "Period ended", no credits. EmptyState "This program ended on Nov 1" / "You finished 6 of 20 days. Your worker won’t run again until the next program. You need $10 to cash out. Your balance carries over to the next period." (the last two sentences are the site's, `onblue/apply.html:2292-2293`) + Earnings as the quiet action. Never "Cash out is open" to a partner who cannot.
     In 7 and 8, when a day is still pending, the body adds "One more day is still being checked and counts once it’s verified." (or "N more days are still being checked and count once they’re verified."). Both use one string set, `V2Kit.endedCopy` (`kit/program.jsx`): the verified count, since cash out depends on it. Home shows this state, not a WorkerCard, once the period ends (section 57).
  9. between programs: no chip, no credits. EmptyState "No program running right now" / "The next one starts Dec 1. We’ll ask you to agree to its Program Terms then, and your worker starts once you do." + Earnings as the quiet action. Dec 1 is a PM-supplied value (`screens/system-states.jsx:176-184`).
  10. left the program (after Leave the program or Delete my data, section 64): no chip, no credits, no nav. EmptyState "You’ve left the program" / "Your worker has stopped and BlueAI no longer uses your accounts." then "Your verified days stay on Earnings, and cash out is open there." (Earnings primary) when `cashOutOpen` is true, "Your balance is under $10, so it can’t be cashed out." when it is false, and "You need $10 to cash out once the program ends on Nov 1. See your balance on Earnings." (Earnings primary) while it is null. `deleting` adds "Your data is being deleted. We’ll email you when it’s done." Secondary "Apply again" (the site).
- **Sizes:** the 419 window; compact 640 keeps the bars pinned and centres the content.
- **States:** each variant; busy on its primary (Try again, Update now, Switch account: spinner in the button, label kept, `aria-busy`).
- **Props:** `SystemStates {state, worker, busy, deleting, compact, reduced, preview, landmark, onRetry, onReport, onUpdate, onRestart, onSwitch}`; `SYSTEM_STATES` lists the keys and labels in order (`period-ended` and `period-under` are the two period-ended variants). The top bar labels here are section 30's chip labels. Settings, Worker settings, Connected apps and Account have their own loading and error bodies (`V2Screens.pendingBody`, `screens/settings.jsx`), not these.
- **Motion:** the EmptyState rises once (180 ms); nothing loops.
- **Accessibility:** the EmptyState title is the h1; error is `role=alert`, the update banner `role=status`; every primary has a 36 target and a verb label.
- **Responsive:** one column at any height.
- **Do / Don't:** Do keep the top bar; say what the state means for today's run or the 20 days; offer exactly one next step. Don't show a blank window or a wordless spinner; don't blame the partner's connection for a service problem; don't show money the program has not decided (D3; the $10 cash-out minimum is decided, both D3 options carry it); don't tell a partner under the minimum that cash out is open.

# Copy and voice

## 70 Copy and voice

**Status:** New (glossary); In V2 (PM copy register).

**Why:** A partner moves between the onBlue site and the app every day. V2 names one thing several ways: "BlueAI Worker v57 skill" (`worker_flow.jsx:63`), "Loading your skills…" (`boot_splash.jsx:25`), "tasks" and "earn rewards" for paid jobs (`worker_flow.jsx:45-46`), "Logout" beside "Sign out" (`index.html:378`, `profile.jsx:62`), "tokens" for AI credits (`ai_mode.jsx:109`). The same thing named five ways reads as five things, and the money words drift most.

### Glossary (New)

- **Purpose:** one word per thing, shared with the site, with the words that must not stand in for it.
- **Anatomy (term: use; never):**
  - BlueAI: the product and brand on screen; never onBlue (internal), "BlueAI Worker v57".
  - BlueAI worker (lowercase worker): the thing that runs, pending D2 (worker or agent); never BlueAI Worker, AI worker, MoneyMaker, skill, blueai-worker, agent (until D2).
  - job, paid job: one paid unit of work; never task (for paid work), campaign (the site should align: "completes campaigns and tasks", `onblue/apply.html:1849`), gig, opportunity.
  - run, today's run: the daily session; never routine, session, scheduled task.
  - day, your 20 days: the program unit; never program days, qualifying day.
  - program, period, your 20 days: the program is what the partner joins and what ends on a date ("This program ended on Nov 1"); the period is the program's dates, used with cash out ("after the period"); "your 20 days" is the count; never say the 20 days end.
  - pending verification, verified: one tick, two ticks; never confirmed, approved, validated.
  - Always ask, Automatic: the mode per app (the PM's names); never Mode (as a label), auto mode, autopilot, Just this job (as a mode).
  - Sign in, Sign out: account entry and exit; never Log in, Login, Logout, Log out (the site should align), logging in.
  - AI credits: the daily allowance, sentence case; never tokens, coins, free credits, claim, earn (for credits), balance (say "left today").
  - Earnings: money, opened on the site's dashboard; never income, rewards (the site should align: "Reward" on the program card and its sheet, "its own reward", "its reward is added to your earnings", `onblue/apply.html:1280, 1470, 1434, 1436`), wallet, BCX, a ↗ character (use the external icon).
  - cash out, Cash out: taking earnings out, which happens on the site's dashboard, from $10 after the period ends (section 01); the app only points there and says the minimum with it. Never withdraw (the site should align: its sheet says "Withdraw", `onblue/apply.html:1445, 1532, 1541`), claim, redeem.
  - Program Terms: the agreement; never Terms of Use (unless that document changed), Terms & Conditions.
  - connected account, Disconnect: the accounts BlueAI uses; never Unlink, ID, enrolled or Not enrolled (say "in the program").
  - Try again: the system retry; never Retry. The PM's failed-day ask says 'Retry'; the screens use the RunCard's and WorkerCard's 'Try again' instead, a proposal in section 73, and the AskCard specimen (section 37) keeps 'Retry' as the V2 reference.
  - BlueStacks, App Player: the dependency; never emulator, player app.
- **Tone:** second person, present tense, short declaratives, a reason rather than an adjective; the consequence for today's day or money on the control, before the choice; errors say what happened, what it means for today and the one next step; empty states name what will appear, disabled states say what unlocks them; sentence case, uppercase only for 11px eyebrows; no emoji in headings, greet by first name or not at all; never promise income; no version strings, slugs or internal names; no em dash (use a comma, colon, full stop or brackets).
- **Formats:** dates "Nov 1", "Oct 12" (a year only when it is not this one); times "11 am", "9 pm", "9 am to 8 pm" in the partner's local time; days "7 of 20 days", "Day 7 of 20"; money "$1", "$30" in ink, never green while pending; credits "1,640 AI credits left today"; the … character only for work in progress ("Signing in…"), never three dots (`chat_product.jsx:266`) and never on a button; curly quotes and apostrophes.
- **Variants, Sizes, States, Props, Motion, Responsive:** n/a (a reference, not a part).
- **Accessibility:** plain words are the accessible version; a label is the same word everywhere, so screen-reader users meet one name per thing.
- **Do / Don't:** Do look a word up here before writing it; use the site's word when the site has one, unless it is marked "the site should align" (each is a recommendation to the site's owner in section 02, P6, and the PM's call if the site keeps it); mark new copy "Proposed copy" until the PM approves it. Don't invent synonyms for variety; don't put versions or slugs on screen; don't use money words for AI credits.

### PM copy register (In V2)

- **Purpose:** the strings the PM or legal own. Design may restyle them, never rewrite them; changes go to the owner as proposals.
- **Anatomy (string; owner; where; status):**
  - "I only run a paid job after you say yes to that specific job."; PM; `worker_flow.jsx:67` (kept live by his call, `:18`); live, conflicts with Automatic (R11).
  - "Today’s run is done.", "No paid job today, but your run counts.", "Day 20 done." / "That’s every day for this month.", "Pending verification", "We check every run within a day. The tick doubles once it’s verified."; PM; `worker_day_done.jsx:91-98`; DRAFT until the PM approves (`:8`).
  - "Run this to begin" / "Okay"; PM; `moneymaker_home.jsx:36, 154`; live, settled after three rounds.
  - "An AI that turns your social accounts into income."; PM; `moneymaker.jsx:154`; live, decision D3.
  - "Sign in with the same account you applied with.", "Run the BlueAI worker: it finds jobs and runs them for you.", "Sign in to get started"; PM; `moneymaker.jsx:51, 55, 187`; live.
  - "Use the account you applied with"; PM; `login.jsx:239`; live.
  - "By continuing, you agree to the Program Terms and acknowledge the Privacy Policy and Cookie Policy."; legal; `terms_sheet.jsx:67-69` (wording rule `:49-53`); legal.
  - "Agree and continue"; PM; `terms_sheet.jsx:71` (`:60`); live.
  - "Run YouTube jobs automatically?" / "I agree, turn on"; PM; `agent_permissions.jsx:186, 191`; live.
  - "BlueAI will take paid YouTube jobs on its own and like, comment, follow and post from your account. Switch back anytime in Settings."; PM and legal; `agent_permissions.jsx:191-192`; live (V2 adopted the minimal consent dialog on 2026-10-14 (a plain frame with no header, divider or X: title, one sentence, a lighter second line, Cancel / I agree, turn on); its agreement paragraph is gone). Proposed change, sent to the owners (R51): "BlueAI will take YouTube jobs and do them on your account without asking first: watching, liking and posting comments in your name." (the ConsentDialog body, section 46): the worker never posts videos, and the comment it does post is not named.
  - "Always ask" / "Automatic"; PM; `worker_flow.jsx:78`; live.
- **States:** live, DRAFT, legal.
- **Do / Don't:** Do quote these exactly in every part marked In V2; send a proposed change to the owner with the reason. Don't fix a typo or a tone in them silently; don't move legal wording between contexts without legal.

# Scene and review

## 71 Scene (not product)

**Status:** Desktop scene: In V2. Taskbar attention and OS toast: In V2. BlueStacks install dialog: In V2.

**Why:** The fake Windows desktop makes V2 read as installed software, which is right for review. Its colours, sizes and fonts are OS or BlueStacks chrome, so they are fenced out of the product tokens (product-scene.css is never a source). What the product carries from it is behaviour and words: when the taskbar asks for attention, what a notification says, and what clicking it opens.

### Desktop scene (In V2)

**Purpose.** A Windows desktop that fits the visible windows into the browser at true proportions.

**Anatomy.** Wallpaper with a painted taskbar; a row of windows, left to right: browser, BlueStacks App Player, BlueAI; the BlueAI taskbar button outside the scaled row.

**Variants.** BlueAI alone (bare URL); BlueStacks + BlueAI (`?bs=1`); browser beside BlueAI (`?signin=browser`).

**Sizes.** BlueAI 421 x 866 (desktop_scene.jsx:18), BlueStacks 1000 x 573 (:17), browser 820 x 700 (:19); gap 16, padding 48, scale between 0.3 and 1 (:20).

**States.** Windows mount and unmount as the URL or Preview panel changes; the surviving window does not jump.

**Props.** `window.DesktopScene` (V2). Not a kit part.

**Motion.** Scene only (window-in, row slide). Not product motion.

**Accessibility.** Not product UI.

**Responsive.** The scale fits any browser size; it hides that 866 does not fit a 1366 x 768 laptop. The product answer is the compact frame (section 29).

**Do / Don't.** Do use it as the stage for review. Don't take a value from it into a token.

### Taskbar attention and OS toast (In V2)

**Purpose.** The two channels that reach a partner who is not looking at BlueAI.

**Anatomy.** Taskbar button: BlueAI logo with an indicator under it. Toast: Windows 11 notification (logo, "BlueAI", "now", dismiss), a bold title and a body.

**Variants.** Taskbar: running (short grey dash), open (longer pill), attention (7 amber flashes, then held; product-scene.css:141) while a job ask waits (index.html:1054). Toast copy, PM-owned and quoted exactly (worker_day_done.jsx:196-203):
- Run done: "Today’s run is done." / "Day {n} of {target}. Pending verification."
- Day 20: "All 20 days done, pending verification."
- Didn't finish: "Today’s job didn’t finish." / "Open BlueAI to retry and count the day."

**Sizes.** Toast 364 wide, bottom right above the taskbar, Segoe UI (product-scene.css:188-192); auto-dismiss 8 s (desktop_toast.jsx:12).

**States.** Toast: enter, shown, leaving, clicked (opens BlueAI), dismissed. V2: `?end=job | nojob | day20 | failed` plays the next day's run and raises the toast.

**Props.** `window.DesktopToast { title, body, onOpen, onClose, ms }` (V2). Not a kit part.

**Motion.** Scene only.

**Accessibility.** The real notification is Windows'; its text must stand alone without the app.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do open the persistent Today record from the toast, where Try again and its deadline live (R15); V2 opens a cold start with no Retry (index.html:1065). Do raise a notification with the deadline when a job waits on Always ask (R08, section 54); V2 has only the taskbar flash. Don't rely on either channel while the window is gone: the tray keeps the app alive (section 29).

### BlueStacks install dialog (In V2)

**Purpose.** BlueStacks' own prompt to install BlueAI (Flow A), in BlueStacks' visual voice by designer rule.

**Anatomy.** Dark BlueStacks card: icon, title, subtitle, body, meta, close, primary; then downloading and done steps.

**Variants.** One skin.

**Sizes.** Card 420 wide, square corners, dark BlueStacks surface, Poppins (install_dialog.jsx:46, 168). Downloading 2.8 s, done 0.9 s (index.html:603-604).

**States.** Idle, downloading, done. V2: `?bs=1&bai=0&dlg=blueai`.

**Props.** `InstallDialog` (V2). Not a kit part.

**Motion.** Scene only.

**Accessibility.** BlueStacks' dialog; not product UI.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do keep the skin. Don't keep the pitch: "finds deals, claims rewards, creates content, and more." (index.html:598) sells a general assistant, not the paid worker the partner was accepted for (R43; or drop Flow A, decision D9).

## 72 Review states (URL params and Preview panel)

**Status:** State catalogue: In V2. Preview panel: In V2.

**Why:** Every V2 state is a specimen, and a review link has to open on exactly the state being discussed. V2 documents its params only in a comment block (index.html:203-245), three of them only work in combination, and Copy link drops several states (index.html:753-775). This section is the index.

### State catalogue (In V2)

**Purpose.** Every V2 URL param: what it opens, what it needs, and whether Copy link carries it.

**Anatomy.** One table; in the guide each row has Show, which loads that V2 state below the table.

**Variants.**

| Param | Values | Needs | Opens | Copy link |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| (bare URL) | | | BlueAI alone, signed out: welcome + first-run terms | n/a |
| `?start` | worker, signin | | worker home, signed in as a new partner; signin is the default | yes |
| `?v` | 1, 2 | start=worker | which control opens the mode sheet: 1 the composer chip; 2 the ribbon above the composer | yes |
| `?setup` | off | start=worker | onboarding without the setup question | yes |
| `?day` | next, returning | implies start=worker | next: the next day's run plays; returning: home before today's run | yes |
| `?end` | job, nojob, day20, failed | implies day=next | how today's run ends: day-done card and toast | yes |
| `?card` | days, money, plain | day=next or ?end | which day-done option draws it | yes |
| `?mode` | auto | start=worker | YouTube on Automatic, consent skipped (the Mode row does the same) | yes |
| `?signin` | browser | | the sign-in handoff with the browser beside | yes |
| `?player` | out, accepted, other | other needs signin=browser | the App Player account on this PC | yes |
| `?browser` | accepted, other, out | signin=browser | what bluestacks.ai holds | yes |
| `?cases` | signin | | the five sign-in cases as a bar, no Preview panel | no |
| `?case` | 1, 2, 3a, 3b, 3c | cases=signin | which case opens | no |
| `?opt` | a, b | cases=signin | sign-in option A or B | no |
| `?ask` | any text | waits for sign-in | auto-sends the message | no |
| `?credits` | 0 | signed in to see it | zero credits: chip 0, banner, popup on send | no |
| `?bs` | 1 | | BlueStacks installed too | yes |
| `?bai` | 0 | bs=1 (alone it is forced on, index.html:271) | BlueAI not installed | yes |
| `?dlg` | blueai | bs=1 and bai=0 (index.html:723) | Flow A's installer | no |

Worker params ride on Copy link only from the signed-in worker home (index.html:762).

**Sizes.** Not applicable.

**States.** Each row is itself a state; a row's canonical URL is the one in the guide's Show.

**Props.** Not a kit part (V2 tooling: `QS`, `LINK`, `START`, index.html:210-273).

**Motion.** None.

**Accessibility.** Not product UI.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do link reviews to one of these URLs. Do make Copy link carry every state and every param stand alone (R46). Don't add a state reachable only from the Preview panel.

### Preview panel (In V2)

**Purpose.** V2's dark review dock (bottom left), the only door to about a third of V2's states.

**Anatomy.** "Preview" header with Copy link and collapse; Installed chips; rows of segmented options shown only when they affect the screen.

**Variants.** Rows and when they show:

| Row | Options | Shown when |
|---|---|---|
| Installed | BlueStacks, BlueAI | always |
| Start at | Sign in, Worker home | BlueAI installed |
| Session | Onboarding, Default, BlueAI worker | BlueAI installed |
| Terms | First run, Terms update, Off | BlueAI installed |
| App Player | Signed out, Accepted account, Other account | signed out, or App Player not signed out |
| Browser | Accepted account, Other account, Signed out | as App Player |
| Chat states | Off, On | the chat tab |
| Scheduled job | Off, Out of credits | the chat tab |
| Change mode from | Composer chip, Ribbon | the worker chat, signed in |
| Mode | Always ask, Automatic | the worker chat, signed in |
| Worker | Day 1, Returning, Next day | the worker chat, signed in |
| Day end | Job done, No job, Day 20, Failed | the worker chat, signed in |
| Card | Days, Money, Plain | the worker chat, signed in |
| Setup question | On, Off | the worker chat, signed in |
| Hardware | Yes, No | the Settings tab |
| AI conflict | None, Detected | the Settings tab |
| Own key | Off, On | signed in |
| Credits | Full, Empty | signed in, own key off |

**Sizes.** Fixed bottom left, 300 wide (index.html:1106); collapses to a 38 gear.

**States.** Open, collapsed; hidden under `?cases=signin`.

**Props.** V2 internals (`PreviewRow`, `InstallChip`). Not a kit part.

**Motion.** Tooling only.

**Accessibility.** Tooling, not product.

**Responsive.** Not applicable.

**Do / Don't.** Do keep it as tooling, following the onBlue site's review-dock convention (onblue-dev.js). Don't take its dark palette (index.html:881-887) into product tokens.

# Meta

## 73 Recommendations for V2

**Status:** Meta (no parts).

**Why:** Every difference between V2 today and the system, as the order to fix V2 in. The system’s sections show the fixed version. Each recommendation’s first V2 location, and every V2 string its "today" quotes exactly, is proved by `guide/refs/compose.js` (ids `compose.rNN.*`) through `tools/ds-check.js`.

- **P1:** breaks the concept, or costs a partner a day, money or control. Fix before the next iteration ships.
- **P2:** makes the product read as several systems, or hides the next action. Fix as screens are touched.
- **P3:** clean-up and polish: dead code, copy drift, review tooling.
- **Effort:** S is one part or one file; M touches several parts or a screen; L changes how state flows through the app.

**Do these first:** R01 (one Worker record, so nothing a partner opens can lose today’s run), R02 (one ledger for the day count), R07 (show the job, channel and exact comment before the partner agrees), R08 (a waiting job tells the partner, with a deadline), R03 (Sign out signs out).

### P1 (20)

#### R01 worker (effort L)

- **V2:** `index.html:965-981; blueai/chat_product.jsx:33, 79`
- **Today:** Opening Credits, History, Profile or any tab unmounts ChatScreen; the run, a waiting job ask, a failed-day Retry and today's result are lost.
- **Recommended:** Own the worker's day in one Worker record above the views; the chat, home card, RunCard and toast read it.
- **Why:** The product is an unattended daily worker; its state must outlive any screen (P1).

#### R02 worker (effort M)

- **V2:** `blueai/worker_day_done.jsx:40, 148; blueai/worker_flow.jsx:42, 168`
- **Today:** DONE_BEFORE=6 makes tonight's popup 'Day 7 of 20' and tomorrow's strip 'Day 6 of 20'; the strip appears right after Day 1 claiming 'Yesterday’s run verified.'
- **Recommended:** One ledger (verified, pending, missed, rejected days; target; ends Nov 1) feeding overlay, strip, toast, Activity and the site tile.
- **Why:** The day count is the partner's pay.

#### R03 shell (effort S)

- **V2:** `index.html:1010`
- **Today:** Logout confirm closes the modal and returns to chat; the partner stays signed in.
- **Recommended:** Confirm signs out, pauses the worker (say so in the confirm) and lands on the signed-out Welcome.
- **Why:** A destructive confirm that does nothing is a broken promise and a dead end for switching accounts.

#### R04 navigation (effort M)

- **V2:** `blueai/shared.jsx:222; blueai/jobs.jsx:140-142; index.html:375`
- **Today:** The first tab, Jobs, is 'Coming Soon'; Schedule shows Coin Master samples (schedule.jsx:5-8); the kebab's Chat History lists past chat sessions.
- **Recommended:** Nav = Home, Activity (run and verification log), Settings; Jobs and Schedule retire (the run window moves to Worker settings), and so does Chat History: runs live in Activity and the chat is one thread opened from Home.
- **Why:** Two of four tabs say nothing about the only thing a partner is here for, and a list of past chats is a second, weaker record of the runs Activity keeps.

#### R05 worker (effort M)

- **V2:** `blueai/settings.jsx:11-20, 144-159; blueai/worker_flow.jsx:67; blueai/agent_permissions.jsx:192`
- **Today:** 'Pause, stop, or disconnect anytime' has no control; 'turn this off anytime' has one since 2026-10-14, the Worker card in Settings (one switch per app). V2 adopted the minimal consent dialog on 2026-10-14 (a plain frame with no header, divider or X: title, one sentence, a lighter second line, Cancel / I agree, turn on), and it still makes the promise.
- **Recommended:** Settings > Worker (pause until, run window, mode per app, interest profile) and Connected apps (Disconnect).
- **Why:** Consent that cannot be found to revoke is weak consent (P4).

#### R06 worker (effort S)

- **V2:** `blueai/worker_flow.jsx:121, 149; blueai/worker_day_done.jsx:118`
- **Today:** 'Not now' on the job ask forfeits the day; the cost is told afterwards.
- **Recommended:** State the cost on the option ('Skip: today won’t count') or keep the job open until 9 pm, the day's deadline (section 41), with Undo. Decision D14.
- **Why:** Learning the cost after the click feels like a trap (P3).

#### R07 worker (effort M)

- **V2:** `blueai/worker_flow.jsx:49-52, 117-121, 136`
- **Today:** The ask shows only 'Watch the video and provide feedback.'; the comment posted under the partner's name is never shown; first chip bundles 'Yes' with switching to Automatic.
- **Recommended:** JobPreviewCard: video, channel, actions, exact comment text, consequence; primary 'Take this job', Automatic as a quiet link. Whether it replaces V2's AskCard for paid jobs is decision D17.
- **Why:** The riskiest moment in the product is agreeing blind to a public comment.

#### R08 notifications (effort M)

- **V2:** `blueai/chat_product.jsx:91-99; blueai/worker_day_done.jsx:191-205`
- **Today:** An unattended Always ask run waits forever behind a 7-flash taskbar cue; no toast, no expiry.
- **Recommended:** Windows notification 'A paid YouTube job is waiting for your OK' with the deadline; at the 9 pm deadline the run closes with outcome expired and the day with status notcounted (section 41; skipped means the partner declined); inbox entry.
- **Why:** Always ask is the default, so most partners would lose days silently.

#### R09 foundations (effort S)

- **V2:** `index.html:25, 351; blueai/credits_popup_parts.jsx:110`
- **Today:** White on #1990FF is 3.24:1 on every primary button; #1990FF links 3.24:1.
- **Recommended:** --v2-action #1268f0 for fills (4.92:1), --v2-blue-ink #0d5ad6 for blue text, --v2-blue #156efe for marks and focus.
- **Why:** Consent and Run are the least legible words on screen today (P7).

#### R10 foundations (effort S)

- **V2:** `blueai/chat_bubbles.jsx:124; blueai/account_gate.jsx:31; blueai/login.jsx:242; blueai/worker_day_done.jsx:147`
- **Today:** Caveats ('App Player will be signed out too.', 'We check every run within a day…', 'Working on it…') at 2.15 to 2.56:1.
- **Recommended:** ink-3 for anything the partner must read, ink-4 #6e737c (4.77:1) for pure meta; no warm link colour.
- **Why:** Unreadable caveats are not disclosure.

#### R11 copy (effort S)

- **V2:** `blueai/worker_flow.jsx:67 vs 76-78; blueai/worker_flow.jsx:46`
- **Today:** 'I only run a paid job after you say yes to that specific job.' then the same onboarding offers Automatic; PLAN step 2 promises 'a quick approval'.
- **Recommended:** Decision D15 for the PM (kept live by his call, worker_flow.jsx:18): a bullet that holds in both modes, for example 'You choose: I can ask before each paid job, or take them on my own. You can change this anytime in Settings.'
- **Why:** The first promise about the partner's account is broken minutes later.

#### R12 worker (effort S)

- **V2:** `blueai/worker_flow.jsx:171; blueai/worker_day_done.jsx:38`
- **Today:** The money rule (20 days, verification, end Nov 1, cash out) is never stated before the first day-done overlay; RULE.programEnd is never shown.
- **Recommended:** ProgramSummary in the onboarding close and behind an info tip on the strip; show 'Ends Nov 1' beside the count on the ProgramStrip (Home, Activity). The day-done moment keeps its bar without the date: it says the count once and nothing else (section 44).
- **Why:** Partners cannot plan around rules they have not been told.

#### R13 worker (effort M)

- **V2:** `blueai/needs_bluestacks.jsx:40; blueai/chat_product.jsx:64-66, 129-135; blueai/moneymaker_home.jsx:70`
- **Today:** The worker never checks for BlueStacks or App Player although the bare URL has neither and the copy narrates App Player.
- **Recommended:** Worker preflight: BlueStacks installed (DependencyCard), App Player signed in, YouTube linked, before every run.
- **Why:** The worker claims to drive an app that is not installed, then blames it.

#### R14 credits (effort M)

- **V2:** `index.html:730; blueai/credits_popup_parts.jsx:14, 105; blueai/login.jsx:160-167; blueai/chat_bubbles.jsx:188-189`
- **Today:** Grant 2000 vs 2500 vs 500 vs 300; 'enough for 4 to 5 jobs' vs '~10 tasks'; 'refreshed tomorrow' vs a countdown; nothing says whether a credits-dead run costs the day; a chat can spend the credits the paid run needs.
- **Recommended:** One DAILY_GRANT; one explainer sentence; countdown everywhere in local time; reserve credits for the worker's run or warn before chat dips into them (decision D6); every zero message states the day status.
- **Why:** Credits are the fuel for income; conflicting numbers make the meter untrustworthy.

#### R15 notifications (effort M)

- **V2:** `blueai/worker_day_done.jsx:174; index.html:1065, 780-787`
- **Today:** 'Open BlueAI to retry and count the day.' opens a cold-started home with no Retry.
- **Recommended:** Toast opens the persistent Today record; Retry lives on the WorkerCard and in Activity until 9 pm, the day's deadline (section 41), stated with it.
- **Why:** This is the exact moment money is at stake.

#### R16 settings (effort S)
