- **V2:** `blueai/settings.jsx:30-32; index.html:268-272`
- **Today:** 'Connected to BlueStacks App Player' shows even when BlueStacks is not installed.
- **Recommended:** Bind the card to the real state: not installed (Get BlueStacks), installed and signed out (Open App Player), connected as {account}.
- **Why:** False status sits where a partner debugs a failed day.

#### R17 shell (effort M)

- **V2:** `index.html:943, 562-564, 1056`
- **Today:** Close unmounts the app; Minimize does nothing; no tray.
- **Recommended:** Close hides to tray (first-time notice with next run time), Minimize works, tray menu shows run state and Quit warns.
- **Why:** A worker that runs when nobody is watching cannot stop when its window closes.

#### R18 consent (effort M)

- **V2:** `blueai/worker_flow.jsx:80-82, 161`
- **Today:** Watch history, subscriptions and channel stats are read without being asked.
- **Recommended:** PermissionRequest card in onboarding (decision D16) and a Revoke row in Connected apps; list the interest profile under Account and privacy.
- **Why:** Reading viewing data is a separate permission from 'use your account like a fan'.

#### R19 worker (effort M)

- **V2:** `blueai/worker_day_done.jsx:54, 63-69, 73-83`
- **Today:** Verification has only pending and verified; a rejected day looks pending forever.
- **Recommended:** StatusTick and DayBar gain rejected (with reason), not counted and late; Activity row offers Report a problem.
- **Why:** The two-tick model implies a day can fail; the partner needs to know and dispute.

#### R51 consent (effort S)

- **V2:** `blueai/agent_permissions.jsx:191`
- **Today:** The Automatic consent says the worker will finish jobs 'including posting videos'; the worker never posts videos, and the comment it does post in the partner's name is not named. V2 adopted the minimal consent dialog on 2026-10-14 (a plain frame with no header, divider or X: title, one sentence, a lighter second line, Cancel / I agree, turn on), and on 2026-10-14 its body became 'BlueAI will take paid YouTube jobs on its own and like, comment, follow and post from your account.' with 'Switch back anytime in Settings.' underneath: the comment in the partner's name is now named, as recommended, and posting stays because the PM's spec lists it for Automatic jobs.
- **Recommended:** Name what a job does, from `WORKER_JOB.actions`: 'BlueAI will take YouTube jobs and do them on your account without asking first: watching, liking and posting comments in your name.' (section 46). Consent copy, so the PM and legal decide it; the record keeps the version shown (section 53).
- **Why:** On Automatic the job preview never appears, so this line is the partner's only notice that comments go out under their name.

### P2 (21)

#### R20 foundations (effort S)

- **V2:** `index.html:10-20, 34`
- **Today:** Body stack puts -apple-system first (Mac shows SF Pro); four typefaces in the first minute; weights 650/750/900 requested but not loaded.
- **Recommended:** Inter first in the stack, weights 400/500/600 (decision D11 if the PM prefers Plus Jakarta Sans).
- **Why:** Reviewers and partners see different products; one face reads as one family.

#### R21 foundations (effort M)

- **V2:** `blueai/chat_bubbles.jsx:30; blueai/settings.jsx:25; blueai/shared.jsx:300`
- **Today:** 154 shadow declarations, 15 coloured glows (user bubble 30% indigo).
- **Recommended:** In-flow surfaces: 1px line, no shadow; shadow-1 for floats, shadow-2 for dialogs.
- **Why:** Quiet surfaces let the next action stand out and match the site (P5, P6).

#### R22 shell (effort S)

- **V2:** `blueai/shared.jsx:305; blueai/login.jsx:256; blueai/settings.jsx:73`
- **Today:** Indigo #4f46e5 on nav active, sign-in spinner, Telegram code; blue elsewhere.
- **Recommended:** One accent: nav active = 2px blue indicator + ink label.
- **Why:** Selected and primary read as two systems.

#### R23 foundations (effort S)

- **V2:** `index.html:335, 342-346`
- **Today:** Gradient on wordmark, pill ring, pill number, balance numeral, bubbles, borders.
- **Recommended:** Gradient only on the mark and the credits glyph; numbers in ink.
- **Why:** Brand identity, not a UI fill.

#### R24 components (effort M)

- **V2:** `blueai/shared.jsx:57-82; blueai/profile.jsx:69-84; blueai/byok.jsx:76-89; blueai/worker_day_done.jsx:129`
- **Today:** Seven overlay builds, seven scrims, five widths, z tie at 120, no focus management.
- **Recommended:** One Dialog and one Sheet on one scrim and the named z-scale, with role, focus in, trap, return and a busy state.
- **Why:** Keyboard users act on content under the scrim; each dialog looks like another product.

#### R25 components (effort S)

- **V2:** `index.html:289-301, 359, 370`
- **Today:** Header menus close only on outside mousedown; no role, Esc, arrows or aria-expanded.
- **Recommended:** Menu component with role=menu, aria-haspopup, aria-expanded, arrows, Esc and focus return.
- **Why:** The only route to Earnings and Sign out is mouse-only.

#### R26 infrastructure (effort S)

- **V2:** `index.html:24; blueai/worker_cards.jsx:28, 49`
- **Today:** Unpinned Tailwind CDN used for one unreachable class; its preflight hides the Plan card's numbers and AskBubble bullets.
- **Recommended:** Remove Tailwind; add the explicit reset in kit/base.css with list markers on.
- **Why:** An unpinned script can change base styles any day.

#### R27 motion (effort S)

- **V2:** `index.html:63-112; blueai/moneymaker_home.jsx:122`
- **Today:** Reduced motion honoured in 4 components; border spin (which cannot animate), shimmer, bounce run for everyone (the day-done ring went with the minimal popup, 2026-10-14).
- **Recommended:** One global reduced-motion rule; remove the border spin.
- **Why:** Perpetual motion on the screens partners sit on.

#### R28 worker (effort S)

- **V2:** `blueai/worker_day_done.jsx:119-138`
- **Today:** V2 adopted the minimal popup on 2026-10-14 (one tick, title, one status line, Got it): the count is said once, no confetti, no ring, money in grey. Still open: the 44 badge is verified green while the day is pending.
- **Recommended:** Keep it minimal; badge in pending treatment (blue tint, one tick) until verified.
- **Why:** Colour says paid while words say not yet.

#### R29 copy (effort S)

- **V2:** `index.html:329; blueai/worker_day_done.jsx:153`
- **Today:** Kebab Earnings and strip 'Earnings ↗' go to two different URLs.
- **Recommended:** One EARNINGS_URL constant; one external glyph.
- **Why:** The partner's money lives behind this link.

#### R30 settings (effort S)

### Decisions pending (D1 to D19)

Choices only the PM (and legal, where named) can make. The system builds the recommended option and keeps the other easy to switch to, with one exception: no part builds either option of D6 yet (section 76). A recommendation that waits on a choice names its decision (R06 D14, R07 D17, R11 D15, R14 D6, R18 D16).

| Decision | Option A | Option B | Recommended |
|---|---|---|---|
| **D1** Primary fill | Blue action #1268f0 (kit default) | Ink #0b0b0d like the site's commit button | A: blue already means the worker's actions in V2; links become underlined ink and selection a 12% tint, so blue no longer collides. |
| **D2** The noun: worker or agent | worker (V2) | agent (site, apply.html:1269-1274) | One noun across site and app, one capitalisation ('BlueAI worker'); PM call. |
| **D3** The money model | $1 per verified day + $10 day-20 bonus (worker_day_done.jsx:5-6) | $30 when the 20-day goal is met (apply.html:1285, 1471) | Pick one sentence and say it everywhere; if B, the app shows days, never partial dollars ('days' style is already the default). Rename the bonus 'day-20 bonus' if it stays. The $10 cash-out minimum is not part of the choice: it is V2's rule (worker_day_done.jsx:6) and the site's (apply.html:1360, 2292), so the app states it now (sections 01, 41, 43). Proposed strings that wait on this choice (PM and legal): the leave and delete line for a balance under the minimum ('a balance under $10 can’t be cashed out once you leave', `screens/settings.jsx` cashLine), and Home's dropped banner sentence 'Every day you run still counts.' (false under B). |
| **D4** Where the mode changes later | Version 1 chip in the composer | Version 2 ribbon (default) | B as the in-chat shortcut plus Settings > Worker as the permanent home either way. V2 added a Worker card to Settings on 2026-10-14; both versions now open one mode sheet. |
| **D5** Sign-in option | Check account (interrupt only on the wrong account) | Continue as (always confirm) | A for fewer stops; drop the losing code path. |
| **D6** Credits and the paid run | Reserve the worker's run cost from the daily grant | Warn before a chat would dip into it | A: a partner should never lose a paid day to an invisible meter. Not built yet: the composer locks only at zero (section 38) and the credits chip turns low once fewer than the run's cost are left (section 47), so a chat can still spend today's run (section 76). |
| **D7** Does Day 1 count | Setup continues into the first daily run and counts | Setup only; first counted day is tomorrow | Either, said in the onboarding close. |
| **D8** The family mark | BlueAI gradient wordmark | Site spark + two-tone 'BlueAI' | B in the long run (already in V2's sign-in pages); A until the PM decides. |
| **D9** Install from BlueStacks (Flow A) | Keep with worker copy and an 'Apply on onBlue' path for non-applicants | Drop from the partner target | B: partners install from the dashboard link after acceptance. |
| **D10** Platform promise | Site drops Mac and per-campaign approval | Product shows them | A until a Mac build exists. |
| **D11** Product face | Inter (site) | Plus Jakarta Sans (V2) | A; the scale and weights hold either way. |
| **D12** Chat as a tab | Three tabs, chat from Home | Four tabs with Chat | A: chat serves the worker. |
| **D13** Yellow for every worker question | Keep the live yellow on every ask (PM-confirmed) | Neutral asks in onboarding, yellow only for unattended job and retry asks | B so yellow keeps meaning 'needs you'. |
| **D14** Skipping a waiting job (R06) | The cost on the option, one tap: 'Skip: today won’t count' | Keep the job open until 9 pm with Undo | A, built in the JobPreviewCard (section 45): the cost is read before the tap, and nothing waits on a toast. |
| **D15** The setup promise (R11) | One bullet that holds in both modes ('You choose: I can ask before each paid job, or take them on my own.') | Keep 'I only run a paid job after you say yes to that specific job.' and drop Automatic from setup | A: Automatic stays a choice, and the first promise stays true. PM copy. |
| **D16** Reading watch history (R18) | Ask first: PermissionRequest in setup, Allow or Not now, revoked in Connected apps | Read it at setup as V2 does, listed under Account with a switch to stop it | A, built (section 46): reading viewing data is its own permission. PM copy. |
| **D17** The paid job ask (R07) | JobPreviewCard replaces V2's AskCard for paid jobs | Keep the AskCard and add the video, channel and exact comment to it | A, built: Chat's waiting state asks in the JobPreviewCard (section 45, screens/chat.jsx jobCard); B is one constant away (D17 = 'B'); the AskCard stays for every other question (section 37). |
| **D18** Feedback on a failed job (section 39) | Offer thumbs on failed jobs too | Successes only, as V2 (`chat_product.jsx:150`) | A: a failed job is where a partner most wants to say something; Report a problem stays the route for a dispute. |
| **D19** Turning an interest topic off (section 62) | A topic can be turned off, and BlueAI stops picking jobs about it | Topics are read only, from watch history | A, as Worker settings draws it; the PM confirms what it does to the jobs offered. |

### V2 strings changed as proposals

The PM's words are kept unless a change goes to the PM as a proposal (section 70's register). These are proposed in the system:

- The setup run window '9am–9pm' (`blueai/worker_flow.jsx:73-74`) becomes '9 am to 8 pm': runs start by 8 pm, so there is time to answer a job or try again before 9 pm (section 62), and times are written with spaces (section 70).
- The handle No line 'Okay. Sign in to the YouTube account you want me to use in App Player, then run the BlueAI worker again.' (`blueai/worker_flow.jsx:162`) invites another account, which section 56 and R39 rule out. Proposed: 'Okay. I can only use the YouTube account you applied with, or a channel under it. If you meant a different channel, report a problem and we’ll help.' with a Report a problem link (section 57).
- The failed-day ask 'Today’s job didn’t finish.' / 'Retry to count the day.' / 'Retry' (`blueai/worker_day_done.jsx:94`, `blueai/worker_flow.jsx:127`) becomes 'Try again' with the day's deadline on the RunCard and the WorkerCard, one string for both ('Today doesn’t count yet. Try again by 9 pm.', `DAY_COPY.failed`, `kit/job.jsx:47`; `kit/run-card.jsx:110`, `kit/worker-card.jsx:80-83`): one word for a retry everywhere (section 70), and the deadline the record keeps (section 41). The failed-day AskCard specimen (section 37) keeps the PM's words as the V2 reference.
- Chat's typed-message replies (section 57), none of which exist in V2: a status question answered from the record ('Did today count?': 'Today’s run is done. Day 7 of 20, pending verification. We check every run within a day.'); an action request, 'I don’t change settings from chat, so nothing has changed. You can set the run time in Worker settings.' with a Link 'Open Worker settings'; out of scope, 'That’s not something I can do here. I run your daily YouTube job, answer questions about your runs and days, and point you to the setting you need.' Chat's empty-state body becomes 'Ask about today’s run, your days, or where to change a setting.', since the worker never changes a setting from typed text.
- An AI credits explainer built around the daily run, an option for the PM and not shown anywhere: 'Your daily run and your chats use AI credits. You get a fresh set every day, and chats use the same set, so leave enough for today’s run.' (formerly `OOC_COPY.explain`). The AI credits screen keeps V2's 'BlueAI runs on credits' lines word for word (`index.html:534-546`, section 66).

## 74 How to add a feature

**Status:** Meta (no parts).

**Why:** The system only pays off if the next feature is built from it. These are the steps from a new task to a screen merged into V2.

### From a new task to a screen in V2

1. **Name the job it serves.** One line: which of the three jobs in section 01 it serves, and which principle in section 02 it leans on. If it serves none, question the feature before designing it.
   - If the feature shows money or cash out (a payout, a balance, an earnings figure), read the money rule in section 01 and decision D3 first (`design-md/01-concept.md:21, 47`; section 73). Until D3 is decided, show days, not dollars, beyond the $10 cash-out minimum. Cash out lives on the site, never in the app: link to it with `EARNINGS_URL` (`kit/program.jsx:120`), and read `cashOutOpen` (section 41), the only cash-out state the record holds; while it is null, say only where to look.
2. **Find its state in the Worker record.** Everything about the worker's day lives in one record (section 41). Name the fields the feature reads or writes and add them to the fixtures in `kit/worker-data.jsx`, never to a screen's local state, so no navigation can lose them (P1). One thing sits outside the record today: the Notifications inbox keeps its items as fixtures in `screens/notifications.jsx` (section 76 lists the gap). A feature that raises a new event adds its row there, its line to section 54 and its kind in section 60, and two code edits: the kind's `[glyph, tone]` in `ICON` (`screens/notifications.jsx:39-44`; a kind missing there falls back to `ICON_OTHER`, a neutral info glyph), and, if its row opens a new target, that target's row in section 32's route table plus the opts its screen needs. `open()` (`screens/notifications.jsx:111-118`) passes no opts except `{state: 'waiting'}` for `chat` and `rowTarget()` for `run`, so a target that needs a state gets its own branch there.
3. **Place it.** Pick the level from the navigation model in section 32: a tab, a pushed screen with Back, a sheet or a dialog. Give a screen an id in section 32's route table (opts, level, lit tab, Back) and handle that id in the host router that holds `{tab, stacks}`; screens only emit `onNavigate(id, opts)`. Check section 55 if it is a setting and section 54 if it has to reach a partner who is away.
4. **Compose it from the kit.** Use parts from Components, Shell, Chat, Worker and Credits. Each part's section lists its props, variants and states. Reach for a token, never a raw value. Add a kit part only when nothing fits (step 8).
   - Wrap a whole screen in `V2Screens.ScreenFrame`, the one frame for every signed-in screen (core, account and system states). Props (`screens/home.jsx:4-13`): `ScreenFrame {worker, tab, loading, compact, landmark, overlays, onNavigate, frame, openMenu, scrollTo, status, statusLabel, credits, nav, offlineBanner, onReconnect, className, children}`. Pass `tab` for the lit tab by the rule in section 32 (a top-bar door keeps the tab you came from), `worker` for the top bar's run chip and credits (`status`, `statusLabel` and `credits` override them, null hides), `onNavigate(id, opts)` for every chrome event, and `frame={false}` to drop the chrome inside a guide specimen (children and overlays come back alone). The screen's Dialog and Sheet go in `overlays`, never in `children`: AppFrame mounts them in its overlay layer over the whole window (`.v2-frame-overlays`, inset 0, `kit/shell.jsx:31`), so the scrim covers the top bar and the nav as well as the content. `nav={false}` drops the bottom nav; `offlineBanner={false}` drops the pinned offline Banner when the screen says it itself (Home), and `onReconnect` is that Banner's Try again; `scrollTo` moves the content band to a `[data-section]`; `openMenu` forces a top-bar menu open (guide); `loading` blanks the run chip, the credits, the Activity badge and the offline Banner while the record loads (`screens/home.jsx:138-147`); `compact` and `landmark` pass to AppFrame.
   - If the screen confirms its changes with a toast, wrap it in `V2Screens.ToastScope` and call `useScreenToast().show()`; AppFrame draws the window's one live region, so every toast is announced.
5. **Write the copy.** Use the glossary in section 70: Always ask and Automatic, AI credits, Sign in and Sign out, job and run. Say the consequence before the choice (P3). Mark every new string Proposed until the PM approves it. No em dash.
6. **Cover every state.** Loading (50), empty, error and offline (51), confirm or undo (52), consent (53). Then the floor: visible focus, labels on icon buttons, 4.5:1 text, 32px targets, reduced motion.
7. **Add it to the guide.** A section file in `guide/sections/`, its entry in `guide/catalog.jsx`, a partial in `design-md/`, a script line in `index.html`, and a ref in `guide/refs/` for every V2 value or string it quotes.
   - The partial and the guide section each carry the part's prose and spec values, and nothing builds one from the other (`tools/build-design-md.js` reads only `design-md/`), so write and change them in the same edit and check one against the other.
   - Write the section with the guide's blocks (`Section`, `Part`, `StateGrid`, `CompareRow`, `SpecDrawer`, `register`), documented with a worked example in the header comment of `guide/kit-guide.jsx`.
   - Write refs in the format in `guide/refs/README.md` (id, file, line, needle, value), in the slice's own `guide/refs/<feature>.js` (one file per slice, never another slice's), loaded from `index.html`.
   - Names follow the catalog's contract (`guide/catalog.jsx:4-6`): the section file is `guide/sections/<group>-<id>.jsx` and calls `V2Guide.register('<id>', Component)`; the partial is `design-md/<number>-<id>.md` and starts with `## <number> <title>`.
   - Numbering: a section number is a stable id that other sections and the PM cite ("section 54"), and the groups use every number from 00 to 77 with no gaps (`guide/catalog.jsx:10-21`), so never renumber. A new section takes the last number of its group plus a letter (a new screen after 69 is `69a`, partial `design-md/69a-<id>.md`, heading `## 69a <title>`); a new Meta section takes the next number after 77. Put its entry at that place in the catalog's `sections` array (array order is the guide's and DESIGN.md's order) and widen the group's `numbers` range ("57-69a").
   - A new kit part or screen also gets a row in the catalog's `kit` or `screens` array (name, file, stylesheet, status), which ds-check reads to prove it is on `window.V2Kit` or `window.V2Screens`.
8. **If it needs a new kit part.** One IIFE file `kit/<name>.jsx` that does `Object.assign(window.V2Kit, { Part })`, reads other parts lazily at render, `v2-` classes in a kit stylesheet, hover, focus, active, disabled, loading and error in CSS, tokens only, 300 lines at most.
9. **Run the checks.** `node tools/ds-check.js` to 0 errors and `node tools/build-design-md.js`. Then look at it in the guide by eye: the checks read files, not pixels.
10. **Merge into V2.** V2 here is a port of the PM's own source, so merge there. Load `tokens.css`, `kit/base.css`, `kit/motion.css`, the kit stylesheets you use and the screen stylesheets the screen uses (`screens/core.css` for the skeletons and core screens, `screens/account.css` for the settings-group column), then the kit scripts after React and Babel, then `screens/home.jsx` (ScreenFrame, ToastScope, ListSkeleton, LoadError and HomeSkeleton, the helpers step 4 uses) and every other screens file that holds a helper the screen reads: `screens/settings.jsx` (CONFIRM_COPY, useConfirmStep, ConfirmDialog, pendingBody), `screens/activity.jsx` (rowTarget, ledgerRows), `screens/help.jsx` (ReportProblemSheet, reportAbout), `screens/welcome.jsx` (Welcome, TermsSheet), `screens/connected-apps.jsx` (ConnectedAppRows) and `screens/system-states.jsx` (SystemStates). Screens read these at render and draw nothing, with no error, when one is missing: without `screens/settings.jsx`, Account's Sign out opens no dialog (`screens/account.jsx:109`). AppFrame holds the window's one toast region, so screens need no host of their own beyond ToastScope. Use the V2 today look until the switch (section 00, Two looks): link `tokens-v2-today.css` right after `tokens.css` and set `data-v2-look="today"` on V2's `html` element, so the screen matches the V2 around it. Build the screen with `window.V2Kit` parts, delete the V2 code the matching recommendation in section 73 names, and keep V2's URL states working (section 72) so review links still open the right state.
11. **Close the loop.** Once it ships in V2, change the part's status to In V2, move its row out of Recommendations, and add a line to the changelog (section 77).

### Worked example: Pause daily runs

How section 62's pause row was built, step by step, so each step can be checked against a part that exists. Proposed copy throughout, as in section 62.

- **Job:** keep the partner in control (P4: every promise has a control). V2 promises "You can pause, stop, or disconnect anytime." (`blueai/worker_flow.jsx:67`) and has no control for it (R05).
- **State:** the record's `paused` field (null, or {until}), section 41. The WorkerCard, the top bar status chip and Settings all read it; `numbers()` gives the cost (`toGo`, `daysLeft`).
- **Place:** a row in Settings > Worker (section 62), a pushed screen; the change applies at once (section 55).
- **Parts:** `ListRow` "Daily runs" with a `Switch`. Turning it off opens a `Dialog` (task variant, md): the `Select` "Pause until" first, then the cost for the date picked (role=status); turning it on resumes at once. A `Toast` confirms each change, with no Undo: the dialog already asked, and turning the switch back on is the way back (section 52, rule 1, for anything that "can cost today's day or future days": "say the cost before the choice, and ask once", and "No Undo: a day cannot be given back by a toast.").
- **Copy:** section 62 owns every string; the example only shows where each sits. The dialog title is the question ("Pause daily runs?"); the Select says until when, first; then the cost in days for the date picked, from `numbers()` (role=status), before the buttons; the actions are Keep running and Pause (danger: it loses days, section 52); a toast says what changed ("Daily runs paused.", "Daily runs are back on."); the row says until when and what it costs while paused.
- **States:** running daily (the switch on, the next run time); pause dialog open (the switch busy while it decides); paused until a date (the row says until when and what it costs); resumed.
- **Checks:** the switch is role=switch named "Daily runs"; dialog focus moves in and returns to the switch; the cost is in the dialog body, read before the buttons; ds-check to 0.

### Do / Don't

- **Do:** start from the Worker record and the job the feature serves; use a kit part as it is and pass props; say what a choice costs before the partner makes it; run ds-check before handing anything over.
- **Don't:** keep the worker's state inside a screen; restyle a kit part in the screen with inline colours; invent a synonym for a glossary word; edit DESIGN.md by hand (edit the partial and rebuild).

## 75 Coverage

**Status:** Meta (no parts).

**Why:** What exists, and where each count comes from. The guide's section 75 counts everything live from the catalog (`guide/catalog.jsx`) and the refs, so its numbers never go stale; this partial does not copy them, because a typed count is wrong after the next change. It lists what is counted, in the same unit the live section uses, and every kit part and screen with its status.

| What | Counted from |
|---|---|
| Sections | the catalog's sections, one per partial in `design-md/` |
| Parts, by status (In V2, Changed from V2, New) | the parts each catalog section names |
| Kit components on window.V2Kit, by status | the catalog's `kit` rows: one row per component, never a bundle of names under one status, the same rows as the table below |
| Screens on window.V2Screens, by status | the catalog's `screens` rows |
| Shared screen helpers | the catalog's `helpers` rows |
| V2 references the checks prove | `node tools/ds-check.js` prints the number it checked ("refs: N checked") |
| Recommendations and decisions | section 73 (P1, P2 and P3 counts, and Decisions pending) |

Most parts are Changed on purpose: the system keeps what V2 does and changes how it looks, reads and behaves. The parts marked In V2 are the PM's copy register and the scene and review tools, which are not product.

| rowTarget / reportAbout | `screens/activity.jsx, screens/help.jsx` | Where a ledger row opens; the about/topic a report opens with. |
| ConnectedAppRows | `screens/connected-apps.jsx` | The connected-account rows, reused by Settings. |
| accountStates / fixBlocked | `screens/connected-apps.jsx` | the record's YouTube and BlueStacks state, and a blocked run's one fix behaviour |
| ReportProblemSheet | `screens/help.jsx` | Report a problem; open it with about: {label, detail} to attach a run. |
| TermsSheet / TERMS_URL | `screens/welcome.jsx` | The Program Terms sheet (first run and update); reuse it, never a second sheet. |

### V2 shown live

Of the 47 window exports in the V2 files the guide loads, 27 render live as "V2 today": AccountGate, AgentPermissions, AiMode, BootSplash, BottomNavbar, Byok, ChatBubbles, ChatCompare, ChatFeedback, ChatHistory, CreditsPopupParts, HomeSkeleton, five Ico* icons, LoginModal, ModalOverlay, MoneyMaker, NeedsBluestacks, Profile, SignInPage, TermsSheet, Toggle, WorkerCards, WorkerDayDone. The guide also renders top-level components V2 declares without an export: SettingsScreen, AISettingsCard, BluestacksCard, TelegramCard.

Not live, and why:

- DesktopScene, DesktopToast, BrowserWindow, InstallDialog: scene, shown as V2 URL states because product-scene.css is not loaded (section 71).
- SiteKit, SiteSignIn: the site’s pages inside V2; sign-in is shown through SignInPage and URL states (section 56).
- MoneyMakerBackdrop, MoneyMakerHomeCard, MoneyMakerIcons, MoneyMakerLogo, MoneyMakerSteps, Shimmer: inner pieces of parts shown whole.
- WorkerFlow: data (the scripted conversation), quoted through refs.
- IcoArrowLeft, IcoBriefcase, IcoLightbulb, IcoPencilEdit, IcoPlusSmall, IcoTrashBin: replaced by the one named icon set (section 08).
- TopNavbar: dead code (section 76).

## 76 Known gaps

**Status:** Meta (no parts).

**Why:** A system is only trustworthy if it says what it cannot prove. These are the limits of the guide and its checks, the open items the build left for the part that owns them, and the V2 code that is not a source.

### What the guide cannot catch

- **How it looks:** Nothing here takes screenshots, by design. ds-check reads files, not pixels; layout, spacing and stroke weight are checked by eye in this guide.
- **Contrast over real pixels:** Ratios are computed from token pairs. Text over an image, a gradient or a blur is not measured.
- **V2 without Tailwind:** V2 loads the Tailwind CDN; this page does not (R26). Live V2 parts sit in a v2-preflight wrapper whose scoped reset mirrors Tailwind’s preflight, but V2’s few Tailwind utility classes (worker_cards.jsx) do nothing here.
- **V2’s own style block:** V2 index.html’s keyframes and ba-* classes are copied into guide/v2-live.css so live parts animate. If V2 changes that block, re-copy it; no check compares them.
- **Scene styles:** product-scene.css is not loaded, so the desktop, the Windows toast and the install dialog are shown as V2 URL states in a frame, not live.
- **URL states and localStorage:** V2 reads two Preview panel rows from localStorage (`index.html:261-265`): `bai_proto_start` (land signed in on the worker, or the real sign-in cold start) and `bai_proto_version` (which control opens the mode sheet: the composer chip or the ribbon). A link that sets its own start (`start`, `day`, `end` or `signin`) or version (`v`) wins (`index.html:246-254`); a framed state without them opens on what an earlier visit stored. Open it in a private window to be sure. The consent record is only written (`agent_permissions.jsx:85`) and the mode per app lives in memory, so neither carries over.
- **Shared V2 stores on this page:** Live parts share V2’s in-page stores: section 46’s specimens set the AgentPermissions mode, which chat’s AutoRibbon (section 38) then shows as Automatic. Use section 46’s Reset.
- **V2 modal markup:** The live V2 TermsSheet (section 26) renders V2’s own role=dialog aria-modal markup, so a screen reader may treat the rest of the guide as inert while it is mounted.
- **What a ref proves:** A ref proves a piece of text is still on its V2 line. It does not prove V2 renders it, or renders it the way the guide describes.
- **Recommendation wording:** Each recommendation’s V2 location is proved, and so is every string its “today” text quotes exactly. Paraphrases are the architect’s reading of V2 and are not machine-checked.

### Open items

Each is a small change in one place; none blocks reading or using the system.

| Area | Item | Closes it |
|---|---|---|
| Kit | TopBar passes balance and state to CreditsChip but not runCost, so “low” shows only when state is passed. | shell kit |
| Kit | Decision D6 is not built: no part holds back the credits today's run needs. The composer locks only at zero (section 38) and the credits chip turns low once fewer than the run's cost are left, so a chat can spend today's paid run. To build option A: CreditsMeter shows a held segment ("360 held for today’s run") and the composer gets a notice and locks at what is left minus the run's cost until today's run is done. | credits kit |
| Kit | The icon set has eye but no eye-off; the secret field shows its state with aria-pressed and a wash. | foundations |
| Kit | Numbered step discs and the one-serif-word title live in screens/entry.css; promote them if Help or ProgramSummary needs them. | display kit |
| Screens | The Notifications unread count has no place in the chrome (More carries no badge); what needs the partner is counted on Activity. | section 54 |
| Screens | The Notifications items are fixtures in screens/notifications.jsx, outside the Worker record. Give the record an inbox list when V2 adopts the kit. | section 41 |
| Screens | A failed RunCard has no Report a problem entry yet; Activity and Run detail open the sheet with the run attached (V2Screens.reportAbout). | screens |
| Screens | Search and load more are deferred. Chat History, V2’s one long list, retires (R04); Activity’s This program tab is at most 20 days and Past adds one row per program. Notifications grows with every event: before a partner’s second program, give it a “Show earlier” row or keep only the current program. | screens |
| Data | The Worker record has no Telegram link state; Settings and Connected apps take it as a prop (`telegram`). | worker kit |
| Screens | The app version and release notes are shown nowhere. Update required (section 69) asks for an update without saying which version is installed or what the update changes, and only the report sheet's diagnostic line mentions the version (section 65). Add an About row to Settings > Help (section 61): the installed version and a What’s new link (proposed copy). | screens, with the PM for the release notes |
| Screens | The sign-in mismatch screens type the second address instead of reading `V2Kit.WORKER_OTHER_ACCOUNT` (`screens/sign-in.jsx:27`, `screens/system-states.jsx:133`). | screens |
| Data | One real dashboard URL for Earnings (R29); the FAQ link is a stand-in next to it. | product team |
| Product | Whether feedback (thumbs) is offered on a failed job (decision D18) and whether a partner can turn an interest topic off (decision D19), section 73. | PM |
| Product | Whether Telegram carries the day-losing events (a job waiting, a run that didn’t start or can’t start) to a partner away from the PC; today it carries only the partner's commands (section 54). | PM |
| Product | How support replies to Report a problem and how soon; the sheet promises email only (section 65). | support |
| Copy | Every string marked Proposed in DESIGN.md waits for the PM; decisions D1 to D19 wait for the PM and legal (section 73). | PM |
| Site | The site's Cash out and program sheets set `backdrop-filter: var(--gate-blur)`, a bare length (14px), which is invalid, so they draw no blur (`onblue-vesper/onblue.css:1365`, `onblue-vesper/apply.html:634`). Wrap it in `blur()`. The app is not affected: its sign-in gate uses `--v2-scrim-blur` inside `blur()`. | onBlue owner |

## 77 Changelog

**Status:** Meta (no parts).

**Why:** Dated, newest first, so anyone building from the system can see what changed since they last looked. Add an entry for every change to tokens, a kit part’s API or a screen, here and in guide/sections/meta-changelog.jsx.

### 2026-10-14: Minimal progress card and plain consent frame in V2

- Consent dialog, wording and spacing: body 'BlueAI will take paid {app} jobs on its own and like, comment, follow and post from your account.', note 'Switch back anytime in Settings.' set 12px apart, title 10px above the body, buttons 20px below, and the plain frame's corners 20 to match the run-complete popup and the mode sheet.
- V2's home progress card is minimal (day + Earnings, 20 thin day segments, one verified line) and the consent dialog uses a plain frame (no header, divider or X); refs and sections updated.

### 2026-10-14: Mode sheet, one-line ribbon and Settings > Worker in V2

- V2's per-app mode is now one switch per app (on = Automatic; on asks for consent, off applies at once) in a bottom sheet opened by the composer chip or the ribbon's Change; the ribbon is one line (Automatic on YouTube, or a count); Settings has a Worker card with the same apps list; the Preview panel's Version row is Change mode from, plus a Mode row. Refs and sections 14, 21, 26, 27, 38, 42, 46, 53, 57, 61, 62 and 72 updated.

### 2026-10-14: Minimal consent in V2

- V2's consent dialog is now minimal (title, one paragraph, Cancel / I agree, turn on); refs and sections updated.

### 2026-10-14: Minimal day-done in V2

- V2's run-complete popup is now minimal (one tick, title, one status line, Got it); refs and section 44 updated.

### 2026-10-14: Two looks

- tokens-v2-today.css adds the "V2 today" look (every token re-valued to what V2 ships now, under [data-v2-look="today"]; tokens.css stays Target), eight look-hook tokens let the kit restore V2's gradient user bubble, indigo nav pill and soft in-flow shadows, the guide header switches the look, and ds-check fails if the two files define different token names.

### 2026-10-13: First version

- The system and its live guide, built next to V2 without changing V2: 78 sections (start, foundations, components, shell, chat, worker, credits and settings, patterns, screens, copy and voice, scene and review, meta).
- tokens.css: every --v2-* token, each with its role, its onBlue Agentic family link and the V2 values it absorbs.
- The kit on window.V2Kit and 14 screens on window.V2Screens, one catalog row per component, each marked In V2, Changed from V2 or New (section 75 lists them; the guide counts them live).
- Every V2 value and string the guide prints carries a ref to its V2 line; tools/ds-check.js proves them and enforces tokens only, no em dash, 300 lines a file and a catalog in sync.
- DESIGN.md generated from one partial per section (tools/build-design-md.js).
- 51 recommendations for V2 (20 P1, 21 P2, 10 P3) and 19 decisions pending for the PM.
- Compose fixes: --v2-success-tint is 8% (not the site’s 10%) so green text on it passes 4.5:1; new --v2-link-underline (the site’s 32%) under ink links; live V2 parts render inside a v2-preflight wrapper and with V2’s own keyframes (guide/v2-live.css); the catalog lists the shared screen helpers; Help is a menu and a sheet, not a pushed screen.
